Vol. II, Part I
[1]
Prāyaścitta Kāṇḍa
atha caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ
vāgīśādyāḥ sumanasaḥ sarārthānām upakrame |
yaṃ natvā kṛtakṛtyāḥ syus taṃ namāmi gajānanam || 1 ||
so ‘haṃ prāpya vivekatīrthapadavīm āmnāyatīrthe param
majjan sajjanatīrthasaṅganipuṇaḥ sadvṛttatīrthaṃ śrayan |
labdhām ākalayan prabhāvalaharīṃ śrībhāratītīrthato
vidyātīrtham upāśrayan hṛdi bhaje śrīkaṇṭham avyāhatam || 2 ||
satyaikavratapālako dviguṇadhīs tryarthī caturveditā
pañcaskandhakṛtī ṣaḍanvayadṛḍhaḥ saptāṅgasarvaṃsahaḥ |
aṣṭavyaktikalādharo navanidhiḥ puṣyaddaśapratyayaḥ
smārtocchrāyadhurandharo vijayate śrībukkaṇakṣmāpatiḥ || 3 ||
indrasyāṅgiraso nalasya sumatiḥ śaibyasya medhātithir
[2]                daumyo dharmasutasya vainyanṛpateḥ svaujā nimer gautamiḥ |
pratyagdṛṣṭir arundhatīsahacaro rāmasya puṇyātmano
yadvat tasya vibhor abhūt kulagurur mantrī tathā mādhavaḥ || 4 ||
prajñāmūlamahī vivekasalilaiḥ siktā balopaghnikā
mantraiḥ pallavitā viśālaviṭapā sandhyādibhiḥ ṣaḍguṇaiḥ |
śaktyā korakitā yaśaḥsurabhitā siddhyā samudyatphalā
saṃprāptā bhuvi bhāti nītilatikā sarvottaraṃ mādhavam || 5 ||
śrīmatī jananī yasya sukīrtir māyaṇaḥ pitā |
sāyaṇo bhoganāthaś ca manobuddhisahodarau || 6 ||
yasya baudhāyanaṃ sūtraṃ śākhā yasya ca yājuṣī |
bhāradvājaṃ kulaṃ yasya sarvajñaḥ sa hi mādhavaḥ || 7 ||
sa mādhavaḥ sakalapurāṇasaṃhitāpravartakaḥ smṛtisuṣamā parāśaraḥ |
parāśarasmṛtijagadīhitāptaye parāśarasmṛtivivṛtau pravartate || 8 ||
[3] śrīparāśareṇa ācārakāṇḍarūpeṇātītenādhyāyatrayeṇa vidhiniṣedhau darśitau | athedānīṃ tadullaṅghananimittapātityaparihāropāyapratipādakaṃ prāyaścittakāṇḍam ārabhyate | prāyaścittaśabdaś ca rūḍhyā yogena ca pāpanivartanakṣamaṃ dharmaviśeṣam ācaṣṭe | prāyaścittaśabdaś cāyaṃ pāpakṣayārthe naimittike karmaviśeṣe rūḍhaḥ – ity āhuḥ saṃpradāyavido nibandhanakārādayaḥ | yogas tv aṅgirā darśitaḥ |
prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittaṃ niścaya ucyate |
taponiścayasaṃyogāt prāyaścittaṃ tad ucyate ||
prāyaśaś ca samaṃ cittaṃ cārayitvā pradīyate |
parṣadā kāryate yat tu prāyaścittam iti smṛtam || iti |
anuṣṭhitena dvādaśavārṣikavratādināvaśyaṃ pāpaṃ nivartete – iti viśvāso niścayaḥ | tena saṃyuktaṃ vratānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇaṃ tapaḥ prāyaścittam | pāpino ‘nutāpinaś cittaṃ vyākulaṃ sat viṣamaṃ bhavati | tac ca parṣadā yena vratānuṣṭhānena prāyaśo ‘vaśyaṃ samaṃ kāryate tad vrataṃ prāyaścittam | cittasamīkaraṇopapādanaṃ “cārayitvā pradīyate” iti | vrataṃ cārayitvā cittavaiṣamyanimittaṃ pāpaṃ pradīyate khaṇḍyate vināśyate – ity arthaḥ | yad vā [4] pariṣady upaviṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāṃ cittaṃ yathā samaṃ bhavati tathā cārayitvaikamatyena vicārya pradīyate vidhīyate kāryate anuṣṭhāpyate – ity arthaḥ |
atredaṃ cintyate – kim idaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nityam, uta naimittikam, atha vā kāmyam iti |
atra kecid āhuḥ “prāyaścittair apaityenaḥ” ityādismṛtiṣu pāpakṣayasya phalatvenāvagamāt kāmukasyādhikārāt kāmyam | yathā vṛṣṭyādiphalakāmasya kārīryādi |
anye manyante – pāpaṃ nimittīkṛtya vidhānān naimittikam | yathā gṛhadāhaṃ nimittīkṛtya pravṛttā kṣāmavatīṣṭiḥ |
apare tv āhuḥ – akaraṇe pratyavāyadarśanān nityam | yathā saṃdhyāvandanādi | akaraṇe pratyavāyaś ca manunā darśitaḥ |
caritavyam ato nityaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye |
nindyair hi lakṣaṇair yuktā jāyante ‘niṣkṛtainasaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.53)
aniṣkṛtainasa iti chedaḥ | yājñavalkyenāpi |
prāyaścittam akurvāṇāḥ pāpeṣu niratā narāḥ |
apaścāttāpinaḥ kaṣṭān narakān yānti dāruṇān ||
tāmisraṃ lohaśaṅkuṃ ca mahānirayaśālmalī |
[5]        rauravaṃ kuḍmalaṃ pūtimṛttikaṃ kālasūtrikam ||
saṅghātaṃ lohitodaṃ ca saviṣaṃ saṃpratāpanam |
mahānarakakākolaṃ saṃjīvanamahāpatham ||
avīcim andhatāmisraṃ kumbhīpākaṃ tathaiva ca |
asipatravanaṃ caiva tāpanaṃ caikaviṃśakam ||
mahāpātakajair ghorair upapātakajais tathā |
anvitā yānty acaritaprāyaścittā narādhamāḥ || iti | (MDh 3.221–25)
atrocyate – naimittikam evedaṃ bhavitum arhati | nimittam eva prādhānyenopajīvya sarvaprāyaścittavidhānāt | tadupajīvanaṃ ca “brahmahā dvādaśābdāni” (YDh 3.243) [6] ityādiṣu smṛtiṣu vispaṣṭam | yat tu phalaśravaṇaṃ tat jāteṣṭinyāyena naimittikatve ‘py aviruddham | tasyāpi phalasya niyojyaviśeṣatvenāśravaṇāt na kāmyatvam | na hi “brahmahā dvādaśābdāni” ityādiṣu phalaṃ niyojyaviśeṣaṇatayā śrutam | ato na prāyaścittasya kārīryādibhiḥ sāmyam | nāpy etan nityam, tallakṣaṇābhāvāt | na hy udāhṛtāni vacanāni prāyaścittākaraṇe nūtanaṃ kiṃcid duritāpūrvaṃ janyate iti bruvate | kiṃ tarhi prāyaścittam akurvato ‘vaśyaṃ niṣiddhācaraṇeṣu pravṛttir bhavatīti etāvanmātraṃ pratipādayanti | etac ca “pāpeṣu niratā narāḥ” (YDh 3.221) ity atra vispaṣṭam avagamyate |
nanu – naimittikānām apy akaraṇe pratyavāyo ‘sti | tathā ca mahābhāratam |
sarvasvenāpi kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ vai rāhudarśane |
akurvāṇas tu tat śrāddhaṃ paṅke gaur iva sīdati || iti |
vṛddhavasiṣṭho ‘pi |
grahaṇe saṃkrame vāpi na snāyād yadi mānavaḥ |
saptajanmani kuṣṭhī syād duḥkhabhāgī na jāyate ||
[7] iti | evaṃ tarhi yatra jīvanam adhikāriviśeṣaṇaṃ tan nityam | yathā “yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhuyāt” iti | prāyaścitte tu na jīvanam adhikāriviśeṣaṇam | api tv anyad eva vihitākaraṇādinimittam adhikāriviśeṣaṇam | tasmāt naimittikaṃ prāyaścittam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ prāyaścittaprakaraṇasyopakramopasaṃhārayoḥ naimittikatvaṃ darśayati |
naimittikaṃ dharmajātaṃ gadato me nibodhata |
vihitasyānanuṣṭhānāt pratiṣiddhaniṣevaṇāt ||
prāyaścittaṃ yat kriyate tan naimittikam ucyate ||
ity upakramya,
naimittikaṃ samākhyātaṃ prāyaścittaṃ samāsataḥ |
ity upasaṃhāraś ca | tac ca nimittaṃ duritāpūrvam | tadutpattikāraṇaṃ yājñavalkyena darśitam |
vihitasyānanuṣṭhānān ninditasya ca sevanāt |
anigrahāc cendriyāṇāṃ naraḥ patanam ṛcchati || iti | (YDh 3.219)
manunāpi |
akurvan vihitaṃ karma ninditaṃ ca samācaran |
prasajjaṃś cendriyārtheṣu prāyaścittīyate naraḥ || (MDh 11.44)
[8]
nanu – ninditasevanād bhāvarūpād duritotpattāv api vihitākaraṇād abhāvāt kathaṃ duritotpattiḥ | na hy abhāvād bhāva utpadyamānaḥ kvacid dṛṣṭaḥ |
atra kecid āhuḥ – saṃdhyāvandanādivihitānanuṣṭhānam agnihotrādyanadhikārarūpāśucitvadyotakam | ayam evāraḥ “akurvan pratyavaiti” ity anenābhidhīyate | na tv anuṣṭhānābhāvād bhāvarūpasya duritasyotpattir abhidhīyate | na cānadhikāraḥ prāyaścittena nivartanīyaḥ – iti |
apare punar āhuḥ – “akurvan” iti lakṣaṇārthe śatṛpratyayaḥ | yad etad vihitākaraṇaṃ tad etat prāgbhavīyaniṣiddhācaraṇajanyaduritāpūrvasadbhāvasya liṅgam | tad eva duritaṃ prāyaścittena nivartate iti |
anye tv evaṃ samādadhate – abhāvād bhāvo notpadyate iti nāyam ekāntaḥ, tārkikamate prāgabhāvasya kāraṇatvāt | tārkikamate prāgabhāvasya kāraṇatvāt | mīmāṃsakamate – bhāṭṭais tāvad abhāvād bhāvotpattir abhyupagatā | yogyānupalabdhilakṣaṇād abhāvāt pramāṇād ghaṭādyabhāvaviṣayapramiter bhāvarūpāyā jananāt | prābhākaraiś cābhāvo bhāva evābhyupagamyate –
[9]        bhāvāntaram abhāvo hi kayācit tu vyapekṣayā |
ity udīraṇāt | tathā ca sati vihitākaraṇam api bhāvāntaram iti kṛtvā tasmāt pratyavāya utpadyatāṃ kā tava hāniḥ |
athocyeta – pratyavāyo nāma duritāpūrvam | tac ca kṛtisādhyatve sati kṛtyuddeśyam | tathā ca vihitākaraṇasya kṛtirūpatvābhāvād apūrvajanakatvaṃ nāstīti | nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | tasya lakṣaṇasya vihitapratiṣiddhakarmajanyāpūrvaviṣayatvena saṅkocanīyatvāt | anyathopekṣālasyābhyām akṛtirūpābhyāṃ duritāpurvānutpādaprasaṅgāt | upekṣājanyaṃ ca duritāpūrvaṃ skandapurāṇe darśitam |
nābhirakṣanti ye śaktā dīnaṃ cāturam āśritam |
ārttaṃ na cānukampante te vai nirayagāminaḥ || iti |
ālasyajanyaṃ cāpamṛtyunimittaṃ duritāpūrvaṃ manunā pradarśitam |
anabhyāsāc ca vedānām ācārasya ca laṅghanāt |
ālasyā annadoṣāc ca mṛtyur viprān jighāṃsati || iti |
atha kathaṃcit tatra kṛtiḥ saṃpādyeta tarhy atrāpi vihitollaṅghanalakṣaṇā kṛtir astu | evaṃ ca sati bahūni smṛtivacanāny akaraṇe pratyavāyaparāṇi svārasyenārthavanti bhaviṣyanti | īśvaravādināṃ tu vihitākaraṇasyājñollaṅghanarūpatvāt kṛtirūpatvaṃ vispaṣṭam | ata eva smaryate |
śrutismṛtī mamaivājñe yas te ullaṅghya vartate |
ājñācchedī mama dveṣī na sa bhakto na vaiṣṇavaḥ ||
[10] iti | sarvathā vihitam akurvataḥ prāyaścittanimittaṃ duritāpūrvam asty eva | ninditasevane tv avivādaṃ durityāpūrvam |
nanu – “anigrahāc cendriyāṇām” (YDh 3.219) iti pṛthagupādānam ayuktam | vihitākaraṇān ninditasevanāc cānyasya tṛtīyasya duritahetor abhāvāt |
maivam, asyobhayātmakatvena tṛtīyatvopapatteḥ | tathā hi,
indriyārtheṣu sarveṣu na prasajyeta kāmataḥ | (MDh 4.16)
iti indriyaprasakter ninditatvaṃ śābdam | snātakavrataprakaraṇamadhye pāṭhāt prasaktipratiṣedhasaṃkalpasya tatra vidheyatvenāvagamāt vihitatvam. ārthikam | tathā ca anigrahasyobhayollaṅghanarūpatvāt tṛtīyanimittatvena pṛthagupādānam | tad evaṃ vihitākaraṇādinimittavantaṃ prati codanān naimittikaṃ prāyaścittam |
nanu – gautamasmṛtāv asya kāmyatvaṃ pratīyate | phalabhāvābhāvāv upajīvya pūrvottarapakṣābhyāṃ tasya kartavyatvanirṇayāt | tadvacanaṃ ca:
ukto varṇadharmaś cāśramadharmaś ca | atha khalv ayaṃ puruṣo yāpyena karmaṇā lipyate yathaitad ayājyayājanam abhakṣyabhakṣaṇam avandyavandanaṃ śiṣṭasyākriyā pratiṣiddhasevanam iti | tatra prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt, [11] na kuryāt iti mīmāṃsante | na kuryād ity āhuḥ, na hi karma kṣīyate iti | kuryād ity apare | punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyantīti vijñāyate | vrātyastomeneṣṭvā tarati sarvaṃ pāpmānam | tarati brahmahatyāṃ yo ‘śvamedhena yajate |
iti | (GDh 19.1–10)
ayam arthaḥ – yāpyaṃ garhitam ayājyayājanādi, tatkṛtasya lepasya nivartayitum aśakyatvāt | tannivṛttaye prāyaścittaṃ na kartavyam | na hi kṛtaṃ karma bhogam antareṇa kṣapayituṃ śakyate | ata eva smṛtyantaram |
nābhuktaṃ kṣīyante karma kalpakoṭiśatair api |
avaśyam anubhoktavyaṃ kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśubham || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi |
yathā pṛthivyāṃ bījāni ratnāni nidhayo yathā |
evam ātmani karmāṇi niṣṭhanti prabhavanti ca || iti | (ŚaṅSm 17.63)
yady antareṇāpi bhogaṃ duritalepaḥ kṣīyeta tadā sukṛtalepo ‘pi kṣīyetety atiprasaṅgaḥ | tasmāt prāyaścittaṃ na kartavyam iti pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ āhuḥ |
[12]        kuryād eva prāyaścittam iti prāmāṇikānāṃ darśanam | te hi śrutim udāharanti: “punaḥstomeneṣṭvā punaḥ savanam āyanti” iti | ayājyajājanādibhir nityakarmādhikārād bhraṣṭā aikāhikeneṣṭvā savanatrayasādhyaṃ karma punaḥ prāpnuvanti tatrādhikriyante iti yāvat | “nābhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma” ity akṛtaprāyaścittaduritaviṣayaṃ sukṛtaviṣayaṃ ca | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kadācit sukṛtaṃ karma kūṭastham iva tiṣṭhati |
majjamānasya saṃsāre yāvat tasmād vimucyate || iti |
yadi “nābhuktam” iti śāstram asaṅkocena niraṅkuśaṃ pravarteta tadā pāpakṣayapratipādikāḥ sarvāḥ śrutaḥ smṛtayaś ca kupyeran | tasmāt pāpakṣayāya prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam iti siddhāntābhidhānāt prāyaścittaṃ kāmyam, na tu naimittikam iti | naiṣa doṣaḥ, jāteṣṭidṛṣṭāntena dattottaratvāt, anyathā pūrvodahṛtabṛhaspativacanavirodhāt |
nanu – naimittikatve grahaṇasnānasyeva prāyaścittasyākaraṇe duritāntaram utpadyeta, tatas tasyāpi prāyaścittāntaram, tasyāpy akaraṇe duritāntaraṃ prāyaścittāntaram ity evam anavasthā duravasthā syāt |
[13]
atra kecid āhuḥ – prāyaścittāni na kevalaṃ naimittikāni, kiṃ tu duritakṣyārthāny api | ata eva āpastambaḥ prāyaścittāni prakramya “doṣanirghātārthāni bhavanti sma” (ĀpDh 1.8.23.3) ity uktvā, anantaram “doṣāt kartavyāni” (ĀpDh 1.8.23.4) ity āha sma | evaṃ ca sati prāyaścittākaraṇe doṣanirghātābhāvena pūrvasiddhadoṣas tadavastha ity etāvanmātraṃ na tu doṣānaram utpadyate – iti | yad vā grahaṇasnānādyakaraṇam iva prāyaścittākaraṇaṃ nimittīkṛtya prāyaścittāntaravidhānasyādarśanāt tannimittaduritāntaraṃ na kalpayituṃ śakyam | tasmāt naimittikatve ‘pi nānavasthā |
nanu – doṣānirghāto ‘pi naikāntikaḥ | tathā hi, dvividhaṃ pāpam | kāmakṛtam akāmakṛtaṃ ca | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
kāmākāmakṛtaṃ teṣāṃ mahāpāpaṃ dvidhā smṛtam | iti |
tayor akāmakṛtasya prāyaścittena nirghāte ‘pi na kāmakṛtasya so ‘sti | tathā ca manuḥ |
iyaṃ viśuddhir uditā pramāpyākāmato dvijam |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || (MDh 11.89)
[14] iti | baudhāyano ‘pi |
amatyā brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā duṣṭo bhavati dharmataḥ |
ṛṣayo niṣkṛtiṃ tasya vadanty amatipūrvake ||
matipūrvaṃ hate tasmin niṣkṛtir nopalabhyate || iti | (BDh 2.1.6)
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | dvādaśābdādinā niṣkṛtyabhāve ‘pi bhṛgupatanādinā tatsaṃbhavāt | tathā ca smaryate |
yaḥ kāmato naraḥ kuryān mahāpāpaṃ kathaṃcana |
na tasya niṣkṛtir dṛṣṭā bhṛgvagnipatanād ṛte || iti |
śātātapo ‘pi: “akāmāvāptau prāyaścittam | kāmakārakṛte tv ātmānam avasādayet” iti | ataḥ prāyaścittena doṣanirghāto nānaikāntikaḥ | tasmān nirhartavyadoṣākhyanimittavān atra prāyaścitte ‘dhikriyate – iti siddham | te ca doṣā anekavidhāḥ | tatra viṣṇuḥ:
atha puruṣasya kāmakrodhalobhākhyaṃ riputrayaṃ sughoraṃ bhavati | tenāyaṃ samākrānto ‘tipātakamahāpātakānupātakopapātakeṣu pravartate | jātibhraṃśakareṣu saṃkarīkaraṇeṣu apātrīkaraṇeṣu [15] malāvaheṣu prakīrṇakeṣu ca | mātṛgamanaṃ duhitṛgamanaṃ snuṣāgamanam ity atipātakāni | brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇaṃ gurudāragamanam iti mahāpātakāni | tatsaṃyogaś ca | yāgasthasya kṣatriyasya vaiśyasya ca vadho rajasvalāyāś cāntarvatnyāś cātrigotrāyā avijñātasya garbhasya śaraṇāgatasya ca ghātanaṃ brahmahatyāsamāni | kūṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ ity etau surāpānasamau | brāhmaṇabhūmyapaharaṇaṃ suvarṇasteyasamam | pitṛvyamātāmahamātula-śvaśuranṛpapatnyabhigamanaṃ gurudāragamanasamam | pitṛṣvasṛmātṛṣvasṛgamanaṃ gurudāragamanasamam | pitṛṣvasṛmātṛśvasṛgamanaṃ ca | śrotriyartvigupādhyāyamitrapatnyabhigamanaṃ ca | svasuḥsakhyāḥ sagotrāyā uttamavarṇāyāḥ kumāryā rajasvalāyāḥ śaraṇāgatāyāḥ pravrajitāyā nikṣiptāyāś ca |
anupātakinas tv ete mahāpātakino yathā | (ViDh 33.1 — 36.7)
[16] iti | manur api |
govadho ‘yājyasaṃyājyaṃ pāradāryātmavikrayaḥ |
gurumātṛpitṛtyāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya ca ||
parivittitā cānujena parivedanam eva ca |
tayor dānaṃ ca kanyāyās tayor eva ca yājanam ||
kanyāyā duṣaṇaṃ caiva vārdhuṣitvaṃ vratacyutiḥ |
taṭākārāmadārāṇām apatyasya ca vikrayaḥ ||
vrātyatā bāndhavatyāgo bhṛtyādhyāpanam eva ca |
bhṛtāc cādhyayanādānam apaṇyānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ ||
sarvākareṣv adhīkāro mahāyantrapravartanam |
hiṃsauṣadhīnāṃ stryājīvo ‘bhicāro mūlakarma ca ||
indhanārtham aśuṣkāṇāṃ drumāṇām avapātanam |
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho ninditānnādanaṃ tathā ||
[17]        anāhitāgnitā stainyam ṛṇānāṃ cānapakriyā |
asacchāstrādhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasya ca kriyā ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam ||
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥkṛtyā ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātibhraṃśakaraṃ smṛtam ||
kharāśvoṣṭramṛgebhānām ajāvikavadhas tathā |
saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ mīnāhisahiṣasya ca ||
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ śūdrasevanam |
apātrīkaraṇaṃ jñeyam asatyasya ca bhāṣaṇam ||
kṛmikīṭavayohatyā madyānugatabhojanam |
phalaidhaḥ kusumasteyam adhairyaṃ ca malāvaham || iti | (MDh 11.59–70)
jātūkarṇyo ‘pi |
skanditaṃ vratinā reto yena syāt brahmacāriṇā |
kāmato ‘kāmataḥ prāhur avakīrṇīti taṃ budhāḥ || iti |
avaśiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ prakīrṇakaśabdavācyam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “yad anuktaṃ tat prakīrṇakam” (ViDh 42.1) [18] iti | tatrāyam ācārya idaṃ prāyaścittakāṇḍaṃ prakīrṇakair upakramya mahāpātakātipātakair upasañjihīrṣati | pūrvādhyāyāvasāne āśaucaprasaṅgena prakīrṇakasyaiva buddhisthatvāt | atrodbandhanamṛtānugamanāgnidānādes taptakṛcchraṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhitsus (?) tadanugamanādikaṃ ninditum ādāv udbandhanamaraṇasyātikaṣṭatvaṃ pratijājīte |
atimānād atikrodhāt snehād vā yadi vā bhayāt |
udbadhnīyāt strī pumān vā gatir eṣā vidhīyate || 1 ||
mātṛbhāryādyadhikṣepādijanyā mānahānir atimānaḥ | putramitrādiṣv īṣatpratikūleṣu satsv aparādhālpatvam aparāmṛśya kriyamāṇo vegāviṣṭo mahān kopo ‘tikrodhaḥ | snehabhayayor apy atiśabdo 'nuṣañjanīyaḥ | anyathā mumūrṣānupapatteḥ | atisnehasya mumūrṣāhetutvaṃ droṇācārya-daśarathādau prasiddham | atibhayasya cāparādhini bhṛtyadau | nimittavaiṣamye 'pi durmaraṇatvaṃ samam iti darṣayitum anekanimittopanyāsaḥ | kiṃcit striyaṃ praty eva niṣiddhyate | tad yathā dharme svātantryam: "asvatantrā dharme strī" (GDh 18.1) iti gautamasmaraṇāt | kiṃcit puruṣaṃ praty eva niṣidhyate | tad yathā -
[19]                 yo 'nadhītya dvijo vedān anyatra kurute śramam |
sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ || iti | [MDh 2.168]
udbandhanaṃ tu dvayos tulyam — iti vivakṣayā "strī pumān vā" ity uktam | gatir api narakaprāptiḥ | eṣā samanantaraślokena vakṣyamāṇā vidhīyate jñāpyate || 1 ||
pratijñātāṃ gatiṃ darśayati —
pūyaśoṇitasaṃpūrṇe tv andhe tamasi majjati |
ṣaṣṭhīr varṣasahasrāṇi narakaṃ pratipadyate || 2 ||
andhaṃtamas tīvro narakaviśeṣaḥ | "tāmisram | andhatāmisram |" (ViDh 43.2, 3) iti udāharanti | tīvratvābhivyaktaye ṣaṣṭhīr iti atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā || 2 ||
astv evam atikaṣṭatvam udbandhanamaraṇasya kiṃ tataḥ | ity āśaṅkya phalitaṃ darśayan vidhitsitasya prāyaścittasya nimittaṃ ca saṃbhāvayann āśaucādīn pratiṣedhati —
nāśaucaṃ nodakaṃ nāgniṃ nāśrupātaṃ ca kārayet |
[20] udakam udakadānam | agnim aginidānam | aśrupātaṃ ceti cakāreṇa śavavahanādikaṃ samuccinoti | na kārayet na kuryād ity arthaḥ | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi, “śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam” ityādy anukramya, “patitās te prakīrtitāḥ” ity abhidhāyānantaram idam uktam:
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syān nāntyeṣṭir nāsthisaṃcayaḥ |
na cāśrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryaṃ śrāddhādikaṃ kvacit || iti |
śātātapo ‘pi |
vṛddhaḥ śaucasmṛter luptaḥ pratyākhyātabhiṣakriyaḥ |
ātmānaṃ ghātayed yas tu bhṛgvagnyanaśanādibhiḥ ||
tasya trirātram āśaucaṃ dvitīya tv asthisaṃcayaḥ |
tṛtīye tūdakaṃ kṛtvā caturthe śrāddham ācaret || iti |
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pāṣaṇḍyanāśritāḥ stenā bhartṛghnyaḥ kāmagādikāḥ |
surāpya ātmatyāginyo nāśaucodakabhājanāḥ || iti | (YDh 3.6)
brahmapurāṇe |
śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrinakhivyālaviṣavahnistriyājalaiḥ |
[21]        sudūrāt parihartavyaiḥ kurvan krīḍāṃ mṛtas tu yaḥ |
nāgānāṃ vipriyaṃ kurvan dagdhaś cāpy atha vidyutā ||
vigṛhītaś ca yo rājñā cauryadoṣeṇa kutracit |
paradārān harantaś ca roṣāt tatpatibhir hatāḥ ||
asamānaiś ca saṃkīrṇaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca vigraham |
kṛtvā tair nihatās tadvat caṇḍālādīn samāśritāḥ ||
krodhāt prāyaṃ viṣaṃ vahniṃ śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam |
girivṛkṣaprapātaṃ ca ye kurvanti narādhamāḥ ||
kusīdajīvino ye ‘pi ye cālaṃkāravarjitāḥ |
mukhebhagāś ca ye kecit klībaprāyā napuṃsakāḥ ||
brahmadaṇḍahatā ye ca ye caiva brāhmaṇair hatāḥ |
mahāpātakino ye ca patitās te prakīrtitāḥ ||
patitānāṃ na dāhaḥ syān nāntyeṣṭir nāsthisaṃcayaḥ |
na cāśrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryaṃ śrāddhādikaṃ kvacit ||
etāni patitānāṃ tu yaḥ karoti vimohitaḥ |
taptakṛcchradvayenaiva tasya śuddhir na cānyathā || iti |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
bhāryāputravihīnāś ca hy agnisaṃskāravarjitāḥ ||
pakṣiṇaḥ pretarūpeṇa hy antarikṣe pralambitāḥ |
agninā bhṛgupātena hy udbandhena jalena vā ||
dantibhiḥ śṛṅgibhir vāpi viṣeṇa garalena vā |
corāntyajāticaṇḍālai raṇe vāpi hatāś ca ye ||
[22]        śrāddhakarmavihīnāś ca luptapiṇḍodakakriyāḥ |
teṣām uddharaṇārthāya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || iti |
aparārke |
mṛgabhakṣai raṇe caiva dāvāgnau prahṛtaṃ yadi |
śūle ‘py ardhaṃ kabandhasya maraṇaṃ pāpakarmaṇām ||
dṛṣṭacchedaṃ samādāya punaḥ saṃskāram ācaret |
vidyudagnipayaḥpanthācaṇḍālabrāhmaṇair hataḥ ||
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca paśubhyaś ca maraṇaṃ pāpakarmaṇām |
ātmānaṃ ghātayed yas tu viṣāgnijalabandhanaiḥ ||
tasya pāpaviśuddhyarthaṃ prājāpatyatrayaṃ caret |
ekadvitricatuḥpañcaṣaḍabdaṃ paryavasyati ||
etat saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
ṣaṇmāsād dviguṇaṃ proktaṃ trimāsāt triguṇaṃ bhavet ||
caturguṇaṃ tripakṣe tu sadyaḥ pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet |
caṇḍālād udakāt sarpād brāhmaṇād vaidyutād api ||
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca paśubhyaś ca maraṇaṃ pāpakarmaṇām |
udakaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ ca pretebhyo yat pradīyate ||
nopatiṣṭhati tat sarvam antarikṣe vinaśyati |
brāhmaṇena vadhe pāśe caṇḍālasya kareṇa vā ||
ātmanāśaḥ sanirghāte śūdravad dāhayet dvijam |
bhasmāsthīni gṛhītvā tu viprāṇām anuśāsanāt ||
[23]        kṣīraprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ saṃskāram ācaret |
yadi kaścit pramāsena mriyetāgnyudakādibhiḥ ||
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kartavyā codakakriyā |
caṇḍālāśaniśastrāhidaṃṣṭriśṛṅgyamburajjubhiḥ ||
vṛkṣāśmaviṣavipraiś ca mṛtānām ātmaghātinām |
nārāyaṇabaliḥ kāryo durmaraṇena mṛtasya ca ||
jīvato yad vrataṃ proktaṃ mṛte taddviguṇaṃ bhavet |
asthini triguṇaṃ proktaṃ pālāśe tu caturguṇam || iti |
idānīṃ durmṛtānāṇ vahanādau prāyaścittaṃ vidadhāti |
voḍhāro ‘gnipradātāraḥ pāśacchedakarās tathā || 3 ||
taptakṛccheṇe śudhdyantīty evam āha prajāpatiḥ |
tathāśabdenāśucodakadānādikartṝn samuccinoti | taptakṛcchralakṣaṇam vakṣyate | yat tu brahmapurāṇe ‘bhihitam,
etāni patitānāṃ tu yaḥ karoti vimohitaḥ |
taptakṛchradvayenaiva tasya śuddhir na cānyathā || iti |
etāni dāhādīni, teṣāṃ tatra prakṛtatvāt | tat kamakāraviṣayam,
vihitaṃ yad akamānāṃ kāmāt tu dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
[24] iti smaraṇāt | yac ca bṛhaspatinoktam,
viṣodbandhanaśastreṇa yas tv ātmānaṃ pramāpayet |
mṛto medhyena leptavyo nānyaṃ saṃskāram arhati ||
pāśacchettā tu yas tasya voḍhā vāgnipradas tathā |
so ‘tikṛcchreṇa śudhyet tu piṇḍado vā narādhamaḥ ||
iti, yac ca yamenoktam,
gobrāhmaṇahataṃ dagdhvā mṛtam udbandhanena ca |
pāśāñ chitvā tathā tasya kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || (YamSm 1.5)
iti, tad ubhayam aśaktaviṣayam, deśakālavayaḥśaktyādīnāṃ prāyaścittatāratamyahetutvāt | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
deśaṃ kālaṃ vayaḥ śaktiṃ jñānaṃ buddhikṛtaṃ tathā |
abuddhikṛtam abhyāsaṃ jñātvā niṣkaraṇaṃ vadet || iti |
śaktyāditāratamyavan nimittatāratamyam api prāyaścittatāratamyakāraṇam | ata eva prajāpatiḥ sparśādyalpanimitte svalpaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
tac chavaṃ kevalaṃ spṛṣṭvā pātayitvāśru vā tathā |
ekarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt trirātraṃ buddhipūrvakam || iti |
evaṃ nimittabhūyastve prāyaścittabhūyastvam | ata evāśeṣāḥ pretakriyāḥ kurvato ‘dhikaṃ prāyaścittam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
[25]        ya ātmatyāgināṃ kuryāt snehāt pretakriyāṃ dvijaḥ |
sa taptakṛcchrasahitaṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || iti | (VaDh 23.16)
prajāpatigrahaṇam uktārthe buddhidāṛḍhyārtham | tato virodhivacanānāṃ viṣayavyavasthā draṣṭavyety uktaṃ bhavati | sā cāsmābhiḥ pradarśitā || 3 ||
udbandhananyāyaṃ gohatādiṣv atidiśati |
gobhir hataṃ tathodbaddhaṃ brāhmaṇena tu ghātitam || 4 ||
saṃspṛśanti tu ye viprā voḍhāraś cāgnidāś ca ye |
anye ye vānugantāraḥ pāśacchedakarāś ca ye || 5 ||
taptakṛcchreṇa śuddhās te kuryur brāhmaṇabhojanam |
anaḍutsahitāṃ gāṃ ca dadyur viprāya dakṣiṇām || 6 ||
atrātatāyitayā gobhiḥ krīḍan yadi hatas tadānīm etad draṣṭavyam | prāmādikamaraṇe pātityābhāvāt | etac cāśaucaprakaraṇe 'smābhir upavarṇitam | pūrvatrodbandhanaviṣaye taptakṛcchramātram uktam | iha tu brāhmaṇabhojanādikam adhikam ucyate  iti nāśaṅkanīyam, parakartṛkodbandhanasyātra vivakṣitatvāt, gohatabrāhmaṇahatayor madhye pāṭhāt | na ca – parakartṛkodbandhane prāyaścittālpatvam iti vācyam | parakartṛkodbandhanaprasaktiṃ jñātvā pravṛttasyātatāyino ‘tra vivakṣitatvāt | gomithunadakṣiṇāṃ cyavano ‘py āha: [26] “ātmaghātakas-parśanadahanavahaneṣu taptakṛcchraṃ caret | vṛṣagāvau dakṣiṇā brāhmaṇeṣu” iti || 4–6 ||
taptakṛcchrasvarūpam āha |
tryaham uṣṇaṃ pibed vāri tryaham uṣṇaṃ payaḥ pibet |
tryahaṃ pibet sarpir vāyubhakṣo dinatrayam || 7 ||
ṣaṭpalaṃ tu pibed ambas tripalaṃ tu payaḥ pibet |
palam ekaṃ pibet sarpis taptakṛcchraṃ vidhīyate || 8 || iti |
vāyubhakṣaṇam upavāsaḥ | taptaśabdena śītakṛcchro vyāvartyate | tatsvarūpaṃ ca yamenoktam |
tryaham uṣṇaṃ pibed ambhas tryaham uṣṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ pibet |
tryaham uṣṇaṃ payaḥ pītvā vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
taptakṛcchraṃ vijānīyāt śītaiḥ śītam udāhṛtam || iti | (YamSm 1.12–13)
yat tu yājñavalkyenoktam,
taptakṣīraghṛtāmbūnām ekaikaṃ pratyahaṃ pibet |
ekarātropavāsaś ca taptakṛcchra udāhṛtaḥ || (YDh 3.318)
iti, tat taptakṛcchrasyāvāntarabhedavivakṣayopapadyate | yathā [27] sāntapanaṃ mahāsāntapanam iti dvaividhyaṃ tathā taptakṛcchraṃ mahātaptakṛcchram iti dvaividhyaṃ draṣṭavyam | palaśabdena suvarṇacatuṣṭayam ucyate, “palaṃ suvarṇāś catvāraḥ” (YDh 1.363) iti yājñavalkyavacanāt | [uktaṃ ca:
pañca guñjātmako māṣo niṣko māsāṣṭanirmitaḥ |
daśaniṣkātmakaṃ proktaṃ palamānaṃ budhaiḥ sadā || iti]
etādṛśaiḥ palaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ saṃmatam ambhaḥ pibet || 7–8 ||
patitasaṃsargaprāyaścittaṃ vidhātuṃ tatsaṃsargasya ninditatvaṃ darśayati |
yo vai samācared vipraḥ patitādiṣv akāmataḥ |
pañcāhaṃ vā daśāhaṃ vā dvādaśāham athāpi vā || 9 ||
māsārdhaṃ māsam ekaṃ vā māsadvayam athāpi vā |
abdārdham abdam ekaṃ vā tadūrdhvaṃ caiva tatsamaḥ || 10 || iti ||
atra vipragrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyāder upalakṣaṇam, manuṣyamātrasya tatsaṃsargasya ninditatvāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ |
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāt tatsaṃsargaviśuddhaye || iti | (MDh 11.181)
[28] “samācaraṇaṃ” sahayānāsanādi | tathā ca kaṇvaḥ |
āsanāc chayanād yānāt saṃlāpāt sahabhojanāt |
saṃkramantīha pāpāni tailabindur ivāmbhasi ||
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran |
yānāsanādibhir nityam ity āhur brahmavādinaḥ || iti |
yājanādau tu sadyaḥ patati | tathā ca baudhāyanaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunāt sadyo ‘nnaśayanāsanāt || iti | (BDh 2.1.2.35)
bṛhaspatir api bahuvidhaṃ samācaraṇaṃ niṣedhati |
ekaśayyāsanaṃ paṅktir bhāṇḍaṃ paktyannamiśraṇam |
yājanādhyāpane yonis tathā ca sahabhojanam ||
navadhā saṃkaraḥ prokto na kartavyo ‘dhamaiḥ saha || iti |
devalo ’pi |
saṃlāpasparśaniśvāsāt sahaśayyāsanāśanāt |
yājanādhyāpanād yaunāt pāpaṃ saṃkramate nṛṇām || iti |
etac ca pūrvasmin yuge | atra tu mūlavacanoktam draṣṭavyam, [29] yājanāder vakṣyamānatvāt | saṃsargaś cātra yājanādir grāhyaḥ | patitādiṣvityādiśabdena tatputrādayo gṛhyante, “patitotpannaḥ patito bhavati” (VaDh 13.51) iti vasiṣṭhena tannindanāt | vidhitsitasya prāyaścittasyālpatvaṃ jñāpayitum akāmata ity uktam | kāmataḥ saṃsarge tu viśeṣaḥ smṛtyantare ‘bhihitaḥ |
pañcāhe tu caret kṛcchraṃ daśāhe taptakṛcchrakam |
parākas tv ardhamāse syān māsaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
māsatraye tu kurvīta kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram |
ṣāṇmāsike tu saṃsarge kṛcchraṃ tv abdārdham ācaret ||
saṃsarge cābdike kuryād abdaṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram || iti |
pṛthak pṛthag vyavasthitaprāyaścittavidhitsayā pañcāhādyanekapakṣopanyāsaḥ | “tadūrdhvaṃ caiva tatsamaḥ” ity abhidhānāt pūrvatra tatsāmyābhāve ‘pi tato ‘rvācīnaṃ pāpaṃ kālatāratamyena bhavati ity avagamyate | yaḥ samācaret sa pāpī bhavati ity adhyāhṛtya nindā yojanīyā || 9–10 ||
idānīṃ kālatāratamyena pūrvokteṣv aṣṭasu pakṣeṣu yathākramaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidadhāti |
trirātraṃ prathame pakṣe dvitīye kṛcchram ācaret |
tṛtīye caiva pakṣe tu kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || 11 ||
caturthe daśarātraṃ syāt parākaḥ pañcame mataḥ |
kuryāc cāndrāyaṇaṃ ṣaṣṭhe saptame tv aindavadvayam || 12 ||
[30]        śuddhyartham aṣṭame caiva ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchram ācaret |
pakṣasaṃkhyāpramāṇena suvarṇāny api dakṣiṇā || 13 || iti |
prathamaḥ pakṣaḥ pañcāhasaṃsargaḥ | tatra trirātrasaṃjñakaṃ kṛcchram ācaret | trirātrakṛcchro nāma trirātropavāsaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “atha kṛcchrāṇi bhavanti | tryahaṃ nāśnīyāt” iti (ViSm 46.1–2) | dvitīyapakṣo daśāhasaṃsargaḥ | tatra prājāptyam ācaret | na ca mūlavacane prājāpatyākhyaḥ kṛcchraviśeṣo na śrūyate iti śaṅkanīyam | smṛtiśāstreṣu nirupapadasya kṛcchraśabdasya prājāpatya eva prācuryeṇa prayogāt, “kṛcchram iti prājāpatyam” iti devalasmaraṇāc ca | prājāpatyasvarūpaṃ manur āha |
tryahaṃ prātas tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryaham adyād ayācitam |
tryahaṃ paraṃ tu nāśnīyāt prājāpatyaṃ caran dvijaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.211)
tṛtīyapakṣo dvādaśāhasaṃsargaḥ | tatra sāntapanaṃ kṛcchram ācaret | sāntapanaṃ caturvidham | dvirātraṃ pañcadaśarātram ekaviṃśatirātraṃ ceti | tatra dvirātrasaptarātrayoḥ svarūpam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[31]        gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
jagdhvā pare ‘hny upavaset kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caran ||
pṛthak sāntapanadravyaiḥ ṣaḍahaḥ sopavāsakaḥ |
saptāhena tu kṛcchro ‘yaṃ mahāsāntapanaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.315–16)
yamenāpi pañcadaśāhasādhyo mahāsāntapano ‘bhihitaḥ |
tryahaṃ pibet tu gomūtraṃ tryahaṃ vai gomayaṃ pibet |
tryahaṃ dadhi tryahaṃ kṣīraṃ tryahaṃ sarpis tataḥ śuciḥ ||
mahāsāntapanaṃ hy etat sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || iti |
jābālena tv ekaviṃśatirātranirvartyo mahāsāntapano ‘bhihitaḥ |
ṣaṇṇām ekaikam eteṣāṃ trirātram upayojayet |
tryahaṃ copavased ante mahāsāntapanaṃ viduḥ || iti |
eteṣāṃ madhye na tāvad atra dvirātraṃ gṛhītuṃ śakyam, tasya dvitīyapakṣoktāt prājāpatyāt nyūnatvāt | nāpi pañcadaśarātraikaviṃśatirātrayor grahaṇam, tayoś caturthapakṣoktadaśarātropavāsād adhikatvāt | tasmāt saptarātraṃ pariśiṣyate | na ca tasya dvādaśāhasādhyāt prājpatyād arvācīnatvaṃ śaṅkanīyam | prājāpatye hi triṣv eva dineṣv aśanavarjanam | atra tu saptasv api dineṣv aśanavarjanam, ato ‘dhikatvāt | evam eva tṛtīyapakṣe prāyaścittam | ardhamāsasaṃsargaḥ caturthapakṣaḥ | tatra daśarātropavāsam ācaret | [32] yady apy atropavased iti na śrutaṃ tathāpi vakṣyamāṇaparākakṛcchrasāhacartāt tal labhyate | māsasaṃsargaḥ pañcamaḥ pakṣaḥ | tatra parākaḥ kṛcchraḥ kartavyatvena saṃmataḥ | parākasvarūpaṃ darśayati manuḥ |
yatātmano ‘pramattasya dvādaśāham abhojanam |
parāko nāma kṛcchro ‘yaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.215)
māsadvayasaṃsargaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ pakṣaḥ | tatra cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt | cāndrāyaṇalakṣaṇaṃ svayam evottaratra vakṣyati | ṣaṇmāsasaṃsargaḥ saptamaḥ pakṣaḥ | tatraindavadvayaṃ kuryāt, aindavaṃ cāndrāyaṇam induvṛddhihrāsopetatvāt | kiṃcidūnasaṃvatsarasaṃsargo ‘ṣṭamaḥ pakṣaḥ | yady api kiṃcidūnatvaṃ na śrutaṃ tathāpi saṃpūrṇasaṃvatsarasaṃsargasya pātityahetutvāt kiṃcidūnatvaṃ kalpyate | tac ca pātityahetutvaṃ yājñavalkyena darśitam:
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena samācaran | iti | (=MDh 11.180)
tatrāṣṭame pakṣe ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchram ācaret | ṣaṇmāsān ity atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyayā kṛcchrair antaryaṃ vivakṣitam | tathā ca sati ṣaṭsu māseṣu prājāpatyakṛcchrāḥ pañcadaśa saṃpadyante | śuddhyartham iti sarveṣu vākyeṣv anuṣajyate | sarveṣv eteṣu pakṣeṣu yathoktaṃ prāyaścittam anuṣṭhāya tadaṅgatvena dakṣiṇā dātavyā | tatra pañcarātrasaṃsarge prathamapakṣe suvarṇam ekam, daśarātrasaṃsarge dvitīyapakṣe suvarṇadvayam, evam itareṣv api pakṣeṣv avagantavyam || 11–13 ||
[33]
itthaṃ patitasaṃsarge prakīrṇake nimittānusāreṇa prāyaścittatāratamyaṃ vyutpāditam | idānīm anayaiva diśā nimittānusāreṇa prāyaścittam unnetuṃ śakyam iti hṛdi nidhāya ṛtusnātety ārabhya yas tu dharmaparāṅmukhaḥ ityantena prāyaścittanimittāny evopanyasyati |
ṛtusnātā tu yā nārī bhartāraṃ nopasarpati |
sā mṛtā narakaṃ yāti vidhavā ca punaḥ punaḥ || 14 ||
ṛtusnātāṃ tu yo bhāryāṃ saṃnidhau nopagacchati |
ghorāyāṃ bhrūṇahatyāyāṃ yujyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 15 ||
daridraṃ vyādhitaṃ mūrkhaṃ bhartāraṃ yāvamanyate |
sā śunī jāyate mṛtvā sūkarī ca punaḥ punaḥ || 16 ||
patyau jīvati yā nārī upoṣya vratam ācaret |
āyuṣyaṃ harate bhartuḥ sā nārī narakaṃ vrajet || 17 ||
apṛṣṭvā caiva bhartāraṃ yā nārī kurute vratam |
[34]        sarvaṃ tad rākṣasān gacched ity evam manur abravīt || 18 ||
bāndhavānāṃ sajātīnāṃ durvṛttaṃ kurute tu yā |
garbhapātaṃ ca yā kuryān na tāṃ saṃbhāṣayet kvacit || 19 ||
yat pāpaṃ brahmahatyāyāṃ dviguṇaṃ garbhapātane |
prāyaścittaṃ na tasyāḥ syāt tasyās tyāgo vidhīyate || 20 ||
na kāryam āvasathyena nāgnihotreṇa vā punaḥ |
sa bhavet karmacāṇḍālo yas tu dharmaparāṅmukhaḥ || 21 || iti |
rajodarśanam ārabhya ṣoḍaśadināny ṛtuḥ | tatra caturthadivase snātā nārī putrotpādanārtham āhūtā satī yadi bhartāraṃ nopasarpati tadā narakam anubhūya paścād bahuṣu janmasu vidhavā bhavati bhartrāhūtāyā anupasarpaṇe doṣo nāradīyapurāṇe darśitaḥ |
āhūtā yā tu vai bhartrā na prayāti tvarānvitā |
sā dhvāṅkṣī jāyate ‘putrī daśa janmāni pañca ca || iti |
yathā vadhvā ṛtukālātikrame pratyavāyas tathā puruṣasyāpi ṛtusnātām anupagacchataḥ pratyavāyaḥ | saṃnidhiśabdas tv atrārogyāder upalakṣakaḥ | ata eva devalaḥ |
yaḥ svadārān ṛtusnātān svasthaḥ san nopagacchati |
bhrūṇahatyām avāpnoti prajāṃ prāptāṃ vināśya saḥ || iti |
atra conneyaṃ yat prāyaścittaṃ tad bṛhaspatir darśayati |
[35]        ṛtau na gacched yo bhāryāṃ so ‘pi kṛcchrārdham ācaret | iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
ṛtau na gacched yo bhāryāṃ niyatāṃ dharmacāriṇīm |
niyamātikrame tasya prāṇāyamaśataṃ smṛtam || iti | (BDh 4.1.21)
na cātra prāyaścittadvayasya samavikalpaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ | prāṇāyāmaśatasya ardhakṛcchrapratyāmnāyatvenānukalpatvāt | ata eva prāṇāyāmaśatadvayaṃ kṛcchrapratyāmnāyatvena caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
kṛcchro deyayutaṃ caiva prāṇāyāmaśatadvayam |
tilahomasahasraṃ tu vedādhyayanam eva ca || iti |
puruṣaṃ prati uktasya prāyaścittasyārdhaṃ striyaṃ prati unneyam | tathā ca bhṛguḥ |
aśītir yasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpy ūnaṣoḍaśaḥ |
prāyaścittārdham arhanti striyo vyādhita eva ca || iti |
na kevalam ṛtvatikrame striyāḥ pratyavāyaḥ kiṃ tu dāridyādinā yena kenāpi nimittena patyur avamāne ‘pi mahān pratyavāyaḥ | tathā ca brahmāṇḍapurāṇam |
kāmād roṣān matsarād vā bhartāraṃ yāvamanyate |
sā saptajanmakaṃ yāvat narake syān na saṃśayaḥ ||
[36] iti | avamānaś ca puruṣāntaragamanavittavañcanādiḥ | etad evābhipretya manur āha |
apatyalobhād yā tu strī bhartāram atilaṅghayet |
seha nindām avapnoti paralokāc ca hīyate ||
vyabhicārāt tu bhartuḥ strī loke prāpnoti nindyatām |
sṛgālayoniṃ prāpnoti pāparogaiś ca pīḍyate ||
patiṃ hitvāpakṛṣṭaṃ svam utkṛṣṭaṃ yopasevate |
nidyaiva loke bhavati parapūrveti cocyate || iti | (MDh 5.161–65)
nāradīye ‘pi |
jīvitenātha vittena bhartāraṃ vañcayet tu yā |
kṛmiyoniśataṃ gatvā cāṇḍālī jāyate tu sā || iti |
atronneyaṃ prāyaścittam āpastambo darśayati: “bhartur vyatikrame kṛcchram” iti (ĀpDh 1.10.28.20) | uśanāpi: “vyabhicāriṇīṃ bhāryāṃ kucailaparibhūtāṃ piṇḍamātropajīvinīṃ nivṛttādhikārāṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ prājāpatyaṃ vā cārayet” iti | avamānābhāve ‘pi patiśuśrūṣām upekṣya vratopavāsādiparāyāḥ [37] striyāḥ pratyavāyo ‘sti | etad evābhipretya vratopavāsādīnāṃ striyaṃ prati śreyohetutvaṃ niṣadhaty atriḥ |
na vratenopavāsena dharmeṇa vividhena ca |
nārī svargam avāpnoti prāpnoti patipūjanāt || iti |
mahābhārate ‘pi patiśuśrūṣāparāyā bhāryāyā uttamāṃ gatim uktvā vratādiparāyā anyasyā bhāryāyās tadabhāvaṃ jñāpayitum idam udāhṛtam |
tāṃ yamo lokapālas tu babhāṣe puṣkalaṃ vacaḥ |
mā śucis tvaṃ nivartasva na lokāḥ santi te ‘naghe ||
svadharmavimukhā nityaṃ kān kokān tvaṃ gamiṣyasi |
daivataṃ hi patiḥ strīṇāṃ sthāpitaḥ sarvadevataiḥ ||
mohena tvaṃ varārohe na jānīṣe svadaivatam |
patimatyāḥ striyā loke dharmaḥ patyarcanaṃ tv iti || iti |
yadā strī patiśuśrūṣānurodhena vratādikaṃ vikīrṣati tadāpi patim apṛṣṭvānuṣṭhitaṃ tad vrataṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavati | tad āha mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
nārī khalv ananujñātā bhartrā pitrā sutena vā |
niṣphalaṃ tu bhavet tasyā yat karoti vratādikam || iti |
ata eva manuḥ sarvatra striyāḥ svātantryaṃ niṣedhati |
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kāryaṃ kiṃcid gṛheṣv api || (MDh 5.147)
[38] iti | tathā ca purāṇe |
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane |
putraḥ sthavirabhāve tu na strī svātantryam arhati || iti | (=MDh 9.3)
apṛṣṭvety abhidhānād anujñāpuraḥsaram anuṣṭhitasya vratādeḥ sāphalyam avagamyate | tac ca śaṅkha-likhitābhyāṃ darśitam: “na bhartāraṃ dviṣyād yady apy aśīlaḥ syāt | patito ‘rthahīno vyādhito vāpi patir daivataṃ strīṇām | na vratopavāsaniyamejyādānaṃ damo vānugrahakaraḥ strīṇām anyatra patiśuśrūṣāyāḥ | kāmaṃ tu bhartur anujñayā vratopavāsaniyamādīnām abhyāsaḥ strīdharmaḥ” iti | kātyāyano ‘pi |
bhāryā bhartur matenaiva vratādīn ācaret sadā | iti |
yā tu nārī bāndhavānām asapiṇḍānāṃ sajātīnāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ durvṛttaṃ vidveṣaṃ kurute yā ca garbhaṃ pātayet te ubhe pātakabāhulyān na saṃbhāṣaṇīye | etad evābhipretya bandhudveṣaḥ saṅkha-likhitābhyāṃ niṣiddhaḥ: “na bandhūn dviṣyāt” iti | atra ca vidveṣo viṣaprayogābhicārādiparyanto vivakṣitaḥ | [39] anyathā svalpasya dveṣasya garbhapātāsāmyenāsaṃbhāṣaṇahetutvāsaṃbhavāt | yady api bandhuvadhagarbhapātanayor asaṃbhāṣaṇahetutvaṃ samānaṃ tathāpi garbhapāte pāpadvaiguṇyena prāyaścittaṃ nāsti atas tasyās tyāga eva |
nanu – prāyaścittābhāvo ‘nupapannaḥ | manunā brahmahatyāvratasya tatropadiṣṭatvāt,
hatvā garbham avijñātam etad eva vrataṃ caret | iti | (MDh 11.87)
avijñātaṃ strītvapuṃstvābhyām aniścitam | vijñāte tu garbhe strīpuruṣavadhayor yathāvihitaṃ draṣṭavyam – ataḥ katham ucyate prāyaścittaṃ nāstīti |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, pātanīyagarbhagataguṇānuguṇābhyāṃ vyavasthopapatteḥ | hananīyabrāhmaṇagataguṇāgunābhyāṃ prāyaścittatāratamyasya darśanāt | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ brahmaghnavratam uktvā dīkṣitaghne dvaiguṇyam āha |
dviguṇaṃ svanasthe tu brāhmaṇe vratam ādiśet | iti | (YDh 3.252)
yady api dīkṣitādīkṣitayor iva garbhayor guṇāguṇau nopapadyete tathāpi pitṛgataguṇāgunābhyāṃ tau kalpanīyau | tasmāt prāyaścittaviṣayāṇi vacanāni garbhamātravadhaviṣayāṇi | idaṃ prāyaścittābhāvavacanaṃ saṃbhāvitaguṇavadgarbhaviṣayam | yad vā pāpadvaiguṇyena prāyaścittābhāvoktis tasyās tyāgavidhiśeṣārthvāda iti na kaścid virodhaḥ | tathā ca viśiṣṭagarbhapātane pāpadvaiguṇyād vratadvaiguṇyam unneyam iti bhāvaḥ |
[40]        yas tu vede viśvāsarahito vedoktāt dharmāt parāṅmukhas taṃ nānutiṣṭhati sa janmanā brāhmaṇo ‘pi karmaṇā cāṇḍālo bhavet | tasya dharmaparāṅmukhatvaṃ tadīyoktirūpeṇa pūrvārdhena dyotyate | āvasathyaḥ pañcamo ‘gniḥ | tena ca gārhapatyādyagnipañcakam upalakṣyate | na cāgnipañcakena vā tatsādhyenāgnihotrādinā vā kiṃcit phalam asti | aihikasya phalasyādarśanāt āmuṣmikasya phalasya saṃdigdhatvāt | tathā ca śrūyate: “ko hi tad veda yady amuṣmin loke ‘sti vā na vā” iti | evam eteṣu vyākhyāteṣu vacaneṣu yāni nimittāny upanyastāni teṣāṃ madhye strīkartṛkasya puruṣakartṛkasya ṛtvatikramasya patyavamānasya ca prāyaścittam unnīya pradarśitam | yat patiśuśrūṣām ullaṅghya tatpratikūlavratādhyācaraṇaṃ tatra patiṃ prasādya taduktaṃ prāyaścittam ācaret | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratikūlaṃ guroḥ kṛtvā prasādyaiva viśudhyati | iti | (YDh 3.283)
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
atha vā yad gurur brūyāt tat kāryam aviśaṅkayā |
vigrahe ‘nugrahe vāpi guruḥ sarvatra kāraṇam || iti |
yac cānujñām antareṇa vratācaraṇaṃ tatra niṣphalaprayāsasya daṇḍarūpatvāt tenaiva śudhyatīti na pṛthak prāyaścittāpekṣā |
durvṛttācaraṇaṃ ca dvividham | vadhāvasānaṃ prayatnamātraṃ ceti | tayor madhye vadhāvasānasya puruṣakartṛkasya durvṛttācaraṇasya prāyaścittam [41] ācāryo vakṣyati | prayatnamātrasya tu prāyaścittaṃ yājñavalkyo ‘tidiśati |
cared vratam ahatvāpi ghātārthaṃ cet samāgataḥ | iti | (YDh 3.252)
na ca hananāhananayor vratasāmyaṃ śaṅkanīyam, atidiṣṭasyopadiṣṭāt kiṃcinnyūnatvāt | yoṣitas tu puruṣakartṛkaprāyaścittād ardhaṃ draṣṭavyam | garbhapātane prāyaścittaṃ garbhabhedād anekadhā bhidyate | garbho dvividhaḥ | patijanyo jārajanyaś ca | jārāś ca savarṇā asarvarṇāś ca | tatra sarvatra prāyaścittaṃ caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam |
garbhapāte samuddiṣṭaṃ yathāvarṇavidhi vratam |
jāragarbhe viśeṣaḥ syāt yathoktam ṛṣibhiḥ purā ||
brahmagarbhavadhe kṛcchram abdaṃ sāntapanādhikam |
kṣatragarbhavadhe caiva carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam |||
vaiśyasya caindavaṃ proktaṃ parākaḥ śūdraghātane |
prāyaścittam idaṃ proktaṃ garbhapāte viśeṣataḥ || iti |
vedaviśvāsarahitasya prāyaścittaṃ vasiṣṭho darśayati: “nāstikaḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caritvā viramen nāstikyāt | nāstikavṛttis tv atikṛcchram” iti (VaDh 21.29–30) | etac ca sakṛtkaraṇaviṣayam | asakṛtkaraṇe tu śaṅkhenoktam: [42] “nāstiko nāstikavṛttiḥ kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī mithyābhiśaṃsīty ete pañcasaṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛhe bhaikṣaṃ careyuḥ” | iti || 14–21 ||
atha bījikṣetriṇoḥ pitroḥ pratyavāyaprāyaścittasadbhāvasūcanārtham asmin prāyaścittaprasaṅge kuṇḍagolakau putrau sadṛṣṭāntam upanyasyati |
oghavātāhṛtaṃ bījaṃ yasya kṣetre prarohati |
sa kṣetrī labhate bījaṃ na bījī bhāgam arhati || 22 ||
tadvat parastriyāḥ putrau dvau smṛtau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍaḥ syāt mṛte bhartari golakaḥ || 23 || iti |
ogho jalapravāhaḥ | vātaḥ pracaṇḍavāyuḥ | tayor anyatareṇāhṛtaṃ parakīyaṃ śālyādibījaṃ yasyānyasya kṣetre prarohati sa kṣetrī bījaṃ bījaphalaṃ labhate | na tu bījī | tad etal lokaprasiddham | tenaiva nyāyena kṣetriṇo bhāryāyāṃ bījinotpāditau kuṇḍagolakau kṣetriṇaḥ putrau bhavataḥ | na tu bījinaḥ | taupñ putrayor madhye kṣetriṇyam ṛte jārajaḥ kuṇḍaḥ | mṛte tasmin jārajaṃ golakaḥ, [43]
amṛte ca mṛte caiva jārajau kuṇḍagolakau |
iti smṛtyantarāt | jārajaśabdena devarādayo vyāvartyante | na hi teṣāṃ jāratvam asti, teṣāṃ gurvanujñātatvāt | teṣāṃ gurvanujñātatvāt | anujñātatvaṃ ca yājñavalkyenoktam |
aputrāṃ gurvanujñāto devaraḥ putramāmyayā |
sapiṇḍo vā sagotro vā ghṛtābhyakta ṛtāv iyāt || iti | (YDh 1.68)
bījikṣetriṇor atra sūcitaḥ pratyavāyo mahābhārate darśitaḥ |
kṣetrikaś caiva bījī ca dvāv etau nirayaṃgamau |
na rakṣati ca yo dārān paradārāṃś ca gacchati ||
garhitās te narā nityaṃ dharmācārabahiṣkṛtāḥ |
[44] iti | jārasya garbhānutpādane yat prāyaścittam abhihitaṃ tad eva bījī dviguṇam ācaret | jāraprāyaścittaṃ ca vyāghreṇa darśitam |
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇīṃ gacched akāmāṃ yadi kāmataḥ |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe kuryād adharmeva pramādataḥ ||
adharmeva sakāmāyāṃ taptakṛcchraṃ sakṛd gatau |
ardham ardhaṃ nṛpādīnāṃ dāreṣu brāhmaṇaś caret ||
etad vrataṃ caret sārdhaṃ śrotriyasya parigrahe |
aśrotriyaś ced dviguṇam aguptāv ardham eva ca || iti|
kṣetriṇaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣānādeśāt sāmānyaprāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tac ca yājñavalkyena darśitam |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kāryaṃ sarvapāpāpanuttaye |
upapātakajātānām anādiṣṭeṣu caiva hi || iti | (YDh 3.306)
“na bījī bhāgam arhati” ity ayam artho manunā dṛṣṭāntaiḥ pratipāditaḥ |
yathā go’śvoṣṭradāsīṣu mahiṣyajāvikāsu ca |
notpādakaḥ prajābhāgī tathaivānyāṅganāsv api ||
ye ‘kṣetriṇo bījabantaḥ parakṣetre pravāpiṇaḥ |
te vai sasyaya jātasya na labhante phalaṃ kvacit ||
[45]        yad anyagoṣu vṛṣabho vatsānāṃ janayec chatam |
gominām eva te vatsā moghaṃ skanditam ārṣabham || iti | (MDh 9.48–50)
ye ‘kṣetriṇa ity atra akṣetriṇa iti chedaḥ || 22–23 ||
idānīṃ kuṇḍagolakaprasaṅgena buddhisthān putrabhedān darśayati |
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś caiva dattaḥ kṛtrimaḥ sutaḥ | iti |
etac ca dvādaśavidhānāṃ putrāṇām upalakṣaṇam | te ca manunā darśitāḥ |
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś caiva dattaḥ kṛtrima eva ca |
gūḍotpanno ‘paviddhaś ca dāyādā bāndhavāś ca ṣaṭ ||
kānīnaś ca sahoḍhaś ca krītaḥ paunarbhavas tathā |
svayaṃdattaś ca śaudraś ca ṣaḍadāyādabāndhavāḥ ||
[46]        svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāṃ tu svayam utpāditaś ca yaḥ |
tam aurasaṃ vijānīyāt putraṃ prathamakalpitam ||
yas talpajaḥ pramītasya klībasya patitasya vā |
svadharmeṇa niyuktāyāṃ sa putraḥ kṣetrajaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
mātā pitā vā dadyātāṃ yad adbhiḥ putram āpadi |
sadṛśaṃ prītisaṃyuktaṃsa jñeyo dattrimaḥ sutaḥ ||
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuryād yaṃ guṇadoṣavicakṣaṇam |
putraṃ putraguṇair yuktaṃ sa vijñeyas tu kṛtrimaḥ ||
utpadyate gṛhe yasya na ca jñāyeta kasya saḥ |
sa gṛhe gūḍha utpannas tasya syād yasya talpajaḥ ||
mātāpitṛbhyām utsṛṣṭaṃ tayor anyatareṇa vā |
yaṃ putraṃ parigṛhṇīyād apaviddhaḥ sa ucyate ||
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayad rahaḥ |
taṃ kānīnaṃ vijājīyād voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavam ||
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātājñātā ca yā satī |
voḍhuḥ sa garbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate ||
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā ||
[47]        yā patyā vā parityaktā vidhavā vā svayecchayā |
utpādayet punar bhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate ||
mātāpitṛvihīno yas tyakto vā syād akāraṇāt |
ātmānaṃ sparśayet yasmai svayaṃdattas tu sa smṛtaḥ ||
yaṃ brāhmaṇas tu śūdrāyāṃ kāmād utpādayet sutam |
sa pārayann eva śavas tasmāt pāraśavaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.159–78)
yājñavalkyenāpi |
aurasaḥ putrikāputraḥ kṣetrajo gūḍhajas tathā |
kānīnaḥ pañcamaḥ prokto ṣaṣṭhaḥ paunarbhavaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
dattaḥ krītaḥ kṛtrimaś ca svayaṃdatta itīritaḥ |
sahoṣhajo ‘paviddhaś ca putrā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ ||
auraso dharmapatnījas tatsamaḥ putrikāsutaḥ |
kṣetrajaḥ kṣetrajātas tu sagotreṇetareṇa vā ||
gṛhe pracchanna utpanno gūḍhajas tu sutaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kānīnaḥ kanyakājāto mātāmahasuto mataḥ ||
akṣatāyāṃ kṣatāyāṃ vā jātaḥ paunarbhavaḥ smṛtaḥ |
dadyān mātā pitā vā yaṃ sa putro dattako bhavet ||
krītaś ca tābhyāṃ vikrītaḥ kṛtrimaḥ syāt svayaṃkṛtaḥ |
[48]        dattātmā tu svayaṃdatto garbhe vinnaḥ sahoḍhajaḥ ||
utsṛṣṭo gṛhyate yas tu so ‘paviddho bhavet sutaḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ ||
sajāṭīyeṣv ayaṃ proktas tanayeṣu mayā vidhiḥ || iti | (YDh 2.128–33)
atra dvādaśānāṃ putrāṇāṃ yāni lakṣaṇāni manunā darśitāni tāni sarvāṇy upalakṣayituṃ dattasya lakṣaṇam āha |
dadyān mātā pitā vāpi sa putro dattako bhavet || 24 || iti |
atra dāne sadṛśaṃ prītisaṃyuktam adbhir āpadīti viśeṣaṇacatuṣṭayaṃ draṣṭavyam || 24 ||
prasakṭanuprasaktaṃ parisamāpya prakṛtam eva prāyaścittam anusarati parivittir ityādiślokadvayena |
parivittiḥ parivettā yayā ca parividyate |
sarve te narakaṃ yānti dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ || 25 ||
dvau kṛcchrau parivittes tu kanyāyāḥ kṛcchra eva ca |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau dātus tu hotā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 26 || iti |
yatra jyeṣṭho nodvahati kaniṣṭhaś codvahati tatra jyeṣṭhaḥ parivittir ity ucyate | kaniṣṭhaḥ parivettā | yayā kanyayā kaniṣṭhaḥ parividyate sā parivedinī | tasyāḥ pitrādir dātā | yājako vivāhahomasya kārayitā | ta ete pañca narakaṃ yānti | tathā ca hārītaḥ: [49] “jyeṣṭhe ‘niviṣṭe kanīyān niviśan parivettā bhavati | parivittir jyeṣṭhaḥ | parivedanī kanyā | paridāyī dātā | pariyaṣṭā yājakaḥ | te sarve patitāḥ” iti | tatra parivitter dvau kṛcchrau prāyaścittam | kanyāyā ekaḥ kṛcchraḥ | dātuḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | tasya lakṣaṇaṃ yājñavalkya āha |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ payasā divasān ekaviṃśatim | iti | (YDh 3.321)
yājakasya cāndrāyaṇam | na cātra parivettuḥ prāyaścittam anuktam iti śaṅkanīyam, parivittiśabdena tasyāpy upalakṣitatvāt | ata eva yamaḥ parivittiparivettror dvayoḥ samaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
kṛcchrau dvau parivettuḥ syāt kanyāyāḥ kṛcchra eva ca |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau dātus tu hotā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
yat tu śaṅkhalikhitābhyām uktam,
parivittiḥ parivettā ca yayā ca parividyate |
vrataṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kuryur dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ || iti |
yad api hārītenoktam: [50] “te sarve patitāḥ saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa paricareyuḥ” iti, tatra sarvatra cirakṣiprajñātājñātabhedena prāyaścittagauravalāghavavyavasthā draṣṭavyā | prāyaścittacaraṇānantaraṃ parivettuḥ kartavyam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “parivividānaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niviśet tāṃ caivopayacchet” iti | tasmai datvā jyeṣṭhāya nivedya punar niveśet punar udvahet | kām ity apekṣāyām āha tām evopayacched iti | ayam arthaḥ | jyeṣṭhabhrātary akṛtakāraparigrahe kṛtadāraparigrahaḥ kaniṣṭhaḥ parivettā parivedanyā svagṛhītayā kanyayā parivittinā jyeṣṭhabhrātrā ca saha uktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nirvartya jyeṣṭhabhrātari kṛtadāraparigrahe kaniṣṭhaḥ pūrvaṃ gṛhītāṃ kanyāṃ jyeṣṭhabhrātre nivedya taddattāṃ svayam eva punar udvahet | ayam eva nyāyo jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhayor ādhānavyutkrame bhaginyor vivāhavyutkrame cānusaṃdheyaḥ | ata eva gautamaḥ: “parivittiparivetṛparyāhita-paryādhātāgredidhiṣudidhiṣūṇāṃ saṃvatsaraṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam” iti | agredidhiṣupatyādau viśeṣo vasiṣṭhenoktaḥ: “agredidhiṣupatiḥ kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ varitvā niviśet [51] tāṃ caivopayacchet | vidhiṣupatiḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā tasmai datvā punar niviśet” iti (VaDh 20.9–10) | agredidhiṣvāder lakṣaṇaṃ devalenoktam |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyāyām ūhyate ‘nujā |
sā cāgredidhiṣur jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣuḥ smṛtā || iti |
tatrāgredidhiṣupatir dvādaśarātra kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caritvā jyeṣṭhāyāṃ paścād anyenoḍhāyāṃ tām evodvahat | didhiṣūpatis tu kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caritvā svoḍhāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ tasmai kanīyasyāḥ pūrvoḍhre datvānyām udvahet || 26–26 ||
parivedane pratyavāya prāyaścittaṃ ca pradarśyedānīṃ viśeṣe tadapavādaṃ darśayati |
kubjavāmanaṣaṇḍheṣu jaḍeṣu ca |
jātyandhe badhira mūke na doṣaḥ parivedane || 27 ||
kubjaḥ pṛṣṭhabhāge māṃsādiviśeṣeṇātyantavikṛtadehaḥ | vāmano ‘tihrasvadehaḥ | ṣaṇḍho napuṃsakaḥ | gadagado jihvādidoṣeṇa sahasā vaktum aśaktaḥ | jaḍo ‘kṣamaḥ kāryeṣv apravṛttaḥ | jātyandhādayaḥ prasiddhāḥ | evaṃvidhasya jyeṣṭhasya vivāhāyogyatvāt kaniṣṭhasya vivāhe nāsti parivedane doṣaḥ | etac ca jyeṣṭhasya pravrajyādeśāntaragamanādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | asmiṃś ca parivedanābhyanujñāne saṃvādavacanāni pūrvam eva śrāddhaprasaṅgād udāhṛtānīti nātra prapañcyante || 27 ||
[52]
kubjatvādidoṣarahiteṣv api bhinnodareṣu privedanam abhyanujānāti |
pitṛvyaputraḥ sāpatnyaḥ paranārīsutas tathā |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge na doṣaḥ parivedane || 28 ||
pitṛvyaputrasāpatnyau prasiddhau | paranārīsuto dattakrītādiḥ | eteṣu jyeṣṭheṣu sthiteṣu kaniṣṭhasya vivāhādhānayor nāsti pratyavāyaḥ || 28 ||
kubjatvādidoṣarahiteṣv ekodareṣu paryādhāne viśeṣam āha |
jyeṣṭho bhrātā yadā tiṣṭhed ādhānaṃ naiva kārayet |
anujñātas tu kurvīta śaṅkhasya vacanaṃ yathā || 29 || iti |
kārayet kuryāt | anujñātaḥ kaniṣṭho jyeṣṭhāt pūrvam ādhānam kuryāt | jyeṣṭhabhrātreva pitrāpy anujñātasya putrasya paryādhānaprāptau caturviṃśatimate tan niṣidhyate |
jyeṣṭhabhrātrā tv anujñātaḥ kuryād agniparigraham |
anujñāto ‘pi san pitrā nādadhyān manur abravīt || iti |
yat tu sumantunoktam,
pitur yasya tu nādhānaṃ kathaṃ putras tu kārayet |
agnihotrādhikāro ‘sti śaṅkhasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
iti, uśanāpi,
pitā pitāmaho yasya agrajo vātha kasyacit |
tapo ‘gnihotramantreṣu na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
iti, tat pitrādīnāṃ vaidhuryādinā pratibandhe sati draṣṭavyam || 29 ||
parivedanaparyādhānayor iva strīṇāṃ punar udvāhasyāpi prasaṅgāt kvacit abhyanujñāṃ darśayati |
naṣṭe mṛte pravrajite klībe ca patite patau |
pañcasv āpatsu nārīṇāṃ patir anyo vidhīyate || 30 || iti |
naṣṭo deśāntaragamanenāparijñānavṛttāntaḥ | ayaṃ ca punar udvāho yugāntaraviṣayaḥ | tathā ca ādityapurāṇam |
ūḍhāyāḥ punar udvāhaṃ jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ govadhaṃ tathā |
kalau paṇca na kurvīta bhrātṛjāyāṃ kamaṇḍalum || iti |
atra ūḍhāśabdena vidhivad ūḍhā grāhyā | anyad eva punar udvāhasyātrāpy aṅgīkṛtatvāt || 30 ||
punar udvāham akṛtvā brahmacaryavratānuṣṭhāne śreyo ‘tiśayaṃ darśayati |
mṛte bhartari yā nārī brahmacaryavrate sthitā |
sā mṛtā labhate svargaṃ yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ || 31 || iti |
mṛtagrahaṇaṃ naṣṭādīnām upalakṣaṇam | caturthapādena smṛtyantaraprasiddhā brahmacārigatir dṛṣṭāntitā | sā ca manunā darśitā |
[54]        anekāni sahasrāṇi kaumārabrahmacāriṇām |
divaṃ gatāni viprāṇām akṛtvā kulasantatim || iti | (MDh 5.159) || 31 ||
uktabrahmacaryād apy adhikaphalam anugamanae darśayati |
tisraḥ koṣṭyo ‘rdhakoṭī ca yāni romāṇi mānuṣe |
tāvatkālaṃ vaset svarge bhartāraṃ yānugacchati || 32 || iti |
tāvatkālaṃ tāvatsahasrasaṃvatsaram | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
mṛte bhartari yā nārī dharmaśīlā dṛḍhavratā |
anugacchati bhartāraṃ śṛṇu tasyās tu yat phalam ||
tisraḥ koṭyo ‘rdhakoṭī ca yāni lomāni mānuṣe |
tāvanty abdasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
mātṛkaṃ paitṛkaṃ caiva yatra kanyā pradīyate |
kulatrayaṃ punāty eṣā bhartāraṃ yānugacchati || iti |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
mṛte bhartari yā nārī samārohed dhutāśanam |
sārundhatīsamācārā svargaloke mahīyate ||
[55] iti |
nanu – idam anugamanaṃ pratyakṣaśrutiviruddham “tasmād u ha na purāyuṣaḥ svargakāī preyāt” iti śrutyā ātmahatyāpratiṣedhāt,
asūryā nāma te lokā andhena samasāvṛtāḥ |
tāṃs te pretyābhigacchanti ye ke cātmahano janāḥ || (ĪśUp 3)
iti śrutyantarāc ca |
maivam, anugamanasmṛter niravakāśatvena prābalyāt | ātmahatyāniṣedhaśrutis tu svargakāmayoṣito ‘nyatra sāvakāśā |
nanu – svargakāminyāḥ patim anugacchantyā api brāhmaṇyā anugamanaṃ smṛtyaiva niṣiddham | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mṛtānugamanaṃ nāsti brāhmaṇyā brahmaśāsanāt |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ strīdharmo ‘yaṃ paraḥ smrtaḥ || iti |
smṛtyantaraṃ ca |
upakāraṃ yathā bhartur jīvantī na tathā mṛtā |
karoti brāhmaṇī śreyo bhartuḥ śokakarī cirāt ||
[56]        anuvarteta jīvantaṃ nānuyāyān mṛtaṃ patim |
jīvya bhartur hitaṃ kuryāt maraṇād ātmaghātinau || iti |
aṅgirā api |
yā strī brāhmaṇajātīyā mṛtaṃ patim anuvrajet |
sā svargam ātmaghātena nātmānaṃ na patiṃ nayet || iti |
vyāghrapādo ‘pi |
na mriyeta samaṃ bhartrā brāhmaṇī śokamohitā |
pravrajyāgatim āpnoti maraṇād ātmaghātinī || iti |
pravrajyā maithunādibhogatyāgaḥ |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, asya niṣedhasya pṛthakcitiviṣayatvāt | ata eva uśanāḥ |
pṛthakcitiṃ samāruhya na viprā gantuam arhati |
anyāsāṃ caiva nārīṇāṃ strīdharmo ‘yaṃ paraḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
ekacityāṃ samārohaṇaṃ kalpasūtrakāreṇa darśitam: “pretasyottarataḥ patnyā saṃveśanam aviśeṣeṇa nityavat” iti || 32 ||
[57]
na kevalaṃ svayam evānugamanena svarge vasati kiṃtu svabhartāraṃ narakād uddharatīty āha |
vyālagrāhī yathā vyālaṃ balād uddharate bilāt |
evaṃ strī patim uddhṛtya tenaiva saha modate || 33 ||
yady api pāpīyān patiḥ svapāpaphalabhogāya narakamārgāyābhimukho bhavet tathāpi yoṣit svakīyena prabalasukṛtena tasya pāpaphalabhogaṃ pratibadhya patim api sveha saha gatiṃ nayati | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
yadi praviṣṭo narakaṃ baddhaḥ pāśaiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ |
saṃprāpto yātanāsthānaṃ gṛhīto yamakiṃkaraiḥ ||
tiṣṭhate vivaśo dīno veṣṭyamānaḥ svakarmabhiḥ |
vyālagrāhī yathā vyālaṃ balād gṛhṇāty aśaṅkitaḥ ||
tadvad bhartāram ādāya divaṃ yāti ca sā balāt |
sā bhartṛparamā nityaṃ stūyamānāpsarogaṇaiḥ ||
krīḍate patinā sārdhaṃ yāvad indrāś caturdaśa |
brahmaghno vā krtaghno vā mitraghno vā bhavet patiḥ |
punāty avidhavā nārī tam ādāya mṛtā tu yā || iti | (VyāSm 4.60–64)
idaṃ cānugamanaṃ pativratayānuṣṭhitaṃ saduktarītyā [58] dampatyor ubhayoḥ śreyohetuḥ pāpīyasyānuṣṭhitaṃ cet pāpakṣayahetur bhavati | tathā ca mahābhāratam |
avamatya tu yāḥ pūrvaṃ patiṃ duṣṭena cetasā |
vartante yāś ca satataṃ bhartṝṇāṃ pratikūlataḥ ||
bhartrānumaraṇaṃ kāle yāḥ kurvanti tathāvidhāḥ |
kāmāt krodhāt bhayān mohāt sarvāḥ pūtā bhavanti tāḥ ||
ādiprabhṛti yā sādhvī patyuḥ priyaparāyaṇā |
ūrdhvaṃ gacchati sā tatra bhartrānumaraṇaṃ gatā || iti |
etac cānumaraṇaṃ na sarvāsām api strīṇāṃ saṃbhavati,
sādhvīnām eva nārīṇām agniprapatanād ṛte |
nānyo dharmo ‘sti vijñeyo mṛte bhartari karhicit ||
yāvan nāgnau dahed dehaṃ mṛte patyau pativratā |
tāvan na mucyate nārī strīśarīrāt kathaṃcana ||
ity aṅgirasā sāmānyena pativratānāṃ sādhvīnām adhikārasya bodhanāt | ayaṃ cādhikāro rajaādibhiḥ pratibadhyate | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
bālasaṃvardhanaṃ tyaktvā bālāpatyā na gacchati |
rajasvalā sūtikā ca rakṣed garbhaṃ ca garbhiṇī ||
[59] iti | atra bālasaṃvardhanaṃ tyaktveti vadan saṃvardhayitṛjanāntaravisrambhe bālāpatyāyā apy adhikāro ‘stīti darśayan rakṣed garbhaṃ ca garbhiṇīti rakṣāṃ darśayan saṃbhāvitagarbhasaṃdehāyā apy adhikāraṃ vārayati | tathā ca nāradīye |
bālāpatyāś ca garbiṇya adṛṣṭaṛtavas tathā |
rajasvalā rājasute nārohanti citāṃ śubhe || iti |
adṛṣṭaṛtavaḥ ṛtvadarśanena saṃbhāvitagarbhasaṃdehāḥ || 33 ||
śrutiviṣayitaṃ prāyaścittaṃ prakīrṇeṣu yanmataṃ
nayaviṣayayor bhedas tallkṣaṇaṃ parivedanam |
prathayati paraṃ turyādhyāye parāśarabhāṣite
vivṛtim akarot śaktyā nirṇīya sa mādhavaḥ || 1 ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravartaka-
parameśvaraśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhvāmātyasya kṛtau pārāśrasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ || 4 ||
[61]
atha pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
yat samāśritya vālmīkapramukhāḥ sām paraṃ gatāḥ |
tadāśraye ‘ṅghrikamalaṃ rāmacandrasya nirmalam ||
itthaṃ caturhādhyāye prakīrṇakapāpānāṃ prāyaścittam abhihitam | tatra yady api parivedanādikaṃ na prakīrṇakam, upapātakeṣu paṭhitatvāt | yady api ca putrabhedādikathanaṃ na prāyaścittarūpaṃ tathāpi prakīrṇakaprāyaścittasya bāhulyāt adhyāyārthatvam aviruddham | tatrādhyāyānte svargasādhanam anugamanaṃ varṇitam | tena brahmalokasādhanam āhitāgnidahanaṃ buddhistham | ataḥ pañcamādhyāye tadvivakṣuḥ ādau prāyaścittaprakaraṇa-vicchedaśaṅkām apanudan pariśiṣṭaṃ prakīrṇakaprāyaścittaṃ tāvan navabhiḥ ślokair āha |
vṛkaśvānasṛgālādyair daṣṭo yas tu dvijottamaḥ |
snātvā japet sa gāyatrīṃ pavitrāṃ vedamātaram || 1 ||
gavāṃ śṛṅgodakaiḥ snānaṃ mahānadyos tu saṃgame |
samudradarśanād vāpi śunā daṣṭaḥ śucir bhavet || 2 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātaḥ śunā daṣṭo dvijo yadi |
[62]        sa hiraṇyodakaiḥ snātvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || 3 ||
savratas tu śunā daṣṭo yad trirātram upāvaset |
ghṛtaṃ kuśodakaṃ pītvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 4 ||
avrataḥ savrato vāpi śunā daṣṭo bhaved dvijaḥ |
praṇipatya bhavet pūto vipraiś cakṣur nirīkṣitaḥ || 5 ||
śunā ghrātāvalīḍhasya nakhair vilikhitasya ca |
adbhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ proktam agninā copacūlanam || 6 ||
śunā tu brāhmaṇī daṣṭā jambukena vṛkeṇa vā |
uditaṃ grahanakṣatraṃ dṛṣṭvā sadhyaḥ śucir bhavet || 7
kṛṣṇapakṣe yadā somo na dṛśyeta kadācana |
yāṃ diśaṃ vrajate somas tāṃ diśaṃ vāvalokayet || 8 ||
asadbrāhmaṇake grāme śunā daṣṭo dvijottamaḥ |
vṛṣaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sadyaḥ snātvā śucir bhavet || 9 ||
[63] iti | vṛkaśunor āraṇyakagrāmyatvād bhedaḥ | sṛgālo jambukaḥ | ādiśabdena varāhādayo gṛhyante | tair daṣṭaḥ pratyavāyaparihārāya snātvā gāyatrīṃ japet | na ca – atra vidhipratiṣedhātikramarahitasya kathaṃ pratyavāyaḥ, iti śaṅkanīyam | śvavarāhādīnāṃ dūrataḥ parihartavyatvena tatsamīpagamanakrīḍāder atikramarūpatvena pratyavāyahetutvāt | vedamātaram ity anena mantrāntarebhyo ‘dhikaṃ pavitratvaṃ darśayati | vedamātṛtvaṃ ca “gāyatrī chandasāṃ mātā” iti smṛter adhyavasīyate | yad vā vedā mataro yasyāḥ sā vedamātā | yady api mantrāntarāṇy api vedajanyāni tathāpi vedatrayajanyatvam asyā viśeṣaḥ |
ata eva manuḥ |
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ padaṃ padam adūduhat |
tad ity ṛco ‘syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ || iti | (MDh 2.77)
atra gāyatrījape saṃkhyāviśeṣ uśanasā darśitaḥ: “daṃṣṭryādidaṣṭo gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ vā” iti || 1 ||
etac cāsamarthaviṣayam samarthas tu gośṛṅgodakasnānādikam ācaret iti | tatra gośṛṅgodakasnānaṃ nāma gośṛṅgapūritenodakena gāyatryā śatavārābhimantritena secanam,
[64] “gośṛṅgena śataṃ snānaṃ gāyatryā” iti hārītasmaraṇāt | gośṛṅgodakasnāna-nadīsaṃgamasnāna-samudradarśanānām adhamamadhyamottamāṅgabhedena vā daṃśatāratamyena vā vyavasthā draṣṭavyā || 2 ||
vedādhyayanaṃ ca prājāpatyasaumyāgneyavaiśvadevādivratāni vā samāpya snāto “vedavidyāvratanātaḥ” | sa yadi śunā daṣṭaḥ tadā hiraṇyam udake nidhāya tenodakena snātvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati | tatrāpi brāhmaṇaś cet gāyatrīṃ śatakṛtvo japet | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
vedavidyāvratasnātaḥ śunā daṣṭas tu brāhmaṇaḥ |
śataparyāyam āvartya gāyatrīṃ śuddhim āpnuyāt || iti || 3 ||
cāndrāyaṇādivratena sahitaḥ “savrataḥ” | sa yadi śunā daṣṭaḥ tadā trirātram upoṣya caturthe ‘hani ghṛtaṃ prāśya kuśodakaṃ ca pītvā paścād vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet | kuśodakasthāne yāvakaṃ vā pibet | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
savratas tu śunā daṣṭaḥ trirātram upavāsayet |
saghṛtaṃ yāvakaṃ pītvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet || iti || 4 ||
[65]
savratāvratāv ubhāv api viprān praṇipatya tair nirīkṣitau yathoktaprāyaścittācaraṇena pūtau bhavataḥ || 5 ||
yas tu śunā na daṣṭaḥ kiṃ tu nāsikayā ghrātaḥ jihvayā vāvalīḍhaḥ nakhair vā likhitaḥ etattraye ‘pi taṃ pradeśaṃ prakṣālya vahninā saṃtāpya śuddhā bhaveyuḥ || 6 ||
yadā brāhmaṇī śvādibhir daṣṭā bhavati tadā sā rātrāv uditān grahān somāṅgārakādīn nakṣatrāṇi ca kṛttikādīny avalokya śuddhā bhavati || 7 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe meghacchanne somadarśanāsaṃbhave śāstradṛṣṭyā tadavasthānayogyāṃ diśaṃ vā cakṣuṣāvalokayet | etac cāvalokanaṃ pañcagavyaprāśanasyopalakṣaṇam | ata eva aṅgirāḥ |
brāhmaṇī tu śunā daṣṭā some dṛṣṭiṃ nipātayet |
yadā na dṛśyate somaḥ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
yāṃ diśaṃ tu gataḥ somas tāṃ diśaṃ vāvalokayet |
somamārgeṇ sā pūtā pañcagavyena śudhyati || iti |
yā tu samudratīravāsinī tasyāḥ somadarśanābhāve taddigavalokanavat samudradarśanam api viśuddhihetuḥ | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
brāhmaṇī tu śunā daṣṭā some dṛṣṭiṃ nipātayet |
samudradarśanād vāpi śunā daṣṭā śucir bhavet || iti || 8 ||
yasmin grāme brāhmaṇā na santi tatra brāhmaṇapraṇipātanrīkṣaṇayoḥ [66] sthāne vṛṣapradakṣiṇanirīkṣaṇe | nanu – smṛtyantareṣv anyathā prāyaścittāni dṛśyante | tatra manuḥ |
śvasṛgālakharair daṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādbhir eva ca |
narāśvoṣṭravarāhaiś ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | (MDh 11.199)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
puṃścalīvānarakharair daṣṭaḥ śvoṣṭrāhivayasaiḥ |
prāṇāyāmaṃ jale kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | (YDh 3.277)
hārīto ‘pi |
śvāno vā kroṣṭuko vāpi nārī vā yadi vā naraḥ |
ākhur nakulamārjārau vāyasagrāmasūkarāḥ ||
etair daṣṭe dvijasyāṅge prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
snānaṃ kuryāt sacailaṃ tu viprāṇām anuśāsanāt ||
prokṣaṇībhis tṛbhir ṛgbhiḥ kārayen mārjanaṃ dvijaḥ |
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt dadyāt gobhyas tṛṇaṃ naraḥ ||
saha dvijaiś ca bhuktena śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ | iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | (VaDh 23.31)
atra śaṅkhaḥ |
sṛgālavṛkagovipraiḥ śunā daṣṭas tathaiva ca |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryāt puṃścalīdaśanakṣataḥ || iti |
hārīto ‘pi: “śunā daṣṭas tryahaṃ yāvakāhāraḥ samudragāṃ nadīṃ gatvā prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya śucir bhavati” iti (HārSm 23.11) | aṅgirā api |
brahmacārī śunā daṣṭas tryahaṃ sāyaṃ payaḥ pibet |
gṛhasthas tu dvirātraṃ vāpy ekāhaṃ vāgnihotravān ||
nābher ūrdhvaṃ tu daṣṭasya tad eva dviguṇaṃ bhavet |
syād etat triguṇaṃ vaktre mastake tu caturguṇam ||
[68]        avratī savratī vāpi śunā daṣṭas tathā dvijaḥ |
dṛṣṭvāgniṃ hūyamānaṃ tu sadya eva śucir bhavet || iti |
paiṭhinasir api: “śunā daṣṭas trirātram upavaset dvijaś ca | brāhmaṇas tu śunā daṣṭo gāyatryaṣṭasahasrābhimantritaṃ kṛtvolmukhena dahec caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ snātaḥ śudhyati | nadīsaṃgame vā | bālasyāturasya vā tatra pitā manasā dhyāyan sarvakāryāṇi kurvīta | piturabhāve saty ācāryaḥ” iti | pulastyo ‘pi |
rajasvalā yadā daṣṭā śunā jambukarāsabhaiḥ |
pañcarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
ūrdhvaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ nābher vaktre tu triguṇaṃ tathā |
caturguṇaṃ smṛtaṃ mūrdhni daṣṭe ‘nyatrāplutir bhavet || iti |
jātūkarṇyo ‘pi |
[69]        brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śunā ca śvāpadair api |
daṣṭā sacailam āplutya śudhyatīti na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
eteṣu vacaneṣu yatra yatra prāyaścittabāhulyaṃ tatra tatrottamāṅgaviṣayatvaṃ daṃśatāratamyaviṣayatvaṃ cohanīyam | brahmacārigṛhasthāgnihotriṣu uttarottaraṃ tapobāhulyāt prāyaścittahrāsaḥ || 1–9 ||
atha durmṛtasyāhitāgner dahane prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālena śvapākena gobhir viprair hato yadi |
āhitāgnir mṛto vipro viṣeṇātmahato yadi || 10 ||
dahet taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vipro lokāgnau mantravarjitam |
spṛṣṭvā voḍhvā ca dagdhvā ca sapiṇḍeṣu ca sarvathā || 11 ||
prājāpatyaṃ caret paścāt viprāṇām anuśāsanāt | iti |
brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrāj jātaś caṇḍāla ity abhidhīyate | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyāt sūto vaiśyād vaidehakas tathā|
śūdrāj jātas tu caṇḍālaḥ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || (YDh 1.93)
[70] iti | kṣattur ugrāyāṃ jātaḥ śvapākaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
kṣattur jātas tathogrāyāṃ śvapāka iti kīrtyate | iti | (MDh 10.19)
hato daṇḍaśastrādinā prahataḥ | mṛtaḥ prāṇair viyuktaḥ | ātmahataḥ svayam eva viṣaṃ pītvā mṛtaḥ | tam āhitāgniṃ mṛtaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu pratyāsanno vipro mantravarjitaṃ dahet | tatra sparśanaṃ dahanaṃ vahanaṃ vā yaḥ karoti sa viprair anujñātaḥ sparśanādidoṣaparihārāya prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchram ācaret | lokāgnāv ity anena gārhapatyādīnāṃ vyāvṛttiḥ | yady apy atītasya caturthādhyāyasyādau durmṛtavahanādiprāyaścittam uktaṃ tathāpi tasya sāmānyarūpatvād anāhitāgniviṣayatvenāpy upapatteḥ | āhitāgniviṣayasya tu ihaivopavarṇanam ucitam | vakṣyamāṇatatsaṃskāraṃ prati upodghātarūpatvāt || 10–11 ||
prājāpatyacaraṇānantaraṃ śāstrīyasaṃskāraḥ kartavya ity āha |
daghāsthīni punar gṛhya kṣīraiḥ prakṣālayed dvijaḥ || 12 ||
punar dahet svāginā tu svatantreṇa pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
uktarītyā laukikāgnau yāni dagdhāny asthīni [71] tāni punaḥ saṃgṛhya kṣīraprakṣālanapūrvakaṃ gārhapatyādyagnibhiḥ kalpoktaprakāreṇa dahet | etac ca hārītena darśitam |
brāhmaṇād vadhasaṃprāptau caṇḍālasya kare ‘tha vā |
ātmanā śastraghāte vā śūdravat dāhayet dvijam ||
prājāpatyaṃ caret paścāt sapiṇḍeṣv eva saptamāt |
tadbhasmāsthi gṛhītvaiva viprāṇām anuśāsanāt ||
kṣīraprakṣālanaṃ kuryāt tadasthi pretavad dahet |
punardahanamantraiś ca yathāvidhi samācaret ||
evam eva vidhiṃ kuryāt maraṇe garhitasya ca || iti || 12 ||
idānīṃ proṣitasyāhitāgneḥ saṃskāraṃ “āhitāgnir dvijaḥ” ity ārabhya “tathā kāryaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ” ityantena granthajātena darśayati |
āhitāgnir dvijaḥ kaścit pravasan kālacoditaḥ || 13 ||
dehanāśam anuprāptas tasyāgnir vasate gṛhe |
pretāgnihotrasaṃskāraḥ śrūyatām ṛṣipuṅgavāḥ || 14 ||
kṛṣṇājinaṃ samāstīrya kuśais tu puruṣākṛtiṃ |
ṣaṭśatāni śataṃ caiva palāśānāṃ ca vṛntataḥ || 15 ||
catvāriṃśat śire dadyāc chataṃ kaṇṭhe tu vinyaset |
[72]        bāhubhyāṃ śatakaṃ dadyād aṅgulīṣu daśaiva tu || 16 ||
śataṃ tu jaghane dadyād dviśataṃ tūdare tathā |
dadyād aṣṭau vṛṣaṇayoḥ pañca meḍhre tu vinyaset || 17 ||
ekaviṃśatim ūrubhyāṃ dviśataṃ jānujaṅghayoḥ |
pādāṅgulīṣu ṣaḍ dadyād yajñapātraṃ tato nyaset || 18 ||
śamyāṃ śiśne viniḥkṣipya araṇīṃ muṣkayor api |
juhūṃ ca dakṣiṇe haste vāme tūpabhṛtaṃ nyaset || 19 ||
karṇe colūkhalaṃ dadyād pṛṣṭhe ca musalaṃ nyaset |
urasi kṣipya dṛṣadaṃ taṇḍulājyatilān mukhe || 20 ||
śrotre ca prokṣaṇīṃ dadyād ājyasthālīṃ tu cakṣuṣoḥ |
karṇe netre mukhe ghrāṇe hiraṇyaśakalaṃ nyaset || 21 ||
[73]        agnihotropakaraṇam aśeṣaṃ tatra niḥkṣipet |
asau svargāya lokāya svāhety ekāhutiṃ sakṛt || 22 ||
dadyāt putro ‘tha vā bhrātāpy anyo vāpi ca bāndhavāḥ |
yathā dahanasaṃskāras tathā kāryaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 23 || iti |
yadā proṣita āhitāgnir deśāntare mriyate agniś ca svagṛhe vasati tadānīm āstrīrṇe kṛṣṇājine palāśavṛntaiḥ dehākṛtiṃ kuśabaddhāṃ nirmāya tadavayaveṣu yajñapātrāṇi niḥkṣipya “asau svargāya lokāya svāhā” ity ekām ājyāhutiṃ juhuyāt | yatra dravyānirdeśena homas tatrājyam eva homadravyam | tataḥ kalpoktaprakāreṇa kṛtsnaṃ saṃskāraṃ samāpayet |
nanu – anyathā palāśavṛntānāṃ saṃkhyā smṛtyantare śrūyate | tatra hārītaḥ |
deśāntaragate vipre vipanne kālaparyayāt |
śarīranāśe kalpaḥ syād āhitāgner viśeṣataḥ ||
kṛṣṇājinaṃ samāstīrya puruṣākṛtim eva ca |
trīṇi ṣaṣṭhiśataṃ vṛntān pālāśāṃs tu samāhitaḥ ||
aśītyardhaṃ śire dadyād grīvāyāṃ daśa eva ca |
bāhubhyāṃ tu śataṃ dadyād aṅgulyor daśa eva ca ||
urasi triṃśataṃ dadyāt jaṭhare viṃśatis tathā |
[74]        aṣṭau vṛṣaṇayor dadyāt pañca meḍhre tu kalpayet ||
ūrubhyāṃ tu śataṃ dadyāt dviśataṃ jānujaṅghayoḥ |
pādāṅgulyor daśaiva syād etat pretasya kalpanam || iti |
bāḍham | atra vyavasthāpakahetor abhāvād vikalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | sa caicchikaḥ || 13–23 ||
āhitāgnisaṃskārasya phalam anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ viśadayati |
īdṛśaṃ tu vidhiṃ kuryāt brahmalokagatiḥ smṛtā |
dahanti ye dvijās taṃ tu ye yānti paramāṃ gatim || 24 ||
anyathā kurvate karma tv ātmabuddhyā pracoditāḥ |
bhavanty alpāyuṣas te vai patanti narake ‘śucau || 25 || iti |
tatra saṃskāryasyāhitāgneḥ brahmalokaprāptiḥ | saṃskartur yathoktakāriṇaḥ paramā gatiḥ | ayathoktakāriṇaḥ paṇḍitaṃmanyasya narakaprāptiḥ || 24–25 ||
prakaṭayati vibhaktaṃ yaḥ prakīrṇāvaśeṣaṃ
samadhikaparibarhaṃ saṃskṛtiṃ cāhitāgneḥ |
prakṛtigahanam etaṃ pañcamādhyāyam evaṃ
viśadam ayam akārṣīt vyākhyayā mādhavāryaḥ || 1 ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravarttika-parameśvaraśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhvāmātyasya kṛtau pārāśravyākhyāyāṃ
pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ || 5 ||
[77]
atha ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
īśvaraṃ sarvalokānāṃ bhaktānāṃ bhadradāyakam |
jānakīvallabhaṃ rāmaṃ manmahe maunilocanam || 1 ||
prakīrṇakaprakāśaprāyaścittaprasaṅgāgatāhitāgnisaṃskāraḥ pañcamādhyāye nirūpitaḥ | atha ṣaṣṭhe ‘dhyāye prakṛtam eva prāyaścittam anuvartayiṣyamāṇo malinīkaraṇasaṃkarīkaraṇopapātakānāṃ prāyaścittāni prādhānyena vivakṣuḥ prathamaṃ pratijānīte |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi prāṇihatyāsu niṣkṛtim |
parāśareṇa pūrvoktāṃ manvarthe ‘pi ca vistṛtām || 1 || iti |
atha parkīrṇakaprāyaścittakathanānantaraṃ malinīkaraṇarūpāsu prāṇihatyāsu prāyaścittaṃ pravakṣyāmi | hatyāsv iti bahuvacanaṃ malinīkaraṇādyavāntarabhedābhiprāyam | mandbuddhīnāṃ smṛtyantaraparyālocane saty āpātato virodhabuddhir udeti | nyāyadarśināṃ tu na tatheti sūcayituṃ ṛṣyantarasaṃmatyupanyāsaḥ | parāśaraśabdena vṛddhaparāśaro vivakṣitaḥ | manvarthe manuprokte dharmaśāstre vistṛtām || 1 ||
pratijñāteṣv ādau malinīkaraṇaviśeṣasya krauñcādivadhasya prāyaścittam āha |
[78]        krauñcasārasahaṃsāś ca cakravākaṃ ca kukkuṭam |
jālapādaṃ ca śarabham ahorātreṇa śudhyati || 2 || iti |
krauñcādayaḥ pakṣiviśeṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ | jālapādaśarabhau yady apy aprasiddhau tathāpi pakṣibhiḥ samabhivyāhārāt tāv api pakṣiviśeṣau draṣṭavyau | atra hatvety adhyāhāraḥ kartavyaḥ | ahorātreṇa śudhyatīti ahorātreṇaikopavāsenety arthaḥ | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
cakravākaṃ tathā kraujcaṃ tittiraṃ śukasārike |
śyenaṃ gṛdhram ulūkaṃ ca tathā pārāvatān api ||
ṭiṭṭibhaṃ jālapādaṃ ca madguṃ kukkuṭam eva ca |
evaṃ pakṣiṣu sarveṣu dinam ekam abhojanam || iti | (SaṃvSm 145–46)
nanu – haṃsādivadhe godānaṃ manur āha |
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākaṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇam eva ca |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayed brāhmaṇāya gām || (MDh 11.135)
[79] iti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
haṃsaśyenakapikravyājjalasthalaśikhaṇḍinaḥ |
bhāsaṃ ca hatvā dadyād gāmakravyādas tu vatsikām || iti | (YDh 3.272)
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | godānasyopavāsāśaktadhanikaviṣayatvāt || 2 ||
balākādiṣu pūrvoktāt nyūnaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
balākāṭiṭṭibhau vāpi śukapārāvatāv api |
ahinakravighātī ca śudhyate naktabhojanāt || 3 || iti |
balākāṭiṭṭibhau śukapārāvatau ca yau tayor hantā naktabhojanāt śudhyati iti yojanīyam | yat tu saṃvartenoktam,
haṃsaṃ bakaṃ balākāṃ ca śvāvidhaṃ barhiṇaṃ tathā |
sārasaṃ cāṣabhāsau ca hatvā trīn divasān kṣapet || (SaṃvSm 144)
iti tat santatavadhe draṣṭavyam || 3 ||
[80]
vṛkādiṣu na pūrvavad bhojanatyāgaḥ | kiṃ tu prāṇasaṃyamaḥ kartavya ity āha |
vṛkakākakapotānāṃ śāritittirighātakaḥ |
antarjala ubhe saṃdhye prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || 4 || iti |
nātra vṛko ‘raṇyaśvā, mṛgasamabhivyāhārābhāvāt | pakṣisamabhivyāhārāt tu pakṣiviśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | vṛkakākakapotānāṃ hantety adhyāhāraḥ | saṃdhe iti “atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā” | tataś ca prāṇāyāmenety ekavacanaśravaṇe ‘py āvṛttir labhyate | yāvadbhiḥ prāṇāyāmair nairantaryeṇa saṃdhyādvayaṃ samāpyate tāvataḥ prāṇāyāmān kuryād ity arthaḥ | yat tu manunā tittiryādivadhe tiladroṇādidānam uktam,
ghṛtakumbhaṃ varāhe tu tiladroṇaṃ tu tittirau |
śuke dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ krauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyanam || (MDh 11.134)
iti, yājñavalkyenāpi śukādivadhe dvihāyanavatsādidānam uktam,
gaje nīlavṛṣāḥ pañca śuke vatso dvihāyanaḥ |
[81]        kharājameṣeṣu vṛṣo deyaḥ krauñce trihāyaṇaḥ || (YDh 3.271)
iti, tat sarvaṃ pūrvavat dhanikaviṣayatvena veditavyam || 4 ||
gṛdhrādivadhe sārdhadinadvayaṃ vratacaryām āha |
gṛdhraśyenaśaśādānām ulūkalasya ca ghātakaḥ |
apakvāśī dinaṃ tiṣṭhet trikālaṃ mārutāśanaḥ || 5 || iti |
śyenaḥ kapotādīn pakṣiṇo nihanti | na tu śaśam atti | ataḥ śyenaśaśādau bhinnajātīyau | apakvāśī vahnipākarahitakandamūlaphalādikam ekasmin dine ‘śnīyāt | tataḥ sārdhaṃ dinaṃ mārutāśanaḥ upavased ity arthaḥ | yat tu kāśyapa āha: “bakabalākahaṃsasārasakāraṇḍavacakravākakukkuṭa-gṛdhraśyenakhañjarīṭaṭiṭṭibholūkaśukasārikātittirimayūrakuraramudgakamecakakalaviṅkakapota-pārāvatādīnāṃ vadhe prāyaścittaṃ ahotrāropoṣitaḥ sarvabījāni ca dadyāt” (KāśSm 4.1) iti, tad idam aśaktaviṣayam || 5 ||
śaktasyāpi valgulyādau balākadisadṛśaṃ prāyaścittaṃ darśayati |
valgulīṭiṭṭibhānāṃ ca kokilākhañjarīṭake |
lāvikāraktapakṣeṣu śudhyate naktabhojanāt || 6 || iti |
atrāprasiddho ‘pi valgulīśabdaḥ sāhacaryāt pakṣiviśeṣavācī draṣṭavyaḥ | yady api ṭiṭṭibhaśabdo balākādivacane ‘pi paṭhitaḥ tathāpy avāntarajātibhedam āśritya paunaruktyaṃ parihartavyam | valgulīṭiṭṭibhānāṃ hantā kokilakhañjarīṭake hate | raktpakṣeṣu hateṣv iti śeṣaḥ | yat tv aṅgirasā darśitam,
kāke bhāse ca gṛdhre ca ṭiṭṭibhe khañjarīṭake |
yathā gavi tathā hatyāṃ bhagavān aṅgirāvravīt ||
[83] iti, tat santatahantṛviṣayam | aṅgirāgrahaṇena vṛddhāṅgirā ucyate || 6 ||
jalakākādīnāṃ vadhe prāyaścittam āha |
kāraṇḍacakorāṇāṃ piṅgalākurarasya ca |
bhāradvājādikaṃ hatvā śivaṃ pūjya viśudhyati || 7 || iti |
ādiśabdena bhāradvājavisadṛśāḥ pakṣiviśeṣā gṛhyante | teṣāṃ ca vadhe śaivamantrakalpoktavidhānena śivaṃ saṃpūjya viśudhyati || 7 ||
pūrvatrānuktānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ vadhe sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ darśayati |
bheruṇḍacāṣabhāsāṃś ca pārāvatakapiñjalau |
pakṣiṇāṃ caiva sarveṣām ahorātram abhojanam || 8 || iti |
ṭiṭṭibhavat pārāvate ‘py avāntarajātibhedo draṣṭavyaḥ | kapiñjalaṃ hatvā | pakṣiṇāṃ hatyāyām iti śeṣaḥ | yad idaṃ malinīkaraṇaviśeṣasya pakṣivadhasya sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittam abhihitaṃ tasya pradarśanārthatvenāvaśiṣṭe ‘pi malinīkaraṇe kṛmikīṭādivadhe madyānugatabhojane ca prāyaścittam unneyam | tac ca viṣṇunā darśitam | [84]
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam |
kṛcchrātikṛcchram atha vā prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam || iti |
pakṣiṣu kṛmyādiṣu ca sāsthyanasthibhedena prāyaścittaviśeṣaḥ smṛtyantare ‘bhihitaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
asthimatāṃ tu sattvānāṃ sahasrasya pramāpaṇe |
pūrṇe cānasy anasthnāṃ tu śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
apraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret |
[85]        asthimatāṃ sahasraṃ tu tathānasthimatām anaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.269)
śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “kṣudrajantūnām anasthnām anasaḥ pramāpaṇe śūdravadho ‘sthimatāṃ sahasraṃ pramāpya śūdravadhaḥ” iti | asthimatsahasravadhe śakaṭaparimitān asthivadhe ca vakṣyamāṇaśūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret | pratyekavadhe manur āha |
kiṃcid eva tu viprāya dadyād asthimatāṃ vadhe |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || iti | (MDh 11.141)
|| 8 ||
malinīkaraṇānāṃ prāyaścittam abhidhāya saṃkarīkaraṇānāṃ prāyaścittam āha |
hatvā mūṣakamārjārasarpājagaraḍuṇḍubhān |
kṛsaraṃ bhojayed viprān lohadaṇḍaś ca dakṣiṇā || 9 || iti |
atra nakulo ‘pi draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “hatvā mūṣakamārjāranakulamaṇḍūka- [86] ḍuṇḍubhājagarāṇām anyatamaṃ kṛsarān bhojayitvā lohadaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt” iti (ViDh 50.31) | kṛsaraṃ tilamudgamiśram annam | lohaśabdena kārṣṇāyasam ucyate | tathā ca manuḥ |
abhriṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt sarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ | iti | (MDh 11.133)
yat tu śaṅkhenoktam,
hatvā dvijas tathā sarpān jaleśayabileśayau |
saptarātraṃ tathā kuryāt vrataṃ brahmahates tu yat || (ŚaṅSm 17.11)
iti, – brahmahaṇo vakṣyamāṇaṃ yat vrataṃ tanmadhye saptarātrocitaṃ vratam ācaret – yad api saṃvartenoktam,
maṇḍūkanakulau hatvā sarpājagaramūṣakān |
trirātropoṣitaḥ samyak śuddho brāhmaṇabhojanāt || (SaṃSm 147)
[87] iti, yad apy uśanasoktam: “sarpahantā māṣamātraṃ dadyāt” iti, yac ca vasiṣṭhenoktam: “śvamārjāranakulamaṇḍūkasarpājagaramūṣakān hatvā kṛcchraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ caret kiṃcid dadyāt” (VaDh 21.24) iti — tatra sarvatra prāyaścittagauravalāghavaparyālocanayā kāmakṛtākāmakṛtābhyāsānabhyāsādiviṣayatvam ūhanīyam || 9 ||
śiśumārādīn hatvā dinam ekaṃ vratatvena vṛntākamātraṃ bhakṣayed ity āha |
śiśumāraṃ tathā godhāṃ hatvā kūrmaṃ ca śalyakam |
vṛntākaphalabhakṣī cāpy ahorātreṇa śudhyati || 10 || iti |
kāśyapas tu lohadaṇḍadānam apy āha: “kṛkalāsasarpanakulagodhāśalyakavadhe ‘horātroṣitaś cānte lohadaṇḍaṃ dadyāt” iti (KSm 5.5) | manus tu pakṣāntarāṇy āha |
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkam eva ca |
śvagodholūkakākāṃś ca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret ||
payaḥ pibet trirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
upaspṛśet sravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet || iti | (MDh 11.131–32)
yamo ‘pi: “śvanakulasarpamārjāramaṇḍūkavikirakikīdivikagodhāgṛdholūkavāyasamayūra-grāmacaṭakasṛgālamūṣakān hatvā ekaikavadhe śūdravadhaḥ’ iti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
mārjāragodhānakulamaṇḍūkaśvapatatriṇaḥ |
[89]        hatvā tryahaṃ pibet kṣīraṃ kṛcchraṃ vā pādikaṃ caret || iti | (YDh 3.270)
viṣṇur api: “godholūkacāṣakākavadhe trirātram upavaset” iti (ViDh 50.32) | īdṛśānāṃ sarveṣāṃ visaṃvādivacanānāṃ sākṣyāsyavasthāpakaṃ vacanāntaraṃ yatra na labhyate | tatra sarvatra vidhīyamānaprāyaścittānusāreṇa saṃhatāsaṃhatabuddhipūrvakābhuddhi-pūrvakābhyāsānabhyāsāpadanāpadādiviṣayatvaṃ kalpanīyam || 10 ||
āraṇyaśvādīnāṃ hantur upavāsatrayapūrvakaṃ tilaprasthadānam āha |
vṛkajambūkaṛkṣāṇāṃ tarakṣuśvānaghātakaḥ |
tilaprasthaṃ dvije dadyāt vāyubhakṣo dinatrayam || 11 || iti |
atropavāsatrayaṃ viṣṇur apy āha: “śvānaṃ hatvā trirātram upavaset” iti (ViDh 50.30) | paiṭhīnasir atra śūdrahatyāvratam āha: “kākolūkakṛkalāsakaṅkakharavṛkasṛgālabhāsabarhiṇa-śukamūṣakacakravākahaṃsapraveṇikanakulamaṇḍūlabiḍālaśvavadhe [90] eteṣām ekaikasmin śūdravadhavad vihitam” |
nanu – śūdrahatyāvrataṃ ṣāṇmāsikam | tac cātyantagurutvād vṛkāihatyāyām ayuktam |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, asya vratasya cirakālanairantaryaviṣayatvena netavyatvāt || 11 ||
gajādivadhe trikālasnānayuktam ekopavāsam āha |
jaḍasya ca turaṅgasya mahiṣoṣṭranipātane |
prāyaścittam ahorātraṃ trisaṃdhyam avagāhanam || 12 || iti |
manus tv atra dānaviśeṣam āha |
vāso dadyād dhayaṃ hatvā pañca nīlān vṛṣān gajam |
ajameṣāv anaḍvāhaṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam || iti | (MDh 11.136)
viṣṇur api: “gajaṃ hatvā pañca nīlavṛṣān dadāt | turaṅgaṃ hatvā vāsaḥ | ekahāyanam anaḍvāhaṃ kharavadhe | meṣājavadhe suvarṇakṛṣṇalam | tilam uṣṭravadhe ca” (ViDh 50.25–29) [91] iti | etat tritayaṃ dhanikaviṣayam | dhanarahitasyāvṛttiviṣayam abhipretya saṃvarta āha |
hastinaṃ turagaṃ hatvā mahiṣoṣṭrau kapiṃ tathā |
eṣu sarveṣu kurvīta saptarātram abhojanam || iti | (SaṃSm 141)
jābālir api |
hastinaṃ turagaṃ hatvā mahiṣaṃ gāṃ tathaiva ca |
kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ kuryāt gobhūkanyānṛteṣu ca || iti || 12 ||
kuraṅgādivadhe brāhmaṇabhojanasahitam upavāsatrayam āha |
kuraṅgaṃ vānaraṃ siṃhaṃ citraṃ vyāghraṃ tu ghātayan |
śudhyate sa trirātreṇa viprāṇāṃ tarpaṇena ca || 13 || iti |
citro vyāghrasadṛśamṛgaviśeṣaḥ | tarpaṇaṃ bhojanam | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
[92]        vyāghraṃ śvānaṃ kharaṃ siṃhaṃ mṛgaṃ sūkaram eva ca |
etān hatvā dvijaḥ kuryāt brāhmaṇānāṃ tu bhojanam || iti | (SaṃSm 142)
jābālis tu āvṛttiṃ saṃghātaṃ cābhipretyāha |
siṃhavyāghravṛkāṇāṃ ca mṛgakhaḍgarurudvipān |
hatvā sāntapanaṃ kuryāt gobhūkanyānṛteṣu ca || iti |
gavādiviṣayāṇy anṛtāni gobhūkanyānṛtāni | sumantur api: “vānarasiṃhamārjāramaṇḍūka-mūṣakaśvavadhe prājāpatyam” iti || 13 ||
mṛgādihatyāyām upavāsapūrvakanīvārādipāraṇam āha |
mṛgarohidvarāhāṇam aver bastasya ghātakaḥ |
aphālakṛṣṭam aśnīyād ahorātram upoṣya saḥ || 14 || iti |
kuraṅgaśabdasya mṛgāvāntarajātivācakatvāt sāmānyajātivācī mṛgaśabdo gobalīvardanyāyena kuraṅgetaraviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ | yuddhayogyo meṣo bastaḥ | dhanikasyāvṛttiviṣaye kaśyapa āha: [93] “ajāvikavadhe trirātraṃ prāyaścittam | cīrṇānte hiraṇyaṃ dadyāt vastraṃ ca” iti (KaśSm 3.34–35) || 14 ||
pūrvatrānuktānāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ vadhe japasahitam upavāsam āha |
evaṃ catuṣpadānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vanacāriṇām |
ahorātroṣitas tiṣṭhej japed vai jātavedasam || 15 || iti |
nyāyatattvavidā pāpatāratamyānusāreṇa prāyaścittatāratamyam ūhanīyam iti vivakṣitvā pūrvoktānāṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣāṇāṃ pradarśanārthatvaṃ dyotayitum evam ity uktam | samūhanīyāḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣāḥ smṛtyantareṣu darśitāḥ | tatra śaṅkhaḥ: “paśūn hatvā tathā grāmyān māsaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ” iti (ŚaṅSm 17.10) | śātātapo ‘pi: “hastyaśvarāsabhavadhe āraṇyapaśuvadhe ca māsenaikena śudhyati” iti | vyāso ‘pi |
[94]        sūkaroṣṭrakharān hatvā tryaham etad vrataṃ caret |
sarvāṃś ca prāṇinaḥ sthūlān maṇḍūkanakuleṣv ahaḥ || iti |
kaśyapo ‘pi: “mṛgamahiṣavarāhakuñjaragaṇḍaśarabhatarakṣuvānarasiṃhavyāghravṛṣacamara-śalyakādīnām anyeṣāṃ ca vadhe ahorātroṣitaś cīrṇānte ghṛtaṃ dadyāt” iti (KaśSm 3.1) | tatra sarvatra prāyaścittagauravalāghavānusāreṇa kāmakṛtākāmakṛtābhyāsānabhyāsaviṣayatvaṃ jñātavyam || 15 ||
saṃkarīkaraṇamalinīkaraṇaprakīrṇakānāṃ prāyaścittam abhidhāyopapātakānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ darśayati |
śilpinaṃ kārukaṃ śūdraṃ striyaṃ vā yas tu ghātayet |
prājāpatyadvayaṃ kṛtvā vṛṣaikādaśa dakṣiṇā || 16 || iti |
śilpī citrakārādiḥ | kāruḥ sūpakārādiḥ | vṛṣaḥ [95] ekādaśo yāsāṃ gavāṃ tā vṛṣaikādaśāḥ | tadrūpā dakṣiṇā vṛṣaikādaśadakṣiṇā | deyeti śeṣaḥ | ata eva gautamaḥ śūdravadhaṃ prakṛtyāha: “ṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gā dadyāt” iti (GDh 22.16) | manur api |
pramāpya śūdraṃ ṣaṇmāsān etad eva vrataṃ caret |
vṛṣabhaikādaśā vāpi dadyād viprāya gāḥ sitāḥ || iti | (MDh 11.130)
|| 16 ||
kṣatriyavaiśyayor vadhe pūrvasmād adhikaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
vaiśyaṃ vā kṣatriyaṃ vāpi nirdoṣaṃ yo ‘bhighātayet |
so ‘pi kṛcchradvayaṃ kuryāt goviṃśaddakṣiṇāṃ dadet || 17 || iti |
nirdoṣam iti viśeṣaṇāt sadoṣavadhe nyūnaṃ prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam | tac ca caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
kṣatriyasya vadhaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇatrayam |
vaiśyasya tu dvayaṃ kuryāt śūdrasyaindavam eva ca || iti |
yat tu saṃvartena tato ‘pi nyūnaṃ prāyaścittam uktam,
nihatya kṣatriyaṃ mohāt tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśudhyati |
[96]        kuryād evānupūrvyeṇa trīn kṛcchrāṃs tu yathāvidhi ||
vaiśyahatyāṃ tu saṃprāpya kathaṃcin mūḍhacetanaḥ |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta sa naro vaiśyaghātakaḥ ||
kuryāt śūdravadhe vipraḥ kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ tathā || (SaṃSm 126–28)
iti, etat sadoṣasyākāmakṛtavadhe proktam ity avirodhaḥ |
nanu – nirdoṣasya vadhe sadakṣiṇātikṛcchradvayādhikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ manunā darśitam |
akāmatas tu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ |
vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt śuddhyartham ātmanaḥ ||
tryabdaṃ cared vā niyato jaṭī brahmahaṇo vratam |
vasan dūratare grāme vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ ||
[97]        evam eva cared abdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyād ekaśataṃ gavām || iti | (MDh 11.128–30)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyāt kṣatravadhe pumān |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ vāpi vatsaratritayaṃ caret ||
vaiśyahābdaṃ cared caiva dadyād vaikaśataṃ gavām |
ṣaṇmāsān śūdrahāpy etad dhenūr dadyād daśātha vā || iti | (YDh 3.266–37)
traivārṣikā apy adhikaṃ gautama āha: “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam ṛṣabhaiksahasrāś ca gā dadyāt | vaiśye traivārṣikam ṛṣabhaikaśatāś ca gā dadyāt | śūdre saṃvatsaram ṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gā dadyāt | anātreyyāṃ caivam” iti (GDh 22.14–17) | śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “pūrvavad amatipūrvaṃ triṣu varṇeṣu pramāpya dvādaśanavaṣaṭtrīn saṃvatsarān vratāny ādiśet | teṣām ante gosahasraṃ tato ‘rdhaṃ tasyārdhaṃ dadyāt sarveṣām ānupūrvyeṇa” iti | śātātapo ‘pi |
[98]        rājanyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ ṣaṭ trīny ekaṃ cared vadhe |
varṣāṇi brahmacaryaṃ tu viśuddhyarthaṃ krameṇa tu ||
godānaṃ tu vratasyānte brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam |
kāryaṃ sadakṣiṇaṃ samyak viśudhyeta yathāvidhi || iti |
hārīto ‘pi |
brāhmṇaḥ kṣatriyaṃ hatvā ṣaḍvarṣāṇi vrataṃ caret |
vaiśyaṃ hatvā hared evaṃ vrataṃ traivāriṣikaṃ dvijaḥ ||
śūdraṃ hatvā cared ekaṃ vṛṣabhaikādaśāś ca gāḥ | iti |
