uśanā api: “rājanyavadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmahatyāvratam | tasyānte vṛṣabhaiksahasragodānaṃ ca” iti | vasiṣṭhas tu ṣaḍvārṣikād adhikam aṣṭavārṣikam āha: “evaṃ rājanyaṃ hatvāṣṭau varṣāṇi caret | ṣaḍ vaiśyam | trīṇi śūdram” iti (VaDh 20.31–33) | baudhāyanas tu navavārṣikam āha: “nava samā rājanyasya | tisro vaiśyasya | saṃvatsaraṃ śūdrasya strīṇāṃ ca | brāhmaṇavad ātreyyāḥ” (BDh 2.1.1.8–12) [99] iti | yamaḥ |
hatvā savaninaṃ vaiśyaṃ rājanyam api dīkṣitam |
hatvā tu brahmahatyaiva tathātreyīṃ ca brāhmaṇīm || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi |
yāgasthaṃ kṣatriyaṃ hatvā vaiśyaṃ hatvā tu yāgagam |
etad eva vrataṃ kuryād ātreyīviniṣūdakaḥ || (ŚaṅSm 17.4)
[100] iti | atraiteṣu udāhṛteṣu vacaneṣu traivārṣikaṣaḍvārṣikāṣṭavārṣikanavavārṣika-dvādaśavārṣikarūpāḥ pañca pakṣā bhānti | ācāryoktaś cātikṛcchradvayapakṣa ekaḥ | atraivaṃ viṣayavyavasthāvadho hi nirdoṣaḥ kṣatriyas trividhaḥ | uttamo madhyamo ‘dhamaś ceti | tatra rājyaparipālanādiyuktaḥ savanastha uttamaḥ | savanarahitaḥ paripālako madhyamaḥ | ubhayarahito jātimātrakṣatriyo ‘dhamaḥ | tatraikaikasya vadhe buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvabhedena dve dve prāyaścitte yojanīye | eṣu vacaneṣu kvacid godānaṃ śrūyate kvacin na | tatra dhanikādhanikaviṣayabhedena vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | etenaiva nyāyena vyavasthitavikalpam abhipretya yājñavalkyena “brahmahatyāvrataṃ vāpi” (YDh 3.266) ity uktam | vaiśyaśūdrayor api uttamamadhyādhamabhedena prāyaścittavyavasthā yojanīyā | yathā hantavyabhedena prāyaścittatāratamyam uktaṃ tathā hantṛbhede ‘py avagantavyam | ata eva viṣṇuḥ |
vipre tu sakalaṃ deyaṃ pādonaṃ kṣatriye smṛtam |
vaiśye ‘rdhaṃ pādam ekaṃ tu śūdrajātiṣu śasyate || iti |
kavaṣo ‘pi |
viprārdhaṃ kṣatriyo proktaṃ tadardhaṃ vaiśyajātiṣu |
tadardham eva śūdrāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ ||
[101] iti | etac cākāmakṛte draṣṭavyam, viṣṇuproktān nyūnatvāt |
nanu – ācāryeṇa strīvadhe prājāpatyadvayaṃ vṛṣabhaikādaśagodānasahitaṃ yat prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad ayuktam | smṛtyantareṣu nyūnādhikaprāyaścittayor darśanāt | tatra manuḥ nyūnaṃ darśayati |}
jīnakārmukabastāvīn pṛthag dadyād viśuddhaye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ || iti | (MDh 11.138)
jīnaṃ carmamayam udakapātram | anavasthitāḥ svairiṇyaḥ | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
durvṛttā brahmaviṭkṣatraśūdrayoṣāḥ pramāpya tu |
dṛtiṃ dhanur bastam aviṃ kramād dadyād viśuddhaye || iti | (YDh 3.268)
vyāghro ‘pi |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ nārīr hatvānavasthitāḥ |
śaṅkhaśuktyajameṣāṃś ca kramāt dadyād viśuddhaye ||
[102] iti |
etāni vacanāni mūlavacanena na virudhyante sadoṣastrīviṣayatāyās teṣu spaṣṭatvāt | mūlavacanaṃ tu nirdoṣakṣatriyādisannidhipāṭhāt nirdoṣastrīviṣayam |
nanu – nirdoṣastrīvadhe adhikaṃ prāyaścittam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
apraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ hatvā śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret | iti | (YDh 3.269)
maivam, tasya kāmakṛtaviṣayatvena vyavasthāpanīyatvāt | yat tv aṅgirasā darśitam,
āhitāgner dvijāgryasya hatvā patīm aninditām |
brahmahatyāvrataṃ kuryād ātreyīghnas tathaiva ca ||
iti, tatra brahmahatyāvratasyāhitāgnistrīviṣayatvābhidhānāt strījātimātraviṣayeṇa mūlavacanena nāsti virodhaḥ | vasiṣṭhas tu strīvadhe rājanyavratam āha: “anātreyīṃ rājanyahiṃsāyāṃ | rājanyāṃ vaiśyahiṃsāyām | vaiśyāṃ śūdrahiṃsāyām | śūdrāṃ hatvā saṃvatsaram” (VaDh 20.37–40) [103] iti | anātreyīṃ hatvā rājanyahiṃsāyāṃ yad vrataṃ tad ācared iti yojanīyam | hārīto ‘pi: “kṣatriyavad brāhmaṇīṣu | vaiśyavat kṣatriyāsu śūdravat vaiśyāyām | śūdrāṃ hatvā nava māsān” iti (HārSm 20.13–15) | idaṃ rājanyavratam āhitāgnipatnīṃ guṇavatīṃ kāmato ghātukasya draṣṭavyam | prāmādikaviṣaye tu vyāsa āha |
akāmataḥ striyaṃ hatvā brāhmaṇīṃ vaiśyavac caret |
kāmato dviguṇaṃ proktaṃ praduṣṭāṃ tu na kiṃcana || iti |
anātreyīvadhe rājanyavratasyābhihitatvāt ātreyīvadhe brahmahatyāvratam iti gamyate | tathā ca yamaḥ |
hatvā savaninaṃ vaiśyaṃ rājanyam api dīkṣitam |
hatvā tu brahmahatyaiva tathātreyīṃ ca brāḥmaṇīm || iti |
ātreyīlakṣaṇaṃ ca sa evāha |
janmaprabhṛtisaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtā brahmacaryayā |
garbhiṇī vātha vā yā syāt tām ātreyīṃ vinirdiśet || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: [104] “rajasvalām ṛtusnātām ātreyīm āhuḥ | atra hy eṣyadapatyaṃ bhavatīti” iti (VaDh 20.35–36) | anṛtumatyā vadhe jātibhedena bhinnaṃ prāyaścittam āha precetāḥ: “anṛtumatīṃ brāhmaṇīṃ hatvā kṛcchrābdaṃ ṣaṇmāsān veti | kṣatriyāṃ hatvā ṣaṇmāsān māsatrayaṃ veti | vaiśyāṃ hatvā māsatrayaṃ sārdhamāsaṃ veti | śūdrāṃ hatvā sārdhamāsaṃ sārdhatrayoviṃśatidināni veti” iti | pratilomapuruṣasaṃsargenāptyaṃ labdhavatīnāṃ vadhe brahmagarbha āha |
[105]        pratilomaprasūtānāṃ strīṇāṃ māsā vadhe smṛtāḥ |
antaraprabhāvāṇāṃ ca sūtādīnāṃ caturdviṣaṭ || iti |
brāhmaṇyā vadhe ṣaṇmāsāḥ kṣatriyāyāś catvāraḥ vaiśyāyā dvāv iti yojanīyam | sūtavaidehakacaṇḍālānāṃ vadhe ‘py eṣaiva yojanā draṣṭavyā || 17 ||
idānīṃ vaiśyādivadhe prakārāntareṇa prāyaścittam āha |
vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ kriyāsaktaṃ vikarmasthaṃ dvijottamam |
hatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ tasya viṃśadgāś caiva dakṣiṇām || 18 || iti |
nanu – etad vaiśyavadhaprāyaścittaṃ vyāhatam | tathā hi, viṭśabdenātra kiṃ sadoṣo vivakṣyate uta nirdoṣajātimātraḥ, atha vā guṇavān – iti | tatra prathame nyūnaprāyaścittābhidhāyibhyāṃ pūrvodāhṛtābhyāṃ caturviṃśatimata-saṃvartavacanābhyāṃ vyāhanyet | dvitīye nirdoṣajātimātraviṣayeṇa samantarapūrvoktamūlavacanenaiva virodhaḥ | tṛtīye tu,
yāgasthakṣatraviḍghātī cared brahahaṇi vratam | (YDh 3.251)
ity anenaiva virodhaḥ |
maivam, īṣatsuvṛttasya vaiśyasyātra vivakṣitatvāt | ata evātyantasuvṛttena vihitasakalakriyāsaktena [106] śūdreṇa sāhacaryam aviruddham | dvijottamaśabdo vipre prasiddhaḥ | kṣatriya ‘pi kathaṃcid vartayituṃ śakyaḥ | dvijaśabdābhidheyaṃ vaiśyam apekṣya tasyottamatvāt | tatra kṣatriyapakṣe vikarmaṇi kasmiṃścid brahmaghātādau tiṣṭhatīti vikarmasthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati caturvarṇaviṣayakam ekaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nyāyadarśinām etat saṃpadyate | hatvā tau yo vartate tasya – iti yojanīyam | dakṣiṇāṃ nirdiśed iti śeṣaḥ || 18 ||
cāturvarṇyakartṛke caṇḍālavadhe krameṇa prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālaṃ hatavān kaścid brāhmaṇo yadi kaṃcana |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadat || 19 ||
kṣatriyeṇāpi vaiśyena śūdreṇaivetareṇa vā |
caṇḍālasya vadhe prāpte kṛcchrārdhena viśudhyati || 20 || iti |
vṛttibhedena brāhmaṇānāṃ bhede ‘pi na prāyaścittabheda iti vivakṣitatvāt kaścit ity uktam | evaṃ caṇḍāle ‘pi kaṃcaneti viśeṣaṇaṃ yojanīyam | itaro mūrdhāvasiktasūtādiḥ | aṅgirās tv atraiva samānaṃ prāyaścittāntaram āha |
antyajānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syād bhagavān aṅgirābravīt || iti |
hārīto ‘pi |
[107]        cāṇḍālavadhasaṃprāptir brāhmaṇena bhaved yadi |
kārayed dvādaśaṃ kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ tato bhavet || iti | (HārSm 20.71)
etac ca jñānapūrvake draṣṭavyam | ajñānapūrvake tu cāndrāyaṇam | tad āha laugākṣiḥ |
hanane pratilomānāṃ śūdrajānāṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
jñānapūrve parākaḥ syād ajñāne caindavaṃ tathā || iti |
sūtādivadhe ‘py etad eva | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret sarvān apakṛṣṭān nihatya tu | iti | (YDh 3.262)
kṛcchrārdhenety ardhagrahaṇaṃ pādasya pādatrayasya copalakṣaṇam | ata eva laugākṣiḥ |
sarvam ardhaṃ tripādaṃ ca pādaṃ caiva vrataṃ caret |
varṇakramād antarajahiṃsāgamanabhojane || iti || 19–20 ||
yadi caṇḍālaśvapākau corau syātām, tadā tayor vadhe svalpaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
corau śvapākacaṇḍālau vipreṇābhihatau yadi |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || 21 ||
[108] iti | yadi vipreṇābhihatas tadā sa vipraḥ śudhyatīti yojanā | yady api caṇḍālavadho nopapātakeṣu parigaṇitaḥ tathāpi manuṣyavadhasāmānyena tatrāntarbhāvam abhipretya tatprakaraṇe prāyaścittam uktam || 21 ||
caṇḍālaprasaṅgāt tadviṣayāṇāṃ prakīrṇakānām api keṣāṃcit prāyaścittam atrābhidhīyate | tatra saṃbhāṣaṇe prāyaścittam āha |
śvapākaṃ vāpi caṇḍālaṃ vipraḥ saṃbhāṣate yadi |
dvijasaṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt sāvitrīṃ tu sakṛj japet || 22 || iti |
dvijaśabdenānūcāno vipro vivakṣitaḥ, nīcaviprasaṃbhāṣaṇasya prāyaścittarūpatvāsaṃbhavāt | dvijasāṃnidhyābhāve gāyatrījapaḥ ity anukalpo draṣṭavyaḥ | ata eva hārīto vikalpam āha |
cāṇḍālaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣya dvijasaṃbhāṣaṇāt śuciḥ |
sāvitrīṃ vyāhared vāpi iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || iti | (HātSm 20.68)
saṃbhāṣamāṇo vipro yady ucchiṣṭaḥ syāt tadā trirātropavāsam āha sa eva |
ucchiṣṭaḥ saha saṃbhāṣet trirātreṇaiva śudhyati | iti (HārSm 20.69) || 22 ||
ekaśayyāsvāpe sahamārgagamane ca pṛthak prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālaiḥ saha suptaṃ tu trirātram upavāsayet |
caṇḍālaikapathaṃ gatvā gāyatrīsmaraṇāt śuciḥ || 23 ||
[109] iti | spaṣṭo ‘rthaḥ || 23 ||
darśanasparśanayoḥ pṛthak prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍāladarśane sadya ādityam avalokayet |
caṇḍālasparśane caiva sacailaṃ snānam ācaret || 24 || iti |
etad eva āpastambo ‘py āha: “caṇḍālasparśne saṃbhāṣāyāṃ darśane ca doṣaḥ | tatra prāyaścittam avagāhanam apām upasparśanaṃ saṃbhāṣāyāṃ brāhmaṇasaṃbhāṣā darśane jyotiṣāṃ darśanam” iti | sumantur api: “caṇḍāladarśane sūryadarśanam | saṃbhāṣaṇe brāhmaṇābhivyāhāraḥ | saṃsparśane sacailaṃ snānam ācaret” iti | devalo ‘pi |
śvapākaṃ patitaṃ vyaṅgam unmattaṃ śavadāhakam |
sūtikaṃ sūtikāṃ nārīṃ rajasā ca pariplutām ||
śvakukkuṭavarāhāṃśca grāmyān saṃspṛśya mānavaḥ |
sacailaḥ saśirāḥ snātvā tadānīm eva śudhyati || iti |
avasthāviśeṣeṇa sparśane viśeṣam āha śātātapaḥ |
[110]        yena kenacid abhyaktaḥ caṇḍālaṃ saṃspṛśed yati |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
aśuddhān svayam apy etān aśuddhaś ca yadi spṛśet |
viśudhyaty upavāsena trirātreṇa tataḥ śuciḥ ||
ucchiṣṭaḥ saṃspṛśed vipro madyaṃ śūdraṃ śuno ‘śucīn |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || iti |
yat tu devalenoktam,
aśuddhān svayam apy etān śuddhaś ca yadi saṃspṛśet |
viśudhyaty upavāsena trirātreṇa tataḥ śuciḥ ||
ucchiṣṭaṃ saṃspṛśed vipraḥ punaḥ kṛcchreṇa śudhyati |
iti, tat sahopaveśanaśayanādinā cirasparśaviṣayam | yadi spṛṣṭo mūtrādikaṃ kuryāt tadā trirātropavāsaḥ | bhuktocchiṣṭo yadi spṛśet tadā ṣaḍrātropavāsaḥ iti | tad āha śātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālaiḥ śvapacaiḥ spṛṣṭe viṇmūtre kurute dvijaḥ |
trirātraṃ tatra kurvīta bhuktocchiṣṭaḥ ṣaḍ ācaret ||
[111] iti | caṇḍālavat cityādisparśe ‘pi sacailaṃ snānam | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
citiṃ ca citikāṣṭhaṃ ca yūpaṃ caṇḍālam eva ca |
spṛṣṭvā devalakaṃ caiva savāsā jalam āviśet || iti || 24 ||
caṇḍālasvāmikavāpyudakapāne jñānājñānakṛte pṛthak prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālakhātavāpīṣu pītvā salilam agrajaḥ |
ajñānāc caikabhaktena tv ahorātreṇa śudhyati || 25 || iti |
ajñānakṛtasyaikabhaktam | jñānakṛtasya tūpavāsaḥ | ata eva āpastambaḥ caṇḍālavāpīṃ prakṛtyāha |
tattoyaṃ yaḥ pibed vipraḥ kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
akāmāt naktabhojī syād ahorātreṇa kāmataḥ || iti |
tatraivābhyāsaviṣayam abhipretya āpastamba āha |
antyajaiḥ khānitāḥ kūpās taḍāgā vāpy eva ca |
eṣu snātvā ca pītvā ca prājāpatyena śudhyati || iti |
yat tu tenaivoktam |
prapāsv araṇye kaṭaka ca saure
droṇyāṃ jalaṃ keśaviniḥsṛtaṃ vā |
śvapākacaṇḍālaparigraheṣu
pītvā jalaṃ pañcagavyena śudhyet || (ĀpDh 2.2)
iti, tad aśaktaviṣayam || 25 ||
caṇḍālabhāṇḍaspṛṣṭasya kūpasthajalasya pāne ‘dhikaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālabhāṇḍasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pītvā kūpagataṃ jalam |
gomūtrayāvakāhāras trirātrāt śuddhim āpnuyāt || 26 || iti |
caṇḍālo bhāṇḍenodakaṃ yasmāt kūpād ānayati tat kūpasthodakaṃ pītvā gomūtrasahitayavapiṣṭādikaṃ dinatrayam āhāratvena svīkuryāt || 26 ||
tadīyabhāṇḍasthodakapāne jaraṇājaraṇbhedena prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālaghaṭasaṃsthaṃ tu yat toyaṃ pibati dvijaḥ |
tatkṣaṇāt kṣipate yas tu prājāpatyaṃ samācaret || 27 ||
yadi na kṣipate toyaṃ śarīre yasya jīryati |
prājāpatyaṃ na dātavyaṃ kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || 28 ||
[113] iti | prathamam ajñānāt pītvā paścāt tadānīm eva vijñāya yadi vamet tadā prājāpatyam | tajjaraṇe sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ pariṣadā dātavyam | na tu prājāpatyam | tasyājñānaviṣayatvam aṅgirā darśayati |
caṇḍālaparigṛhītaṃ yad ajñānād udakaṃ pibet |
tasya śuddhiṃ vijānīyāt prājāpatyena nityaśaḥ ||
yas tu caṇḍālasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pibet kiṃcid dhi kāmataḥ |
sa tu sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ caret śuddhyartham ātmanaḥ || iti |
yat tu devalenoktam,
yas tu cāṇḍālabhāṇḍastham ajñānād udakaṃ pibet |
sa tu tryaheṇa śudhyet tu śūdras tv ekena śudhyati ||
iti, tad āpadviṣayam || 27–28 ||
buddhipūrvakaṃ tatpāne varṇabhedena prāyaścittabhedam āha |
caret sāntapanaṃ vipraḥ pājāpatyam anantaraḥ |
tadardhaṃ tu cared vaiśyaḥ pādaṃ śūdras tad ācaret || 29 || iti |
anantaraḥ kṣatriyaḥ | etasya buddhipūrvaviṣayatvam āpastamba āha |
caṇḍālakūpabhāṇḍasthaṃ naraḥ kāmāj jalaṃ pibet |
prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ tatra varṇe varṇe vinirdiśet ||
caret sāntapanaṃ vipraḥ prājāpatyaṃ tu bhūmipaḥ |
tadardhaṃ tu cared vaiśyaḥ śūdre pādaṃ vinirdiśet || iti || 29 ||
caṇḍālabhāṇḍodakapānaprasaṅgād itarāntyajabhāṇḍodakādipāne ‘pi prāyaścittam āha |
bhāṇḍastham antyajānāṃ tu jalaṃ dadhi payaḥ pibet |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdraś caiva pramādataḥ || 30 ||
brahmakūrcopavāsena dvijātīnāṃ tu niṣkṛtiḥ |
śūdrasya copavāsena tathā dānena śaktitaḥ || 31 || iti |
antyajā rajakādayaḥ,
rajakaś carmakāraś ca naṭo baruḍa eva ca |
kaivartamedabhillāś ca saptaite cāntyajāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt | brahmakūrcam ācārya evopariṣṭād vakṣyati | tatsahita upavāsaḥ traivarṇikasya | tadrahitaś caturthasya | [115] brahmakūrcasthāne yathāśakti dānaṃ draṣṭavyam | āmādiṣu bhāṇḍāntaraprāpteṣu nāsti kaścid doṣaḥ | tathā ca caturviṃśatimate |
āmaṃ māṃsaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ snehāś ca phalasaṃbhavāḥ |
antyabhāṇḍasthitā hy ete niṣkrāntāḥ śucayaḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
mahatsu tu taṭākādiṣu caṇḍālādisaṃbandhe ‘pi nāsti kaścid doṣaḥ | alpeṣu tu kūpanyāyaḥ | tad āha viṣṇuḥ |
jalāśayeṣv athālpeṣu sthāvareṣu mahītale |
kūpavat kathitā śuddhir mahatsu tu na dūṣaṇam || iti || 30–31 ||
ajñānapūrvake caṇḍālānnabhojane prāyaścittam āha |
bhuṅkte ‘jñānād dvijaśreṣṭhaś caṇḍālānnaṃ kathaṃcana |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro daśarātreṇa śudhyati || 32 || iti |
spaṣṭam || 32 ||
tatra pramāṇam āha |
ekaikaṃ grāsam aśnīyād gomūtrayāvakasya ca |
[116]        daśāhaṃ niyamasthasya vrataṃ tat tu vinirdiśet || 33 || iti |
gomūtreṇa pācitaṃ yad yavamayam annaṃ tasya grāsanekaikasmin dine bhuñjīta | niyamaḥ snānamaunādiḥ | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
snānaṃ maunopavāsejyāsvādhyāyopasthanigrahāḥ |
niyamā guruśuśrūṣāśaucākrodhāpramādatāḥ || iti | (YDh 3.314)
īdṛśaniyamaniṣṭhasya tad yāvakāhāravrataṃ nirdiśet | buddhipūrvake tv aṅgirā āha |
antyāvasāyinām annaṃ aśnīyād yaś ca kāmataḥ |
sa tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā || iti |
antyāvasāyinaś caṇḍālādayaḥ | tāṃś ca sa evāha |
caṇḍālaḥ śvapacaḥ kṣattā sūto vaidehakas tathā |
māgadhāyogavau caiva sarve hy antyāvasāyinaḥ || iti |
āmapakvayoś caṇḍālānnayor vratabhedam āha viṣṇuḥ: [117] “cāṇḍālānnaṃ bhuktvā trirātram upavaset | siddhaṃ bhuktvā parākaḥ” iti (ViSm 51.57–58) | atra mūlavacanokto gomūtrayāvakāhāraḥ siddhānnaviṣayo bhaviṣyati | yāvakasahitasya daśarātropavāsasya parākasamatvāt | yat tu hārītenoktam,
caṇḍālānnaṃ pramādena yadi bhuñjīta vai dvijaḥ |
tataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret ||
śūdro vāpy ardhamāsaṃ vai bhuktvā caiva jitendriyaḥ |
trirātram upavāsī ca brāhmaṇāṃs tarpayet śuciḥ || (HārSm 2.73–75)
iti, tad gomūtrayāvakāhārāśaktaviṣayam | buddhipūrvakasya cirakālānuvṛttau manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
pataty ajñānato vipro jñānāt sāmyaṃ tu gacchati || iti | (MDh 11.175)
atra cīrṇavratasya punar upanayanaṃ kūrmapurāṇe darśitam |
cāṇḍālānnaṃ dvijo bhuktvā samyak cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
buddhipūrve tu kṛcchrābdaṃ punaḥ saṃskāram eva ca || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “caṇḍālapatitānnabhojane punar upanayanam” iti (VaDh 20.17) || 33 ||
atha kaṃcit kālaṃ ekasmin gṛhe caṇḍālena saha vyavahartuḥ “avijñātas tu caṇḍālaḥ” ityādinā “bhūmidoṣo na vidyate” ityantena śokasamūhena prāyaścittam āha |
avijñātas tu caṇḍālo yatra veśmani tiṣṭhati |
vijñāte tūpsannasya dvijāḥ kurvanty anugraham || 34 || iti |
anena ślokena pariṣadupasattir vihitā | yady api sarveṣu prāyaścitteṣu pariṣadupasattiḥ samānā tathāpi prakaṭapāpeṣv eva sā | na tu rahasyeṣu | atra tu rahasyam api prakaṭīkartavyam ity abhipretya prātisvikam upasattyabhidhānam | anugrahaś caritavyaprāyaścittaviśeṣopadeśaḥ || 34 ||
upasadanayogyaṃ pariṣadviśeṣaṃ darśayituṃ dvijān viśinaṣṭi |
munivaktrodgatān dharmān gāyanto vedapāragāḥ |
patantam uddhareyus taṃ dharmajñāḥ pāpasaṃkarāt || 35 ||
[119] iti | vaktrodgatān gāyanta ity anena dharmaśāstram uccārya prāyaścittaṃ vidheyam ity uktaṃ bhavati | yuktaṃ caitat | tathā sati prāmādikāny athābhidhānaśaṅkānudayāt | yady api caṇḍālasahavāsa ekam eva pāpaṃ tathāpi tasmin sati anuṣṭhitānāṃ nityanaimittikānāṃ bahūnāṃ vaikalpyasaṃbhavam abhipretya pāpasaṃkarād ity uktam || 35 ||
pariṣadābhidheyaṃ vrataviśeṣaṃ darśayati |
dadhnā ca sarpiṣā caiva kṣīragomūtrayāvakam |
bhuñjīta saha sarvaiś ca trisaṃdhyam avagāhanam || 36 || iti |
dadhnā sarpiṣā ca saṃyuktam iti śeṣaḥ | gṛhasvāmivad gṛhavāsino bālavṛddhādayaḥ sarve ‘pi yāvakabhojanatrisaṃdhyāvagāhane kuryuḥ || 36 ||
tatra dadhyādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ samāhāraprasaktau vibhajati |
tryahaṃ bhuñjīta dadhnā ca tryahaṃ bhuñjīta sarpiṣā |
tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa bhuñjīta ekaikena dinatrayam || 37 || iti |
dadhnā saṃyuktaṃ gomūtrayāvakaṃ dinatrayaṃ bhuñjīta | ghṛtakṣīrayor apy evam eva yojyam | tathā sati nava dināni saṃpadyante | punar apy ekaikena dadhyādinā saṃyuktam ekaikasmin dine buñjīteti dinatrayaṃ saṃpadyate | etat sarvaṃ militvā dvādaśarātraṃ bhavati || 37 ||
yāvake varjyam āha |
[120]
bhāvaduṣṭaṃ na bhuñjīta nocchiṣṭaṃ kṛmidūṣitam | iti |
bhāvena duṣṭaṃ bhāvaduṣṭam | yasminn avalokite sati atyantasādṛśyena tasminn amedhyādibhāvaḥ sahasā buddhim ārohet tādṛśaṃ na bhoktavyam ||
dadhyādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ parimāṇam āha |
dadhikṣīrasya triphalaṃ palam ekaṃ ghṛtasya tu || 38 || iti |
dadhi kṣīraṃceti dadhikṣīre tayoḥ pratyekaṃ palatrayaṃ parimāṇam | yāvakasya parimāṇaṃ grāsamātraṃ pūrvoktanyāyena veditavyam || 38 ||
cetanānāṃ śuddhihetum abhidhāyācetanānām apy āha |
bhasmanā tu bhavet śuddhir ubhayos tāmrakāṃśyayoḥ |
jalaśaucena vastrāṇāṃ parityāgena mṛṇmayam || 39 || iti
mṛṇmayasy bhāṇḍasya parityāga eva kartavyaḥ | śuddhis tu nāstīty abhiprāyaḥ || 39 ||
gṛhasya dāhaḥ kartavya ity āha |
kusumbhaguḍakārpāsalavaṇaṃ tailasarpiṣī |
dvāre kṛtvā tu dhānyāni dadyād veśmani pāvakam || 40 || iti |
kusumbhaṃ tailasādhanadhānyam | kusumbhādīni dhānyāny atidāhyatvena dvāri sthāpanīyāni || 40 ||
gṛhadāhādyantaraṃ kartavyam āha |
[121]        evaṃ śuddhas tataḥ paścāt kuryāt brāhmaṇatarpaṇam |
triṃśataṃ govṛṣaṃ caikaṃ dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām || 41 || iti |
spaṣṭam || 41 ||
bhāṇḍavad bhūmer na parityāgaḥ | kiṃ tu lepanādikam kartam ity āha |
punarlepanakhātena homajapyena śudhyati |
ādhāreṇa ca viprāṇāṃ bhūmidoṣo na vidyate || 42 || iti |
lepanaṃ kuḍyasya | khananaṃ sthalasyeti yathāyogam avagantavyam | tad ubhayaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇān praveśya śāntikajapahomau kuryāt | tāvatā bhūmiḥ śudhyati |na tu bhāṇḍavad atyantadoṣo bhūmer vidyate | yadā punar dīrghakālaṃ caṇḍālo nivaset tadā hārītoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
caṇḍālaiḥ saha saṃvāsaṃ dīrghakālam akāmikam |
vijñānān mṛṇmayaṃ pātraṃ sarvaṃ tyajati tadgṛhe ||
bālakṛcchraṃ tataḥ kuryāt taptakṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca |
brāhmaṇāṃs tarpayet paścāt brahmakūrcena śudhyati || iti | (HārSm 20.19–21)
tatra ca gośataṃ dakṣinā | tad āha cyavanaḥ:
caṇḍālasaṃkare svabhavanadahanam | sarvamṛṇmayabhāṇḍabhedanam | [122] dāravāṇāṃ tu takṣaṇam | śaṅkhaśuktisuvarṇarajatacailānām adbhiḥ prakṣālanam | kāṃsyatāmrapātrāṇām ākareṇa śuddhiḥ | sauvīrapayodadhitakrāṇāṃ parityāgaḥ | gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsaṃ kṣapayet | bālavṛddhastrīṇām ardhaṃ prāyaścittam | ā ṣoḍaśād bālāḥ | sapatatyūrdhvagatā vṛddhāḥ | cīrṇe prāyaścitte brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ gośataṃ dadyāt | abhāve sarvasvam |
iti || 42 ||
gṛhavyatiriktakṣetrārāmagrāmāntarayātrādāv ajñānena caṇḍālasaṃkare prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālaiḥ saha saṃparkaṃ māsaṃ māsārdham eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsārdhena viśudhyati || 43 || iti |
māsaṃ ca ardhamāsaṃ ca māsārdham | tena viśudhyati | māsasaṃkare māsavratena śudhyati | ardhamāsasaṃkare ardhamāsavratena viśuddhir ity arthaḥ | saṃparkaṃ karoti ced iti śeṣaḥ | caṇḍālaśabdena pulkasādayo ‘py upalakṣyante | ata eva saṃvartaḥ |
caṇḍālaiḥ saṃkare vipraḥ śvapākaiḥ pulkasair api |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsārdhena viśudhyati || (SaṃSm 180)
[123] iti | uktakālādhikakālasaṃkare hārītenoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |\
caṇḍālaiḥ saha saṃyoge prājāpatyena śudhyati |
viprān daśāvarān kṛtvā tair anujñāpya śāsanāt ||
ā kaṇṭhasya pramāṇaṃ tu kuryād gomayakardamam |
tatra sthitvā tv ahorātraṃ vāyubhakṣaḥ samāhitaḥ ||
bālakṛcchraṃ tataḥ kuryāt goṣṭhe vasati sarvadā |
sakeśavapanaṃ kuryāt paramāṃ śuddhim āpnuyāt || iti | (HārSm 20.30–32)
|| 43 ||
rajakyādibhiḥ saha saṃvāse caṇḍālasaṃvāsāt nyūnaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
rajakī carmakārī ca lubdhakī veṇujīvinī |
cāturvarṇyasya ca gṛhe tv avijñātā tu tiṣṭhati || 44 ||
jñātvā tu niṣkṛtiṃ kuryāt pūrvoktasyārdham eva ca |
[124]        gṛhadāhaṃ na kurvīta śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ ca kārayet || 45 || iti |
dadhnā ca sarpiṣā cetyādikaṃ pūrvoktam || 44–45 ||
idānīṃ caṇḍālasya sakṛdgṛhapraveśe kartavyāṃ śuddhim āha |
gṛhasyābhyantaraṃ gacchet caṇḍālo yadi kasyacit |
tam agārād vinirvāsya mṛdbhāṇḍaṃ tu visarjayet || 46 ||
rasapūrṇaṃ tu yad bhāṇḍaṃ na tyajet tu kadācana |
gomayena tu saṃmiśrair jalaiḥ prokṣed gṛhaṃ tathā || 47 || iti |
kasyacid iti brāhmaṇādīnām anyatamasya | prokṣya ajāṃ gāṃ vā gṛhaṃ praveśayet | tad āha hārītaḥ |
gṛhasyābhyantare yasya caṇḍālo yadi gacchati |
pārthivānāṃ hi pātrāṇāṃ tyāgaḥ sadyo hi sarvaśaḥ ||
anaṣṭarasabhāṇḍānāṃ gṛhavan manyate yathā|
ajasya goḥ praveśād dhi śuddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti | (HārSm 20.35–36)
anaṣṭarasāni rasapūrṇabhāṇḍāni | yaḥ punaḥ gṛhāntaravāsī sann api caṇḍālapraveśavati gṛhe kadācid bhuṅkte, bhuktavatas tasyāpi gṛhe yo ‘nyo gṛhāntaravāsī samāgatya bhuṅkte tayor ubhayoḥ prāyaścittam āpastambo darśayati |
[125]        avijñātaś ca caṇḍālas tiṣṭhate yatra veśmani |
sa vijñātas tu kālena tatra kāryaṃ viśodhanam ||
prājāpatyaṃ tu śūdrāṇāṃ teṣāṃ tadanusārataḥ |
yais tatra bhuktaṃ pakvānnaṃ kṛcchrārdhaṃ teṣu dāpayet ||
teṣām api tu yair buktaṃ pādam ekaṃ vidhīyate |
kūpaikapānair duṣṭānāṃ sparśasaṃparkadūṣitaḥ ||
naro hy ekopavāsena pañcagavyena śudhyati |
bālāpatyā tathā rogī garbhiṇī yā tu dūṣitā ||
teṣāṃ naktaṃ pradātavyaṃ bālānāṃ praharadvayam ||
iti || 46–47 ||
atha kṛmyupahatadehasya varṇabhedena bhinnāṃ śuddhim āha |
[126]        brāhmaṇasya vraṇadvāre pūyaśoṇitasaṃbhave |
kṛmir utpadyate yasya prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet || 48 ||
gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa dadhnā kṣīreṇa sarpiṣā |
tryahaṃ snātvā ca pītvā ca kṛmiduṣṭaḥ śucir bhavet || 49 ||
kṣatriyo ‘pi suvarṇasya pañca māṣān pradāya tu |
godakṣiṇāṃ tu vaiśyasyāpy upavāsaṃ vinirdiśet || 50 ||
śūdrāṇāṃ nopavāsaḥ syāt śūdro dānena śudhyati | iti |
yady api kṛmyutpattir vihitātikramapratiṣiddhācaraṇarūpā na bhavati tathāpi janmāntarasañcitaduritaphalarūpāyās tasyā vihitakarmānuṣṭhānādhikāravirodhyaśuddhy-āpādakatvāt tannivṛttaye ‘vaśyaṃ śuddhiḥ kartavyā | sā ca śuddhiḥ prāyaścittasamānatvāt prāyaścittaprakaraṇe vaktum ucitā | prāyaścittasāmyām abhipretya praśnavākye prāyaścittaśabdena vyavahāraḥ | mukhyaprāyaścittābhāvaṃ dyotayituṃ saṃdehadyotakaḥ kathaṃśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | tatra brāhmaṇasya dinatrayaṃ pañcagavyasnānapāne | kṣatriyasya pañcamāṣaparimitaṃ suvarṇadānaṃ ca | pañcakṛṣṇalātmako māṣaḥ | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[127]        jālasūryamarīcisthaṃ trasareṇū rajaḥ smrtam |
te ‘ṣṭau likṣā tu tās tisro rājasarṣapa ucyate ||
gauras tu te trayaḥ ṣaṭ te yavo madhyas tu te trayaḥ |
kṛṣṇalaḥ pañca te māṣas te suarṇas tu ṣoḍaśq || iti | (YDh 1.362–63)
vaiśyasya godānopavāsau | śūdrasya godānam eva | etac ca nābher adhobhāge draṣṭavyam | uparibhāge tu manur āha |
brāhmaṇasya vraṇadvāre pūyaśoṇitasaṃbhave |
kṛmir utpadyate yasya prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa trisaṃdhyaṃ snānam ācaret |
trirātraṃ pañcagavyāśī tv adhonābhyā viśudhyati ||
nābhikaṇṭhāntarodbhūte vraṇe cotpadyate kṛmiḥ |
ṣaḍrātraṃ tu tadā proktaṃ prājāpatyaṃ śirovraṇe || iti |
yat tu cyavanenoktam: “kṛmidarśane sāntapanaṃ vṛṣabho dakṣiṇā” iti | tad yugapad bahupradeśaviṣayam || 48–50 ||
caryamāṇe prāyaścitte nyūnātirekadoṣopaśāntir vipravākyād bhavatīy āha |
acchidram iti yad vākyaṃ vadanti kṣitidevatāḥ || 51 ||
praṇamya śirasā grāhyam agniṣṭomaphalaṃ hi tat | iti |
vipravākyasya chidraparipūrakatvaṃ saṃbhāvayitum agniṣṭomasāmyena [128] tadvākyaṃ praśasyate | anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ vipravākyasya prayojakatvam āpastambaḥ |
pūrṇe ‘pi kālaniyame na śuddhir brāhmaṇair vinā |
apūrṇeṣv api kāleṣu śodhayanti dvijottamāḥ || iti || 51 ||
mahāphalasādhaneṣu yajñādiṣv api chidrapūrttir vipravākyasādhyā | kim utāsmin prāyaścittavrate ity abhipretyāha |
japacchidraṃ tapacchidraṃ yacchidraṃ yajñakarmaṇi || 52 ||
sarvaṃ bhavati niśchidraṃ brāhmaṇair upapāditam | iti |
yady api vipravākyasya samastavrataśeṣatvād idaṃ prāyaścittaprakaraṇānte vaktum ucitam tathāpi dravyaśuddhiprakaraṇasyātrāpi svīkṛtatvād avāntaraprāyaścittaprakaraṇasya samāptāv idam uktam ity avirodhaḥ || 52 ||
āpatkāle svayaṃ vrataṃ kartum aśaktaś cet tadā brāhmaṇaiḥ kārayed ity āha |
vyādhivyasaini śrānte durbhikṣe ḍāmare tathā || 53 ||
[129]        upavāso vrataṃ homo dvijasaṃpāditāni vai | iti |
vyādhinā vyasanī rājayakṣmādiprabalarogagrastaḥ | śrānta āhavādhvagamanādinā | tayor ubhayoḥ vrataṃ putrādir anutiṣṭhet | tad āha āpastambaḥ |
aśaktasyāpi bālasya pitā vā yadi vā guruḥ |
tadvrataṃ tasya gṛhṇīyāt tato mucyeta kilbiṣāt || iti |
durbhikṣaṃ daiviko doṣaḥ | ḍāmaraṃ pararājādyupaplavaḥ | tatrobhayatrāvaśyakabahukuṭumba-bharaṇādyavaruddhaḥ tadanavaruddhena sakhyādinā vrataṃ kārayet || 53 ||
atyantāpadam abhipretya pakṣāntaram āha |
atha vā brāhmaṇās tuṣṭāḥ sarvaṃ kurvanty anugraham || 54 ||
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti dvijasaṃpāditair iha | iti |
brāhmaṇā vedapāragā mahāntaḥ pūrvakṛtopakārādinā tuṣṭāḥ santo ‘syām āpadi nirdoṣas tvam ity anugṛhṇanti tadā tāvataivāsya viśuddhiḥ | anuṣṭhānam antareṇāpi mahāpuruṣavacanamātrasaṃpāditair āśīrviśeṣair aśeṣakāmaprāptir bhavati | tatra pāpakṣayo bhavatīti ko vismayaḥ | mahāpuruṣasya gurupitrādeḥ saṃkalpamātrād eva kāmaprāptir ātharvaṇe śrūyate |
[130]        yaṃ yaṃ lokaṃ manasā saṃvibhāti
viśuddhasattvaḥ kāmayate yāṃś ca kāmān |
taṃ taṃ lokaṃ jayate tāṃś ca kāmān
tasmād ātmajñaṃ hy arcayed bhūtikāmaḥ || (MuṇUp 3.1.10)
iti || 54 ||
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ mahadanugrahasya viṣayaṃ darśayati |
durbale ‘nugrahaḥ proktas tathā vai bālavṛddhayoḥ || 55 ||
ato ‘nyathā bhaved doṣaḥ tasmān nānugrahaḥ smṛtaḥ | iti |
durbalaḥ pūrvoktavyādhigrastādiḥ | durbalabālavṛddhānām anugrahaṇe yogyā mahānto devalena darśitāḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ yathoddiṣṭam aśakyaṃ durbalādibhiḥ |
iṣyate ‘nugrahas teṣāṃ lokasaṃgrahakāraṇāt ||
[131]        eko nārhati tat kartum ajño vā nāpy anugraham |
dharmajñā bahavo viprāḥ kartum arhanty anugraham || iti |
ato ‘nyathā prabalasyānugrahe pratyavāyaḥ | tasmāt tān nānugṛhṇīyāt || 55 ||
prabalaṃ yuvānam anugṛhṇataḥ pratyavāyaṃ viśadayati |
snehād vā yadi vā lobhād bhayād ajñānto ‘pi vā || 56 ||
kurvanty anugrahaṃ ye tu tat pāpaṃ teṣu gacchati | iti |
snehād anugrahaḥ putramitrādiṣu | lobhād dhanikeṣu | ajñānād durbaleṣv anugraha iti śāstram ajñātvā paṇḍitaṃmanyatayā yasmin kasmiṃścid anugrahaḥ | yady api parakīyaṃ pāpam itarasmin na saṃkrāmati tathāpi parīkṣām akṛtvā prāyaścittaṃ vidhātur ayathāśāstrānuṣṭhānanimittaṃ pāpāntaram udetīty abhipretya tat pāpaṃ teṣu gacchatīty uktam iti | evaṃ prāyaścittādhikāriṇaṃ samyak parīkṣya prāyaścittaṃ vidheyam | tad āha devalaḥ |
kartāraṃ deśakālau ca pramāṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ kriyām |
avekṣya ca balaṃ caiva prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi
śarīraṃ balam āyuś ca vayaḥ kālaṃ ca karma ca |
parīkṣya dharmavid buddhyā prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || iti | (BDh1.1.1.15)
|| 56 ||
[132]
anugraham anarhataḥ prabalasyānugrahe yathā pratyavāyaḥ tathānugrahayogyasya durbalasya niyamavidhāne ‘pi pratyavāya ity āha |
śarīrasyātyaye prāpte vadanti niyamaṃ tu ye || 57 ||
mahatkāryoparodhena na svasthasya kadācana | iti |
śarīrasyātyaye mumūrṣor bhūmyāṃ śayanam | tasmin prāpte sati tena kartum aśakyaṃ prāyaścittavratādiniyamaṃ kartavyatvena ye vadanti teṣu tat pāpaṃ gacchatīti pūrvavākyād anuṣajyate | tatra hetuḥ:| mahatkāryoparodheneti | mahatāṃ devatopāsakānāṃ yogināṃ kāryaṃ mahatkāryam | antakāle devatāsmaraṇādi | tasyoparodhaḥ pratibandhaḥ | mumūrṣur hi parisaravartibhir āptair bodhito devatāṃ smartum udyuṅkte tadānīm etāṃ taduktavratakartavyatāṃ śrutvā kartum aśknuvan vyākulacittaḥ pūrvam udyuktāṃ devatāsmṛtim api parityajati | so ‘yaṃ puruṣārthapratibandhaḥ | tasya ca nimittaṃ prāyaścittavidhātāra iti yuktas teṣāṃ pratyavāyaḥ | evaṃ tarhi sarvatra prāyaścittaṃ vidhātuṃ bhītāḥ syur ity āśaṅkyoktaṃ na svasthasya kadācaneti | svasthaśarīrasya pūrvoktaḥ kāryoparodhaḥ kadācid api nāstīti taṃ prati prāyaścittaṃ vidhātuṃ na bhetavyam iti bhāvaḥ || 57 ||
“vyādhivyasanini” (6.53) ity atra pratinidhitvena vratam anuṣṭheyam ity uktam | idānīṃ tasya vyatirekam āha |
[133]
svasthasya mūḍhāḥ kurvanti vadanty aniyamaṃ tu te || 58 ||
te tasya vighnakartāraḥ patanti narake ‘śucau | iti |
ye tu mūḍhā vyādhyādigrastasyaiva pratinidhir iti śāstrarahasyam ajānantaḥ svasthasya kasyacit parivṛḍhādeḥ dākṣiṇyādinā tadīyaṃ vrataniyamam anutiṣṭhanti tasya ca niyamābhāvaṃ śāstrīyatvena tasya svasthasya pāpanivṛttivighnaṃ varanto rauravādike narake patanti || 58 ||
idānīṃ paṇḍitamṃanyatayā vipravākyam avajñāya svecchayaiva kiṃcid vrataṃ śāstrīyatvena parikalpyānutiṣṭhato na pāpaniṛttir ity āha |
svayam eva vrataṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇaṃ yo ‘vamanyate || 59 ||
vṛthā tasyopavāsaḥ syān na sa puṇyena yujyate | iti |
svakapolakalpitavratasya pāpanivartakatvābhāvaḥ śātātapena spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ |
[134]        yad vinā dharmaśāstreṇa prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
na tena śuddhim āpnoti prāyaścitte kṛte ‘pi saḥ || iti || 59 ||
vyatirekam uktvānvayam āha |
sa eva niyamo grāhyo yady eko ‘pi vaded dvijaḥ || 60 ||
kuryād vākyaṃ dvijānāṃ tu anyathā bhrūṇahā bhavet | iti |
dharmarahasye ‘bhijñānāṃ bahūnāṃ dvijānāṃ prāyaścittavidhānaṃ mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | ekasya vidhāyakatvam anukalpaḥ | ubhayathāpi vipreṇānujñāta eveti niyamo jñātavyaḥ | anyathā dvijānujñām antareṇa svayam eva śāstraṃ paryālocya prakaṭapāpasya prāyaścittaṃ kurvan pratyavāyī syāt || 60 ||
vipravākyasya prāyaścittaprayojakatvaṃ pratipadayati |
brāhmaṇā jaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ tīrthabhūtā hi sādhavaḥ || 61 ||
teṣāṃ vākyodakenaiva śudhyanti malinā janāḥ | iti |
sādhūnāṃ tīrtharūpatvaṃ śuddhasvarūpatvāt | tathā ca śrūyate |
[135]        agniḥ śucivratatamaḥ śucir vipraaḥ śuciḥ kaviḥ | iti | (RV 6.6.40.1) || 61 ||
viśiṣṭamātāpitṛjanyatvaṃ brāhmaṇatvam | na kevalaṃ tāvataiva brāhmaṇavākyasya praśastatvam | kiṃ tv anekadevatāpratipādakānāṃ vaidikamantrāṇāṃ dhāraṇayā sarvadevatātmakatvenāpi tadvākyaṃ praśastam ity āha |
brāhmanā yāni bhāṣante manyante tāni devatāḥ || 62 ||
sarvadevamayo vipro na tadvacanam anyathā | iti |
brahma vedaḥ | nirantaraṃ tanniṣṭhāḥ santas tadarthaṃ yathāvad bruvāṇā brāhmaṇāḥ | ato vedavidvacanaṃ devatā anumanyante | tasya ca sarvadevamayatvaṃ śrūyate |
yāvatīr vai devatās tāḥ sarvā vedavidi brāhmaṇe vasanti | iti |
aṅgirā api |
brāhmaṇo devatāḥ sarvāḥ sa ca sarvasya daivatam || iti || 62 ||
[136]
“yad eva vidyayā . . . karoti tad eva vīryavattaraṃ bhavati” (ChUp 1.1.10) iti śrutau vidyāsaṃyuktaṃ karma kevalāt karmaṇaḥ praśastam iti śrutam | tenaiva nyāyena vidyāsaṃyuktaṃ vrataṃ kevalād vratāt praśastam ity āha |
upavāso vrataṃ caiva snānaṃ tīrthaṃ japas tapaḥ || 63 ||
viprasaṃpāditaṃ yasya saṃpūrṇaṃ tasya tat phalam | iti |
viprasaṃpāditaṃ viprair anujñātam upavāsādikam | phalasaṃpūrttir niḥśeṣeṇāśuddhirnivṛttiḥ
|| 63 ||
kṛmyupahatasya dehasya śuddhim uktvā tādṛśasyaivānyasya śuddhim āha |
annādye kīṭasaṃyukte makṣikākeśadūṣite || 64 ||
tadantarā spṛśec cāpas tad annaṃ bhasmanā spṛśet || iti |
attuṃ yogyam adyam | annaṃ ca tad adyaṃ ca annādyaṃ pakvam annam | na tv āmam ity arthaḥ | tad yadi bhojanapātre bhāṇḍe vā kīṭena [137] makṣikayā keśena vā saṃyujyate tadā tat kīṭādikam apanīya tasyānnasyābhyantare ‘dbhiḥ saṃprokṣya kiṃcid bhasma prakṣipet | makṣikādidūṣitatvaṃ goghrātatvādīnām upalakṣaṇam | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
goghrāte ca kṣute vānte makṣikākeśadūṣite |
mṛdbhasmasalilaṃ caiva prakṣeptavyaṃ viśuddhaye || iti |
manur api |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavāgrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mṛtkṣepeṇa viśudhyati || iti | (MDh 5.125)
vastāvadhūnanareṇuspṛṣṭam avadhūtam | kṣutotpannabinduspṛṣṭam avakṣutam | yat tu gautamenoktam: “nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam” iti, tat keśakīṭādibhiḥ saha pakvaviṣayam || 64 ||
keśakīṭādidūṣitānnaśodhanaprasaṅgena buddhisthaṃ bhojanakālīnaṃ kaṃcin niyamaviśeṣam āha |
bhuñjānaś caiva yo vipraḥ pādaṃ hastena saṃspṛśet || 65 ||
svam ucchiṣṭam asau bhuṅkte pāṇinā muktabhājane |
[138]        pādukāstho na bhuñjīta paryaṅke saṃsthito ‘pi vā || 66 ||
śvānacaṇḍāladṛṣṭau ca bhojanaṃ parivarjayet | iti |
vāmahastena pādasaṃsparśe sati svocchiṣṭabhojane yāvān pratyavāyaḥ tāvān eva bhavati | vāmahastena pātram anālabhya bhojane ‘pi tāvān eva pratyavāyaḥ | pīṭhe samupaviṣṭaḥ padbhyāṃ pāduke ākramya na bhuñjīta | tathā paryaṅke sthitvā vordhvaṃ sthitvā vā na bhuñjīta | bhojanamadhye śvādidarśane bhojanam eva parityajet | pādukāstha ity etāvad bhinnāsanagatādīnām apy upalakṣaṇam | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
na ca bhinnāsanagato na śayānaḥ sthito ‘pi vā |
na pādukāsthito vāpi na hasan vilapann api || iti || 65–66 ||
“annādya” (6.64) ity anena prāsaṅgikam annaśodhanam uktam | idānīṃ prādhānyena suddhiprakaraṇam evānuvartayitum icchan pratijānīte |
yad annaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ syād annaśuddhis tathaiv ca || 67 ||
[139]        yathā parāśareṇoktaṃ tathaivāhaṃ vadāmi vaḥ | iti |
yad annaṃ pratiṣiddhaṃ tathaiva yā cānnasya śuddhiḥ tat sarvaṃ vṛddhaparāśareṇa pūrvakalpīyasmṛtikartrā yathoktaṃ tathā vadāmi | tatra tatra vacane parāśaraparāmarśasyāyam abhiprāyaḥ:
dhātā yathāpūrvam akalpayat | (RV 8.8.48.3)
iti,
yathartāv ṛtuliṅgāni nānārūpāṇi paryaye |
dṛśyante tāni tāny eva tathā bhāvo yugadiṣu || (MDh 1.30)
ityādiśrutismṛtibhyāṃ pratikalpam adhikāripuruṣāṇāṃ manuvasiṣṭhādīnāṃ samānasṛṣṭipratibhānāt kaliyugadharmaśāstrādhikārī parāśaro ‘pi tasmiṃs tasmin kalpe sṛjyate | ato ‘sminn api kalpe kaliyugadharmeṣu maduktasya granthasya prādhānyaṃ na vismartavyam iti || 37 ||
pratijñāte pratiṣiddhaśuddhī darśayati |
śṛtaṃ droṇāḍhakasyānnaṃ kākaśvānopaghātitam || 68 ||
[140]        kenedaṃ śudhyate ceti brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet | iti |
droṇāḍhakaśabdayor arthaṃ vakṣyati | droṇenāḍhakena vā parimitasya vrīhiyavādidravyasa saṃbandhi pakvam annaṃ śvakākābhyāṃ yadi lehanādinopaghātitaṃ syāt tadā svayaṃ śāstrajño ‘pi tacchudhisiddhaye dharmajñāṇ brāhmaṇān vācayituṃ kenedaṃ śudhyatīti pṛcchet || 68 ||
tato dharmarahasyavidbrāhmaṇanirdiṣṭāṃ śuddhiṃ cikīrṣus tad annaṃ saṃgṛhṇīyāt na tu parityajet ity āha |
kākaśvānāvalīḍhaṃ tu droṇānnaṃ na parityajet || 69 || iti |
droṇaśabdenāḍhako ‘py upalakṣyate || 69 ||
idānīṃ droṇāāḍhakayoḥ parimāṇam āha |
vedavedāṅgavidviprair dharmaśāstrānupālakaiḥ |
prasthā dvātriṃśatir droṇaḥ smṛto dviprastha āḍhakaḥ || 70 || iti |
nānādeśīyair vidvadbhis tātkālikasvasvavyavahāranirvāhāya svecchayaiva droṇādiparimāṇāni nirṇīyante | tāni nāśastrīyāṇīty abhipretya vedavedāṅgetyādinā viśeṣitam || 70 ||
[141]
yavavarāhādhikaraṇanyāyena śāstrajñaprasiddha eva śāstrārtho ‘tra grahītavyo na tu mlecchaprasiddha ity āha |
tato droṇāḍhakasyānnaṃ śrutismṛtivido viduḥ | iti |
tatas tasmāt śāstrīyaparimāṇāt parimitaṃ yat droṇāḍhakasyānnaṃ tad evātra śuddham iti śāstre vivakṣitam iti śrutismṛtikuśalā manyante |
nanu – śāstra eva droṇāḍhakaparimāṇam anyathābhihitam | tathā ca bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
paladvayaṃtu smṛtaṃ dviguṇaṃ kuḍavaṃ matam |
caturbhiḥ kuḍavaiḥ prasthaḥ prasthāś catvāra āḍhakaḥ ||
āḍhakais taiś caturbhis tu droṇas tu kathito budhaiḥ |
kumbho droṇadvayaṃ proktaṃ khārī droṇās tu ṣoḍaśa || iti |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, deśabhedena śāstradvayasya vyavasthāpanīyatvāt ||
droṇāḍhakaśabdavyāvarttyam āha |
kākaśvānāvalīḍhaṃ tu gavāghrātaṃ khareṇa vā || 71 ||
svalpam annaṃ tyajed vipraḥ śuddhir droṣṇāḍhake bhavet |
[142] iti | dhanikaviṣayaṃ droṇaparimāṇam | nirdhanaviṣayam āḍhakaparimāṇam | tatra yathāyathaṃ droṇāḍhakāc ca svalpam annaṃ kākādibhir avalīḍhaṃ cet tat parityājyam eva | vivakṣitā śuddhir droṇāḍhakaviṣayeti draṣṭavyam || 71 ||
tāṃ śuddhiṃ darśayati |
annasyoddhṛtya tanmātraṃ yac ca lālāhataṃ bhavet || 72 ||
suvarṇodakam abhyukṣya hutāśenaiva tāpayet |
hutāśanena saṃspṛṣṭaṃ suvarṇasalilena ca || 73 ||
viprāṇāṃ brahmaghoṣeṇa bhojyaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt | iti |
droṇāḍhakaparimitasyānnasya madhye yāvati bhāge saṃsparśasaṃbhāvanā tāvantaṃ bhāgam apanayet | avaśiṣṭe ‘pi yāvati lālāsparśaḥ saṃbhāvitaḥ tāvad apanayet | itarat sarvaṃ suvarṇayuktenodakena saṃprokṣye sakṛt vahnau tāpayet | tābhyāṃ suvarṇodakavahnibhyāṃ saṃskṛtaṃ punaḥ śuddhihetūnāṃ pavamānasūktādīnāṃ ghoṣeṇa saṃskṛtya viprair anujñātaṃ tad bhuñjīta | ata eva baudhāyanaḥ: “siddhahaviṣām | mahatāṃ śvavāyasaprabhṛtyupahatānāṃ tadagraṃ piṇḍamātram uddhṛtya pavamānāḥ suvarjana ity anenābhyukṣaṇam | madhūdake [143] payovikāre pātrāt pātrāntarānayanena śaucam | evaṃ tailasarpiṣī” iti (BDh 1.6.14.14–16) | jamadagnir api |
śṛtānnaṃ droṇamātrasya śvakākādyupaghātitam |
grāsam uddhṛtyāgniyogāt prokṣaṇaṃ tatra śodhanam ||
annam ekāḍhakaṃ pakvaṃ śvakākādyupghātitam |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca tad apy evaṃ viśudhyati ||
krītasyāpi vinirdiṣṭaṃ tadvad eva manīṣibhiḥ | iti |
śātātapo ‘pi |
keśakīṭaśunā spṛṣṭaṃ vāyasopahataṃ ca yat |
klībābhiśastapatitaiḥ sūtikodakyanāstikaiḥ ||
dṛṣṭaṃ vā syād yad annaṃ tu tasya niṣkṛtir ucyate |
abhyukṣya kiṃcid uddhṛtya tad bhūñjīta viśeṣataḥ ||
bhasmanā vāpi saṃspṛśya saṃspṛśed ulmukhena vā |
suvarṇarajatābhyāṃ vā bhojyaṃ ghrātam ajena ca || iti |
hārīto ‘pi: “śvakākagṛdhropaghāte keśakīṭapipīlikādibhir annādyupaghāte kāñcanabhasmarajatatāmravajravaiḍūryagovālājinebhadantānām [144] anyatamenādbhiḥ saṇspṛṣṭaṃ mantraprokṣaṇaparyagnikaraṇādityadarśanāt śuddhir bhavati” iti (HārSm 20.46) |
|| 72–73 ||
annasya śuddhim uktvā rasasya śuddhim āha |
sneho vā goraso vāpi tatra śuddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 74 ||
alpaṃ parityajet tatra snehasyotpavanena ca |
analajvālayā śuddhir gorasasya vidhīyate || 75 || iti
snenas tailādiḥ | gorasaḥ kṣīrādiḥ | tatrālpaṃ cet śvādibhir upahataṃ tat tyājyam eva | analpasya snehasya pākena śuddhiḥ | analpasya gorasasya vahnijyālayā paryagnikaraṇena śuddhiḥ | tad āha laugākṣiḥ |
payodadhivikārādi śuci pātrāntare sthitam |
plāvanotpavanābhyāṃ ca paryagnikaraṇena ca || iti |
plāvanaṃ prokṣaṇam | śaṅkho ‘pi |
[145]        plāvanaṃ ghṛtatailānāṃ pāvanaṃ gorasasya ca |
bhāṇḍāni plāvayed adbhiḥ śākaṃ mūlaphalāni ca ||
siddham annaṃ tathā sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ ca dadhi cāmbu ca |
eṣāṃ śunāvalīḍhānāṃ tejasā śuddhir iṣyate || iti |
śātātapas tu vilīnāvalīnabhedena vyavasthitāṃ śuddhiṃ darśayati |
tāpanaṃ ghṛtatailānāṃ madhuno gorasasya ca |
tanmātram uddhṛtaṃ śudhyet kaṭhinaṃ tu payo dadhi ||
avalīnaṃ tathā sarpir vilīnaṃ pacanena tu | iti |
kvacit tu doṣābhāvam āha śaṅkhaḥ: “ghṛtadadhipayastakrāṇām apavarakabhāṇḍe sthitānām adoṣaḥ | ādhāradoṣe tu nayet pātrāt pātrāntaraṃ dravyam | ghṛtaṃ tu pāyasaṃ kṣīraṃ tathaivekṣuraso guḍaḥ | śūdrabhāṇḍasthitaṃ takraṃ tathā madhu na duṣyati” iti | yamo ‘pi |
devadroṇyāṃ vivāheṣu yajñeṣu prakṛteṣu ca |
kākaiḥ śvabhis tu saṃspṛṣṭam annaṃ na tu visarjayet || iti ||
devadroṇī devayātrā | śātātapo ‘pi |
[146]        gokule yajñaśālāyāṃ tikacakrekṣuyantraṇe |
na mīmāṃsyāni śaucāni strīṣu bālātureṣv api || iti |
prakīrṇakaraṇābhidhe malinatāvahākhyādhare
vadantam upapātake ‘py ucitasatkriyāṃ niṣkṛtim |
rasānnapariśuddhim apy akṛtaṣaṣṭhamādhyāyam
apy udāravivṛttiṃ vaśī madhuravāṅmayo mādhavaḥ ||
iti śrīpārāśarasaṃhitāyāṃ prāyaścittakāṇḍe ṣaṣho ‘dhyāyaḥ || 6 ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravartakaparameśvara-śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhavāmātyasya kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ || 6 ||
atha saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
annarasayoḥ śuddhiḥ ṣaṣṭhādhyāye varṇitā | saptamādhyāye ‘vaśiṣṭānnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddhiḥ vivakṣur mahāprakaraṇaprāptaprāyaścittaśaṅkāvyudāsāya pratijānīte |
athāto dravyaśuddhis tu parāśaravaco yathā | iti |
avāntaraprakaraṇam ārabhyānnarasaśuddhikathanānantaraṃ yasmād itaradravyaśuddhir buddhisthā yasmāc cāntareṇa dravyaśuddhiṃ mahāprakaraṇagataprāyaścittavratacaryāyā nityakarmādīnāṃ cānuṣṭhānaṃ nipuṇaṃ na bhavati tatas tatsādguṇyahetudravyaśuddhir abhidhīyate ||
tatra darvīsruksruvādīnām ucchiṣṭāmedhyādidūṣitānāṃ śuddhim āha |
dāravāṇāṃ tu pātrāṇāṃ takṣaṇāt śuddhir iṣyate || 1 || iti |
mūtrapurīṣādipradeśe cirāvasthānena dṛḍhavāsitau gandhalepau yadi mṛjjalaprakālanena nāpagaccheyātāṃ tadā vāsyādinā dāravāṇi takṣaṇīyāni | takṣaṇenāpy anapagame parityāgaḥ | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ: “dārumayāṇāṃ pātrāṇām ucchiṣṭasamanvārabdhānām avalekhanam | ucchiṣṭalepopahatānāṃ takṣaṇam” iti (BDh 1.6.13.26–27) | mūtrapurīṣaretaḥprbhṛtīnām alpakālasaṃsparśe tu gandhalepayor apanetuṃ śakyatvāt tadapanayanaparyantaṃ mṛjjalābhyāṃ prayatnena prakṣālanīyāni,
yāvan nāpaity amedhyāktād gandho lepaś ca tatkṛtaḥ |
tāvan mṛdvāri deyaṃ syāt sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu || iti | (MDh 5.126)
iti smaraṇāt | yatra tu gandhalepaparyantaḥ saṃsargo nāsti kiṃ tu kevalaṃ saṃsparśamātraṃ tatra prokṣaṇāt śuddhiḥ | etad evābhipretya manur āha |
sphyaśūrpaśakaṭānāṃ ca musalolūkhalasya ca |
adbhis tu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām || (MDh 5.117–18)
[149] iti | yajñakāle tu sa eva viśeṣam āha |
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi |
camasānāṃ grahāṇāṇ ca śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu ||
carūṇāṃ sruksruvāṇāṃ ca śuddhir uṣṇena vāriṇā || iti | (MDh 5.116–17)
pātralagnasnehanivṛttyartham uṣṇodakam | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
carusruksruvasasnehapātrāṇy uṣṇena vāriṇā | iti | (YDh 1.183) || 1 ||
kāṃsyatāmrayor alpopahatayoḥ śuddhim āha |
bhasmanā śudhyate kāṃsyaṃ tāmram amlena śudhyati | iti |
amlaṃ kṣārasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
trapusīsakatāmrāṇāṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ | iti | (YDh 1.190)
tāmrādīnāṃ taijasānām upaghātatāratamyena śuddhiviśeṣam āha baudhāyanaḥ: “taijasānāṃ mūtrapurīṣāsṛṅmadyair atyantam āhatānām āvartanam | alpasaṃsarge tu parilekhanam | sparśamātropaghāte tu triḥsaptakṛtvo bhasmanā parimārjanam | ataijasānām evaṃbhūtānām utsargaḥ” (BDh 1.6.13.32–35) [150] iti ||
striyā jārasaṃparkopahatau śuddhim āha |
rajasā śudhyate nārī vikalaṃ yā na gacchati || 2 || iti |
vikalaṃ vaikalyaṃ garbhadhāraṇam | tasmin sati parityāga eva | na tu śuddhiḥ | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vyabhicārād ṛtau śuddhiḥ garbhe tyāgo vidhīyate | iti | (YDh 1.72)
etat śūdragarbhaviṣayam | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ striyaḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatāḥ |
aprajātā viśudhyanti prāyaścittena netarāḥ || iti | (VaDh 21.12)
varṇāntaragarbhe tu prāyaścittena śuddhir asti | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
vipragarbhe parākaḥ syāt kṣatriye tu tathaindavam |
vaiśye tad eva kartavyaṃ parākena samanvitam ||
śūdragarbhe tu saṃtyāgas tatra cāṇḍāladarśanāt | iti |
garbhaparyavasānahīno ‘pi vyabhicāras trividhaḥ | kāyiko mānaso vācikaś ceti | tatra kāyike pracetā āha |
viprā śūdreṇa saṃyuktā na cet tasmāt prasūyate |
[151]        prāyaścittaṃ smṛtaṃ tasyāḥ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
cāndrāyṇe dve kṛcchrasya viprāyā vaiśyasaṃgame |
kṛcchracāndrāyaṇe syātāṃ tasyāḥ kṣatriyasaṃgame ||
kṣatriyā śūdrasaṃparke kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ sakṛcchraṃ tu cared vaiśyena saṃgatā ||
śūdraṃ gatvā cared vaiśyā kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram |
ānulomyena kurvīta kṛcchraṃ pādāvaropitam || iti |
etad abhyāsaviṣayam | anabhyāse tu caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
rajasā śudhyate nārī parapuṃśābhigāminī |
tathāpi muninā proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ||
kṛcchrārdhaṃ brāhmaṇī kuryāt viprasya gamane sati |
kṣatriyasya caret kṛcchraṃ vaiśye sāntapanaṃ caret ||
śūdrasya gamane caiva parākaṃ tu samācaret | iti |
vācikamānasayor vasiṣṭha āha: “manasā bhartur aticāre trirātraṃ yāvakaṃ kṣīrodanaṃ ca [152] bhuñjānādhaḥ śayīta | ūrdhvaṃ trirātrād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśataiḥ śirobhir juhuyāt pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vākyasaṃbandhe etad eva māsaṃ caritvā ūrdhvaṃ māsād apsu nimagnāyāḥ sāvitryāś caturbhir aṣṭaśataiḥ śirobhir juhuyāt” iti (VaDh 21.68) | mānase yad idaṃ prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad ṛtudarśanād arvāg vyavahāryatvasiddhyartham | ṛtudarśanānantaraṃ tu tenaiva vyavahāryatvasiddhiḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhyaṃ nadī vegena śudhyati |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭā saṃnyāsena dvijottamaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.108)
tad etat sarvam abhipretya “rajasā śudhyate nārī” ity ācāryeṇoktam || 2 ||
nadyā uparibhāge rathyodakādīnāṃ upahatau śuddhim āha |
nadī vegena śudhyeta lepo yadi na dṛśyate | iti |
pravahantyāṃ nadyāṃ patitāny ucchiṣṭāmedhyādīni yadā pravāhavegena snānatīrtham atikramya gacchanti tadā tasmiṃs tīrthe lepaśeṣo yāvad dṛśyate tāvan nāsti śuddhiḥ | tadadarśane tu snānapānādikarmārthaṃ śuddhaiveyaṃ nadī | saty api lepe yady akṣobhyodakayuktā sā nadī tadā lepayuktaṃ tattīrthamātraṃ varjyam | itarapradeśeṣu sā nadī śuddhā | tad āha devalaḥ |
[153]        akṣobhyāni taḍāgāni nadīvāpīsarāṃsi ca |
kaśmalāśuciyuktāni tīrthaṃ tat parivarjayet || iti |
nadīśuddhim uktvā kūpādiśuddhim āha |
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu dūṣiteṣu kathaṃcana || 3 ||
uddhṛtya vai ghaṭaśataṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati | iti |
kūpādidūṣaṇaṃ dvidhā śrūyate | śvamārjārādīnāṃ tatra patitānāṃ maraṇāt mṛtaśavānāṃ tatraiva ciraṃ jaraṇāc ca | tatra maraṇamātraviṣayam idaṃ viśodhanam | etad eva hārīto ‘py āha |
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu dūṣiteṣu viśodhanam |
ghaṭānāṃ śatam uddhṛtya pañcagavyaṃ kṣipet tataḥ || iti | (HārSm 11.3)
saṃvarto ‘pi |
vāpīkūpataḍāgānāṃ dūṣitānāṃ ca śuddhaye |
apāṃ ghaṭaśatoddhāraḥ pañcagavyena śodhanam || iti | (SaṃSm 186)
iyam eva śuddhir upānadādidūṣaṇe ‘pi | tad āha āpastambaḥ |
upānacchleṣmaviṇmūtraṃ strīrajo madyam eva ca |
patitair dūṣite kūpe kumbhnāṃ śatam uddharet ||
[154] iti | mṛtaśarīrajaraṇakṛtāyām atyantopahatau viṣṇur āha |
mṛtapañcanakhāt kūpād atyantopahatāt tathā |
apaḥ samuddharet sarvāḥ śeṣaṃ śāstreṇa śodhayet ||
vahniprajvālanaṃ kṛtvā kūpe pakveṣṭakācite |
pañcagavyaṃ nyaset tatra navatoyasamudbhave || iti | (ViSm 3.43)
manuṣyaśarīrajaraṇe ‘py evam eva śuddhiḥ | tad āha hārītaḥ |
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu mānuṣaṃ śīryate yadi |
asthicharmavinirmuktaṃ dūṣitaṃ śvakharādibhiḥ ||
uddhṛtya tajjalaṃ sarvaṃ śodhanaṃ parimārjanam || iti | (HārSm 5.23–24)
prauḍheṣu nāsti doṣaḥ | tad āha viṣṇuḥ |
jalāśayeṣu svalpeṣu sthavareṣu mahītale |
kūpavat kathitā śuddhir mahatsu ca na dūṣaṇam || iti | (ViDh 3.48)
devalo ‘pi |
akṣudrāṇām apāṃ nāsti prabhūtānāṃ ca dūṣaṇam |
stokānām uddhṛtānāṃ ca kaśmale dūṣaṇaṃ bhavet ||
[155] iti | alpodakeṣv api pūrvodāhṛād doṣād alpe doṣe viṣṇur āha |
avyāptaṃ ced amedhyena tadvad eva śilāgatam |
somasūryāṃśupātena mārutasparśanena ca ||
gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa śudhyanty āpa iti smṛtāḥ || iti | (ViDh 3.13–14)
ucchiṣṭādyupaghātābhāve ‘pi gavāṃ pānād yad udakaṃ na kṣīyate tad eva śuddham | na tu tato ‘lpam | tad āha devalaḥ |
avigandhā rasopetā nirmalāḥ pṛthivīgatāḥ |
akṣīṇāś caiva gopānād āpaḥ śuddhikarāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
manur api |
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gor bhavet |
avyāptāś ced amedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ || iti | (MDh 5.128)
navodake kālāt śuddhim āha yamaḥ |
[156]        ajā gāvo mahiṣyaś ca nārī caiva prasūtikā |
daśarātreṇa śudhyanti bhūmiṣṭhaṃ ca navodakam || iti |
uddhṛtodakaṃ prati devala āha |
uddhṛtāś cāpi śudhyanti śuddhaiḥ pātraiḥ samuddhṛtāḥ |
ekarātroṣiāś cāpas tyājyāḥ śuddhā api svayam || iti |
yamo ‘pi |
apo niśi na gṛhṇīyāt gṛhṇann api kadācana |
nidhāyāgnim uparyāsāṃ dhāmno dhāmna itīrayet || iti || 3 ||
pūrvaṃ rajasā śudhyate nārī ity atra yoṣito vivāhottarakālīnā śuddhir vivecitā | idānīṃ vivāhāt prācīnāṃ śuddhiṃ vivinakti |
aṣṭavarṣā bhaved gaurī navavarṣā tu rohiṇī || 4 ||
daśavarṣā bhavet kanyā ata ūrdhvaṃ rajasvalā | iti |
gauryādayas tisra udvāhāya śuddhāḥ, taddātuḥ phalaviśeṣasmaraṇāt | rajasvalā tv aśuddhā, taddānasya ninditatvāt | tatra phalaninde darśayati bṛhaspatiḥ |
gaurī dadan nākapṛṣṭhaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ rohiṇīṃ dadat |
kanyāṃ dadad brahmalokaṃ rauravaṃ tu rajasvalām || (BṛSm 2.37)
[157] iti | yady apy ekādaśe varṣe rajodarśanaṃ na pratiniyataṃ tathāpi kāsucid darśanāt tatsaṃbhāvanayā rajasvaleti nirdeśaḥ | tāṃ dadad rauravaṃ narakaṃ prāpnoti iti śeṣaḥ || 4 ||
kanyāyām aśuddhim abhipretya “ata ūrdhvaṃ rajasvalā” ity uktam | tām evāśuddhiṃpradarśayitum adātāraṃ nindati |
prāpte tu dvādaśe varṣe yaḥ kanyāṃ na prayacchati || 5 ||
māsi māsi rajas tasyāḥ pibanti pitaraḥ svayam | iti |
ṛtudarśanam upalakṣayituṃ dvādaśe varṣe ity uktam | ata eva gautamena “pradānaṃ prāg ṛtoḥ” (GDh 18.21) ity uktam | yamo ‘py etad evāha |
tasmād udvāhayet kanyāṃ yāvan nartumatī bhavet | iti |
ataś ca ṛtudarśanāt prāg yo na prayacchati tasya pitaraḥ pratimāsaṃ tadrajaḥ pibanti | asaty ṛtudarśane dvādaśe ‘pi varṣe kanyādānapratigrahau na niṣiddhau | ata eva manuḥ |
triṃśadvarṣo vahet kanyāṃ hṛdyāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm |
[158]        tryaṣṭavarṣo ‘ṣṭavarṣāṃ vā dharme sīdati satvaraḥ || iti | (MDh 9.94)
iti || 5 ||
ṛtudarśane saty apradātā na kevalaṃ pitṝn eva narake pātayati kiṃ tu svayam api sakuṭumbaḥ pated ity āha |
mātā caiva pitā caiva jyeṣṭho bhrātā tathaiva ca || 6 ||
trayas te narakaṃ yānti dṛṣṭvā kanyāṃ rajasvalām | iti |
mātrādayas trayaḥ kanyāpradānādhikāriṇaḥ sarvān upalakṣayanti | te ca pūrvam eva “pitā pitāmahaḥ” ityādivacanodāharaṇena vivāhaprakaraṇe pradarśitāḥ || 6 ||
rajodarśanāt prāg adānaṃ tathā narakahetuḥ tathā rajasvalodvāho ‘pi narakahetur ity āha |
yas tāṃ samudvahet kanyāṃ brāhmaṇo madamohitaḥ || 7 ||
asaṃbhāṣyo hy apāṅkteyaḥ sa vipro vṛṣalīpatiḥ | iti |
tāṃ dṛṣṭarajasam | asaṃbhāṣyatvāpāṅkteyatvayor hetur [159] vṛṣalīpatitvam | vivāhāt pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭarajaskā vṛṣalī | tathā ca manuḥ |
pitur gṛhe tu yā kanyā rajaḥ paśyaty asaṃskṛtā |
sā kanyā vṛṣalī jñeyā tatpatir vṛṣalīpatiḥ || iti | (MDh 3.19) || 7 ||
yas tu madamohādinātikrāntaniṣedhas tām udvāhya sakṛd bhuktvā kathaṃcid daivayogād anutapyet tadā tasya śuddhiprakāram āha |
yaḥ karoty ekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanaṃ dvijaḥ || 8 ||
sa bhaikṣabhug japan nityaṃ tribhir varṣair viśudhyati | iti |
japyaviśeṣānabhidhānāt sāmānyaprāptāyā gāyatryā japyatvamātrāvagantavyam || 8 ||
rātrau caṇḍālādisparśe śuddhiṃ praśnapūrvakam āha |
astaṃgate yadā sūrye caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ striyam || 9 ||
sūtikāṃ spṛśataś caiva kathaṃ śuddhir vidhīyate |
jātavedaḥsuvarṇaṃ ca somamārgaṃ vilokya ca || 10 ||
brāhmaṇānumataś caiva snānaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati | iti |
jtavedāś ca svarṇaṃ ca jātavedaḥsuvarṇam | śuklapakṣe [160] somadarśanasaṃbhave somo vilokanīyaḥ | tadalābhe vahniḥ | tasyāpy abhāve suvarṇam | tasyāpy abhāve somamārgaḥ | eteṣām anyatamaṃ vilokya viprair anujñātaḥ snāyāt || 9–10 ||
rajasvalayor yoṣitayor anyonyasaṃsparśe varṇakrameṇa śuddhim āha |
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī brāhmaṇī tathā || 11 ||
tāvat tiṣṭhen nirāhārā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati |
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā tathā || 12 ||
ardhakṛcchraṃ caret pūrvā pādam ekam anantarā |
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī vaiśyajā tathā || 13 ||
pādahīnaṃ caret pūrvā pādam ekam anantarā |
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī śūdrajā tathā || 14 ||
kṛcchreṇa śudhyate pūrvā śūdrā dānena śudhyati | iti |
dvayor brāhmaṇyo rajasvalayor anyonyaṃ sparśe sati tat ārabhya snānaparyantam ubhayor āhāratyāgaḥ | trirātrakṛcchraṃ caret | etac ca sahaśayanādicirasparśaviṣayam | sakṛt sparśe tu kāśyapoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
rajasvalā ca saṃspṛṣṭā brāhmaṇyā brāhmaṇī yadi |
ekarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyati || iti |
brāhmaṇīkṣatriyayoḥ sparśe brāhmaṇyāḥ kṛcchrārdhaṃ kṣatriyāyāḥ pādakṛcchram | brāhmaṇīvaiśyayoḥ sparśe brāhmaṇyāḥ [161] pādonakṛcchraṃ vaiśyāyāḥ pādakṛcchram | brāhmaṇīśūdrayoḥ sparśe brāhmaṇyāḥ prājāpatyaṃ śūdrāyāḥ pādonam | etat sarvaṃ kāmakāraviṣayam | tathā ca vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī śūdrajāpi ca |
kṛcchreṇa śudhyate pūrvā śūdrā dānena śudhyati ||
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī vaiśyajāpi ca |
pādahīnaṃ caret pūrvā kṛcchrapādaṃ tathottarā ||
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā tathā |
kṛcchrārdhāt śudhyate pūrvā uttarā tu tadardhataḥ ||
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ kṣatriyā śūdrajāpi ca |
upavāsais tribhiḥ pūrvā tv ahorātreṇa cottarā ||
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ kṣatriyā vaiśyajāpi ca |
trirātrāt śudhyate pūrvā tv ahorātreṇa cottarā ||
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalānyonyaṃ vaiśyā śūdrī tathaiva ca |
trirātrāt śudhyate pūrvā uttarā tu dinatrayāt ||
varṇānāṃ kāmataḥ sparśe vidhir eṣa sanātanaḥ | iti |
akāmatas tu vṛddhaviṣṇunoktam: “rajasvalā hīnavarṇāṃ rajasvalāṃ spṛṣṭvā na tāvad aśnīyāt yāvan na śuddhiḥ syāt | sarvarṇām adhikavarṇāṃ vā spṛṣṭvā sadyaḥ snātvā śudhyati” (ViDh 22.73–74) [162] iti | rajasvalāyāḥ caṇḍālādisparśe viśeṣo vṛddhavasiṣṭhenoktaḥ |
caṇḍālādyaiḥ śvapākena saṃspṛṣṭā ced rajasvalā |
tāny ahāni tv atikramya prāyaścittaṃ samācaret ||
prathame ‘hni trirātraṃ syāt dvitīye dvyaham eva tu |
ahorātraṃ tṛtīye ‘hni parato naktam ācaret ||
śūdrayocciṣṭayā spṛṣṭā śunā tu dvyaham ācaret |
tāny ahāni vyatikramy prāyaścittaṃ samacaret || iti |
vyatikramyeti anaśanena tīrtvety arthaḥ | etad buddhipūrvakasparśaviṣayam | abuddhipūrve tu baudhāyanoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
rajasvalā tu saṃspṛṣṭā caṇḍālāntyaśvavāyasaiḥ |
tāvat tiṣṭhen nirāhārā yāvat kālena śudhyati | iti |
bhojanakālasparśe baudhāyana āha |
rajasvalā tu bhuñjānā śvāntyjātīn spṛśed yadi |
gomūtrayāvakāhārā ṣaḍrātreṇa viśudhyati ||
aśaktā kāñcanaṃ dadyāt viprebhyo vāpi bhojanam | iti | (BDh 1.3.4–9)
yadā tūcchiṣṭayoḥ parasparasparśo bhavati tadā atriṇā viśeṣaḥ pradarśitaḥ |
[163]        ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃsparśā kadācit strī rajasvalā |
kṛcchreṇa śudhyate pūrvā śūdrā dānair upoṣitā || iti | (AtrSm 5.7)
ucchiṣṭadvijasaṃsparśe mārkaṇḍeya āha |
dvijān kathaṃcid ucchiṣṭān rajaḥstrī yadi saṃspṛśet |
adhocchiṣṭe tv ahorātram ūrdhvocchiṣṭe tryahaṃ japet || iti |
bhojanakāle rajasvalāntaradarśane āpastamba āha |
udakyā yadi vā bhuṅkte dṛṣṭvānyāṃ tu rajasvalām |
ā snānakālaṃ nāśnīyād brahmakūrcaṃ tataḥ pibet || iti |
caṇḍāladarśane tv atrir āha |
rajasvalā tu bhuñjānā caṇḍālaṃ yadi paśyati |
upavāsatrayaṃ kuryāt prājāpatyaṃ tu kāmataḥ || iti | (VṛAtrSm 5.55,56)
śavādisparśe śātātapa āha |
ārtavābhiplutā nārī spṛśec cet śavasūtakam |
ūrdhvaṃ trirātrāt snātāṃ tāṃ trirātram upavāsayet || iti | (ŚāSm 2.127)
sparśapūrvakabhojanādau viśeṣam āha atriḥ |
[164]        ārtavābhiplutā nārī mṛtasūtakayoḥ spṛśet |
bhuktvā pītvā caret kṛcchraṃ spṛṣṭvā tu tryaham eva ca || iti | (AtSm 5.8)
śvādisaṃśane vyāsa āha |
rajasvalā yadā daṣṭā śvānajambūkarāsabhaiḥ |
pañcarātraṃ nirāhārā pañgavyena śudhyati || iti | (VyāSm 3.69)
bandhumaraṇaśravaṇādau sa evāha |
malavadvasanāyāṃ tu aprāyatyaṃ bhavet yadi |
abhiṣekeṇa śuddhiḥ syān nāśanaṃ vā dinatrayam ||
ārtavābhiplutā nārī nāvagāhet kadācana |
uddhṛtena jalenaiva snātvā śeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
svakaṃ gātraṃ bhaved adbhiḥ sāṅgopāṅgamalair yutam |
na vastrapīḍanaṃ kuryāt nānyavāsā bhavet punaḥ || iti | (VyāSm 3.42–44)
|| 11–14 ||
idānīṃ rajonimittāṃ śuddhiṃ darśayati |
snātā rajasvalā yā tu caturthe ‘hani śudhyati || 15 ||
kuryād rajonivṛttau tu daivapitryādi karma ca | iti |
rajodarśanam ārabhya dinatrayaṃ nāsti śuddhiḥ | caturthe [165] ‘hani snātāyā rajonivṛttiyabhāve ‘pi bhartuḥ śuśrūṣādau śuddhiḥ | pañcame ‘hani daivapitryayoḥ | tad āha āpastambaḥ |
śraddhā bhartuś caturthe ‘hni snātā nārī rajasvalā |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca pañcame ‘hani śudhyati || iti |
kānicit dināni rajo yady anuvarteta tadā tannivṛttiparyantaṃ daivapitryayoḥ śuddhir nāsti | nivṛtte tu rajasi daivaṃ pitryaṃ ca kartavyam || 15 ||
rogajanyānuvṛttau viśeṣam āha |
rogeṇa yad rajaḥ strīṇām anvahaṃ tu pravartate || 16 ||
nāśuciḥ sā tatas tena tat syāt vaikālika matam | iti |
rāgarogādinimittabhedād anekavidhā hi rajaḥpravṛttiḥ | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
rāgajaṃ rogajaṃ caiva kālotpannaṃ tathaiva ca |
dravyajaṃ caiva saṃproktaṃ tac caturdhā pradṛśyate ||
arvāk prasūter utpannaṃ medovṛddhyāṅganāsu yat |
tad rāgajam iti proktaṃ madodrekasamudbhavam ||
atyarthaṃ yad rajaḥ strīṇāṃ tad rāgajam iti smṛtam |
aṣṭādaśadinād ūrdhvaṃ snānaprabhṛti saṃkhyayā ||
[166]        yad rajas tu samutpannaṃ tat kālotpannam ucyate |
bhakṣyadravyasya vaiṣamyād dhātuvaiṣamyasaṃbhavam ||
dravyajaṃ raja ity uktaṃ tat kadācitkasaṃbhavam || iti |
pratidinaṃ nairantaryasrāveṇa rogajaṃ vidyāt | tena rogajena rajasā tato rajodarśanānantaraṃ sā strī kālaprāptarajasevāśucir na bhavati | tatra hetuḥ vaikālikam iti | sāmānyena rajoyogyaḥ kālo māsaḥ, “māsi māsi rajaḥ strīṇām” (YDh 2.34) iti śāstrāt, lokaprasiddheś ca | yasyāḥ kasyāścit dhātusvabhāvaviśeṣād viṃśtirātrādikaḥ kālaviśeṣaḥ pratiniyato bhavati tato viparītakālo vikālaḥ | pratidinaṃ tatrānuvartanād vaikālikatvam | yat tu garbhiṇyāḥ prākprasavād rāgajam udāhṛtaṃ tatra kālaprāptarajovad dinatrayāśaucaṃ vijñeyam | dravyaje tv aṅgirā āha |
ā dvādaśāhān nārīṇāṃ mūtravat śaucam iṣyate |
aṣṭādaśāhāt snānaṃ syāt trirātraṃ parato ‘śuciḥ ||
etat tu dravyaje vidyād rogaje pūrvam īritam | iti | (AṅSm 1.127)
prasūtikāviṣaye śuddhiviśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ |
[167]        prasūtikā tu yā nārī snānato viṃśateḥ param |
ārtavī rajasā proktā prāk tu naimittikaṃ rajaḥ ||
na tu naimittikena syāt rajasā strī rajasvalā |
rajasy uparate tatra snānenaiva śucir bhavet ||
anyatra garbhaviśleṣāt pātanād vā rajasvalā |
garbhasrāve ‘pi sā snānād viṃśateḥ parato śuciḥ ||
garbhasya patane caiva snānāt kṣālanato ‘py adhaḥ |
niḥsaṃdigdhe parijñāte cārtave śuddhikāraṇam ||
saṃdigdhamātre snānaṃ syād ity uvāca prajāpatiḥ |
ato divā vā rātrau vā malavadvasanā yadi ||
taddinādi trirātraṃ syān na saṃkyā nāḍikāvaśāt |
tṛtīyabhāge saṃprāpte malaṃ syān niśi cet striyāḥ ||
prabhātāt tu trirātreṇa śuddhiṃ tasyā vinirdiśet | iti | (PrajSm 3.17–23)
|| 16 ||
[168]
rogajanyarajo ‘nuvṛttau aspṛśyatvalakṣaṇāsucitābhāve ‘pi daive pitrye cāsty evāśucitvam ity āha |
sādhvācārā na tāvat syāt rajo yāvat pravartate || 17 ||
rajonivṛttau gamyā strī gṛhakarmaṇi caiva hi | iti |
sādhuḥ daivapitryārthaṃ pākādirūpa ācāro yasyāḥ sā sādhvācārā | rajasi nivṛtte paścāt puruṣeṇa gamyā bhavati | uktapākādigṛhakarmaṇi ca yogyā bhavati || 17 ||
pūrvaṃ “caturthe ‘hani śudhyati” ity uktaṃ tataḥ prācīne dinatraye śuddhyabhāvaṃ viśadayati |
prathame ‘hani caṇḍālī dvitīye brahmaghātinī || 18 ||
tṛtīya rajakī proktā caturthe ‘hani śudhyati | iti |
caṇḍālyādigamane yāvān pratyavāyaḥ tāvān udakyāgamane ity abhipretya tair nāmabhir vyavahāraḥ | yathā kuṇḍapāyināmayane [169] “māsam agnihotraṃ juhoti” iti agnihotranāmanirdeśena nityāgnihotradharmātideśaḥ tadvat || 18 ||
yadā jvarādibhir āturasya rajasvalābhisparśane pratyāsannabandhumaraṇe vā snānaṃ prāpnoti tadā kathaṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
ātura snāna utpanne daśakṛtvo hy anāturaḥ || 19 ||
snātvā snātvā spṛśed enaṃ tataḥ śudhyet sa āturaḥ | iti |
tatra pratisnānam āturasya vāso viparivartanīyam | tad āha atriḥ |
āturasnāna utpanne daśakṛtvo hy anāturaḥ |
spṛṣṭvā spṛṣṭvāvagāheta sa viśudhyeta āturaḥ ||
vāsobhir daśabhiś caiva paridhāya yathākramam |
dadyāt tu śaktito dānaṃ puṇyāhena viśudhyati || iti | (LAtrSm 5.60–62)
uśanā api |
jvarābhibhūtā yā nārī rajasā ca pariplutā |
kathaṃ tasyā bhavet śaucaṃ śuddhiḥ syāt kena karmaṇā ||
caturthe ‘hani saṃprāpte spṛśed anyā tu tāṃ striyam |
sā sacailāvagāhyāpaḥ snātvā caiva punaḥ spṛśet ||
daśa dvādaśakṛtvo vā ācāmec ca punaḥ punaḥ |
170]        ante ca vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ tataḥ śuddhā bhavet tu sā ||
dadyāc chaktyā tato dānaṃ puṇyāhena viśudhyati | iti |
tathā sūtikāmaraṇe smṛtyantare viśeṣo darśitaḥ |
sūtikāyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tu kathaṃ kurvanti yājñikāḥ |
kumbhe salilam ādāya pañcagavyaṃ tathaiva ca ||
puṇyargbhir abhimantyāpo vācā śuddhiṃ labhet tataḥ |
tenaiva snāpayitvā tu dāhaṃ kuryāt yathāvidhi || iti |
rajasvalāmaraṇe tu,
pañcabhiḥ snāpayitvā tu gavyaiḥ pretāṃ rajasvalām |
vastrāntarāvṛtāṃ kṛtvā dāhayed vidhipūrvakam ||
iti tatraiva viśeṣa uktaḥ || 19 ||
ucchiṣṭasya dvijasyānyenocchiṣṭena dvijena śunā śūdreṇa vā saṃsparśe śuddhim āha |
ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ śunā śūdreṇa vā dvijaḥ || 20 ||
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati | iti |
ucciṣṭena saṃspṛṣṭaḥ ucchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ | ucchiṣṭaś cāsāv ucchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭaś ceti vigrahaḥ | rajanīm upoṣya rātribhojanaṃ parityajed ity arthaḥ | yat tu atriṇoktam,
ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭo brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇena tu |
daśarūpaṃ japet paścāt gāyatryāḥ śodhanaṃ bhavet ||
iti, tad etad aśaktaviṣayam | śunā sparśe viśeṣam āha saṃvartaḥ |
kṛtamūtrapurīṣo vā bhuktocchiṣṭo ‘tha vā dvijaḥ |
śvabhiḥ sparśe japet devyāḥ sahasraṃ snānapūrvakam || iti || 20 ||
[171]
anucchiṣṭasya viprasyocchiṣṭaśūdrasparśe snānamātram | ucchiṣṭasya viprasyocchiṣṭaśūdrasparśe prājāpatyakṛcchram ity āha |
anucchiṣṭena śūdreṇa sparśe snānaṃ vidhīyate || 21 ||
tenocchiṣṭena saṃspṛṣṭaḥ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret | iti |
yady apy anucchiṣṭocchiṣṭaśabdau śūdraviśeṣaṇatayā śrutau tathāpi vidhīyamānasnānaprājāpatyānusāreṇa vipre ‘pi tau yojanīyau || 21 ||
pūrvaṃ kāṃsyasya yā śuddhir uktā tām anūdya tatra viśeṣam āha |
bhasmanā śudhyate kāṃsyaṃ surayā yan na lipyate || 22 ||
surāmātreṇa saṃspṛṣṭaṃ śudhyate ‘gnyupalekhanaiḥ | iti |
surāspṛṣṭasya kāṃsyasya na bhasmagharṣaṇamātreṇa śuddhiḥ | kiṃ tu upalekhanatāpanābhyām | upalekhanaṃ nām śastreṇoparibhāgasya takṣaṇam | mūtrapurīṣalepeṣv eṣaiva śuddhiḥ | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
[172]        bhasmanā śudhyate kāṃsyaṃ surayā yan na lipyate |
surāmūtrapurīṣais tu śudhyate ‘gnyupalekhanaiḥ ||
āmiṣeṇa tu yal liptaṃ punardāhena śudhyati | iti || 22 ||
gavāghrātādīnāṃ daśakṛtvo bhasmagharṣaṇena śuddhir ity āha |
gavāghrātāni kāṃsyāni śvakākopahatāni ca || 23 ||
śudhyanti daśabhiḥ kṣāraiḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāni yāni ca | iti |
etad eva śātātapo ‘py āha |
gavāghrāteṣu kāṃsyeṣu śūdrocchiṣṭeṣu vā punaḥ |
daśabhir bhasmabhiḥ śuddhiḥ śvakākopahateṣu ca || iti (ŚātSm 3.61)
|| 23 ||
gaṇḍūṣādyupahatasya bhūnikṣepa ity āha |
gaṇḍūṣaṃ pādaśaucaṃ ca kṛtvā vai kāṃsyabhājane || 24 ||
ṣaṇmāsān bhuvi niḥkṣipya uddhṛtya punar āharet | iti |
[173]
ayaṃ ca bhūniḥkṣepaḥ kāṃsyakāragharṣaṇasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ata eva aṅgirāḥ |
gaṇḍūṣaṃ pādaśaucaṃ ca kṛtvā vai kāṃsyabhājane |
ṣaṇmāsaṃ bhuvi niḥkṣipya punar ākāram ādiśet || iti | (AṅSm 2.19)
yat tu baudhāyanenoktam: “taijasānāṃ pātrāṇām ucchiṣṭopahatānāṃ triḥsaptakṛtvaḥ parimārjanam | tathā mūtrapurīṣalohitaretaḥprabhṛtyupahatānāṃ punaḥkaraṇam” (BDh 1.6.14.4–6) iti, tatra punaḥkaraṇaṃ ciralepaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam || 24 ||
āyasādīnāṃ kaṭāhādīnāṃ sīsapātrasyāpy atyantopahatasya śuddhim āha |
āyaseṣv āyasānāṃ ca sīsasyānau viśodhanam || 25 || iti |
ayomayeṣu gharṣaṇasādhaneṣu āyasānāṃ gharṣaṇena śodhanam | sīsasya tv agnau pravilāpanam | ayaḥsaṃgharṣaṇam aśmasaṃgharṣaṇasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ata eva mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇam |
gātrāṇāṃ ca manuṣyāṇām ambunā śaucam iṣyate |
tathāyasānāṃ toyena tv aśmasaṃgharṣaṇena ca || (MārPu 35.5–6)
[174] iti | toyenety alpopahataviṣayam || 25 ||
gajadantādīnām śuddhim āha |
dantam asthi tathā bhṛṅgaṃ rūpyaṃ sauvarṇabhājanam |
maṇipāṣāṇapātrāṇīty etān prakṣālayej jalaiḥ || 26 ||
pāṣāṇe tu punar gharṣaḥ śuddhir eva udāhṛtā | iti |
asthiśabdena gajāsthyādinirmitaṃ karaṇḍakādi | śṛṅgaśabdena mahiṣaśṛṅgādinirmitaṃ karaṇḍakādi | manipātrāṇi pravālasphaṭikādīni | pāṣāṇapātrasya tu prakṣālanaṃ pāṣāṇāntaragharṣaṇaṃ cety ubhayaṃ veditavyam | jalaiḥ prakṣālanaṃ nirlepaviṣayam | tad āha manuḥ |
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍam adbhir eva viśudhyati |
abjam aśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskaram || (MDh5.112)
[175] iti | abjaṃ śaṅkhaśuktyādi | anupaskṛtaṃ nirlepam | yat tu manunaivoktam,
taijasānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca sarvasyāśmamayasya ca |
bhasmanādbhir mṛdā caiva śuddhir uktā manīṣibhiḥ || (MDh 5.111)
iti, tat salepaviṣayam | śuddhyantarāṇi munibhir darśitāni | tatra śātātapaḥ |
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ trapu kṛṣṇāyasaṃ tathā |
rītikāsīsalohāni śudhyante ‘śmapragharṣaṇāt || iti | (ŚāSm 3.48)
yamaḥ |
rajatasya suvarṇasya tāmrasya trapuṇas tathā |
rītyayaḥkāṃsyasīsānāṃ bhasmanā śaucam iṣyate || iti | (YamSm 5.39)
uśanasāpi: “suvarṇarajatatāmratrapusīsakāṃsyānām adbhir eva bhasmasaṃyuktābhiḥ | maṇimayānām adbhir eva mṛtsaṃyuktābhiḥ | taijasānāṃ cocchiṣṭānāṃ bhasmanā triḥ prakṣālanam | kanakamaṇirajataśaṅkhaśuktyupalānāṃ vajravidalanarajjucarmaṇāṃ cādbhiḥ śaucam” iti (UśSm 5.17–20) | viṣṇuḥ:
[176]        viṇmūtraretāsavaraktaliptam
āvartanollekhanatāpanair vā |
triḥsaptakṛtvaḥ parimārjanair vā
bhasmāmbunā śudhyati taijasānām | iti | (ViSm 11.67)
eteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ yathāyogaṃ cirakṣipralepālepabhedena vyavasthā draṣṭavyā || 26 ||
mṛdbhāṇḍasya dhānyasya cocchiṣṭābhyupahatau śuddhim āha |
mṛṇmaye dahanāt śuddhir dhānyānāṃ mārjanād api || 27 || iti |
dahanaṃ salepaviṣayam | ata eva baudhāyanaḥ: “mṛṇmayānāṃ pātrāṇām ucchiṣṭasamanvārabdhānām avacūrṇanam | ucchiṣṭalepopahatānāṃ punar dahanam | mūtrapurīṣalohitaretaḥprabhṛtyupahatānām utsargaḥ” (BDh 1.6.13.31–33) [177] iti | etac ca śvādisparśaviṣayam | caṇḍālādisparśe tu smṛtyantaroktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
caṇḍālādyais tu saṃspṛṣṭaṃ dhānyaṃ vastram athāpi vā |
kṣālanena viśudhyeta parityāgān mahīmayam || iti |
mārjanaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ prakṣālanaṃ vā | tatrobhayor vyavasthitaviṣayatvaṃ darśayati manuḥ |
adbhis tu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām |
prakṣālanena tv alpānām adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate || iti | (MDh 5.118)
viṣṇuḥ: [178] “alpasya dhānyasya yanmātram upahanyate tanmātram utsṛjya śeṣasya kaṇḍanaprakṣālane kuryāt” iti (ViDh 23.25) | baudhāyanaḥ: “caṇḍālādisparśane ‘nekapuruṣoddhāryāṇāṃ prokṣaṇam | mūtrādisaṃparke tanmātrāpahāraḥ | asṛkpāṃsvādidravyasaṃyoge nistuṣīkaraṇam” iti (BDh 1.6.13.17–19) | kāśyapaḥ: “prokṣaṇaparyagnikaraṇavagāhanair vrīhiyavagodhūmānām | vimarśanaprokṣaṇaiḥ phalīkṛtānām | vigharṣaṇavidalanaprokṣaṇaiḥ śamīdhānyānām” iti (KāśSm 12.3–4) | asyārthaḥ – anekapuruṣoddharyāṇāṃ vrīhiyavagodhūmānāṃ yathākramaṃ prokṣaṇaparagnikaraṇāvagahanaiḥ śuddhiḥ | avagāhanaṃ prakṣālanam | vrīhyāditaṇḍulānāṃ phalīkṛtānāṃ vimarśanena | [179] vimarśanaṃ karābhyāṃ gharṣaṇam | kaṇḍanena śuklīkaraṇaṃ vā | śamīdhānyānāṃ mudgādīnāṃ anekapuruṣoddhāryāṇāṃ vigharṣaṇena | ādipurāṇe |
gṛhadāhe samutpanne saṃsthite paśumānuṣe |
abhojyas tadgato vrīhidhātudravyasya saṃgrahaḥ ||
mṛṇmayenāvaguptānām adhobhuvi ca tiṣṭhatām |
yavamāṣatilādīnāṃ na doṣaṃ manur abravīt ||
tataḥ saṃkramamāṇe ‘gnau sthāne ca dahyate |
na ca prāṇivadho yatra kevalaṃ gṛhadīpanam |
tatra dravyāṇi sarvāṇi gṛhṇīyād avicārayan || iti || 27 ||
veṇvādīnām ekaikasyālopahatau saṃhatānāṃ mahopahatau caikavidhāṃ śuddhim āha |
veṇuvalkalacīrāṇāṃ kṣaumakārpāsavāsasām |
aurṇanetrapaṭānāṃ ca prokṣaṇāt śuddhir iṣyate || 28 || iti |
veṇuśabdena tatkāryāṇi kaṭavyajanādīni gṛhyante | [180] valkalacīrāṇy araṇyavāsināṃ prasiddhāni | kṣaumaṃ dukūlam | kārpāsavāsāṃsi prasiddhāni | aurṇaḥ kambalaḥ | netrapaṭā araṇyavāsinām eva prasiddhā bhūrjatvagādayaḥ | asyāś cālpaviṣayaśuddhitvaṃ devalo darśayati |
aurṇakauśeyakutapapaṭṭakṣaumadukūlajāḥ |
alpaśaucā bhavanty ete śoṣaṇaprokṣaṇādibhiḥ ||
tāny evāmedhyayuktāni kṣālayec choṣakaiḥ svakaiḥ |
dhānyakalkais tu phalajai rasaiḥ kṣārānugair api || iti |
ekaikasya lepopahatau yājñavalkya āha |
soṣair udakagomūtraiḥ śudhyaty āvikakauśikam |
saśrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭaṃ sāriṣṭaiḥ kutapaṃ tathā ||
sagaurasarṣapaiḥ kṣaumaṃ punaḥ pākāc ca mṛṇmayam || iti | (YDh 1.186–87)
āvikaṃ kambalaḥ | kauśeyaṃ kṛmikośottham | aṃśupaṭṭaṃ netrapaṭaḥ | ariṣṭāni putrajīvaphalāni | kutapaḥ pārvatīyacchāgaromanirmitaḥ kambalaviśeṣaḥ | manur api |
kauśeyāvikayor ūṣaiḥ kutapānām ariṣṭakaiḥ |
śrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ ||
[181]         kṣaumavat śaṅkhaśṛṅgāṇām asthidantamayasya ca |
śuddhir vijānatā kāryā gomūtreṇodakena vā || iti | (MDh 5.120–21)
aṅgirā api |
śaucaṃ mahārgharomṇāṃ tu vāyvagnyarkenduraśmibhiḥ |
retaḥspṛṣṭaṃ śavaspṛṣṭam āvikaṃ ca praduṣyati || iti |
atra ca retaḥsparśo leparahito vivakṣitaḥ || 28 ||
muñjādīnāṃ veṇuvat śuddhim āha |
muñjopaskaraśūrpāṇāṃ śaṇasya phalacarmaṇām |
tṛṇakāṣṭhasya rajjūṇām udakābhyukṣaṇaṃ matam || 29 || iti |
muñjo raśanādiprakṛtibhūtas tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ | tena saṃpādita upaskaro viṣṭarādiḥ | yady api pūrvavacane veṇuvikārāṇāṃ kaṭādīnāṃ prokṣaṇasyoktatvāt tenaiva śūrpaśuddhir abhihitā tathāpi taṇḍulaphalīkaraṇādau tallepasya lagnatvāt śuddhyantarāśaṅkā bhavati tan mā bhūd iti punar iha grahaṇam | śaṇo goṇyādihetur valkalaviśeṣaḥ | tena tadvikārāḥ sarve ‘pi vivakṣitāḥ | phalam āmrādi | carma kṛṣṇājinādi | tṛṇaṃ ca kāṣṭhaṃ ca tṛṇakāṣṭham | rajjvaḥ prasiddhāḥ | atra phalaśabdena śākamūlādīny upalakṣyante | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
[182]        śākarajjūmūlaphalavāsovidalacarmaṇām |
pātrāṇāṃ camasānāṃ ca vāriṇā śuddhir iṣyate || iti | (YDh 1.182–83)
śākaṃ vāsukādi | rajjur balvajādinirmitā | mūlam ārdrakādi | phalam āmrādi | vaidalaṃ vaiṇavādi | carma ajādīnām | pātrāṇi prokṣaṇapātraprabhṛtīni | camasā hotṛcamasādayaḥ | tatra vāriśuddhiḥ prokṣaṇaṃ prakṣālanaṃ ca | tad ubhayaṃ yathāyogaṃ draṣṭavyam | ata eva manunā dhānyaṃ dṛṣṭāntitam |
cailavac carmaṇāṃ śuddhir vaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca |
‘        śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca dhānyavat śuddhir iṣyate || iti | (MDh 5.119)
kāśyapo ‘pi: [183] “tṛṇakāṣṭharajjumuñjacarmaveṇuvidalaphalapatramūlādīnām cailavat śaucam | mṛddārucarmaṇāṃ cātyantopahatānāṃ tyāgaḥ” iti (KāśSm 12.8–9) | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
śavaviṇmūtraśukrais tu dūṣitaṃ ca mṛdambubhiḥ |
śodhyādau śodanīyaṃ ca gomūtrakṣāravāribhiḥ ||
rajjuvalkalapatrāṇāṃ camasālābucarmaṇām |
kṛtvā śaucaṃ tataḥ śuddiḥ govālair gharṣaṇaṃ punaḥ || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
vastraṃ mṛdambhasā śuddhaṃ rajjur vaidalam eva ca |
rajjvādikaṃ cātiduṣṭaṃ tyājyaṃ tanmātram eva ca || iti | (VyāSm 4.17)
uśanā api: “mūlaphalapuṣpabhūmitṛṇadārupalāladhānyānām abhyukṣaṇam” iti (AuśSm 3.41) | yat tu baudhāyanenoktam: “kṛṣṇājinānāṃ bilvataṇḍulaiḥ” iti (BDh 1.6.13.13), paiṭhīnasir api: [184] “rauravādyajinānāṃ bilvataṇḍulavat” iti, tat nūtanacarmaviṣayam || 29 ||
satūlaśayyādīnāṃ śuddhim āha |
tūlikādyupadhānāni raktavastrādikāni ca |
śoṣayitvātapenaiva prokṣaṇāt śuddhitām iyuḥ || 30 || iti |
tūlaṃ śālmaliphalādijanyaṃ tena nirmitā śayyā tūlikā | ādiśabdenāsanopāśrayādīni gṛhyante | upadhānam ucchīrṣakam | tūlikādīni copadhānaṃ ca tūlikādyupadhānāni | raktavastraṃ māñjiṣṭham | ādiśabdena kausumbhahāridrādīni | eteṣām amedhyādileparahitopahatau ātapaśoṣaṇaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ ca | etac ca karonmārjanasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ata eva devalaḥ |
tūlikādyupadhānāni puṣparakāmbarāṇi ca |
śoṣayitvā tathā kiṃcit karair unmārjayen muhuḥ ||
paścāt tu vāriṇā prokṣya śucīny evam upāharet || iti |
nirlepakausumbhapuṣparaktāni kuṅkumakusumbhādiraktāni | puṣparaktagrahaṇaṃ anyasyāpi haridrādiraktasya kṣālanāsahasya [185] prāptyartham | na māñjiṣṭhādeḥ, tasya kṣālanasahatvāt | salepopahatau sa evāha |
alpaśauce bhavet śuddhiḥ śoṣaṇaprokṣaṇādibhiḥ |
tāny evāmedhyayuktāni nirṇijyāt kṣāraparyayaiḥ ||
tāny apy atibaliṣṭhāni yathāvat pariśodhayet | iti |
nirlepakausumbhādau ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘bhihitam |
kusumbhakuṅkumai raktaṃ tathā lākṣārasena vā |
prokṣaṇenaiva śudhyeta caṇḍālasparśane sadā || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi |
kusumbhakuṅkumānāṃ ca aurṇakārpāsayos tathā |
prokṣaṇāt kathitā śuddhir ity āha bhavān yamaḥ || iti || 30 ||
dravyopahatau śuddhir varṇitā | idānīṃ śvādibhir iva mārjārādibhir apy upahate śaṅkāprasaktāv apavadati |
mārjāramakṣikākīṭapataṅgakṛmidardurāḥ |
medhyāmedhyaṃ spṛśanto ‘pi nocchiṣṭaṃ manur abravīt || 31 ||
[186] iti | medhyaṃ cāmedhyaṃ ca medhyāmedhyam | yady api mārjārādaya ubhayaṃ spṛśanto vartante tathāpi medhyaṃ tāvatā nocchiṣṭam iti yojanīyam || 31 ||
bhūmispṛṣṭodakādāv aśuddhim apavadati |
mahīṃ spṛṣṭvāgataṃ toyaṃ yāś cāpy anyonyavipruṣaḥ |
bhuktocchiṣṭaṃ tathā snehaṃ nocchiṣṭaṃ manur abravīt || 32 || iti |
pādaprakṣālanācamanādāv adhaḥ patitaṃ yad udakaṃ bhūmiṃ spṛṣṭvā punar bindurūpeṇāgatya spṛśati, ye cānyonyamukhodgatā bindavaḥ saṃbhāṣaṇe sarīre patanti, yaś ca sneho bhojanānantaraṃ prakṣālanenānirhāryaḥ, tat sarvaṃ nāsuddhyāpādakam || 32 ||
mukhāntargatatāmbūlādīnām ucchiṣṭaśaṅkām apavadati |
tāmbūlekṣuphale caiva bhuktasnehānulepane |
madhuparke ca some ca nocchiṣṭaṃ dharmato viduḥ || 33 || iti |
tāmbūlaṃ ca ikṣuś ca phalaṃ ca tāmbūlekṣuphalam | pūrvavacanokto ‘pi bhuktasneho ‘tra dṛṣṭāntatvena punar upāttaḥ | madhuparko yajñavivāhādau dadhyādibhakṣaṇam | somo yāge somapānam | eteṣu nāsty ucchiṣṭadoṣaḥ || 33 ||
rathyodakādau prayatnena kartavyaḥ śuddhiviśeṣo nāstīty āha |
rathyākardamatoyāni nāvaḥ panthās tṛṇāni ca |
mārutārkeṇa śudhyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca || 34 ||
[187] iti | pakveṣṭakacitāni caityavṛkṣavedikādīni | nirdiṣṭānām eteṣāṃ caṇḍālādisparśe ‘pi vāyvātapābhyāṃ śuddhiḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
rathyākardamatoyāni spṛṣṭāny antyaśvavāyasaiḥ |
mārutārkeṇa śudhyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca ||
panthānaś ca visudhyanti somasūryāṃśumārutaiḥ || iti | (YDh 1.194–97)
baudhāyano ‘pi |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ nāvaḥ panthās tṛṇāni ca |
mārutārkeṇa śudhyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca || iti | (BDh 1.5.9.7)
uparibhāgasparśe śaṅkha āha |
rathyākardamatoyena ṣṭhīvanādyena vā tv atha |
nābher ūrdhvaṃ naraḥ spṛṣṭaḥ sadyaḥ snānena śudhyati || iti | (ŚaṅSm 11.36)
adhobhāgasparśe yama āha |
[188]        sakardamaṃ tu varṣāsu praviśya grāmasaṃkaram |
jaṅghayor mṛttikās tisraḥ pādayor mṛttikā smṛtā || iti | (YamSm 4.19)
|| 34 ||
udakapānagatadhārādīnām āsuddhim apavadati |
aduṣṭāḥ santatā dhārā vātoddhūtāś ca reṇavaḥ |
striyo vṛddhāś ca bālāś ca na duṣyanti kadācana || 35 || iti |
kamaṇḍalvādikāt nirgatya mukhaparyantam avicchinnā api udakadhārā nocchiṣṭāḥ | nānāvidhāśucipradeśād vāyunotthāpitā api reṇavaḥ sparśārhāḥ | puruṣavat prātaḥsnānādyabhāve ‘pi yoṣidādayaḥ spṛśyāḥ śuddhāś ca | reṇuśucitvaṃ rāsabhādibhyo ‘nyatra draṣṭavyam | tad āha śātātapaḥ |
reṇavaḥ śucayaḥ sarve vāyunā samudīritāḥ |
anyatra rasabhājāviśvakākolūkavāyasām || iti |
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
śvakākoṣṭrakharolūkasūkaragrāmyapakṣinām |
ajāvireṇusaṃsparśād āyur lakṣmīś ca hīyate ||
[189] iti | gavādīnāṃ reṇavo na kevalaṃ doṣaharitāḥ praty uta praśastāḥ | tad āha uśanāḥ |
gavāśvarathayānānāṃ praśastā reṇavaḥ sadā | iti |
viṣayaviśeṣeṇa śuddhim āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
raśmir agnir ajacchāyā gaur aśvo vasudhānilaḥ |
vipruṣo makṣikāḥ sparśe vatsaḥ prasravaṇe śuciḥ || iti | (YDh 1.193)
vipruṣo nīhārabindavaḥ | ete caṇḍālādispṛṣṭā api sparśe śucayaḥ | vatsa ūdhogatakṣīrākarṣaṇe śuddhaḥ | vatsanyāyo bālastanyapāne ‘py avagantavyaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śvahatāś ca mṛgā medhyāḥ pātitaṃ ca dvijaiḥ phalam |
bālair anuparikrāntaṃ strībhir ācaritaṃ ca yat || iti | (VaDh 3.45)
dvijaiḥ pakṣibhiḥ | uśanāḥ: “gaur medhyā pṛṣṭhe | purastād ajādayaḥ | striyaḥ sarvataḥ | hṛdayam āsām aśuci” iti (UśSm 3.17–20) | bṛhaspatir api |
[190]        pādau śucī brāhmaṇānām ajāśvasya mukhaṃ śuci |
gavāṃ pṛṣṭhāni medhyāni sarvagātrāṇi yoṣitām || iti |
sumantuḥ: “strībālamaśakamakṣikāmārjāramūṣikāchāyāsanaśayanayānāmbuvipruṣo nityaṃ medhyāḥ” iti | bṛhaspati-hārītau |
mārjāraś caiva darvī ca mārutaś ca sadā śuciḥ | iti |
śaṅkhaḥ: “mārjāraś caṃkrame śuciḥ” iti (ŚaṅSm 16.14) | manuḥ |
nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇyaṃ yac ca prasāritam |
brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣaṃ nityaṃ medhyam iti sthitiḥ || iti | (MDh 5.129)
yamaḥ |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ strīmukhaṃ kutapaṃ kṣuram |
na dūṣayanti vidvāṃso yajñeṣu camasaṃ tathā ||
[191]        gaur ajo vipruṣaś chāyā makṣikāḥ śalabhāḥ śukāḥ |
aśvo hastī raṇe chatraṃ raśmayaś candrasūryayoḥ ||
bhūmir agnir ajo vāyur āpo dadhi ghṛtaṃ payaḥ |
sarvāṇy etāni śuddhāni sparśe medhyāni nityaśaḥ ||
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatāḥ śucir nārī pativratā |
śucir dharmaparo rājā saṃtuṣṭo brāhmaṇaḥ śuciḥ ||
nityam āsyaṃ śuci strīṇāṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane |
prasrave tu śucir vatsaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ || iti | (YamSm 4.31–35)
paiṭhīnasir api: “strīṇāṃ mukhaṃ ratisaṃsarge” iti | vasiṣṭha-atri-baudhāyana-śātātapāḥ |
striyaś ca ratisaṃsarge śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ | iti |
manuḥ |
[192]        śvabhir hatasya yan māṃsaṃ śuci tan manur abravīt |
kravyādbhiś ca hatasyānyaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca dasyubhiḥ || iti | (MDh 5.131)
devalaḥ |
taravaḥ puṣpitā medhyā brāhmaṇāś caiva sarvadā |
bhasma kṣaudraṃ suvarṇaṃ ca sadarbhāḥ kutapās tilāḥ ||
apāmārgaśirīṣārkapadmam āmalakaṃ maṇiḥ |
mālyāni sarṣapā dūrvāḥ sadā bhadrāḥ priyaṃgavaḥ ||
akṣatāḥ sikatā lājā haridrā candanaṃ yavāḥ |
palāśakhadirāśvatthās tulasī dhātakī vaṭaḥ ||
etāny āhuḥ pavitrāṇi brahmajñā havyakavyayoḥ |
pauṣṭikāni malaghnāni śodhanāni ca dehinām ||
akaśmalaiḥ samiddho ‘gnir durmanuṣyair adūṣitaḥ |
sarveṣām apy aśaucānāṃ samarthaḥ śodhanāya saḥ ||
agner vṛṣalabhuktasya grahaṇaṃ nāsty anāpadi |
śvapāko vṛṣalo bhoktuṃ brāhmanāgniṃ ca nārhati ||
caṇḍālāgner amedhyāgneḥ sūtakāgneś ca karhicit |
[193]        patitāgneś citāgneś ca śiṣṭair grahaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
agrāmyā mṛd bhavet śuddhā ślakṣṃāviṇmūtradūṣitā | iti |
brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
grāmād daṇḍaśataṃ tyaktvā nagare ca caturguṇam |
bhūmiḥ sarvatra śuddhā syāt yatra lepo na dṛśyate || iti |
lepe tu yājñavalkya āha |
bhūśuddhir mārjanād dāhāt kālād gokramaṇāt tathā |
sekād ullekhanāl lepād gṛhaṃ mārjanalepanāt || iti | (YDh 1.188)
yamo ‘pi |
khananāt pūraṇāt dāhād adbhir gharṣaṇalepanāt |
gobhir ākramaṇāt kālād bhūmiḥ śudhyati saptadhā || iti |
devalas tv amedhyaduṣṭamalinatvabhedais trividhām aśuddhiṃ tadviśuddhiṃ ca viśadayati |
yatra prasūyate nārī mriyate dahyate naraḥ |
caṇḍālādhyuṣitaṃ yatra tatra viṣṭhādisaṅgatiḥ ||
evaṃ kaśmalabhūyiṣṭā bhūr amedhyā prakīrthitā |
[194]        śvasūkarakharoṣṭrāddisaṃspṛṣā duṣṭatāṃ vrajet ||
aṅgāratuṣakeśāsthibhasyādyair malinā bhavet |
pañcadhā ca caturdhā ca bhūr amedhyā viśudhyati ||
duṣṭāpi yā tridhā dvedhā śudhyate malinaikadhā | iti |
tatra pañcavidhā śuddhir manunā darśitā |
saṃmārjanenāñjanena sekenollekhanena ca |
gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ śudhyati pañcadhā || iti | (MDh 5.124)
eteṣv eva pañcavidheṣu yathāyogaṃ cāturvidhyādikaṃ yojanīyam | yad vā dahanādayaḥ pañcavidhā devaloktā draṣṭavyāḥ |
dahanāt khananāc caiva upalepanadhānavāt |
parjanyavarṣaṇāt bhūmeḥ śaucaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam || iti |
gṛhaśuddhiṃ saṃvarta āha |
gṛhaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi antaḥsthaśavadūṣaṇe |
saṃprokṣya mṛṇmayaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ siddham annaṃ tathaiva ca ||
gṛhād apāsya tat sarvaṃ gomayenopalepayet |
[195]        gomayenopalipyātha dhūmair āghrāpayed budhaḥ ||
brāhmaṇair mantrapūtaiś ca hiraṇyakuśavāriṇā |
sarvam abhyukṣayed veśma tataḥ śuddhyaty asaṃśayam || iti | (SaṃSm 198–200)
baudhāyanaḥ: “udbandhaśavopaghāte veśmano bhittitakṣaṇam” iti (BDh 1.6.13.33) | dehaviṣaye śuddhyaśuddhī vibhajati manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ nābher yāni khāni tāni medhyāni sarvaśaḥ |
yāny adhastāe amedhyāni dehāc caiva malāś cyutāḥ ||
viṇmūtrotsargaśuddhyarthaṃ mṛdvāry ādeyam arthavat |
daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca śuddhiṣu dvādaśasv api || iti | (MDh 5.132–34)
arthavat śāstroktavat | atra ca karṇaviṭprabhṛtiṣūttareṣu ṣaṭsu mṛdgrahaṇaṃ vaikalpikam,
ādadīta mṛdo ‘paś ca ṣaṭsu pūrveṣu śuddhaye |
uttareṣu tu ṣaṭsv adbhiḥ kevalābhis tu śudhyati ||
iti smṛtyantare vacanāt | mṛdambhasor iyattām āha manuḥ |
[196]        yāvan nāpaity amedhyākto gandho lepaś ca tatkṛtaḥ |
tāvan mṛd vāri cādeyaṃ sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu || iti | (MDh 5.126)
vidyamānāpy upahatir yatra na dṛṣṭā tatra taddarśanāt pūrvaṃ śuddham eva tad vastu | tad āha sa eva |
trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayan |
adṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇiktaṃ yac ca vācā praśasyate || iti | (MDh 5.127)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vākśastam ambunirṇiktam ajñātaṃ ca sadā śuciḥ | iti | (YDh 1.191)
|| 35 ||
yeyaṃ pūrvoktā śuddhiḥ sā sarvāpy anāpadviṣayety abhipretyāha |
deśabhaṅge pravāse vā vyādhiṣu vyasaneṣv api |
rakṣed eva svadehādi paścād dharmaṃ samācaret || 36 || iti |
deśabhaṅgaḥ parasaiyāpāditaḥ | pravāsas tīrthayātrādau paragṛhādyavasthānam | vyādhayo jvarādayaḥ | vyasanāni svāmikopādijanitāni | eteṣu prāpteṣu śraddhājāḍyena śuddhyaśuddhī na [197] vicārayitavye | kiṃ tarhi śucibhir aśucibhir vā dravyair ātmaputrakalatrādīnāṃ yathā rakṣā bhavati tathā kṛtvā śāstāyām āpadi paścāt dravyaśuddhyādirūpaṃ śāstroktaṃ dharmam ācaret |
|| 36 ||
“rakṣed eva svadehādi” ity amum arthaṃ prapañcayati |
yena kena ca dharmeṇa mṛdunā dāruṇena vā |
uddhared dīnam ātmānaṃ samartho dharmam ācaret || 37 || iti |
śuddhadravyādisaṃpādito dharmo mṛduḥ | tadviparīto dharmo dāruṇaḥ | stanyapānamāṃsabhakṣṇādiḥ | tayor madhye yena kenāpi vyādhyādibhir dīnam ātmānam ā padbhya uddhṛtya samartho vyādhyādirahito yathāśāstraṃ dharmam ācaret || 37 ||
tatrācaraṇīyo dharmo dvividhaḥ | ācārakāṇḍoktavihitānuṣṭhānapratiṣiddhavarjanarūpa ekaḥ prāyaścittakāṇḍoktavidhiniṣedhātikramasamādhānarūpo ‘paraḥ | tad ubhayaṃ vivicyobhayatrāpi śāntāpad evādhikārīty āha |
āpatkāle tu nistīrṇe śaucācāraṃ tu cintayet |
śuddhiṃ samuddharet paścāt svastho dharmaṃ samācaret || 38 ||
iti | śaucācāram ity anena prathamakāṇḍokto dharmaḥ parāmṛṣṭaḥ | [198] śuddhim ity anena prāyaścittakāṇḍokto dharmo nirdiṣṭaḥ | śuddho yathā bhavati tathā pāpāt samuddhared ity arthaḥ | svastha ity upasaṃhāraḥ | uktaṃ dvividham api dharmaṃ svastha eva samācaret | na tv āpannaḥ | etad evābhipretya yājñavalkya āha |
kṣātreṇa karmaṇā jīved viśāṃ vāpy āpadi dvijaḥ |
nistīrya tām athātmānaṃ pāvayitvā nyaset pathi || iti | (YDh 3.35)
|| 38 ||
ākhyāya saptame ‘sminn adhyāye dravyaśuddhir avaśiṣṭā |
saiṣā mādhavavibhunā vyākhyāyi parāśarasmṛtau viduṣā ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravartakaparameśvara-
śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhavāmātyasya
kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ || 7 ||
athāṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
