saptamādhyāye dravyaśuddhiḥ pratipāditā | tatrānte “śuddhiṃ samuddharet” ity uktam | tatroddhāraprakāraḥ prakīrṇakādiprāyaścittarūpaś caturthāditriṣv adhyāyeṣūpavarṇitaḥ | athāvaśiśiṣṭa upavarṇanīyaḥ | ṣaṣṭhādhyāye ca "vaiśyaṃ vā kṣatriyaṃ vāpi” ityādinopapātakānāṃ prāyaścittāny upavarṇayan bahuvaktavyasadbhāvād upapātakaviśeṣasya govadhasya prāyaścittaṃ tatropekṣitam | tad idānīm adhyāyadvayena vivakṣur ādau munivipratipattiṃ sūcayituṃ pṛcchati |
gavāṃ bandhanayoktreṣu bhaven mṛtyur akāmataḥ |
akāmakṛtapāpasya prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet || 8.1 || iti |
yoktrāṇi pāśāḥ | bandhanārthāni yoktrāṇi bandhanayoktrāṇi | teṣu baddhānāṃ gavām itas tataḥ saṃcaraṇādinā gale niruddhānāṃ kathaṃcit mṛtyur bhavet | sa cākāmakṛtaḥ | puruṣeṇa [200] tanmaraṇāya prayatnasyākṛtatvāt | tādṛśasya govadhasya prāyaścittaṃ vaktavyam | sarvamunisaṃmataṃ mukhyaṃ prāyaścittādhikāraṃ dyotayitum akāmakṛtasyety anuvādaḥ | kāmakṛtasya tu prāyaścittaṃ kaiścid eva munibhir abhyupagamyate | na tu sarvaiḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
kāmakārakṛte ‘py āhur eke śrutinidarśanāt || iti | (MDh 11.45)
jābālir api |
akāmakṛtapāpānāṃ bruvanti brāhmaṇā vratam |
kāmakārakṛte ‘py eke dvijānāṃ vṛṣalasya ca || iti |
devalo ‘pi |
yat syād anabhisaṃdhāya pāpaṃ karma sakṛt kṛtam |
tasyeyaṃ niṣkṛtiḥ proktā dharmavidbhir manīṣibhiḥ ||
vidheḥ prāthamikād asmād dvitīye dviguṇaṃ smṛtam |
[201]        tṛtīye triguṇaṃ kṛcchraṃ caturthe nāsti niṣkṛtiḥ ||
abhisaṃdhikṛte pāpe sakṛd vā neha niṣkṛtiḥ |
apare niṣkṛtiṃ brūyur abhisaṃdhikṛte ‘pi ca || iti |
baudhāyanas tu kāmakṛtasya prāyaścittābhāvam āha |
amatyā brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā duṣṭo bhavati dharmataḥ |
ṛṣayo niṣkṛtiṃ tasya vadanty amatipūrvake ||
matipūrvaṃ hate tasmin niṣkṛtir nopalabhyate || iti | (BDh 2.1.1.6)
chāgaleyo ‘pi |
prāyaścittam akāmānāṃ kāmāvāptau na vidyate | iti |
aṅgirās tu kāmakṛtasya dviguṇaṃ vratam āha |
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittaṃ na kāmataḥ |
syāt tv akāmakṛte yat tu dviguṇaṃ buddhipūrvake || iti |
evaṃ ca kāmakṛte vipratipattāv api akāmakṛte tadabhāvāt tādṛśaḥ sarvasaṃmato mukhyādhikārī |
atredaṃ vicāryate – bhavatv akāmakṛtasya prāyaścittasadbhāve munīnām avivādaḥ kāmakṛtasya tūktarītyā munivipratipattau ko nirṇaya itīdaṃ vicāryate |
atra kecid nirṇayam āhuḥ – dvividhā hi pāpasya śaktiḥ | narakotpādikā vyavahāranirodhikā ceti atas tannivartakasya [202] prāyaścittasyāpi śaktir dvidhā bhidyate | narakanivārikā vyavahārajananī ceti | tatra prāyaścittābhāvavādināṃ munīnāṃ narakanivāraṇābhāvo ‘bhipretaḥ | sadbhāvavādināṃ tu vyavahārajananī śaktir abhipretā | ayaṃ ca nirṇayo yājñavalkyena vispaṣṭam abhihitaḥ |
prāyaścittair apaity eno yad ajñānakṛtaṃ bhavet |
kāmato ‘vyavahāryas tu vacanād iha jāyate || iti | (YDh 3.226)
asyāyam arthaḥ – yad eno brahmaghātādikam ajñānakṛtaṃ tat vihitair dvādaśavārṣikādibhir apaiti | kāmatas tu kṛtaṃ cet sa pumān śiṣṭair vyavahāryaḥ kevalam iha loke bhavati | na tu tasya narakāpādakam enaḥ prāyaścittair apaiti |
nanu – evaṃ sati prāyaścittaṃ pāpasya kāṃcic chaktim apanudati kāṃcin nety ardhajaranīyaṃ prasajyeta | na hi kukkuṭyā eko bhāgaḥ pacyate aparo bhāgaḥ prasavāya kalpata iti kvacid dṛṣṭam |
na, vacanād ardhajaratīty asyāpy aṅgīkāryatvāt, “kiṃ hi vacanaṃ na kuryāt | nāsti vacanasyātibhāraḥ” iti nyāyāt | anyathā yauktikaṃ manyaḥ pāpaśaktiṃ prāyaścittaśaktiṃ ca kena dṛṣṭāntena samarthayiṣyati | vacanaṃ ca kāmakṛtānāṃ dviguṇaṃ vrataṃ darśayati |
vihitaṃ yad akāmānāṃ kāmāt tata dviguṇaṃ bhavet | iti |
ato dviguṇaprāyaścitteneha loke vyavahāraḥ sidhyati | [203] yas tu vyavahāram anapekṣya paralokanirvāham eva kevalam apekṣite tasya buddhipūrvakeṣu mahāpātakeṣu maraṇāntikam eva prāyaścittam | tatra śātātapaḥ: “akāvāvāptau prāyaścittam | kāmakārakṛte tv ātmānam avasādayet” iti | smṛtyantare ‘pi |
yaḥ kāmato mahāpāpaṃ naraḥ kuryāt kathaṃcana |
na tasya niṣkṛtir dṛṣṭā bhṛgvagnipatanād ṛte || iti |
manur api |
prāsyed āstmānam agnau vā samiddhe trir avākśirāḥ |
lakṣyaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syāt viduṣām icchayātmanaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.73)
tasmāt kāmakāriṇo maraṇena narakapātanivṛttiḥ vratacaryayā tu vyavahārasiddhir iti nirṇayaḥ |
apare punar evam āhuḥ – yad uktaṃ maraṇāntikaprāyaścittena narakanivṛttir iti tat tathaiva | yat tu vratacaryayā vyavahārasiddhir eva na tu narakanivṛttir iti tat viparyeti | cīrṇavratasya narakas tāvan nivartate | iha loke tu tasya śiṣṭaiḥ saha vyavahāro ‘sti | etac ca “avyavahāryaḥ” iti yājñavalkyavacane padaṃ chittvā yojanīyam | kāmataś cet pāpaṃ kṛtaṃ sa pāpī kṛtaprāyaścitto ‘py avyavahārya iha loke jāyate | tac cāvyavahāryatvaṃ vacanabalād avagantavyam | vacanaṃ ca mānavam etat |
bālaghnāṃś ca kṛtaghnāṃś ca viśuddhān ap dharmataḥ |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃś ca strīhantṝṃś c na saṃvaset || iti | (MDh 11.190)
ataḥ kṛtaprāyaścittā mahāpātakinaḥ paraloke śuddhā api śiṣṭair iha bahiḥkāryāḥ ||
nanu – upapātakinām api kṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ bahiṣkāra evocitaḥ | tathā ca vaiyyāsikaṃ nyāyasūtram: “bahis tūbhayathāpi smṛter ācārāc ca” (ŚāSū 3.4.43) [205] iti | asyāyam arthaḥ – yady upapātakaṃ yadi vā mahāpātakaṃ ubhayathāpi kṛtaprāyaścittāḥ śiṣṭair bahiḥkāryāḥ | “prāyaścittaṃ ca paścyāmi” iti nindāsmṛteḥ śiṣṭācārāc ca – iti cet |
maivam – ayaṃ hi bahiṣkāra ūrdhvaretoviṣayam | na tu gṛhasthaviṣayaḥ | ūrdhvaretovicārāṇām eva tatra prastutatvāt | idaṃ ca kauśikena spaṣṭīkṛtam |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ cāvakīrṇinām |
śuddhānām api loke ‘smin prayāpattir na vidyate || iti |
tad evam aihikavyavahārāya paralokāya vā kāmakṛtānāṃ mahāpātakānām upapātakānāṃ cāsty eva prāyaścittam iti siddham || 8.1 ||
“prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet” iti setikartavyatākasya prāyaścittasya pṛṣṭatvāt pariṣadupasattirūpām ādyām itikartavyatām āha |
vedavedāṅgaviduṣāṃ dharmaśāstraṃ vijānatām |
svakarmarataviprāṇāṃ svakaṃ pāpaṃ nivedayet || 8.2 || iti |
nāmadhārakaviprāṇāṃ pariṣattvaṃ nāstīti vakṣyamāṇam arthaṃ hṛdi nidhāya tadvyāvṛttisamarthair viśeṣaṇair pariṣadyogyā brāhmaṇā viśeṣyante | yady api manvādidharmaśāstrajñānamātreṇa brāhmaṇāḥ prāyaścittaṃ vidhātuṃ sarmarthāḥ tathāpi [206] “śunālīḍhaṃ havir yathā” (DaSm 7.43) iti nyāyena anadhītavedaiḥ svakarmānuṣṭhānarahitair nirdiṣṭaṃ prāyaścittaṃ na pāpāpanodanakṣamam | tasmāt svakarmaniṣṭhān vedapāraṃgatān upetya teṣām agre cikīrṣitaprāyaścittanimittaṃ pāpam aśeṣeṇa nivedayet | seyaṃ pariṣad upasattiḥ | tām etām upasattim aṅgirā api spaṣṭaṃ darśayati |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi upasthānasya lakṣaṇam |
upasthito hi nyāyena vratādeśanam arhati ||
sadyo niḥsaṃśaye pāpe na bhuñjītānupasthitaḥ |
bhuñjāno vardhayet pāpaṃ parṣad yatra na vidyate ||
saṃśaye tu na bhoktavyaṃ yāvat kāryaviniścayaḥ |
pramādaś ca na kartavyo yathaivāsaṃśaye tathā ||
kṛtvā pāpaṃ na gūheta gūhyamānaṃ vivardhate |
svalpaṃ vātha prabhūtaṃ vā dharmavidbhyo nivedayet ||
te hi pāpakṛto vaidyā hantāraś caiva pāpmanām |
[207]        vyādhitasya yathā vaidyā buddhimanto rujāpahāḥ ||
prāyaścitte samutpanne hrīmān satyaparāyaṇaḥ |
mṛdur ārjavasaṃpannaḥ śuddhiṃ yāceta mānavaḥ ||
sacailaṃ vāgyataḥ snātvā klinnavāsāḥ samāhitaḥ |
kṣatriyo vātha vaiśyo vā tataḥ parṣadam āvrajet ||
upasthāya tataḥ śīghram ārtimān dharaṇīm vrajet |
gātraiś ca śirasā caiva na ca kiṃcid udāharet || iti | (Aṅ/Sm 3.74–81)
|| 2 ||
yathoktaviśeṣaṇarahitānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ niṣeddhuṃ teṣāṃ svarūpam āha |
sāvitryāś cāpi gāyatryāḥ saṃdhopāstyagnikāryayoḥ |
ajñānāt kṛṣikartāro brāhmaṇā nāmadhārakāḥ || 3 || iti |
“tat savitur vareṇyam” iti savitṛpratipādakatvāt sāvitrī | ṇikārayakārayor viśleṣeṇa caturviṃśatyakṣaratvād gāyatrī, “caturviṃśatyakṣarā gāyatrī” iti śruteḥ | sāvitryādīnām ajñānān na mukhyā brāhmaṇāḥ | [208] brahma vedaḥ | tam adhītyārthaṃ cāvagatya ye ‘nutiṣṭhanti mukhyās te brāhmaṇāḥ | tad āha aṅgirāḥ |
janmaśārīravidyābhir ācāreṇa śrutena ca |
dharmeṇa ca yathoktena brāhmaṇatvaṃ vidhīyate || iti | (AṅSm 1.51)
ye tu sāvitrīṃ samyak na jānanti dūre tadanuṣṭhānaṃ te viśiṣṭamātāpitṛjanyā api brāhmaṇanāmamātraṃ dhārayanti | na tu yathoktabrāhmaṇaśabdārthatvaṃ teṣu vidyate | ata eva yājanādhyāpanādijīvanahetvasaṃbhavāj jīvanāya kṛṣiṃ kurvanti | teṣu brāhmaṇaśabdārthatvābhāvaṃ vyāsa āha |
brahmabījasamutpanno brāhmasaṃskāravarjitaḥ |
jātimātropajīvī yaḥ so ‘brāhmaṇa iti smṛtaḥ || iti | (VyāSm 4.42)
nāmadhārakavat brāhmaṇabruvasyāpi na mukhyaṃ brāhmaṇyam | brāhmaṇabruvaś ca caturviṃśatimate darśitaḥ |
garbhādhānādisaṃskārair vedopanayanair yutaḥ |
nādhyāpayati nādhīte sa bhaved brāhmaṇabruvaḥ || iti ||
|| 3 ||
amukhyabrāhmaṇānāṃ vṛddhavyavahāradarśanena prāyaścittaviśeṣaparijñāne ‘pi pariṣattvayogyatvaṃ nāstīty āha |
[209]        avratānām amantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām |
sahasraśaḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate || 4 || iti |
saumyaprājāpatyādivratahīnā avratāḥ | anadhītavedā amantrāḥ |
nanu – adhyātmavidyāyuktasyaikasyaiva pariṣattvam asti, “eko vādhyātmavittamaḥ” (YDh 1.9) iti smaraṇāt | adhyātmavidyārahitasya tv ekasya pariṣattvābhāve ‘pi caturṇāṃ pariṣattvaṃ smaryate: “catvāro vedadharmajñāḥ pariṣat” (YDh 1.9) iti | anayaiva diśādhyayanādirahitānāṃ pañcaṣāṇāṃ pariṣattvābhāve ‘pi śatasahasrādisaṃkhyāyuktānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ bhaviṣyatīty āśaṅkya tannivāraṇāya “sahasraśaḥ sametānām” ity uktam | adhyaynādihīnavan nāstikādīnām api pariṣattvaṃ nāsti | yad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
vedapāram anītā ye dharmaśāstravivarjitāḥ |
pariṣattvaṃ na teṣāṃ syān nāśtikānāṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
anāhitāgnayo ‘jñānāḥ kevalā vedapāragāḥ |
[210]        piśunāḥ krūrakarmāṇaḥ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate ||
śāstrajñā duṣṭakarmāṇaḥ pratikūlās tv asūyakāḥ |
haitukā bhinnamaryādās te ‘pi parṣadvivarjitāḥ ||
iti || 4 ||
pūrvatra “dharmaśāstraṃ vijānatām” (72) iti viśeṣaṇena tadvijñānarahitasya prāyaścittavaktṛtvaṃ nāstīti yad arthāt sūcitaṃ tad evātra ślokadvayena prapañcayati |
yad vadanti tamomūḍhā mūrkhā dharmam atadvidaḥ |
tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā tadvaktṝn adhigacchati || 5 ||
ajñātvā dharmaśāstrāṇi prāyaścittaṃ dadāti yaḥ |
prāyaścittī bhavet pūtaḥ kilbiṣaṃ parṣadi vrajet || 6 || iti |
ajñānaṃ dvividham | saty api dharmaśāstrapāṭhe nyāyanirṇayakauśalābhāvena prakṛtasya sūkṣmasya prāyaścittaviśeṣasyāparijñānam ekam | vṛddhavyavahāreṇa saty api parijñāne dharmaśāstrāpariśīlanam aparam | tad ubhayam abhilakṣya ślokadvayam | tamomūḍhāḥ prajñāmāndyena tamasā sūkṣmanyāyeṣu vibhrāntāḥ | mūrkhāḥ paṇḍitaṃmanyāḥ | atadvidaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣam ajñānantaḥ | tādṛśāḥ puruṣā yat pāpam uddiśya dharmaṃ prāyaścittam anyathā vadanti tat pāpaṃ śataguṇāṃ bhūtvā tān mūrkhān vaktṝn prāpnoti | aṅgirāś caitad evāha |
[211]        yat tu dattam ajānadbhiḥ prāyaścittaṃ sabhāgataiḥ |
tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā dātṝn evopatiṣṭhati || iti | (AṅS 3.54)
prāyaścittī cānyathākāritvān na śudhyati | yas tu dharmaśāstrapāṭhahīnaḥ prāyaścittaviśeṣaṃ lokato ‘vagatya nirdiśati tatra yathāśāstrānuṣṭhāyitvāt prāyaścittī pūto bhavati | sa tu vaktā kilbiṣaṃ vrajet, dharmaśāstrāṇām apaṭhitatvāt | tatpāṭhābhāve ca vidhāyakavacanasyodāhartum aśakyatvāt | udāhṛtyaiva vacanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nirdeṣṭavyam iti hi pūrvam uktam:
munivaktrodgatān dharmān gāyanto vedavittamāḥ | iti | (PārSm 6.35)
aṅgirā api |
vacaḥ pūrvam udāhārtyaṃ yathoktaṃ dharmavaktṛbhiḥ |
paścāt kāryānusāreṇa śaktyā kuryur anugraham ||
na hi teṣām atikramya vacanāni mahātmanām |
prajñānair api vidvadbhiḥ śakyam anyat prabhāṣitum || iti | (AṅSm 3.41)
|| 5–6 ||
[212]
dharmaśāstraparijñānasya nyāyanirṇayakauśalasya ca sadbhāve ‘py anvadhānādicittadoṣaśaṅkāvyudāsāya svasamānais tricaturaiḥ saha saṃvādo ‘pekṣīta ity abhipretyāha |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi yaṃ brūyur vedapāragāḥ |
sa dharma iti vijñeyo netarais tu sahasraśaḥ || 7 || iti |
bahūnām anyonyasaṃvādena yo niścitaḥ sa eva dharma iti vijñeyaḥ | itarair anyonyasaṃvādam anādriyamāṇaiḥ sahasrasaṃkhyākavacanapāṭhapuraḥsaram abhihito ‘pi visaṃvādaśaṅkāyā anirākṛtatvān nāsau dharmatvena svīkāryaḥ || 7 ||
saty api tricaturāṇāṃ parasparasaṃvāde viprakīrṇeṣv anekeṣu dharmaśāstreṣu kvāpi kasyacid viśeṣasya saṃbhavāt prāyaścittanirdeśavelāyāṃ punaḥ śāstrāṇi paryālocyaiva nirdeṣṭavyam ity āha |
pramāṇamārgaṃ mārganto ye dharmaṃ pravadanti vai |
teṣām udvijate pāpaṃ sadbhūtaguṇavādinām || 8 || iti |
pramāṇamārgo dharmaśāstratātparyam | tasya mārgaḥ nyāyena [213] niṣkarṣaḥ | taṃ kurvanti eva ye dharmaṃ pravadanti tebhyaḥ pāpam udvijate nāśayogyatām āpadyate iti yāvat | tatra hetuḥ sadbhūta iti | sadbhūtaḥ satyabhūto ‘nyathābhāvam aprāptaḥ, parasparasaṃvādenānavadhānādi-doṣābhāvāt | punaḥ śāstraparyālocanena nigūḍharahasyāvagamāc ca guṇo dharmaḥ, śreyohetutvāt | tādṛśadharmavāditvāt tebhyaḥ pāpasyodvego yuktaḥ | parasparasaṃvādaḥ punar dharmaśāstrapāṭhaś ca aṅgirasā darśitaḥ |
tasminn utsārite pāpe yathāvad dharmapāṭhakāḥ |
te tathā tatra jalpeyur vimṛśantaḥ parasparam || iti | (AṅSm 3.97)
pāpe pāpakāriṇi puruṣe || 8 ||
nanu – pariṣadudvignaṃ pāpaṃ yadi kartary evāvatiṣṭhate tadā prāyaścittam anarthakam | atha pariṣadaṃ prāpnuyāt tarhi katham udvegaḥ | — iti ata āha |
yathāsmani sthitaṃ toyaṃ mārutārkeṇa śudhyati |
evaṃ pariṣadādeśāt nāśayet tasya duṣkṛtam || 9 ||
[214]        naiva gacchati kartāraṃ naiva gacchati parṣadam |
mārutārkādisaṃyogāt pāpaṃ naśyati toyavat || 10 || iti |
nimne mahati vā pāṣāṇe varṣadhārābhiḥ sthitam udakaṃ mārutārkādisaṃyogo vināśaṃ prāpayati | evam iyaṃ pariṣat śāstrīyasya prāyaścittasyādeśāt tasya duṣkṛtiṃ nāśyati | na hi mārutādāv āsajjate | kiṃ tu śuṣyaty eva kevalam | evam idam api pāpaṃ na kartary avatiṣṭhate nāpi pariṣadaṃ prāpnoti | kiṃ tu svarūpanāśam eva prāpnoti | etad eva caturviṃśatimate ‘pi abhihitam |
yathā bhūmigataṃ toyam arkapādair vinaśyati |
evaṃ pariṣadādiṣṭaṃ naśyate tasya duṣkṛtam ||
naiva gacchati kartāraṃ naiva gacchati parṣadam |
mārutārkasamāyogāt pāpaṃ naśyati toyavat || iti |
aṅgirās tu praśnapūrvakam etad eva viśadayati |
prāyaścitte yadā cīrṇe brāhmaṇe dagdhakilbiṣe |
sarvaṃ pṛcchāmi tattvena tat pāpaṃ kva nu tiṣṭhati ||
naiva gacchati kartāraṃ naiva gacchati parṣadam |
[215]        mārutārkasamāyogāj jalavat saṃpralīyate ||
yathāśmani sthitaṃ toyaṃ nāśam ety arkamārutaiḥ |
tadvat kartari yat pāpaṃ nāśayed viduṣāṃ sabhā ||
teṣāṃ netrāgnidagdhaṃ sat pāpaṃ tasya tu dhīmataḥ |
naśyate nātra saṃdehaḥ sūryadṛṣṭaṃ himaṃ yathā || iti || 9–10 ||
atha mukhyānukalpabhedena pariṣadbhedān āha |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi vedavanto ‘gnihotriṇaḥ |
brāhmaṇānāṃ samarthā ye pariṣat sābhidhīyate || 11 ||
anāhitāgnayo ye ‘nye vedavedāṅgapāragāḥ |
pañca trayo vā dharmajñāḥ pariṣat sā prakīrtitā || 12 ||
munīnām ātmavidyānāṃ dvijānāṃ yajñayājinām |
vedavrateṣu snātānām eko ‘pi pariṣad bhavet || 13 ||
pañca pūrvaṃ mayā proktās teṣāṃ cāsaṃbhave trayaḥ |
svavṛttiparituṣṭā ye pariṣat sā prakīrtitā || 14 || iti |
[216]
grāmamadhye vidyamānānāṃ vedavidyādiguṇayuktānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ madhye ye ‘tyantaṃ samarthās te catvāro mukhyā pariṣat | tadasaṃbhave trayo vā pariṣattvena grāhyāḥ | etat pakṣadvayam āhitāgiṣv abhihitam | tadasaṃbhave vānāhintāgnyaḥ | tatrāpi pañceti mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | traya ity anukalpaḥ | tasyāpi pakṣadvayasyāsaṃbhave saty eka eva pariṣad bhavet | tam ekaṃ viśeṣṭuṃ munīnām ityādy uktam | ātmani brahmaṇi vidyānubhavo yeṣāṃ te ātmavidyāḥ | tathāvidhāḥ kṛtakṛtyāḥ santo ‘pi lokānugrahāya yajñair iṣṭavanto yajñayājinaḥ | vedānām ṛgādīnāṃ caturṇām adhyayanāyānuṣṭhitāni tattadvedoktāni vratāni vedavratāni | tattadvratasahiteṣu vedeṣu samāpteṣu yathāvidhi snātāḥ | “śrovratena snātānām” iti vā pāṭhaḥ | ātharvaṇikānāṃ vedavrateṣu mukhyaṃ vrataṃ śirovratam | tathā ca ātharvaṇikā āmananti |
kriyāvantaḥ śrotriyā brahmaniṣṭhāḥ
svayaṃ juhvata ekarṣi śraddhayantaḥ |
teṣām evaitāṃ brahmavidyāṃ vadeta
śirovrataṃ vidhivad yais tu cīrṇam || (MuUp 3.2.10)
[217] iti | tasmin śirovrate saty anantaram adhyayanaṃ parisamāpya yathāvidhi snātāḥ | teṣām eteṣāṃ yathoktasarvaguṇasaṃpannānāṃ madhye yaḥ ko ‘py ekaḥ pariṣad bhavet | atra sāgnikānām anagnikānāṃ dvau dvau pakṣau adhyātmavidas tv ekaḥ pakṣaḥ – iti pañca pakṣā mayā proktāḥ | teṣāṃ sarveṣām asaṃbhave kecana vidyamānāḥ śreṣṭhās trayaḥ | te ca yady āyudhajīvanādyadharmavṛttim anupajīvya yathāsaṃbhavavṛttimātreṇa parituṣṭās teṣām api pariṣattvam aviruddham | aṅgirasāpi pariṣadvikalpā darśitāḥ |
catvāro vā trayo vāpi vedavanto ‘gnihotriṇaḥ |
ye samyaṅniyatā viprāḥ kāryākāryaviniścitāḥ ||
prāyaścittapraṇetāraḥ sapta te ‘pi prakīrtitāḥ |
ekaviṃśatibhiś cānyaiḥ pariṣattvaṃ samāgataiḥ ||
sāvitrīmātrasārais tu cīrṇavedavratair dvijaiḥ |
yatīnām agravidyānāṃ dhyāyinām ātmayājinām ||
śirovrataiś ca snātānām eko ‘pi pariṣad bhavet |
eṣā lāghavakāryeṣu madhyameṣu tu madhyamā ||
mahāpātakacintāsu śataśo bhūya eva ca || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
[218]        lokavedāṅgadharmajñāḥ sapta pañca trayo ‘pi vā |
yatropaviṣṭā viprāḥ syuḥ sā yajñasadṛśī sabhā || iti |
|| 11–14 ||
nāmadhārakaviprāṇāṃ pariṣattvaṃ nāstīti yat pūrvam udāhṛtaṃ tad eva dṛṣṭāntena dṛḍhīkartuṃ siṃhāvalokananyāyena punaḥ parāmṛśati |
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu ye viprāḥ kevalaṃ nāmdhārakāḥ |
pariṣattvaṃ na teṣv asti sahasraguṇiteṣv api || 15 || iti |
ata ūrdhvaṃ varṇiteṣu pariṣatpakṣeṣu avagamyamānebhyo guṇavadbhyo brāhmaṇebhya ūrdhvaṃ tadvyatiriktā guṇarahitā iti yāvat || 15 ||
guṇarahiteṣu brāhmaṇeṣu brāhmaṇa iti nāmamātraṃ kevalaṃ vartate | na tu brāhmaṇaśabdapravṛttinimittaṃ mukhyo ‘rtho ‘stīty evamarthaṃ dṛṣṭāntābhyāṃ viśadayati |
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
brāhmaṇs tv anadhīyānas trayas te nāmadhārakāḥ || 16 || iti |
hastitvaṃ mṛgatvaṃ jātidvayaṃ tattacchabdapravṛttinimittam | na caitat kāṣṭhamayacarmamayayor vidyate | tathāpi hastimṛganāmanī sādṛśyamātreṇa prayujyete | tadvad vedādhyayanahīne brāhmaṇaśabdprayogaḥ || 16 ||
nanu – brāhmaṇaśabdasyāvayavārthatas tasmin mā bhūt | jātis tu [219] vidyate | viśiṣṭamātāpitṛjanyatvaṃ brāhmaṇatvajātiḥ | tathā ca brāhmaṇaśabdasya tasmin yaugikatvābhāve ‘pi rūḍheḥ sadbhāvād viṣamau dṛṣṭāntau – tiy āśaṅkyāha |
grāmasthānaṃ yathā śūnyaṃ yathā kūpas tu nirjalaḥ |
yathā hutam anagnau ca amantro brāhmaṇs tathā || 17 || iti |
grāmaśabdo hi jananivāsasthāne bhūbhāgaviśeṣe mukhyayā vṛttyā vartate | kūpaśabdaś ca jalādhāre khāte | homaśabdaś ca śāstrasaṃskṛtavahnau haviḥprakṣepam āha | tatra janaviśiṣṭasthānaṃ mukhyo grāmaḥ | janaśūnyagrāmas tu grāmābhāsaḥ | kūpahomayor apy ābhāsatvam evaṃ yojanīyam | tair etair dṛṣṭāntair ayaṃ nāmadhārako jātimātrasadbhāve ‘pi yogahīnatvāt brāhmaṇābhāsaḥ | na tu mukhyaḥ | paṅkajaśabdasya ca yadi api jātiḥ pravṛttinimittaṃ tathāpy avayavārthas tasmin nimitte ‘ntar bhavaty eva | tato yogarūḍha iti vyavahriyate | evaṃ brāhmaṇaśabdasyāpi yogarūḍhatvāt jātisadbhāve ‘pi yogahīnatvād ābhāsatvaṃ draṣṭavyam || 17 ||
nanu – mukhyasaṃbhave saty amukhyaṃ na grāhyam | tadasaṃbhave tv amukhyam api grāhyam – iti lokavedasthitiḥ | tathā cādhyayanādisaṃpannamukhyabrāhmaṇānāṃ asaṃbhave sati brāhmaṇābhāsānām api kvacit pariṣattvaṃ syāt – ity āśaṅkyāha |
[220]        yathā ṣaṇḍho ‘phalaḥ strīṣu yathā gaur ūṣarāphalā |
yathā cājñe ‘phalaṃ dānaṃ tathā vipro ‘nṛco ‘phalaḥ || 18 || iti |
amukhyam api yatra kāryakṣamaṃ bhavati tatra mukhyāsaṃbhave tad upādeyam | yathā vaidike karmaṇi somābhāve pūtīkābhiṣavaḥ | yathā vā loke śālyābhāve kodravādiḥ | tadvad brāhmaṇābhāsasyāpi kāryakṣamatve syāt kvacid upādeyatvam | na tv adhyayanahīnasya prāyaścittavidhānakṣamatāsti | na hi puṃstvopetasya vatasyāsaṃbhave ‘pi ṣaṇḍhaḥ prajām utpādayann upalabhyate | nāpi bhūmir ūṣarā kvacit phalati | napy ajñaḥ pratigṛhṇan dātre phalahetuḥ | evam asāv api ṛgādimantrahīno na pāpanivṛttiphalahetuḥ || 18 ||\\
nanu – nāmadhārakaḥ ṣaṇḍhādivad aphalaś cet tarhi saṃskārair api tasya ko ‘tiśayaḥ syāt | na hi śṛṅgārasahasreṇāpi ṣaṇḍhaḥ prajanayituṃ prabhavati – ity āśṅkya dṛṣṭāntena saṃskārakṛtotkarṣaṃ saṃbhāvayati |
[221]        citrakarma yathānekair aṅgair unmīlyate śanaiḥ |
brāhmaṇyam api tadvad dhi saṃskārair mantrapūrvakaiḥ || 19 ||
iti | citrakāraḥ prathamaṃ paṭādau maṣīrekhābhiḥ sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇāni manuṣyādirūpāṇi likhati | na ca tāni tāvatā darśanīyatvam āpadyante | punas tāny eva rūpāṇi nānāvidhavarṇaprakṣepeṇonmīlitāni darśanīyatām āpadyante | evaṃ jātibrāhmaṇyaṃ śāstrīyasaṃskārair utkṛṣyate | mantrasaṃskāreṇa vidyādayo ‘py upalakṣyante | ata eva aṅgirāḥ |
janmaśārīravidyābhir ācāreṇa śrutena ca |
dharmeṇa ca yathoktena brāhmaṇatvaṃ vidhīyate ||
citrakarma yathānekair aṅgair unmīlyate śanaiḥ |
brāhmaṇyam api tadvat syāt saṃskārair vidhipūrvakaiḥ || iti || 19 ||
nanu – yatra saṃskṛtā bahavo na santi tatraikena saṃskṛtena satare nāmadhārakāḥ prāyaścittavidhāyinaḥ syur ity āśaṅkyāha |
prāyaścittaṃ prayacchanti ye dvijā nāmadhārakāḥ |
te dvijā pāpakarmāṇaḥ sametā narakaṃ yayuḥ || 20 || iti |
sametāḥ ekena saṃskṛtena saṃgatāḥ | yayuḥ yāntīty arthaḥ | [222] yady api saṃskṛtaḥ svayaṃ narakaṃ nārhati tathāpy anarhaiḥ sahaikasyāṃ pariṣady upaveśanāt tasya narakaprāptiḥ | etac ca caturviṃśatimate spaṣṭīkṛtam |
prāyaścittaṃ prayacchanti ye dvijā nāmadhārakāḥ |
te sarve pāpakarmāṇaḥ sameto narakaṃ vrajet || iti |
sameto nāmadhārakaiḥ saṃgataḥ | tāvataivāparādhena mantrasaṃskṛto ‘pi narakaṃ prāpnoti |
|| 20 ||
nanu – saṃskṛtānāṃ bahūnām api gṛhiṇāṃ kathaṃ pariṣattvaṃ syāt | nāmadhārakavat teṣām indriyaratatvād ity āśaṅkyāha |
ye paṭhanti dvijā vedaṃ pañcayajñaratāś ca ye |
trailokyaṃ tārayanty ete pañcendriyaratā api || 21 || iti |
vedo hi nikhilapāpāpanayane samarthaḥ | tathā ca taittirīyabrāhmaṇe vahnyādiparamparayā prāptaṃ pāpaṃ svādhyāyo ‘pahantīti śrūyate: “agniṃ vai jātaṃ pāpmā jagrāha | taṃ devā āhutībhiḥ pāpmānam apāghnann āhutīnāṃ yajñena yajñasya dakṣiṇābhir [223] dakṣiṇānāṃ brāhmaṇena brāhmaṇasya chandobhiś chandasāṃ svādhyāyenāpahatapāpmā svādhyāyo devapavitraṃ vā etat” iti (TS 3.7.34–39) | manur api |
vedābhyāso ‘nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā |
nāśayanty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakajāny api ||
yathaidhāṃsy ojasā vahniḥ prāśya nirdahati kṣaṇāt |
tathā jñānāgninā pāpaṃ sarvaṃ dahati vedavit || (MDh 11.246–47)
[224]        yathā jātabalo vahnir dahaty ārdrān api drumān |
tathā dahati vedajñaḥ karmajaṃ doṣam ātmanaḥ || (MDh 12.101)
iti | ye tu śraddhālava eva santaḥ prajñāmāndyādidoṣeṇa paṭhitaṃ vedaṃ dhārayitum asamarthāḥ te ‘pi pañcayajñānuṣṭhānāt kṣīṇapāpā eva | pañcayajñānāṃ pāpakṣayahetutvaṃ ca prathamādhyāye prapañcitam | ataś ca pañcendriyaprasaktānām api kṣīṇapāpatvena pariṣattvam aviruddham || 21 ||
nanu – evam api pañcayajñādibhiḥ pariśuddheṣu gṛhiṣu parakīyaṃ pāpam anupraviśati, parānnabhojanādes tatprāpakatvāt,
duṣkṛtaṃ hi manuṣyāṇām annam āśritya tiṣṭhati |
annāder bhrūṇahā mārṣṭi ............. |
ityādismaraṇāt – ity ata āha |
saṃpraṇītaḥ śmaśāneṣu dīpto ‘gniḥ sarvabhakṣakaḥ |
evaṃ ca vedavid vipraḥ sarvabhakṣo ‘pi daivatam || 22 || iti |
na hy agniḥ śmaśāne nītaḥ śavādibhakṣakaḥ ity etāvatā devatvaṃ parityajati | evaṃ vedavido bhūdevasya abhakṣyābhojyādidoṣamātreṇa bhūdevatvaṃ nāpaiti, ekaikasya mantrasyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇādyaśeṣadoṣanivartakatvāt | tathā ca mantraliṅgam |
[225]        yad annam admy anṛtena devā dāsyannadāsyannuta vā kariṣyan |
yad devānāṃ cakṣuṣyāgo ‘sti yad eva kiṃca
pratigrāham agnir mā tasmād anṛṇaṃ kṛṇotu ||
yad annam admi bahudhā virūpaṃ vāso hiraṇyam uta gām ajāvim | (RV 3.6.21.5)
ityādi | anṛtena parakīyādau svakīyatvādivacanena | dāsyaṇnanarhebhyo naṣṭaśaucācārādibhyaḥ snehānubandhena havyakavyayoḥ prayacchan | adāsyannātithyadibhyo yogyebhyo ‘prayacchan | kariṣyann annabhakṣaṇavyatirikatam pratiṣiddhaṃ vā kurvan cakṣuṣy āgo nagnaparayoṣidavalokanādi | tathā devānām āgaḥ viṣṇvādyaṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvāpy anamaskarādi | yad eva kiṃca aśvamahiṣakālapuruṣādyapratigrāhyaṃ yat kiṃcit pratijagrāhaṃ pratigṛhītavān asmi | bahudhā virūpaṃ gaṇānnagaṇikānnādirūpeṇānekadoṣayuktaṃ yad annam admi bhakṣitavān asmi | yathoktān sarvasmād doṣajātāt mām anṛṇaṃ pūtam agniḥ karotv iti mantrārthaḥ | tathā pavamānasūktādimahimānam abhidhīyate |
krayavikrayād yonidoṣād rakṣād bhojyāt pratigrahāt |
asaṃbhojanāc cāpi nṛśaṃsaṃ tat pāvamānībhir ahaṃ punāmi ||
iti (RV 7.2.3, pariśiṣṭam 2)
tathā mām avidhāne samagāḥ paṭhanti |
[226]        abhojyabhojane ete panthā adho divaḥ | (SāmViBr 2.5.3)
iti sāma gāyed iti śeṣaḥ | manur api |
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā vānnaṃ vigarhitam |
japaṃs taratsamandīyaṃ pūyate mānavas tryahāt ||
hatvā lokān apīmāṃs trīn aśnann api yatas tataḥ |
ṛgvedaṃ dhārayan vipro nainaḥ prapnoti kiṃcana || (MDh 11.253, 261)
iti || 22 ||
nanu – uktarītyā vedavidaḥ svakīyapāpam apanayantu nāma | tathāpi vailokyaṃ tārayanty ete – iti yad uktaṃ tat katham | ityāśakkya dṛṣṭāntena tad upapādayati |
[227]        amedhyāni tu sarvāṇi prakṣipyante yathodake |
tathaiva kilbiṣaṃ sarvaṃ prakṣipec ca dvijānale || 23 || iti |
gaṅgādijale snātuṃ praviṣṭāḥ śarīrāt svedādīny amedhyāni prakṣipanti | naitāvatā tajjalam apūtaṃ bhavati | praty uta malopetān puruṣān malāpanayanena punāti | evaṃ prāyaścittī svakīyam aśeṣaṃ pāpaṃ vedavitsu prakṣipati | tatra prakṣepaṇaṃ nāma tadapanayanāya teṣām agre nivedanam | te ca tadapanayāṅgīkāramātreṇam na duṣyanti praty uta tadapanayanasamarthaṃ śāstrīyam upāyam upadiśya pāpinam uddharanti tasmāt tārayantīty upapannam || 23 ||
nanu – vedavidyāyāś ced īdṛśo mahimā tarhi vedavidāṃ dainandinagāyatryupāstyādiṣv alasānām api pariṣattvaṃ prasajyetety ata āha |s
gāyatrīrahito vipraḥ śūdrād apy aśucir bhavet |
gāyatrībrahmatattvajñāḥ saṃpūjyante janair dvijāḥ || 24 || iti |
gāyatrī ca brahma ca gāyatrībrahmaṇī | tayos tattvaṃ gāyatrībrahmatattvam | tatra gāyatryas tattvam aṅganyāsakālavarṇaviśeṣadhyānādi | brahmaṇas tattvaṃ vedokto niḥśeṣānuṣṭheyo ‘rthaḥ | tad ubhayaṃ yathāvad avagatyānutiṣṭhantas tattvajñā iha vivakṣitāḥ | [228] yad vā brahma paramātmā | tasya tattvaṃ vedāntapratipādyaṃ svarūpam | tad gāyatryantargatatacchabdavācyatvenāvagatyopāsate ye te gāyatrībrahmatattvajñāḥ || 24 ||
nanu – yasmin grāme jitendriyaḥ śāstroktasvadharmavartī śūdro vidyate tatra pañcendriyaratād duḥśīlād varaṃ tasyaiva śūdrasya pariṣattvam ity ata āha |
duḥśilo ‘pi dvijaḥ pūjyo na tu śūdro jitendriyaḥ |
kaḥ parityajya gāṃ duṣṭāṃ duhec chīlavatīṃ karīm || 25 || iti |
kaḥ parityajya ityādir dṛṭāntaḥ | jātiśīlayor madhye jātyutkarṣa eva prādhānyenopādeyaḥ | śīlaṃ tu yathāsaṃbhavam | ata eva caturviṃśatimate śūdropadeśasyānupādeyatvaṃ prapañcayati |
śvacarmaṇi yathā kṣīram apeyaṃ brāhmaṇādibhiḥ |
tadvat śūdramukhād vākyaṃ na śrotavyaṃ kathaṃcan ||
paṇḍitasyāpi śūdrasya śāstrajñānaratasya ca |
vacanaṃ tasya na grāhyaṃ śunocchiṣṭaṃ havir yathā ||
[229]        śūdro jñānāvalepena brāhmaṇān bhāṣate yadi |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
kṛṣṇasarpaṃ dvijaṃ naṣṭaṃ śūdraṃ ca pratipāṭhakam |
gardabhaṃ jārajātaṃ ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet || iti || 25 ||
nanu – duḥśīlānāṃ pariṣattvābhyupagame tannirdiṣṭaprāyaścittasyānyathābhāvaḥ śakyate ity ata āha |
dharmaśāstrarathārūḍhā vedakhaḍgadharā dvijāḥ |
krīḍārtham api yad brūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ paramaḥ smṛtaḥ || 26 || iti |
yathā yuddhārthaṃ ratham āruhya khaḍgaṃ dhṛtvā samāgatasya yoddhuṃ muhūrtmātraṃ vilambite ‘pi yuddhe khaḍgacālanādirūpā yuddhocitaiva līlā pravartate | na tv aprastutākṣadyūtādrūpā | tadvat dharmaśāstravāsanāvasitatvāt krīḍann api yathāśāstram eva brūte | kim u vaktavyaṃ buddhipuṛvaṃ pariṣady upaveśya,
`        abruvan vibruvan vāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī | (MDh 8.13)
ityādiśāstraṃ cāvagatyānyathā na brūte iti || 26 ||
itthaṃ yogyāyogyaparaṣadau vivicya tatra yogyāyāḥ pariṣado ‘pekṣitaṃ viśeṣaṇajātaṃ darśayati |
[230]        cāturvedyo vikalpī ca aṅgavid dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
trayaś cāśramiṇo mukhyāḥ parṣad eṣā daśāvarā || 27 || iti |
tatra cāturvedyādyādiśabdārthān aṅgirā vivṛṇoti |
caturṇām api vedānāṃ pāragā ye dvijottamāḥ |
svaiḥ svair aṅgair vināpy ete cāturvidyā iti smṛtāḥ ||
dharmasya parṣadaś caiva prāyaścittakramasya ca |
trayāṇāṃ yaḥ pramāṇajñaḥ sa vikalpī bhavet dvijaḥ ||
śabde chandasi kalpe ca śikṣāyāṃ ca suniścitaḥ |
jyotiṣām ayane caiva sanirukte ‘ṅgavid bhavet ||
vedavidyāvrasnātaḥ kulaśīlasamanvitaḥ |
anekadharmaśāstrajñaḥ procyate dharmapāṭhakaḥ ||
brahmacaryāśramād ūrdhvaṃ vipro ‘yaṃ vṛddha ucyate | iti |
cāturvedyatvādyuktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā gārhasthyādyanyatamāśramavartino daśa saṃkhyākāḥ pariṣacchabdavācyāḥ | daśasaṃkhyā avarā yasyāṃ pariṣadi sā daśāvarā | daśatvam avaraḥ pakṣaḥ | tato py arvācīnaḥ pakṣo nāstīty arthaḥ | ye tu “catvāro vā trayo vāpi” (ParSm 8.7) [231] ityādipakṣāḥ pūrvam upanyasthāḥ | te sarve ‘pi govadhād arvācīnaviṣaye draṣṭavyāḥ | seyaṃ daśasaṃkhyopetā pariṣad brāhmaṇasya prāyaścittitve saty avagantavyā | yadā kṣatriyavaiśyau prāyaścittinau bhavataḥ tadā viśeṣam aṅgirā āha |
pariṣad yā brāhmaṇānāṃ sā rājñāṃ dviguṇā matā |
vaiśyānāṃ triguṇā caiva pariṣac ca vrataṃ sthitam ||
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇānāṃ tu kṣatriyāṇāṃ purohitaḥ |
vaiśyānāṃ yājakaś caiva ta eva vratadāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
aguruḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca vaiśyānāṃ cāpy ayājakaḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ samādiśya taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || iti |
yathā kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ pariṣadvṛddhiḥ tathā vratam api vardhate | iyaṃ ca vratavṛddhir uttamajātihanane draṣṭavyā, itaraviṣaye vratahrāsasyābhidhānāt | tathā ca caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
prāyaścittaṃ yad āmnātaṃ brāhmaṇasya maharṣibhiḥ |
pādonaṃ kṣatriyaḥ kuryād ardhaṃ vaiśyaḥ samācaret ||
[232]        śūdraḥ samacaret pādam aśeṣeṣv api pāpmasu || iti || 27 ||
atha vratādeśanam
itthaṃ prāyaścittinā kartavyam upasthānam upastheyā pariṣac cety ubhayaṃ nirūpitam | atha vratādeśanaṃ nirūpaṇīyam, tasyopasthānānantarabhāvitvāt | anantrabhāvaś ca aṅgirasā darśitaḥ |
upasthānaṃ vratādeśaḥ svīyāśuddhiprakāśanam |
prāyaścittacatuṣkaṃ ca vihitaṃ dharmakartṛbhiḥ || iti |
tad etad vratādeśanaṃ rājānumatyā kartavyam ity āha |
rājñaś cānumate sthitvā prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet |
svayam eva na kartavyaṃ kartavyā svalpaniṣkṛtiḥ || 28 || iti |
atra govadhasya prakṛtatvāt tam ārabhyādhikeṣu rājānujñayaiva vrataṃ nirdiśet | na tu rājānaṃ vañcayitvā svayaṃ tat kartavyam | govadhd alpeṣu tu pāpeṣu vināpi rājānujñāṃ niṣkṛtir dātuṃ śakyate | etac ca devalo ‘py āha |
yācitās tena te cāpi brāhmaṇāḥ pāpabhiruṇā |
niṣkṛtiṃ vyavahārārthaṃ kuryāt tasmai nṛpājñayā ||
[233]        svayaṃ vā brāhmaṇaiḥ kṛcchram alpadoṣe vidhīyate |
rājñā ca brāhmaṇaiś caiva mahatsu suparīkṣya ca || iti |
hārīto ‘pi |
rājño ‘numatim āśritya prāyaścittāni dāpayet |
svayam eva kurvīta kartavyaṃ svalpam eva ca || iti || 28 ||
yathā pariṣad rājānaṃ nātikramet tathā rājāpi pariṣadaṃ nātikrāmed ity āha |
brāhmaṇāṃs tān atikramya rājā kartum yad icchati |
tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā rājānam anugacchati || 29 || iti |
kartuṃ prāyaścittaṃ kārayitum, antarbhāvitaṇyarthasy vivakṣitatvāt || 29 ||
idānīṃ pariṣadā samācaraṇīyān niyamaviśeṣān āha |
prāyaścittaṃ sadā dadyād devatāyatanāgrataḥ |
ātmakṛcchraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā japed vai vedamātaram || 30 ||
[234] iti | dadyād ity atrāyogavyavacchedo vivakṣitaḥ | pariṣady upaviśyādāne pratyavāyasmaraṇāt | tad āha aṅgirāḥ |
ārttānāṃ mārgamāṇānāṃ prāyaścittāni ye dvijāḥ |
jānanto na prayacchanti te teṣāṃ samabhāginaḥ ||
tasmād arttaṃ samāsādya brāhmaṇāṃs tu viśeṣataḥ |
jānadbhiḥ parṣadaḥ panthā na hātavyaḥ parāṅmukhaiḥ ||
tasya kāryo vratādeśaḥ pāvanārthe hitepsubhiḥ |
ajñānām upadeṣṭavyaṃ kramaśaḥ sarvam eva ca ||
yathābhyuddharate kaścid bhayārttaṃ brāhmaṇam kvacit |
evaṃ pāpāt samuddhṛtya tena tulyaṃ phalaṃ labhet || iti |
sarvathā dadyād ity ayaṃ niyamo yathāvidhyupapannaviṣayaḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
yatnād eva puraskṛtya viprāṃs tatra daśāvarān |
praṇipatya ca bhaktyā ca prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || iti |
vinirdiśet yāyeta iti yāvat | yathāvidhyupapattyabhāve prāyaścittaṃ na deyam | tad āha aṅgirāḥ |
anarcitair anāhūtair apṛṣṭaiś ca yathāvidhi |
prāyaścittaṃ na dātavyaṃ jānadbhir api ca dvijaiḥ || iti |
jñānenājñānena vā prāyaścittasyānyathānirdeśe pratyavāyaṃ sa evāha |
[235]        ajānan yas tu vibrūyāj jānan yaś cānyathā vadet |
ubhayor iha tayor doṣaḥ pakṣayor ubhayor api || iti |
vidhivatkṛtasya prāyaścittasya gauravalāghavanirṇayāya deśakālādayaḥ parīkṣaṇīyāḥ | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
śarīraṃ balam āyuś ca vayaḥ kālaṃ ca karma ca |
samīkṣya dharmavid buddhyā prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || iti | (BDh 1.1.1.15)
hārīto ‘pi |
yathāvayo yathākālaṃ yathāprāṇaṃ ca brāhmṇe |
prāyaścittaṃ pradātavyaṃ brāhmaṇair dharmapāṭhakaiḥ ||
yena śuddhim avāpnoti na ca prāṇair viyujyate |
ārtiṃ vā mahatīṃ yāti na caitad vratam ādiśet || iti |
viśvāmitro ‘pi |
jātiśaktiguṇāpekṣaṃ sakṛd buddhikṛtaṃ tathā |
anubandhādi vijñaya prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
pariṣaddharmapraśnādītikartavyatā aṅgirasā darśitā |
tatas te praṇipātena dṛṣṭvā taṃ samupasthitam |
viprāḥ pṛcchanti tat kāryam upaviśyāsane sthitam ||
kiṃ te kāryaṃ kim arthaṃ vā sadā mṛagayase dvijān |
parṣadi brūhi tat sarvaṃ yat kāryaṃ hitam ātmanaḥ ||
satyena dyotate rājā satyena dyotate raviḥ |
[236]        satyena dyotate vahniḥ sarvaṃ satye pratiṣṭhitam ||
bhūr bhuvaḥ svas trayo lokās te ‘pit satye pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
asmākaṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ satyam eva paraṃ balam ||
yadi ced vakṣyase satyaṃ niyataṃ prāpsyase śubham |
yady āgato ‘sy asatyena na tu śudhyasi kenacit ||
satyena tu viśudhyanti śuddhikāmāś ca mānavāḥ |
tasmāt tad vada yat satyam ādimadhyāvasānikam ||
evaṃ taiḥ samanujñātaḥ sarvaṃ brūyād aśeṣataḥ |
tasmin nivedite kārye niṣkāsyo yas tu kāryavān ||
tasminn utsārite pāpe yathāvad dharmapāṭhakāḥ |
te tathā tatra jalpeyur vimṛśantaḥ parasparam ||
āpaddharmeṣu yat proktaṃ yac ca sānugrahaṃ bhavet |
pariṣatsaṃpadaś caiva kāryāṇāṃ ca balābalam ||
prāpya deśaṃ ca kālaṃ ca yac ca kāryāntaraṃ bhavet |
parṣat saṃcintya tat sarvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet ||
sarveṣāṃ niścitaṃ yat syād yac ca prāṇān na ghātayet |
āhūya śrāvayed ekaḥ parṣadā yo niyojitaḥ ||
śṛṇuṣva bho idaṃ vipra yat te ādiśyate vratam |
yathā bhavet tathā cīrṇe tadā śuddhiprakāśanam ||
kāryaṃ sarvaṃ prayatnena śaktyā viprābhibhāṣitam |
[237] iti | etac ca sarvaṃ kasyacit devatāyatanasya śaivasya vaiṣṇavasya vā puraḥ sthitvā nirdeṣṭavyam | “devatāyatanāgrataḥ” ity etat puṇyatīrthāder apy upalakṣaṇam | evaṃ prāyaścittaṃ nirdiśyānantaraṃ nirdeṣṭāraḥ sarve ‘py ātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ tattatprāyaścittānusāreṇa svalpam adhikaṃ vā kiṃcit kṛcchraṃ caritvā tadante vedamātaraṃ gāyatrīṃ yathāśakti japeyuḥ iti | etad eva hārīto ‘py āha |
prāyaścittaṃ tu nirdiśya kathaṃ pāpāt pramucyate |
yat pavitraṃ vijānīyāt japed vā vedamātaram || iti || 30 ||
“vedavedāṅgaviduṣām” (8.1) ity ārabhya “japed vai vedamātaram” (8.30) ityantena granthasaṃdarbheṇa yat pariṣannirūpaṇaṃ tat sarvam atītādhyāyokeṣu āgāmyadhyāye vakṣyamāṇeṣu ca prāyaścitteṣu samānam avagantavyam | adhyāyādau yat pṛṣṭaṃ govadhaprāyaścittaṃ tad idānīm adhyāyaparisamāpter nirūpayati | tatra “saśikham” ityādinā bahuvidham itikartavyam āha |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā trisaṃdhyam vagāhnam |
gavāṃ madhye vased rātrau divā gāś cāpy anuvrajet || 31 || iti |
śikhāpi yathā na pariśiṣyate tathā vapanaṃ [238] kuryāt | saṃdhyātraye ca nadyādāv avagāhet | rātrau goṣṭhe gomadhye śayīta | divase tu carantīr gā anucaret || 31|
itikartavyatānantaram āha |
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ || 32 || iti |
nidāghasantāpe prathamato gavāṃ chāyāṃ saṃpādya paścāt svayaṃ chāyāyām upaviśet | evaṃ varṣādau | varṣati sati vṛṣṭipīḍitānāṃ gavāṃ śālādisaṃpādanena rakṣāṃ kuryāt | tathā hemantaśiśirayor niśi nivāsāya śītarahitaṃ sthānaṃ saṃpādayet | tathā yadā kadācit mārute bhṛśaṃ vāti sati sthānādisaṃpādanena tadupadravāt saṃrakṣet | yadā gavāṃ rakṣāṃ kartuṃ na śaknuyāt tadā svātmano ‘pi rakṣāṃ na kuryāt || 32 ||
anyad itikartavyam āha |
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gṛhe kṣetre khale ‘tha vā |
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayet pibantaṃ caiva vatsakam || 33 || iti |
svakīyeṣu parakīyeṣu vā gṛhādiṣu saṃcitaṃ śālyādikaṃ yadi kācid gaur bhakṣayet tadā tāṃ paśyann api nivārakāraṇām agre na kathayet | tathā vatso yadi dohakālād anyatra stanaṃ [239] pibet tadā tam api vatsaṃ na kathayet || 33 ||
punar apy anyad itikartavyam āha |
pibantīṣu pibet toyaṃ saṃviśantīṣu saṃviśet |
patitāṃ paṅkamagnāṃ vā sarvaprāṇair samuddharet || 34 || iti |
svayam atyantatṛṣṇārto ‘pi gavām udakapānāt pūrvam udakaṃ na pibet | tathā cirasthitigatibhyāṃ pādayor vyathāṃ prāpyāpi gosaṃveśanāt prāk svayaṃ na saṃviśet | yatra kvāpi rogārttā satī bhūmau patitā vā jalapānādyarthaṃ gatvā paṅke magnā vā gaur yadā dṛṣṭā tadānīm eva savprāṇeṣu laulyaṃ parityajya sarveṇāpi prayatnena tāṃ gām uddharet || 34 ||
nanu – svaprāṇeṣv alubdhasya prakrāntavratasamāptyabhāve duritakṣayo na syād ity ata āha |
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā yas tu prāṇān parityajet |
mucyate brahmahatyāyā goptā gor brāhmaṇasya ca || 35 || iti |
anuṣṭheyebhyaḥ sarvebhyo ‘pi vratebhyaḥ idam adhikaṃ vrataṃ yad gobrāhmaṇādyarthe maraṇam | tatra kuto vratabhaṅgaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ | svayaṃ maraṇam abhyupagamya gobrāhmaṇarakṣaṇe pravṛtto yadi kathaṃcij jīvet tadā gobrāhmaṇayoḥ saṃgoptā jīvann api brahmahatyāyā mukto bhavati || 35 ||
aṅgāni vidhāyāṅginaṃ vrataviśeṣaṃ vidadhāti |
[240]        govadhasyānurūpeṇa prājāpatyaṃ vinirdiśet |
prājāpatyaṃ tataḥ kṛcchraṃ vibhajet tac caturvidham || 36 || iti |
ekabhaktādirūpaṃ prājāpatyavrataṃ govadhasya prāyaścittam | tac ca vrataṃ vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa caturvidham | tatra govadhasya tāratamyaṃ nyāyāgamābhyāṃ niścitya tattāratamyānusāreṇānyatamaṃ prājāpatyavrataviśeṣaṃ nirdiśet | yato vadhatāratamyānusāreṇa prāyaścittatāratamyaṃ vinirdeśyaṃ tato vratatāratamyāvagataṃ prājāpatyavrataṃ caturvidhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vibhajet || 36 ||
idānīṃ vibhāgaṃ prakaṭayati |
ekāham ekabhaktāśī ekāhaṃ naktabhojanaḥ |
ayācitāśy ekam ahar ekāhaṃ mārutāśanaḥ || 37 ||
dinadvayaṃ caikabhakto dvidinaṃ caikabhojanaḥ |
dinadvayam ayācī syād dvidinaṃ mārutāśanaḥ || 38 ||
tridinaṃ caikabhaktāśī tridinaṃ naktabhojanaḥ |
dinatrayam ayācī syāt tridinaṃ mārutāśanaḥ || 39 ||
caturahaṃ caikabhakāśī caturahaṃ naktabhojanaḥ |
[241]        caturdinam ayācī syāc caturahaṃ mārutāśanaḥ || 40 || iti |
ekabhaktanaktāyācitopavāsāḥ prājāpatyasvarūpam | so ‘yam ādyaḥ kalpaḥ | dviguṇībhūtaikabhaktādicatuṣṭayaṃ yat tat dvitīyaḥ kalpaḥ | triguṇībhūtaikabhaktādicatuṣṭayaṃ tṛtīyaḥ kalpaḥ | caturguṇībhūtaikabhaktādicatuṣṭayaṃ caturthaḥ kalpaḥ | ta ete catvāraḥ kalpā vadhabhedeṣu yojanīyāḥ | tad yathā – ekahāyaṇasya vadhe prathamaḥ | dvihāyaṇasya vadhe dvitīyaḥ | trihāyaṇasya vadhe tṛtīyaḥ | uparitanavayaskasya vadhe caturthaḥ iti || 37–40 ||
yathoktavratacaraṇāntarakartavyam āha |
prāyaścitte tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
viprāṇāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt pavitrāṇi japed dvijaḥ || 41 || iti |
pavitrāṇi pavamānasūktādīni | bhojanadakṣiṇājapāḥ śaktyanusāreṇa draṣṭavyāḥ || 41 ||
upasthānavratādeśanacaryātmakān trīn prāyaścittapādān abhidhāya śuddhiprakāśanarūpaṃ caturthapādam āha |
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu goghnaḥ śuddho na saṃśayaḥ | iti |
svakīyaviśuddhikhyāpanārthaṃ svabandhūn aśeṣān brāhmaṇān bhojayet | etāvatā catuṣpādaprāyaścittasyānuṣṭhitatvāt asya śuddhau nāśti kaścit saṃśayaḥ |
saśikham ityādinā sāṅgaṃ prāyaścittaṃ yad uktaṃ tad eva cyavana āha: [242] “prājāpatyavrataṃ govadhe prāyaścittam | nakhāni romāṇi chittvā saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā triṣavaṇasnānaṃ gavām anugamanaṃ saha śayanaṃ sumahattṛāṇi rathyāsu cārayet | vratānte brāhmaṇabhojanam” iti | ācāryeṇaikahāyanādicaturvidhavadhyabhedam abhipretya vrate pakṣacatuṣṭayam upanyastam | tad eva bhaṅgyantareṇa vṛddhapracetā āha |
ekavarṣe hate vatse kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate |
abuddhipūrve puṃsāṃ syāt dvipādas tu dvihāyane ||
trihāyaṇe tripādaḥ syāt prājāpatyam ataḥ param | iti |
nanu – ācāryoktād govadhaprāyaścittād adhikāni bahuvidhāni govadhaprāyaścittāni nānāmunibhir darśitāni | tatra kānicit kālenādhikāni | tad yathā – ekamāsadvimāsatrimāsādivratāni kālato 'dhikāni | tathā kānicid dānenādhikāni | tad yathaikagodānam ārabhya sahasragodānaparyantāni kānicit kāyakleśenādhikāni | tad yathā – dvādaśarātropavāsādīni |
bāḍham | tatra nimittagauravaṃ vratagauravaṃ ca paryālocya nyāyena viṣayavyavasthā kalpanīyā | tāṃ ca vayaṃ pradarśayāmaḥ | tatra yājñavalkyo vratacatuṣṭayam āha |
pañcagavyaṃ pibed goghno māsam āsīta saṃyataḥ |
goṣṭheśayo go ‘nugāmī gopradānena śudhyati ||
[246]        kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca cared vāpi samāhitaḥ |
dadyāt triratraṃ copoṣya vṛṣabhaikādaśās tu gāḥ || iti | (YDh 3.263–64)
tatra gosvāmibhedam upajīvya vyavasthā | jātimātrabrāhmaṇasvāmike trirātropavāso vṛṣabhaikādaśagodānaṃ ca | kṣatriyasvāmike pañcagavyāśanam | vaiśyasvāmike māsam atikṛcchram | śūdrasvāmike kṛcchram | viṣṇur vratatrayam āha |
goghnasya pañcagavyena māsam ekaṃ nirantaram |
prājāpatyaṃ parāko vā cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā || iti | (ViDh 2.14)
etat trayam api kṣatriyasvāmikaviṣayam | yat tu śaṅkhalikhitābhyām uktam: “goghnaḥ pañcagavyāhāraḥ pañcaviṃśatirātram upavaset | [247] saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā gocarmaṇā prāvṛto gāś cānugacchant goṣṭheśayo gāṃ dadyāt” iti, etat tu vaiśyasvāmikaviṣayam | yat tu saṃvartenoktam,
goghnasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi niṣkṛtiṃ tattvataḥ śubhām |
goghnaḥ kurvīta saṃsthānaṃ goṣṭhe gorūpasaṃsthite ||
tatraiva kṣitiśāyī syān māsārdhaṃ saṃyatendriyaḥ |
saktuyāvakabhaikṣāśī payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ śakṛt ||
etāni kramaśo ‘śnīyāt dvijas tatpāpamokṣakaḥ |
śudhyate so ‘rdhamāsena nakharomavivarjitaḥ ||
snānaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ kuryād gavām anugamanaṃ tathā |
etat samāhitaḥ kuryāt sa naro vītamatsaraḥ ||
sāvitrīṃ ca japen nityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ |
tataś cīrṇavrataḥ kuryāt viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ param ||
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu gāṃ ca dadyāt sadakṣiṇām |
[245]        vṛṣabhaṃ tiladhenuṃ vā tato mucyeta kilbiṣāt || iti | (SaṃSm 129–34)
yac ca sumantunoktam: “goghnasya gopradānaṃ goṣṭhe śayanaṃ dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyaprāśanaṃ gavāṃ cānugamanam” iti, tat śūdrasvāmikaviṣayam | yat tu manunoktam,
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavān pibet |
kṛtavāpo vased goṣṭhe carmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ ||
[246]         caturthakālam aśnīyād akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam |
gomūtreṇa caret snānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ ||
divānugacchet tā gās tu tiṣṭhann ūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet |
śuśrūṣitvā namaskṛtya rātrau vīrāsanī bhavet ||
tiṣṭhantīṣv anutiṣṭheta vrajantīṣv apy anuvrajet |
āsīnāsv api cāsīno niyato vītamatsaraḥ ||
āturām abhiyuktāṃ vā cauravyāghrādibhir bhayaiḥ |
patitāṃ paṅkamagnāṃ vā sarvaprāṇair vimokṣayet ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gṛhe kṣetre khale ‘pi vā |
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayet pibantaṃ caiva vatsakam ||
[247]        anena vidhinā yas tu goghno gā anugacchati |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhir māsair vyapohati ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || (MDh 11.109–17)
iti, etat brāhmaṇaparigṛhītāyā buddhipūrvakavadhaviṣaye draṣṭavyam | yat tv āṅgirasoktam,
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno bhuñjīta yāvakam |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ ṣaṣṭhe kāle ‘sya bhojanam ||
kṛtavāpo vaset goṣṭhe carmaṇā tena saṃvṛtaḥ |
dvau māsau snānam apy asya gomūtreṇa vidhīyate ||
pādaśaucakriyā kāryā adbhiḥ kurvīta kevalam |
vrativad dhārayed daṇḍaṃ samantrāṃ caiva mekhalām ||
gāṃ caivānuvrajen nityaṃ raja āsāṃ sadā pibet |
tiṣṭhantīṣu ca tiṣṭhec ca vrajantīṣv apy anuvrajet ||
śuśrūṣitvā namaskṛtvā rātrau vīrāsanī bhavet |
gomatīṃ ca japed vidyām oṃkāraṃ vedam eva ca ||
āturām abhiśastāṃ ca rogavyāghrādibhir bhayaiḥ |
[248]        patitāṃ paṅkalagnāṃ ca sarvaprāṇair vimokṣayet ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
na kurvītātmanas trāṇaṃ gor akṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gehe kṣetre ‘tha vā khale |
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayet pibantaṃ caiva vatsakam ||
anena vidhinā goghno yas tu gā anugacchati |
sa tadvadhakṛtaṃ dvābhyāṃ māsābhyāṃ tu vyapohati ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāś ca dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet ||
iti, tat brāhmaṇasvāmikakāmakṛtagovadha eva tāruṇyādiguṇavadviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | atra japyāṃ gomatīṃ vidyāṃ yama āha |
gomatīṃ kīrtayiṣyāmi sarvapāpapraṇāśinīm |
tāṃ tu me vadato viprāḥ śṛṇudhvaṃ susamāhitāḥ ||
gāvaḥ surabhayo nityaṃ gāvo guggulagandhikāḥ |
gāvaḥ pratiṣṭhā bhūtānāṃ gāvaḥ svastyayanaṃ mahat ||
annam eva paraṃ gāvo devānāṃ havir uttamam |
pāvanaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ rakṣanti ca vahanti ca ||
haviṣā mantrapūtena tarpayanty amarān divi |
ṛṣīṇām agnihotreṣu gāvo homaprayojikāḥ ||
[249]        sarveṣām eva bhūtānāṃ gāvaḥ śaraṇam uttamam |
gāvaḥ pavitraṃ paramaṃ gāvo maṅgalam uttamam ||
gāvaḥ svargasya sopānaṃ gāvo dhanyāḥ sanātanāḥ |
namo gobhyaḥ śrīmatībhyaḥ saurabheyībhya eva ca ||
namo brahmasutābhyaś ca pavitrābhyo namo namaḥ | iti | (YamSm 5.2–8)
yat tu hārītenoktam: “goghnas tvacam ūrdhvalāṅgūlāṃ paridhāya gā anutiṣṭheta tiṣṭhamānāsu | āsīnāsv āsīnaḥ | saṃveśamānāsu saṃviśet | saṃvītāṅgo vītamatsaraḥ | śītavātoṣṇātivarṣeṣu nātmanas trāṇaṃ kuryāt gor akṛtvā | corapaṅkavyāghrādibhyo bhayebhyo mocayet | evaṃ sucaritaprāyaścitto vṛṣabham aikādaśaṃ gāś ca datvā trayodaśamāsena pūto bhavati” iti (HārSm 23.40–47), yad api vasiṣṭhena: “gāṃ ced dhanyāt tasyāś carmaṇārdreṇa pariveṣṭitaḥ [250] ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vātiṣṭhet ṛṣabhavaihatau ca” iti (VaDh 21.18–22) — dadyād iti śeṣaḥ — , devalenāpi: “goghnaḥ ṣaṇmāsān taccarmaparivṛto gogrāsāhāro govrajanivāsī gobhir eva saha caran mucyate” iti, – etat tritayam api svanasthabrahmaṇagovadhe kāmakṛte draṣṭavyam |
yat tu kātyāyanenoktam,
goghnas taccarmasaṃvīto vased goṣṭhe ‘tha vā punaḥ |
gāś cānugacchet satataṃ mauñjī vīrāsanādibhiḥ ||
[251]        varṣaśītātapakleśavahnipaṅkabhayadibhiḥ |
mokṣayet sarvayatnena pūyate vatsarais tribhiḥ || (KSm 2.9.15)
iti, yac ca śaṅkhenoktam,
pādaṃ tu śūdrahatyāyām udakyāgamane tathā |
govadhe ca tathā kuryāt parastrīgamane tathā || (ŚaṅSm 13.32)
iti, yac ca gautamo ‘pi: “gāṃ ca vaiśyavat” iti (GDh 22.23), iti – etat tritayam api kāmakṛte savanasthabrāhmaṇagovadhe draṣṭavyam |
yat tu yamena,
gosahasraṃ śataṃ vāpi dadyāt sucaritavrataḥ |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || (YamSm 5.19)
iti, – tat savasthaśrotriyaguṇavadgovadhe draṣṭavyam | ata eva pracetāḥ: “strīgarbhiṇīgogarbhiṇī-bālavadheṣu bhrūṇahā bhavati” iti | anayaiva diśā vyāsādivacaneṣv api prāyaścittagauravalāghave gogataguṇāguṇau parīkṣya viṣayavyavasthā yojanīyā | vacanāni tu pradarśyante | tatra vyāsaḥ |
[252]        māsaṃ vāpi pibed goghnaḥ śuci gomūtrayāvakam |
dadyād vā daśakaṃ caiva dhenūnāṃ brāhmaṇāya vā || iti | (VyāSm 6.18)
paiṭhīnasiḥ: “goṣṭhe vasan yavāgūṃ prasṛtitaṇḍulaśṛtāṃ māsaṃ bhuktvā gobhyaḥ priyaṃ kurvāṇo goghnaḥ śudhyati” iti | viśvāmitraḥ |
kṛcchrāṃś ca caturaḥ kuryād govadhe buddhipūrvake |
amatyā tu dvayaṃ kuryāt tadardhaṃ vṛddhabālayoḥ || iti |
jābāliḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ caren māsaṃ gohatyā ced akāmataḥ |
gohato go’nugāmī syād gopradānena śudhyati || iti |
jātūkarṇyaḥ |
gāṃ ced akāmato hanyāt brāhmaṇānujñayā tataḥ |
prājāpatyatrayaṃ kṛtvā sparśayed brāhmaṇāya gāḥ || iti |
vyāghraḥ |
gāṃ ced dhanyān naro ‘kāmāt paṇasaptaśataṃ tathā |
śrotriyāya daridrāya dadyāt śuddhyartham ātmanaḥ || iti |
hārītaḥ |
akāmād vā sakāmād vā goghnasya puruṣasya vai |
[253]        brāhmaṇān jñāpayet tatra yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ ||
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret |
payośī pañcagavyāśī goṣṭhaśāyī jitendriyaḥ ||
gavām anugamaṃ caiva tasmāc ca niyatavrataḥ |
adbhir yas tarpayen nityaṃ śodhayed duṣkṛtaṃ smaran ||
ekarātraṃ jale sthitvā trirātraṃ tadanantaram |
godānaṃ sarvaśaktyā ca brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prayacchati ||
sa kilbiṣān samutthāya mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti | (HārSm 2.38–42)
caturviṃśatimate |
goghnaḥ pāvanaṃ samyag gadato me nibodhata |
śastrādibhiś ca hatvā gāṃ mānavaṃ vratam ācaret ||
rodhādinā cāṅgrasam āpastamboktam eva vā |
[254]        pādaṃ cared rodhavadhe kṛcchrārdhaṃ bandhapātane ||
ativāhya ca pādonaṃ kṛcchram ajñānatāḍane |
garbhiṇīṃ kapilāṃ dogdhrīṃ homadhenuṃ ca suvratām ||
rodhādinā ghātayitvā dviguṇaṃ vratam ācaret |
ativṛddhām atikṛśām atibālāṃ ca rogiṇīm ||
hatvā pūrvavidhānena cared ardhaṃ vrataṃ dvijaḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayet śaktyā dadyād dhema tilāṃś ca gāḥ ||
hemagotiladānena naraḥ pāpāt pramucyate || iti || 42 ||
gāvaḥ ślādhyās trilokyām iti nigamagiras tādṛśīnāṃ vadhe yat
prāyaścittaṃ praṇītaṃ kim api gaṇayatas tac ca sāmānyagītam |
bhāradvājānvavāyaḥ prathitasamudayaḥ svoditair eva sūktair
adhyāyasyāṣṭamasya vyatanuta vivṛt śāśvatīṃ mādhavāryaḥ ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirāja-vaidikamārgapravartaka-parameśvara-
śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhavāmātyasya
kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ aṣṭamo ‘dhyāyaḥ || 8 ||
atha navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
[255]
aṣṭamādhyāye govadhasya sāmānyaprāyaścittam upavarṇitam | atha navamādhyāye rodhādinimittaviśeṣān upajīvya prāyaścittaviśeṣā abhidhīyante | tatra
ekapādaṃ cared rodhe dvau pādau bandhane caret | (ParSm 9.3)
iti vakṣyati |
tayoḥ purastād apavādam ādyaślokena darśayati |
gavāṃ saṃrakṣaṇārthāya na duṣyed rodhabandhayoḥ |
tadvadhaṃ tu na taṃ vidyāt kāmākāmakṛtaṃ tathā || 1 || iti |
vraje śālāyāṃ vā gāḥ praveśya tṛṇādibhakṣaṇapratibandho rodhaḥ | gale pāśena dohādyarthaṃ pādeṣu rajvā vā niyantraṇaṃ bandhaḥ | tau ca rodhabandhau dvividhau hitāv ahitau ca | rogagrastāyā gor apathyabhakṣaṇanivāraṇaṃ hito rodhaḥ | kṣuditāyā goḥ tṛṇādibhakṣaṇanivāraṇam ahito rodhaḥ | evaṃ bandhasyāpi dvaividhyam | [256] tatra hitayor bandharodhayoḥ pratyavāyajanakatvābhāvān na prāyaścittanimittatvam | etad eva aṅgirā āha |
sāyaṃ saṅgopanārthaṃ tu na duṣyed rodhabandhayoḥ | iti | (AṅSm 2.29)
|| 1 ||
laguṣādibhir nipātane prāyaścittam āha |
daṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ yad anyena prahārād yadi pātayet |
prāyaścittaṃ tadā proktaṃ dviguṇaṃ govadhe caret || 2 || iti |
daṇḍasya lakṣaṇam “aṅguṣṭhamātaḥ” (9.10) ity anena vakṣyati | tasmād daṇḍād ūrdhvam adhikapramāṇenety arthaḥ | anyena laguḍādinā pātayen mārayet | akāmakṛte govadhe yat prāyaścittaṃ pūrvādhyāye proktaṃ tad evātra dviguṇībhūtam ācaret || 2 ||
idānīṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣān abhidhātum anyāni vadhanimittāny upanyasyati |
rodhabandhanayoktrāṇi ghātaś ceti caturvidham | iti |
vadhanimittam iti śeṣaḥ | caturvidhān rodhādivadhān svayam eva spaṣṭīkariṣyati |
teṣu bandheṣu prāyaścittāny āha |
[257]        ekapādaṃ cared rodhe dvau pādau bandhane caret || 3 ||
yoktreṣu pādahīnaṃ syāt caret sarvaṃ nipātane | iti |
pūrvādhyāyoktaprājāpatyavratasya pādabhedena rodhādivadheṣu prāyaścittāny avagantavyāni |
nanu – etad aṅgirovacanena virudhyate | aṅgirā hi māsadvayakṛcchraṃ govratatvenābhidhāya rodhādivadheṣu tatpādādīni vidhatte:
pādaṃ caret rodhavadhe dvau pādau bandhane caret |
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāt caret sarvaṃ nipātane || iti | (AṅSm 2.31)
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, guṇavadaguṇavadviṣayatvena aṅgiraḥ-parāśaravacanayor vyavasthāpanīyatvāt | anyathā aṅgirasaḥ saṃvarta-āpastambābhyāṃ saha virodhaḥ kathaṃ parihriyeta | saṃvartaḥ pakṣakṛcchraṃ govadhavratam abhidhāyaitad āha |
prāyaścittasya pādaṃ tu virodhe vratam ācaret |
dvau pādau bandhane caiva pādonaṃ yojane tathā ||
pāṣāṇair loṣṭakair daṇḍais tathā śastrādibhir naraḥ |
nipātya saṃcaret sarvaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye || (SaṃSm 139–40)
[258] iti | āpastambo ‘pi dinacatuṣṭayakṛtyaṃ pādatvena prakṛtyedam āha |
ekabhaktena naktena tathaivāyācitena ca |
upavāsena caikena pādakṛcchraḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
ekapādaṃ cared rodhe dvau pādau bandhane caret |
yojane pādahīnaṃ syāt caret sarvaṃ nipātane || iti | (ĀpSm 1.16)
nanu – evam api pūrvāparavirodho duṣpariharaḥ | pūrvādhyāye hy akāmakṛte ṣoḍaśarātrakṛcchro ‘bhihitaḥ | atra tu kāmakṛte rodhavadhe catūrātro ‘bhidhīyate |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, atra prāmādikasya bālavatsādivadhasya prāyaścittam idam iti pariharaṇīyatvāt |
evaṃ tarhi catūrātrāṣṭarātradvādaśarātraṣoḍaśarātrarūpāṇāṃ caturvidhānāṃ kṛcchrāṇāṃ bālavatsādiviṣayāṇāṃ pūrvam abhihitattvāt punar uktam – iti cet,
na, rodhanīyasya vatsādeḥ śūdrasvāmikatvādilakṣaṇasya hīnanimittasyātra kalpanīyatvāt | tasmān na ko ’pi virodhaḥ || 3 ||
vadhanimittasya rodhasya svarūpam āha |
govāṭe vā gṛhe vāpi durge vāpy asamasthale || 4 ||
[259]        nadīṣv atha samudreṣu tv anyeṣu ca nadīmukhe |
dagdhadeśe mṛtā gāvaḥ stambhanād rodha ucyate || 5 || iti |
durgāṇi parvatasaṃkīrṇamārgādīni | asamasthalaṃ nimnonnatapradeśaḥ | anyeṣu vāpītaḍāgādiṣu nadīmukhaṃ samudranadīsaṃgamapradeśaḥ | dagdhadeśo dāvāgnyādinā dagdhaḥ sann uṣṇabhasmākrāntapradeśaḥ | yathokteṣu sthāneṣu stambhanād gāvo mṛtā bhavanti | atas tādṛśas stambhanam atra rodha ucyate | īdṛśarodhanimittaṃ prāyaścittam āpastamba āha |
kāntāreṣv atha durgeṣu gṛhadāhabhayeṣu ca |
yadi tatra vipattiḥ syāt pāda eko vidhīyate || iti | (ĀpSm 1.19)
|| 45 ||
vadhanimittabandhanasya svarūpam āha |
yoktradāmakaḍoraiś ca kaṇṭhābharaṇabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
gṛhe vāpi vane vāpi baddhā syād gaur mṛtā yadi || 6 ||
tad eva bandhanaṃ vidyāt kāmākāmakṛtaṃ ca tat |
[260] iti | yoktraṃ pāśaḥ śakaṭayugacchidrasaṃbaddhaḥ | dāmakaṃ dhenubandhanahetuḥ pāśaḥ | ḍoraṃ rajjumātram | kaṇṭhābharaṇaṃ ghaṇṭākiṅkiṇyādi | bhūṣaṇaṃ lalāṭādau nibaddhavarāṭakādi | īdṛśaiḥ sādhanair baddhā gaur gṛhe sthitā vā caraṇāya araṇye gatā vā yadi mriyate tadā maraṇanimittaṃ yoktrādikam atra bandhanam ucyate | goṣu yoktrādiyojanaṃ kāmakṛtaṃ bandhanam, gośālāyāṃ sthāpiteṣu yoktrādiṣu yadi gāvo lagnāḥ syus tadādīm akāmakṛtaṃ bandhanam iti dvividhaṃ bandhanaṃ vijñeyam | etac ca saprāyaścittam āpastamba āha |
kaṇṭhābharaṇadoṣeṇa gaur vā yatra vipadyate |
cared ardhaṃ vrataṃ tatra bhūṣaṇārthaṃ hi tatkṛtam || iti | (ĀpSm 1.17)
|| 6 ||
vadhanimittasya yoktrasya svarūpam āha |
hale vā śakaṭe paṅktau pṛṣṭhe vā pīḍito naraiḥ || 7 ||
gopatir mṛtyum āpnoti yoktro bhavati tadvadhaḥ || iti |
paṅktiḥ gale meḍhībandhanam | gopatir balīvardaḥ | sa ca sthāvareṇa rogeṇa vā voḍhum aśakto halarathapaṅktigoṇībhāreṣu vāhito mriyeta tadā tanmaraṇanimittavahananiyojanaṃ yoktram [261] ity ucyate | ata eva āpastambaḥ |
damane dāmane rodhe saṅghāte caiva yojane |
nastaḥ sakalapāśair vā mṛte pādonam ācaret || iti | (ĀpSm 1.18)
aśikṣitavalīvardasya voḍhuṃ śikṣaṇaṃ damanam | dāmnā pāśena saṃpādito dāmanaḥ | rodho galanirodhaḥ | saṃghātaḥ samūhaḥ | paṅktiḥ pūrvoktā | tatra yojanaṃ bandhanam | nastaḥ nāsikāyāṃ doraprakṣepaḥ | sakalapāśā itare sarve bandhanaviśeṣāḥ || 7 ||
vadhanimittasya ghātasya svarūpam āha |
mattaḥ pramatta unmattaś cetano vāpy acetanaḥ || 8 ||
kāmākāmakṛtakrodho daṇḍair hanyād athopalaiḥ |
prahṛtā vā mṛtā vāpi tad dhi hetur nipātane || 9 || iti |
matto dhanādinā dṛptaḥ | pramatto madyapānādinā paravaśaḥ | unmatto vyādhyādinā vibhrāntaḥ | cetano lokavyavahāradakṣaḥ | acetano mugdhaḥ | pātayiṣyāmīti buddhiḥ kāmaḥ | vyarthaceṣṭāmātram akāmaḥ | tābhyām utpāditaḥ krodho yasyāsau daṇḍādibhir yadi hanyāt tadā maraṇam antareṇa kevalaṃ prahṛtā syāt tatra kāmakṛtasaṃprahāro ghāta ity ucyate | akāmakṛtaṃ tu māraṇaṃ ghātaḥ | daṇḍopalābhyāṃ śastrādīny upalakṣyante | ata eva āpastambaḥ |
[262]        pāṣāṇair laguḍaiḥ pāśaiḥ śastreṇānyena vā balāt |
nipātayati yo gā vai sarvam eva samācaret || iti | (ĀpSm 1.19)
|| 8–9||
“daṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ yad anyena” (9.2) ity atra daṇḍād adhikena laguḍādinā kṛtasya prahārasya prāyaścittanimittatvaṃ vadatārthād daṇḍaprahārasya nirdoṣatvam aṅgīkṛtam | tatra ko ‘sau daṇḍa ity ākāṅkṣāyām āha |
aṅguṣṭhamātrasthūlas tu bāhumātraḥ pramāṇataḥ |
ārdras tu sapalāśaś ca daṇḍa ity abhidhīyate || 10 || iti |
aṅguṣṭhamātra ity anena tatodhikaṃ sthaulyaṃ vyāvartyate | na svalpam, alpasya vadhahetutvābhāvāt | bāhumātra ity anenāpy adhikadairghyavyāvṛttiḥ, atidīrgheṇa prahāre mukhapartyantaprasāraṇena cakṣurādyupaghātaprasaṅgāt | ārdratvaṃ sapalāśatvaṃ ca vedanānutpattyartham | uktalakṣaṇena daṇḍena bhītim evotpadayet | na tu vedanām ity āśayaḥ |
|| 10 ||
yathoktadaṇḍaprahāreṇa rugnasya goḥ pātamūrcchanayoḥ satoḥ pratyavāyaprāyaścittaprasaktāv apavadati
mūrcchitaḥ patito ‘vāpi daṇḍenābhihataḥ sa tu |
utthitas tu yadā gacchet pañca sapta daśaiva vā || 11 ||
[263]        grāsaṃ vā yadi gṛhṇīyāt toyaṃ vāpi pibed yati |
pūrvaṃ vyādhyupasṛṣṭaś cet prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 12 || iti |
yas tu gauś ciraṃ vyādhipīḍito ‘tyantakṛśaś cāraṇārthaṃ yathoktadaṇḍenābhihataḥ san patito mūrcchito vāpi bhavet sa punar utthāya kānicit padāni gacchet tarhi prahartuḥ pratyavāyo nāsti | athotthātum aśaktas tadāpi tṛṇe bhakṣite nāsti pratyavāyaḥ | bhakṣayitum aśakto yadi jalaṃ pibet tadāpi nāsti pratyavāyaḥ | utthānatṛṇabhakṣaṇajalapānāny akṛtvā mūrcchānantaram eva mriyeta cet tadā prahartā pratyavaitīty arthād avagantavyam || 11–12 ||
gogarbhasya vadhe prāyaścittam āha |
piṇḍasthe pādam ekaṃ tu dvau pādau garbhasaṃmite |
pādonaṃ vratam uddiṣṭaṃ hatvā garbham acetanam || 13 || iti |
catasro garbhāvasthāḥ | piṇḍaḥ saṃmito ‘cetanaḥ sacetanaś ceti | garbhāśaye patitaṃ vīryaṃ kalalabudbudādikrameṇārdhamāse piṇḍabhāvam āpadyate | sa eva māsamātreṇa dārḍhyam āpadya saṃmita ity ucyate | saṃyaṅmitaḥ parivṛto dṛḍhībhūtaḥ saṃmitaḥ | sa ca saptame māsi prāṇavāyusaṃcārarūpāṃ cetanām āpadyate | māsād ūrdhvsm ā saptamān māsād acetan ity ucyate | tadūrdhaṃ patito mṛto bhavet | atra piṇḍasthe garbhe prājāpatyasya pādam ācaret | saṃmitavadhe ardhakṛcchram | acetanavadhe pādatrayam | sacetanasya caturthasya vadhe prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyati || 13 ||
[264]
pādakṛcchrādīnāṃ caturṇām aṅgabhūtāṃś caturo vapanaviśeṣān āha |
pāde ‘ṅgaromavapanaṃ dvipāde śmaśruṇo ‘pi ca |
tripāde tu śikhāvarjaṃ saśikhaṃ tu nipātane || 14 || iti |
romavapanena nakhanikṛntanam apy upalakṣyate | nitarāṃ pātanaṃ nipātanam | saṃpūrṇasya sacetanasya garbhasya vadhe ity arthaḥ | idaṃ caturvidhaṃ vapanaṃ śaṅkho ‘py āha |
romāṇi prathame pāde dvipāde śmaśruvāpanam |
pādahīne śikhāvarjaṃ saśikhaṃ tu nipātane || iti |
nakhanikṛntanaṃ āpastamba āha |
pāde tu nakharomāṇi dvipāde śmaśruvāpanam |
tripāde tu śikhā dhāryā caturthe saśikhaṃ smṛtam || iti | (ĀpSm 1.33)
|| 14 ||
pavanaviśeṣavac caturdhā vyavasthitān dānaviśeṣān āha |
pāde vastrayugaṃ caiva dvipāde kāṃsyabhājanam |
tripāde govṛṣaṃ dadyāc caturthe godvayaṃ smṛtam || 15 ||
[265] iti | govṛṣo balīvardaḥ | dvipāde kāṃsyabhājanam aśaktasya | śaktasya tv ardhasuvarṇam | tad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
kṛcchrapāde vastradānaṃ kṛcchrārdhe tv ardhakāñcanam |
pādanyūne tu gāṃ dadyāt pūrṇe gomithunaṃ smṛtam || iti | (ŚaṅSm 18.29)
|| 15 ||
caturthāvasthasya sacetanasya garbhasya vadhe prāyaścittam āha |
niṣpannasarvagātras tu dṛśyate vā sacetanaḥ |
aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃpūrṇo dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ caret || 16 || iti |
aṣṭame māse sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇo bhavati | prasavapratyāsannakāle sacetana itas tataḥ saṃcarann upalakṣayituṃ śakyate | aṅgāni śiraādīni | pratyaṅgāni grīvādīni | tathā ca smaryate |
aṅgāny atra śiro hastau vakṣaḥ pārśvau kaṭī tathā |
pādāv iti ṣaḍ uktāni śāstravidbhiḥ samāsataḥ ||
pratyaṅgāni punar grīvā bāhū pṛṣṭhaṃ tathodaram |
ūrū jaṅghe ṣaḍ ity āhus tathā romanakhaṃ param || iti |
tair aṅgaiḥ pratyaṅgaiś ca sampūrṇe garbhe nihate prājāpatyadvayam ācaret | tad etac caturvidhaṃ gogarbhavadhaprāyaścittaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘pi darśitam |
pāda utpannamātre tu dvau pādau dṛḍhatāṃ gate |
pādonaṃ vratam uddiṣṭaṃ hatvā garbham acetanam ||
[266]        aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃpūrṇe garbhe cetaḥsamanvite |
dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ tatra eṣā goghnasya niṣkṛtiḥ || iti |
āpastambo ‘pi |
pādo ‘saṃpannamātre tu dvau pādau ca ghane caret |
pādonaṃ vratam ācaṣṭe hatvā garbham acetanam ||
aṅgapratyaṅgasaṃpūrṇe cetanena samanvite |
dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ tatra eṣā goghnasya niṣkṛtiḥ || iti | (ĀpSm 10.35–36)
|| 16 ||
avayavaviśeṣeṣu prāyaścittaviśeṣān āha |
pāṣāṇenātha daṇḍena gāvo yenābhighātitāḥ |
śṛṅgabhaṅge caret pādaṃ dvau pādau netraghātane || 17 ||
[267]        lāṅgūle pādakṛcchraṃ tu dvau pādāv asthibhañjane |
tripādaṃ caiva karṇe tu caret sarvaṃ nipātane || 18 || iti |
yena puṃsā pāṣāṇādisādhanair gavām upaghātaḥ kriyate sa pumān gomaraṇābhāve ‘py avayavabhaṅgapratyavāyanivṛttaye nirdiṣṭaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣam ācaret | yat tv aṅgirasā darśitam |
asthibhaṅgaṃ gavāṃ kṛtvā lāṅgūlacchedanaṃ tathā |
pātanaṃ caikaśṛṅgasya māsārdhaṃ yāvakaṃ pibet || iti | (AṅSm 2.28)
āpastambo ‘pi |
asthibhaṅgaṃ gavāṃ kṛtvā śṛṅgabhaṅgam athāpi vā |
tvakchede pucchanāśe vā māsārdhaṃ yāvakaṃ pibet || (ĀpSm 1.28)
iti, – tat śṛṅgādibahvavayavabhaṅgaviṣayaṃ guṇavadgoviṣayaṃ vā | yad apy aṅgirasoktam,
śṛṅgabhaṅge ‘sthibhaṅge ca carmanirmocane ‘pi vā |
daśarātraṃ pibed vajraṃ svasthāpi yadi gaur bhavet || (AṅSm 30)
[267] iti | vajraṃ kṣīrādi | etac cāśaktaviṣayam || 17–18 ||
nanu – varṇitam aṅgabhaṅgādiprāyaścittaṃ na paryāptam, aṅgabhaṅgādeḥ kiyatāpi kālena maraṇaparyavasāyitvasaṃbhavāt | vadhaprāyaścittasyaiva tatrocitatvāt – ity āśaṅkyāha |
śṛṅgabhaṅge ‘sthibhaṅge ca kaṭibhaṅge tathaiva ca |
yadi jīvati ṣaṇmāsān prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 19 || iti |
śṛṅgabhaṅgānantaraṃ ṣaṇmāsamadhye yadi mriyate tadānīṃ śṛṅgādibhaṅgasya tannimittatvaśaṅkayā vadhaprāyaścittaṃ kartavyaṃ bhavet | yadā punaḥ padābhibhaṅge ṣaṭsu māseṣu jīvati tadā vadhaprāyaścittaṃ na vidyate | kiṃ tu bhaṅgādinimittam eva prāyaścittam anuṣṭheyam || 19 ||
vraṇabhaṅgādau na kevalaṃ prāyaścittācaraṇam | kiṃ tu taccikitsāpi kartavyety āha |
vraṇabhaṅge ca kartavyaḥ snehābhyaṅgas tu pāṇinā |
yavasaś copahartavyo yāvad dṛḍhabalo bhavet || 20 || iti |
vraṇaś ca bhaṅgaś ca vraṇabhaṅgau | tvakcchedo vraṇaḥ | śṛṅgādyasthicchedo bhaṅgaḥ | snehābhyaṅga ity auṣadhaprakṣepasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | yavasas tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ | tena bhakṣyaṃ sarvam upalakṣyate | cikitsāṃ śaṅkho ‘py āha |
[269]        yavasaś copahartavyo yāvad roheta tadvraṇaḥ |
saṃpūrṇe dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt tataḥ pāpāt pramucyate || iti | (ŚaṅSm 11.19)
|| 20 ||
“yāvad dṛḍhabalaḥ” iti cikitsāyā avadhir abhihitaḥ | tam evānūdyānantarakartavyam āha |
yāvat saṃpūrṇasarvāṅgas tāvat taṃ poṣayen naraḥ |
gorūpaṃ brāhmaṇasyāgre namaskṛtvā visarjayet || 21 || iti |
aṅgasaṃpūrtiparyantaṃ poṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā puṣṭaṃ tadgosvarūpaṃ kṣamāpaṇabuddhyā namaskṛtya cikitsāyā uparamet || 21 ||
samyak cikitsāyāṃ kṛtāyām api prahāradārḍhyāt puṣṭyabhāve kiṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
yady asaṃpūrṇasarvāṅgo hīnadeho bhavet tadā |
goghātakasya tasyārdhaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || 22 || iti |
goghātakasya yat prāyaścittaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tadardham anutiṣṭhet || 22 ||
idānīṃ cauryādidoṣeṇa kāmakṛtagovadhe nimittaviśeṣān upajīvya prāyaścittaviśeṣān āha |
[270]        kāṣṭhaloṣṭakapāṣāṇaiḥ śastreṇaivoddhato balāt |
vyāpādayati yo gāṃ tu tasya śuddhiṃ vinirdiśet || 23 ||
caret sāntapanaṃ kāṣṭhe prājāpatyaṃ tu loṣṭake |
taptakṛcchraṃ tu pāṣāṇe śastre caivātikṛcchrakam || 24 || iti |
prājāpatyataptakṛcchrayoḥ svarūpaṃ pūrvam evopadarśitam | sāntapanātikṛcchrayoḥ svarūpaṃ tūpariṣṭād vakṣyate |
na ca – kāṣṭhādīnāṃ vadhanimittatve samāne sati prāyaścittavaiṣamyam ayuktam iti śaṅkanīyam | śāstraikagamye ‘rthe yuktibhir upālambhāsaṃbhavāt | evaṃ tarhi sarvatre nyāyavicāro nirarthaka iti cet,
na, vacanānāṃ parasparavirodhe sati nyāyasya nirṇāyakatvāt || 23–24 ||
yathokeṣu caturṣu krameṇa caturvidhān dakṣiṇāviśeṣān āha |
pañca sāntapane gāvaḥ prājāpatye tathā trayaḥ |
taptakṛcchre bhavanty aṣṭāv atikṛcchre trayodaśa || 25 || iti |
gāvo deyā iti śeṣaḥ | trayas tisraḥ || 25 ||
pramāpitasya goḥ parakīyatve tasmai gosvāmine gāṃ datvā paścāt yathoktaṃ vratam ācared ity āha |
pramāpaṇe prāṇabhṛtāṃ dadyāt tat pratirūpakam |
tasyānurūpaṃ mūlyaṃ vā dadyād ity abravīn manuḥ || 26 ||
[271] iti | mahiṣāśvādiṣv api svāmine dānaṃ samānam iti vivakṣayā prāṇabhṛtām ity uktam | tad etat pratirūpadānaṃ manur abravīt | tathā ca mānavaṃ vacanam |
yo yasya hiṃsyāt dravyāṇi jñānato ‘jñānato ‘pi vā |
sa tasyotpādayet tuṣṭiṃ rājñe dadyāc ca tatsamam || iti || 26 ||
“rodhabandhanayoktrāṇi” ity atra rodhabandhanayoḥ pratyavāyahetutvaṃ varṇitam | idānīṃ tasyāpavādam āha |
anyatrāṅkanalakṣmabhyāṃ vāhane mocane tathā |
sāyaṃ saṅgopanārthaṃ ca na duṣyed rodhabandhayoḥ || 27 || iti |
vṛṣotsargādau dāhādinā sthiracihnakaraṇam aṅkanam | tatraiva gomayaharidrādinā tātkālikachinakaraṇaṃ lakṣma | tābhyām anyatra uktacihnarahiteṣu balīvardeṣv ity arthaḥ | vāhanaṃ goṇībhārādyāropaṇam | mocanaṃ tasyaiva bhārasyāvaropaṇam | [272] tatrobhayatraiva balīvardaśarīre yady api vyathā jāyate tathāpi pratyavāyo nāsti | cihnadvayarahiteṣu balīvardeṣu vāhanamocanayoḥ śāstreṇaivāṅgīkṛtatvāt | cihnopeteṣu vṛṣeṣu vāhanaṃ na śāstrīyam, vāhananivṛttijñāpanāyaiva cihnakaraṇāt | tathā rātrau saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ rodhabandhanayoḥ kṛtayor api nāsti pratyavāyaḥ || 27 ||
punar api vyavasthitān prāyaścittaviśeṣān vidhātuṃ catvāri nimittāny upanyasyati |
atidāhe ‘tivāhe ca nāsikābhedane tathā |
nadīparvatasaṃcāre prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || 28 || iti |
rogacikitsārtham aṅknārthaṃ vā yāvad dahanam apekṣitaṃ tāvato ‘dhikadahanam atidāhaḥ | yāvantaṃ bhāram akleśena voḍhuṃ śaknoti tāvato ‘py adhikasya bhārasyāropaṇam ativāhaḥ | rajjusthityarthaṃ nāsikāyāṃ chidrakaraṇaṃ bhedanam | pātavadhasaṃbhāvanopetayor durgamayor nadīparvatayoḥ preraṇaṃ nadīparvatasaṃcāraḥ || 28 ||
teṣu caturṣu nimitteṣu krameṇa prāyaścittāny āha |
atidāhe caret pādaṃ dvau pādau vāhane caret |
nāsikye pādahīnaṃ tu caret sarvaṃ nipātane || 29 || iti |
durgam anadīparvatasamcāreṇa pramādāt patitvā maraṇaṃ nipātanam | “atidohe” iti vā pāṭhaḥ | tasmin pakṣe vatsārthapayo ‘navaśeṣya kṛtsnakṣīradoham atidohaḥ | tatra vatsāpaghātapratyavayanimittaṃ [273] dheṇūpaghātanimittaṃ vā prāyaścittam | yat tu hārītenātidāhātivāhanādau cāndrāyaṇam uktam,
nāsābhedanadāheṣu karṇacchedanabandhane |
atidāhātidohābhyāṃ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || (HārSm 19.10)
iti, tad bahunimittasaṃnipātaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam || 29 ||
yas tu gaur gṛhe baddhaḥ pramādād gṛhadāhe sati yadi mriyeta tadā kiṃ prāyaścittam ity ata āha |
dahanāt tu vipadyeta anaḍvān yoktrayantritaḥ |
uktaṃ parāśareṇaiva hy ekapādaṃ yathāvidhi || 30 || iti |
vṛddhaparāśareṇa yathāvidhi uktam ekaṃ pādakṛcchraṃ caret iti śeṣaḥ | etac ca āpastambo ‘py āha |
kāntāreṣv atha vā durge gṛhadāhe khaleṣu ca |
yadi tv iha vipattiḥ syāt pāda eko vidhīyate || iti | (ĀpSm 3.16) || 30 ||
buddhisamādhānāyoktāny anuktāni ca nimittāni saṃgṛhṇāti |
rodhanaṃ bandhanaṃ caiva bhāraḥ praharaṇaṃ tathā |
durgapreraṇayoktraṃ ca nimittāni vadhasya ṣaṭ || 31 ||
[274] iti | tatra rodhabandhanayoktrāṇi pūrvam eva vyākhyātāni | bhāro ‘tivāhanam | praharaṇaṃ śastrādibhis tāḍanam | durgapreraṇaṃ prauḍhabhāraṃ vāhayitvā atyunnataparvatāgre nayanam || 31 ||
pūrvaṃ gṛhe baddhasya paśor dāhanimititaṃ prāyaścittam abhihitam | idānīṃ tasyaiva maraṇe vadhanimittaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
bandhapāśasuguptāṅgo mriyate yadi gopaśuḥ |
bhavane tasya pāpī syāt prāyaścittārdham arhati || 32 || iti |
bandhahetuḥ pāśo bandhapāśaḥ | tena suguptāni rodhitāni galapādādīny aṅgāni yasyāsau bandhapāśasuguptāṅgaḥ | sa ced bhavane mriyeta tadā kṛcchrārdham ācared iti | yady api pūrvatra "dvau pādau bandhane caret" ity anenedaṃ prāyaścittam upavarṇitaṃ tathāpy atra bandhanayogyāyogyarajjuvivekacikīrṣayā tasyaiva punar anuvāda ity adoṣaḥ | yad vā pūrvoktasyaiva prāyaścittasya vihitarajjubandhaviṣayatvam anena samarpyate | niṣiddharajjubandhane tu saṃpūrṇaṃ kṛcchram avagantavyam || 32 ||
tatra tāvad varjyān rajjuviśeṣān darśayati |
na nārikelair na ca śāṇavālaiḥ
na vāpi mauñjair na ca valkaśṛṅkhalaiḥ |
[275]        gāvo na nibandhanīyā
badhvāpi tiṣṭhet paraśuṃ gṛhītvā || 33 || iti |
valkāni vaṃśādijanitāni | śṛṅkhalāny ayastāmrādinirmitāni nārikelādibhir gāvo na na bandhanīyāḥ | yadi dāmāntarāsaṃbhavād etair dāmabhir badhyeran tadā tannimittopadravaprasaṅge sahasā chettuṃ hastena paraśuṃ gṛhītvā sāvadhānas tiṣṭhet | nārikelādibhiḥ kārpāsatantvādijanyā dṛḍhā rajjavaḥ sarvā upalakṣyante | ata eva aṅgirāḥ |
na nārikelair na ca phālekena
na mauñjinā nāpi ca valkalena |
etair anaḍvān na hi bandhanīyo
badhvāpi tiṣṭhet paraśuṃ gṛhītvā || iti | (AṅSm 3.5)
phālakaṃ kārpāsajanyam || 33 ||
upādeyān rajjuviśeṣān āha |
kuśaiḥ kāśaiś ca badhnīyād gopaśuṃ dakṣiṇāmukham | iti |
yathā pāśā anupadravakāriṇo vivakṣitāḥ tathāsthānam api nimnonnatādi doṣarahitaṃ vivakṣitam | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
kuśaiḥ kāśaiś ca badhnīyāt sthāne doṣavivarjite | iti | (VySm 5.17)
[276]
nanu – nārikelādipāśānām upadravakāritvasaṃbhāvanayā pratiṣedhaḥ kṛtaḥ | sa ca doṣaḥ kuśādipāśeṣv api samānaḥ | teṣāṃ sahasā troṭane ‘pi gṛhadāhādau palāyanamānānāṃ gavāṃ galagatapāśeṣu lagnenāgninopadravasaṃbhavād ity ata āha |
pāśalagnāgnidagdhāsu prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 34 || iti |
kuśādimayapāśānāṃ śīghraṃ bhasmībhāvāt sakṛt saṃtāpamātraṃ saṃpadyate | na tu prāṇāntika upadravaḥ | ato na tatra pratyavāyaḥ || 34 ||
tatrāpi kenacit prakāreṇopadravasaṃbhāvanām udbhāvya tatrocitaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
yadi tatra bhavet kāṣṭhaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
japitvā pāvanīṃ devīṃ mucyate tatra kilbiṣāt || 35 || iti |
galagate pāśe dahyamāne yadi kuśamūlādirūpaṃ kiṃcit kāṣṭhaṃ tasmin pāśē ‘vatiṣṭhet tadā tadulmukasaṃsparśād īṣattvagdāho bhavet | tatra gāyatrījapenaiva śuddhiḥ | pāvamānīyam iti pāṭhe pavamānasūktaṃ japitveti vyākhyeyam || 35 ||
[277]
yasmin deśe maraṇāntikaḥ pramādaḥ saṃbhāvitaḥ tatra gāvo na preṣaṇīyāḥ | yadi buddhimāndyāt preṣayet tadā tatra maraṇe prāyaścittaṃ caraṇīyam ity āha |
prerayan kūpavāpīṣu vṛkṣacchedeṣu pātayan |
gavāśaneṣu vikrīṇaṃs tataḥ prāpnoti govadham || 36 || iti |
yasmin cāraṇapradeśe jīrṇakūpavāpyādayo vidyante tatra cāraṇārthaṃ preṣito yadi mriyate tadā, yatra prauḍhā vṛkṣāś chidyante tatra preṣitaḥ prauḍhaśākhāpātena maraṇaṃ prāpnoti, gomāṃsabhakṣiṇo mlecchā gavāśanāḥ teṣu vikrītā gaus tair māryate atas teṣu triṣv api sthāneṣu prerako govrataṃ caret || 36 ||
udvṛṣabhayajñādāv upaghāte pādatrayaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
ārādhitas tu yaḥ kaścit bhinnakakṣo yadā bhavet |
śravaṇaṃ hṛdayaṃ magno vā kūpasaṅkaṭe || 37 ||
kūpād utkramaṇe caiva bhagno vā grīvapādayoḥ |
sa eva mriyate tatra trīn pādāṃs tu samācaret || 38 || iti |
udvṛṣabhayajñe hi balīvardam ārādhya dhāvayanti | tatrātitvarayā dhāvataḥ kadācit kakṣo bhidyate | kenacid upaghātena karṇe vā hṛdaye vā bhedo bhavati | kūpasaṅkaṭaṃ nāma saṃkīrṇatṛṇatvaṃ jalapūrṇatvaṃ ca | tatra vā magno bhavati | tādṛśo kūpe patitasya kathaṃcid utkramaṇe grīvādir bhagno bhavati | tenaiva ca nimittena yadi mriyeta, tadā pādatrayakṛcchram ācaret | avāritaḥ iti vā pāṭhaḥ | tasmin pakṣe cāraṇārthaṃ preṣayatā puruṣeṇa saṃbhāvitapramādakhalebhyo nivāraṇīyatvāt anivāraṇe yathoktaṃ prāyaścittam iti vyākhyeyam || 37–38 ||
yatra tṛṣārttā gāvaḥ svayam evāgādhakūpādiṣu praviśya vipadyante svāmī tu tat na jānāti, tatra svaminaḥ prayavāya ity āha |
kūpakhāte taṭābandhe nadībandhe prapāsu ca |
pānīyeṣu vipannānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 39 || iti |
agādhaḥ kūpaḥ kūpakhātaḥ | taṭena nadītīreṇa ā samatād (?) badhyate iti taṭābandhaḥ prauḍhataṭākaḥ | nadībandhaḥ setuḥ | gharmakāle gavām udakapānārthaṃ nirmitāḥ prauḍhāḥ pāṣāṇādidroṇyaḥ prapā | teṣu pātuṃ praviśya mṛtāsv api na svāminaḥ prāyaścittaṃ vidyate || 39 ||
evaṃ tarhi tādṛśakūpādinirmātuḥ pratyavāyo ‘stīty āśaṅkyāha |
kūpakhāte taṭākhāte dīrghīkhāte (?) tathaiva ca |
anyeṣu dharmakhāteṣu prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 40 || iti |
khananaṃ khātaḥ | dīrghīvāpyaḥ | anyaśabdena jaladroṇaukulyādaya ucyante | eteṣāṃ dharmārthaṃ nirmitatvāt kathaṃcit tatra patitvā mṛtāsv api goṣu na nirmātuḥ pratyavāyo ‘sti || 40 ||
kūpakhātādivad veśmakhātādāv api pratyavāyābhāvam āśaṅkya dharmārthatvābhāvād asti pratyavāya ity āha |
veśmadvāre nivāseṣu yo naraḥ khātam icchati |
svakāryagṛhakhāteṣu prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || 41 || iti |
veśmadvāraṃ gṛhapraveśanirgamamārgaḥ | nivāsāḥ śālāvrajādīni gavāṃ sthānāni | tatra yo naraḥ khanati, tasya puruṣasya khātapātena gomaraṇe pūrvoktaṃ pādatrayaṃ prāyaścittaṃ [280] vinirdiśet | tathānyatrāpi gṛhabhittivedyādikaraṇārtham aṅganopavanādiṣu gosaṃcārasaṃbhāvanāvatsu pradeśeṣu kṛtāḥ khātāḥ gṛhakhātāḥ, svena kṛtāḥ svakāryāḥ, svakāryāś ca te gṛhakhātāś ca svakāryagṛhakhātāḥ | teṣu govipattau pādatrayaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || 41 ||
gṛhakhātādivad gṛhe sarpādinā vadhena pratyavāyaprāptāv apavadati |
niśi bandhaniruddheṣu sarpavyāghrahateṣu ca |
agnividyudvipannānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 42 || iti |
rātrau saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ pāśabandhena niruddhā gāvaḥ sarpavyāghrādibhir yadi hanyeran tadā svāminaḥ pratyavāyo nāsti | agnir grāmadāhaḥ | vidyud aśaniḥ | tābhyāṃ vipannānāṃ na pratyavāyahetutvam |
nanu – “dahanāt tu vipadyante” (7.13) ity atra prāyaścittam upavarṇitam | iha tu tat nirākriyate iti pūrvāparavirodha iti cet |
na, nirākaraṇasyāśaktaviṣayatvāt | satyāṃ śaktāv upekṣāyāṃ pūrvoktaṃ prāyaścittam | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
[281]        jalaughapalvale magnā meghavidyuddhatāpi vā |
śvabhre vā patitākasmāt śvāpadenāpi bhakṣitā ||
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ gosvāmī vratam uttamam |
śītavātāhatā vā syād udbandhanamṛtāpi vā ||
śūnyāgāre upekṣāyāṃ prājāpatyaṃ vinirdiśet || iti | (VyāSm 3.39–41)
idaṃ tu kāryāntaravirahe saty upekṣāyāṃ veditavyam | kāryāntaravyagratayopekṣāyāṃ tv ardham |
palvalaughamṛgavyāghraśvāpadādinipātane |
śvabhraprapātasarpādyair mṛte kṛcchrārdham ācaret ||
apālanāt tu kṛcchraṃ syāt śūnyāgāra upaplave || (ViDh 21.13)
iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt || 42 ||
vidyuddāhāder iva grāmaghātāder api na pratyavāyahetutvam ity āha |
grāmaghāte śaraugheṇa veśmabhaṅgān nipātane |
atipṛṣṭihatānāṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 43 || iti |
śatrusainyena grāme hanyamāne sati tadantarvartino gāvaḥ śaraugheṇa nipātyante | tathā stambhasaṃśadiśaithilyena [282] gṛhe bhagne sati tadantarvartināṃ gavāṃ nipāto bhavati | tathā budhaśukrasāmīpyādinā pravṛttāyām ativṛṣṭau śītavātapīḍitā gāvo mriyante tatra tatsvāminaḥ pratyavāyo nāsti || 43 ||
saṃgrāmāder api na pratyavāyahetutvam ity āha |
saṃgrāme prahatānāṃ ca ye dagdhā veśmakeṣu ca |
dāvāgnigrāmaghāteṣu prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 44 || iti |
senayor ubhayor yuddhe sati tatra prahatā gāvo mriyante | skandhāvāranirmitāni tṛṇamayāni veśmakānīty ucyante | teṣu vairibhir dahyamāneṣu tatra gāvo mriyante | tathā kvacid dāvāgninā araṇyasamīpavartino grāmā dagdhā bhavanti tatra gāvā upahanyante | na caiteṣu viṣayeṣu svāmī pratyavāyaṃ prāpnoti || 44 ||
gavāṃ hite pravṛttasya prāmādikaṃ gomaraṇaṃ na pratyavāyajanakam ity āha |
yantritā gauś cikitsārthaṃ mūḍhagarbhavimocane |
yatne kṛte vipadyeta prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 45 || iti |
vraṇarogādau dāhacchedādiciktsārthaṃ rajjvādinā yantritā gauḥ pramādād vipadyeta | tathā yonidvāri samāgatasya [283] mṛtasya garbhasya nirgamanārthaṃ tadākarṣaṇādiprayatne kriyamāṇe kathaṃcid gaur mriyet tatra hitam ācaran puruṣo na pratyavaiti | etad eva saṃvarta āha |
yantraṇe goś cikitsārthe mūḍhagarbhavimocane |
yadi tatra vipattiḥ syāt na sa pāpena lipyate ||
auṣadhaṃ sneham āhāraṃ dadyād gobrāhmaṇeṣu ca |
dīyamāne vipattiś cen na sa pāpena lipyate ||
dāhacchedaśirābhedaiḥ prayogair upakurvatām |
dvijānāṃ gohitārthaṃ ca prāyaścittam na vidyate || iti | (SaṃSm 137–39)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
kriyamāṇopakāre tu mṛte vipre na pātakam |
vipāke govṛṣāṇāṃ tu bheṣajāgnikriyāsu ca || iti | (YDh 3.284)
aṅgirā api |
auṣadhaṃ sneham āhāraṃ dadyād gobrāhmaṇeṣu yaḥ |
vipāke tu vipattiḥ syāt na sa doṣeṇa lipyate || iti | (AṅSm 1.28) || 45 ||
[284]
rodhabandhanābhyāṃ govipattau prāyaścittaṃ pūrvam upavarṇitam | yadā bahava ekena vyāpāreṇa vipadyante tadā kiṃ vyāpāraikyād ekaṃ vrataṃ syāt | kiṃ vā prahatagosaṃkhyāyā vratāvṛttiḥ – iti viciktsāyām idam āha |
vyāpannānāṃ bahūnāṃ ca bandhane rodhane ‘pi vā |
bhiṣaṅmithyopacāre ca prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet || 46 || iti |
pūrvoktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dviguṇam ācared ity āśayaḥ | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
vyāpannānāṃ bahūnāṃ ca bandhane rodhane ‘pi vā |
dviguṇaṃ govratasyāsya prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye || iti | (SaṃSm 135|
āpastambo ‘pi |
vipannānāṃ bahūnāṃ tu bandhane rodhane ‘pi vā |
bhiṣaṅmithyāprayoktā ca dviguṇaṃ govrataṃ caret || (ĀpSm 3.19)
[285] iti | ayathāśāstraṃ cikitsanaṃ bhiṣaṅmithyāprayogaḥ iti || 46 ||
satyāṃ śaktāv upekṣakasya pratyavāyo ‘stīty uktam | na kevalaṃ svāmina evaitat | kiṃ tv anyeṣām apīty āha |
govṛṣāṇāṃ vipattau ca yāvantaḥ prekṣakā janāḥ |
anivārayatāṃ teṣāṃ sarceṣāṃ pātakaṃ bhavet || 47 || iti |
paṅkamajjanādir vipattiḥ | tatra ye kecin mārge gacchanto vipannāṃ gāṃ vilokya yathāśakti pratīkāraṃ na kurvanti teṣāṃ sarveṣām upekṣakāṇāṃ pratyavāyo bhavati | prekṣakā ity anenānumantrādayaḥ sarve ‘py upalakṣyante | ata eva paiṭhīnasiḥ |
hantā mantopadeṣṭā ca tathā saṃpratipādakaḥ |
protsāhakaḥ sahāyaś ca tathā mārgānudeśakaḥ ||
āśrayaḥ śastradātā ca bhaktadātā vikarmiṇām |
upekṣakaḥ śaktimāṃś ced doṣavaktānumodakaḥ ||
akāryakāriṇas teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet | iti || 47 ||
“vyāpannānām” (9.46) ity atra vadhyabahutvaṃ ghātakaikatvaṃ [286] copajīvya prāyaścittam uktam | idānīṃ vadhyaikatve hantṛbahutve ca kathaṃ – ity āśaṅkyāha |
eko hato yair bahubhiḥ sametair
na jñāyate yasya hato ‘bhighātāt |
divyena teṣām upalabhya hantā
nivartanīyo nṛpasaṃniyuktaiḥ || 48 || iti |
yatra bahūnāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ prahārair eko gaur hato bhavati tatra sarveṣāṃ prahārakāṇāṃ vedanāhetutve saty api yadīyaḥ prahāraḥ prāṇaviyoganimittam iti na jñāyate | atas teṣāṃ bahūnāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ madhye prāṇaviyogakāriprahārakartāyam iti puruṣaviśeṣas tulāgnyādidivyena niścitya rājapuruṣair hantṛsamūhāt sa pṛthak kartavyaḥ | pṛthak kṛtya ca govrataṃ cārayet || 48 ||
itareṣāṃ prahantṝṇāṃ prāyaścittam āha |
ekā ced bahubhiḥ kācid daivād vyāpāditā yadi |
pādaṃ pādaṃ tu hatyāyāś careyus te pṛthak pṛthak || 49 || iti |
gohatyāyā yad vratam abhihitaṃ tasya vratasya pādam [287] ekaikaḥ puruṣo ‘nutiṣṭhet | etac cākāmakāraviṣayam | daivād ity abhidhānāt | kāmakāre tu dviguṇam,
ekāṃ ghnatāṃ bahūnāṃ tu yathoktād dviguṇo damaḥ |
iti smṛtyantare ‘bhidhānāt || 49 ||
“sarpavyāghrahateṣu” (9.42) ity atra sarpādibhir gavi hate pratyavāyo nāstīy uktam | puruṣaprahāreṇa hate tv asti pratyavāyaḥ | yatr abhuṣu nimitteṣu saṃdehaḥ tatra vadhanimittaṃ kathaṃ niśceyam – ity āśaṅkya liṅgaviśeṣair ity abhipretya tāni liṅgāni pradarśayati |
hate tu rudhiraṃ dṛśyaṃ vyadhigrastaḥ kṛśo bhavet |
lālā bhavati daṣṭeṣu evam anveṣaṇaṃ bhavet || 50 || iti |
yatra rudhiraṃ dṛśyate tatra prahāro nimittam iti niścetavyam | kārśyamātropalambhe vyādhir mṛtihetuḥ | lālādarśane sarpadaṃśanaṃ nimittam | evan anyair api tatr tatrocitair liṅgair nimittasvarūpam anviṣya niścetavyam || 50 ||
[288]
śṛṅgāsthibhaṅgādau maraṇābhāve ‘pi prāyaścittaviśeṣam abhihitam | astv evaṃ bāhyāvayavabhaṅge | yadā tv antarāvayavabhaṅgo na vispaṣṭaḥ tadā katham – ity āśaṅkya tanniścayahetuṃ liṅgaviśeṣaṃ darśayati |
grāsārthaṃ codito vāpi adhvānaṃ naiva gacchati | iti |
evam anyair api liṅgaviśeṣais taṃ taṃ nimittaviśeṣam abhiniścitya tatra tatrocitaṃ prāyaścittam anuṣṭheyam ||
“daṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ yad anyena” (9.2) ityādinā granthasaṃdarbheṇa nimittaviśeṣān anūdya prāyaścittaviśeṣā abhihitāḥ | ye tu pūrvatrānuditā nimittaviśeṣāḥ ye ca liṅgair apy aniśceyāḥ śaṅkyamānāḥ teṣu sarveṣu sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ darśayati |
manunā caivam ekena sarvaśāstrāṇi jānatā || 51 ||
prāyaścittaṃ tu tenoktaṃ gāghnaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
yathā kalidharmarahasyaṃ tathā tatpratipādakāni vedaśāstrāṇi jānatā parāśareṇa mayā govadhaviśeṣāṇāṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣā abhihitāḥ | evaṃ manunāpy upapātakajātasya sarvasādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittaṃ govadham udāhṛtya upavarṇitam | kiṃ tat: “goghnaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret” iti | na cātra kenāpi muninā vipratipattuṃ śakyam ity abhipretya ekenety uktam | munimukhyeneti yāvat || 51 ||
pūrvādhyāye govadhasyetikartavyeṣu vapanam uktam: “saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā” (PārSm 8.31) iti | tathāsminn apy adhyāye ‘bhihitam:
pāde ‘ṅgaromavapanaṃ dvipāde śmaśruṇo ‘pi ca |
tripāde tu śikhāvarjaṃ saśikhaṃ tu nipātane || (PārSm 9.14)
[290] iti | tatra kenāpi nimittena vapanākaraṇe yathoktaṃ vrataṃ dviguṇam anuṣtheyam ity āha |
keśānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya dviguṇaṃ vratam ācaret || 52 ||
dviguṇe vrata ādiṣṭe dviguṇā dakṣiṇā bhavet | iti |
yatra yatra vratadvaiguṇyaṃ tatra tatra dakṣiṇādvaiguṇyam uktam || 52 ||
keśarakṣaṇaṃ na sarvaviṣayam | kiṃ tu rājādiviṣayam ity āha |
rājā vā rājaputro vā brāhmaṇo vā bahuśrutaḥ || 53 ||
akṛtvā vapanaṃ tasya prāyaścittaṃ vinirdiśet | iti |
keśarakṣaṇasya rājādiviṣayatvaṃ śaṅkho ‘py āha |
rājā vā rājaputro vā brāhmaṇo vā bahuśrutaḥ |
akṛtvā vapanaṃ teṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
[291]        keśānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya dviguṇaṃ vratam ācaret |
dviguṇe vrata ācīrṇe dakṣiṇā dviguṇā bhavet || iti | (ŚaṅSm 17.39)
vapanadviguṇavratayor anyatarasyāpy abhāve cīrṇam api prāyaścittaṃ na pāpanivartakaṃ bhavet | tad āha āpastambaḥ |
rājā vā rājaputro vā brāhmaṇo vā bahuśrutaḥ |
yas tu no vapanaṃ kuryāt prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
keśānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya dviguṇaṃ vratam ācaret |
dviguṇe tu vrate cīrṇe dviguṇā dakṣiṇā bhavet ||
yasya na dviguṇaṃ dānaṃ keśāṃś ca parirakṣataḥ |
tat pāpaṃ tasya tiṣṭheta vaktā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
yat kiṃcit kriyate pāpaṃ sarvaṃ keśeṣu tiṣṭhati |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena saśikhaṃ kārayet dvijaḥ || iti | (ĀpSm 7.4–7)
|| 53 ||
strīṇāṃ tu vapane viśeṣam āha |
sarvān keśān samuddhṛtya chedayed aṅguladvayam || 54 ||
evaṃ nārīkumārīṇāṃ śiraso muṇḍanaṃ smṛtam | iti |
nārīgrahaṇenaiva kumārīṇām api grahaṇe siddhe pṛthagupādānaṃ vidhavāsu vapanāṅgīkāradyotanārtham | nātra nārīśabdaḥ strīmātravācī | kiṃ tu sabhartṛkastrīvācī | kumāryas tu vivāharahitāḥ | tathā ca sabhartṛkāṇāṃ vivāharahiānāṃ caitad uktaṃ [292] bhavet | tato vidhavānāṃ sarvātmanā mauṇḍyam aṅgīkṛtaṃ bhavati || 54 ||
pūrvokteṣu vratāṅgeṣu kānicid aṅgāni strīṇām apavadati |
na striyāḥ keśavapanaṃ na dūre śayanāśanam || 55 ||
na ca goṣṭhe vased gātrau na divā gā anuvrajet |
nadīṣu saṃgame caiva araṇyeṣu viśeṣataḥ || 56 ||
na strīṇām ajijaṃ vāso vratam eva samācaret | iti |
