na striyāḥ keśavapanaṃ śiraso muṇḍanaṃ ity anayor nānyonyavirodhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ, niṣedhasya kṛtsnakeśaviṣayatvāt | keśāgrakartanam eva muṇḍanaṃ pūrvatra vihitam | ato na virodhaḥ | “saṃviśantīṣu saṃviśet” (PārSm 8.34) ity anena gṛhamadhye grāmād bahir dūre vā yatra gāvas tatra śayanaṃ prāptam | tannirākaraṇāya na dūre śayanam ity uktam | rātrau [293] goṣṭhāvasthānaṃ divā gavām anuvrajanaṃ ca puṃsām eva | na striyāḥ | nadīsaṃgameṣv araṇyeṣu ca vāso yady api na sākṣāt pūrvam uktaḥ, tathāpi “go’nugāmī” (YDh 3.263) iti śāstrād arthataḥ prasaktaṃ tadubhayaṃ nivāryate | “carmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛtaḥ” (MDh 11.108) ityādiśāstrataḥ prasaktam ajinavāsstvaṃ niṣidhyate | vratam evety evakāreṇa yathoktāṅganivṛttir evānūdyate || 55–56 ||
vapanādīnām iva trisaṃdhyasnānādīnām api niṣedhaprasaktāv āha |
trisaṃdhyaṃ snānam ity uktaṃ surāṇām arcanaṃ tathā || 57 ||
bandhumadhye vrataṃ tāsāṃ kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādikam | iti |
“trisaṃdhyam avagāhanam” (6.9) iti yat pūrvam uktaṃ tat strīṇām ap samānam | surārcanaṃ namaskārādi | kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādikaṃ yad vrataṃ tad yoṣito bandhumadhye ‘nutiṣṭheyuḥ | na puruṣā iva vijane deśe || 57 ||
niyamāntaram āha |
gṛheṣu satataṃ tiṣṭhet śucir niyamam ācaret || 58 || iti |
prakrāntasya vratasya samāptiparyantaṃ gṛhasīmno na bahir gacchet || 58 ||
[294]
adhyāyadvayena yat prāyaścittaṃ prapañcitaṃ tasyānanuṣṭhāne dvividhaṃ divyaṃ bhaumaṃ ca narakaviśeṣaṃ darśayati |
iha yo govadhaṃ kṛtvā pracchādayitum icchati |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ kālasūtram asaṃśayam || 59 ||
vimukto narakāt tasmāt martyaloke prajāyate |
klībo duḥkhī ca kuṣṭhī ca saptajanmāni vai naraḥ || 60 || iti |
mitrādīnām utkocadānādinā janāpavādamātraparihāraḥ pracchādanam | ghorasya narakaviśeṣasya nāmadheyaṃ kālsūtram iti | so ‘yaṃ divyo narakaḥ | nairantaryeṇa saptajanmasu klībatvādidoṣayogo bhaumo narakaḥ || 59–60 ||
adhyāyadvaye pratipāditaṃ prakaraṇārtham upasaṃharati |
tasmāt prakāśayet pāpaṃ svadharmaṃ satataṃ caret |
strībālabhṛtyagovipreṣv atikopaṃ vivarjayet || 61 || iti |
yasmāt pracchādane dvividho narakaḥ proktaḥ tasmād apracchādya vedavidām agre prakāśya tair vinirdiṣṭaṃ svasyocitaṃ dharmaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣam ācaret | tata ūrdhvam īdṛśapāpānutpattaye strībālādiṣu koparahito bhavet || 61 ||
[295]
adhyāye navame parāśaramuniproktasmṛter manthane
rodhodbandhanatāḍanādyucitaṃ yat syāt gavāṃ pīḍanam |
karmaitasya viśeṣataḥ samuditā sarvottarā niṣkṛtiḥ
tadvyākhyām akarot kṛtī śubhadhiyāṃ bhāgyāmbudhir mādhavaḥ ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravartaka-
parameśvaraśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyaduhrandharasya
mādhavāmātyasya kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ
mādhavīyāyāṃ navamo ‘dhyāhaḥ || 9 ||
[296]
atha daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ
aṣṭamanavamādhyāyayor upapātakasya govadhasya sāmānyaviśeṣābhyāṃ prāyaścittāni prapañcitāni | athāgamyāgamanarūpopapātakasya prāyaścittaṃ daśamādhyāye ‘bhidhīyate |
tatra pratijñāpūrvakaṃ sāmānyaprāyaścittam āha |
cāturvarṇyeṣu sarveṣu hitāṃ vakṣyāmi niṣkṛtim |
agamyāgamane caiva śuddhyai cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 1 || iti |
caturṣu varṇeṣv anulomapratilomabhedenāvāntarajātibahutvam abhipretya sarveṣv ity uktam | pratyavāyaḥ niṣkriyate nivāryate vināśyate yayā prāyaścittyā sā prāyaścittiḥ niṣkṛtiḥ | tasyā anarthaphalanivārakatvād dhitatvam | “ṛtau bhāryām upeyāt” iti śāstrād gamyā svabhāryā | taditarā sarvāpi yoṣid [297] agamyā | tadgamane prāpte sati tacchuddhinimittaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ prāyaścittam ācaret || 1 ||
cāndrāyaṇasya lakṣaṇam āha |
ekaikaṃ hrāsayed grāsaṃ kṛṣṇe śukle ca vardhayet |
amāvāsyāyāṃ na bhuñjīta hy eṣa cāndrāyaṇo vidhiḥ || 2 || iti |
dvividhaṃ hi cāndrāyaṇam, yavamadhyaṃ pipīlikāmadhyaṃ ceti | yathā yavasya madhyaṃ sthūlam ubhāv antau sūkṣmau tathā śuklapratipadam ārabhya pratidinam ekaikagrāsavṛddhyā pūrṇamāyāṃ pañcadaśagrāsāḥ | kṛṣṇapratipadam ārabhya pratidinam ekaikagrāsahrāse saty amāvāsyāyām upavāsa iti madhyabhāgasthaulyāt yavamadhyatvam | yathā pipīlikānāṃ śiraḥpṛṣṭabhāgau sthūlau madhyaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tathā yasya ca cāndrāyaṇasya madhyame amāvāsyādine sarvagrāsahrāsaḥ tasya madhyamabhāgasaukṣmyāt papīlikāmadhyatvam | tad idaṃ pipīlikāmadhyam ācāryeṇopanyasyate | tathā hi kṛṣṇapratipadi vrataṃ saṃkalpya caturdaśa grāsān bhuñjīta | tato dvitīyām ārabhya pratidinam ekaikasya grāsasya hrāse sati amāvāsyāyām upavāsaḥ saṃpadyate | punaḥ śuklapratipadi grāsam ekam upakramya pratidinam ekaikagrāsavṛddhyā paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsāḥ saṃpadyante | sa eṣa paipīlikāmadhyasya cāndrāyaṇasyānuṣṭhānaprakaraḥ | tad idaṃ vasiṣṭho ‘py āha |
[299]        māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣādau grāsān adyāc caturdaśa |
grāsāpacayabhojī san pakṣaśeṣaṃ samāpayet ||
tathaiva śuklapakṣādau grasaṃ bhuñjīta cāparam |
grāsopacayabhojī san pakṣaśeṣaṃ samāpayet || iti | (VaDh 23.44–45)
cāndrāyaṇāṅgatvena triṣavaṇasnanaṃ kartavyam | tad āha manuḥ |
ekaikaṃ hrāsayet piṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇe śukle ca vardhayet |
upaspṛśaṃs triṣavaṇam etac cāndrāyaṇavratam || iti | (MDh 11.216)
vapanādītikartavyatāṃ tu gautama āha |
athātaś cāndrāyaṇam | tasyokto vidhiḥ kṛcchre | vapanavrataṃ caret | śvobhūtāṃ paurṇamāsīm upavaset | āpyāyasva sante payāṃsi navo nava iti caitābhis tarpaṇam | ājyahomo haviṣaś cānumantraṇam | upasthānaṃ candramasaḥ | yad devā devahelanam iti catasṛbhir ājyaṃ juhyāt | devakṛtasyeti cānte samidbhiḥ | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar mahar janas tapaḥ satyaṃ yaśaḥ śrīḥ kavir biḍaujojaḥ puruṣo dharmaḥ śiva ity etair grāsānumantraṇam | pratimantraṃ namasā namaḥ svāheti vā | sarvagrāsapramāṇam āsyāvikāreṇa | [300] carur bhaikṣasaktukaṇayāvaka-śākapayodadhighṛtamūlaphalodakāni havīṃṣyottarottaraṃ praśastāni | dvādaśaitāni | paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsān bhuktvaikāpacayenāparapakṣam aśnīyāt | amāvāsyāyām upoṣyaikopacayena pūrvaṃ pakṣam | vaparītam ekeṣām | eṣa cāndrāyaṇo māsaḥ || iti | (GDh 28.1–18)
ekeṣāṃ munīnāṃ mate pūrvoktāt paipīlikāmadhyapakṣād viparītaṃ yavamadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam | tac ca manur vispaṣṭam āha |
etam eva vidhiṃ kṛtsnam ācaret yavamadhyame |
śuklapakṣādiniyataś caraṃś cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam || iti | (MDh 11.217)
tad etac cāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ devalo py āha: “cāndrāyaṇaṃ dvividham | yavamadhyaṃ pipīlikāmadhyam iti | ekagrāsam amāvāsyādi yavamadhyam | pañcadaśagrāsaṃ paurṇamāsyādi pipīlikāmadhyam” iti | yamo ‘pi: “athātaś cāndrāyaṇakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tad yathā |
paurṇamāsyām upetas tu brāhmaṇaḥ susamāhitaḥ |
keśaśmaśrūṇi lomāni kakṣopassthaṃ ca vāpayet ||
prayato vidhivat kṛtvā gṛhṇīyāt susamāhitaḥ |
madhu māṃsaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ śuktāni parivarjayet ||
[301]        strīśūdrau nābhibhāṣeta satyavādī ca saṃyataḥ |
pālāśaṃ dhārayed daṇḍaṃ śucir mauñjīṃ ca mekhalām ||
yajñopavītī karakaṃ dhārayen niyataḥ śuciḥ |
śāṇī valkalacīrī vā mārgaṃ vā vāsa ucyate ||
sarvāṇy ahāni saṃtiṣṭhet snānamaunadhṛtavrataḥ |
snānāsanābhyāṃ vihared brahmacārī kṣapāśanaḥ ||
gomayena gocarmamātraṃ sthaṇḍilam upalipyābhyukṣya agniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya brāhmaṇaṃ dakṣiṇata upaveśyottarata udakapātraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya prāgudagagrān darbhān āstīryājyabhāgaṃ kṛtvā mahāvyāhṛtibhir ājyāhutīr juhuyāt | vratapataye ṛtaṃ satyaṃ prajāpatiṃ mitram agnīṣomau bṛhaspatiṃ yajeta |
etān evam arcayitvā mahāvyāhṛtayas tathā |
oṃkārapūrvam ākhyātās tathā sarvāḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||
ṛtaṃ satyaṃ ca gāyatrīm ity etāni sadā japet |
daśasāhasrikaṃ vāpi gāyatrīm āhnikaṃ japet ||
indraḥ śuddhā ṛcaś cāpaḥ praviśya manasā japet |
tatraiva gāyet sāmāni atha vā vyāhṛtīr japet ||
vṛkṣamūlaniketaḥ syād rātrau vīrāsanī bhavet |
ārdravāsāś caret kṛcchraṃ snātvā vastraṃ na pīḍayet ||
caruṃ vā śrapayen nityaṃ gobhyo niṣkrāntayāvakaiḥ |
[302]        pāyasaṃ śākam annaṃ vā bhaikṣaṃ vā vāgyataś caret ||
āyasaṃ taijasaṃ pātraṃ cakrotpannaṃ vivarjayet |
asurāṇāṃ hi tat pātram ayastaijasacakrajam ||
tathaivānyatamālābhe mṛdaṃ kurvīta vaidikīm |
yajñiyānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ teṣāṃ parṇaiḥ svayaṃ cyutaiḥ |
hasan na tu nirīkṣeta nābhibhāṣet parastriyam ||
godohamātraṃ tiṣṭheta na saptānāṃ paraṃ vrajet |
tadvidheṣu cared bhaikṣaṃ niyateṣu dvijātiṣu ||
agnayo yatra hūyante brahma vā yatra paṭhyate |
prayataḥ kṛtaśaucas tu bhaikṣam abhyukṣayet tataḥ ||
ādityābhimukho bhūtvā gurave tan nivedayet |
anujñātas tataḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛpūjanam ||
śākaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ mūlaṃ vā yadi vā phalam |
saṃpādayed yam āhāraṃ tenāgnau juhuyāt sadā ||
āhārārthaṃ dvije datvā tad annaṃ niyamaṃ śritaḥ |
grāsaṃ śeṣeṇa kurvīta yo ‘syāsyaṃ praviśet sukham ||
kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇaṃ vā grāsaṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ |
aṅgulyagre sthitaṃ vāpi gāyatryā cābhimantrayet ||
na tu vikhyāpayet piṇḍaṃ rasān nāsvādayet punaḥ |
na ninden na praśaṃseta svādv asvādu ca bhakṣayet ||
[303]        prākmukho nityam aśnīyād vāgyato ‘nnam akutsayan |
hrāso vṛddhir yathā some māsi māsi pradṛśyate ||
amāvāsyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vrataṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
grāsān vardhayate somaḥ pañca pañca ca pañca ca ||
ekaikaṃ vardhayet piṇḍaṃ śukle kṛṣṇe ca hrāsayet |
amāvāsyāṃ na bhuñjīta eṣa cāndrāyaṇo vidhiḥ ||
ekaikaṃ hrāsayet piṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇe śukle ca vardhayet |
etat pipīlikāmadhyaṃ cāndrāyaṇam udāhṛtam ||
vardhayet piṇḍam ekaikaṃ śukle kṛṣṇe ca hrāsayet |
etac cāndrāyaṇaṃ nāma yavamadhyaṃ prakīrtitam || iti | (YaSm 6.26–64)
punar api prakārāntareṇa cāndrāyaṇaṃ trividham, ṛṣicāndrāyaṇaṃ śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ yaticāndrāyaṇam iti | teṣāṃ svarūpaṃ yama āha |
trīṃs trīn piṇḍān samaśnīyān niyatātmā dṛḍhavrataḥ |
haviṣyānnasya vai māsam ṛṣicāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
caturaḥ prātar aśnīyāc caturaḥ sāyam eva ca |
piṇḍān etad dhi bālānāṃ śuśucāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
piṇḍān aṣṭau samaśnīyān māsaṃ madhandine ravau |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ hy etat sarvakalmaśanāśanam || (YaSm 6.21–23)
[304] viṣnur api pañcavidhaṃ cāndrāyaṇam āha: “atha cāndrāyaṇam | grāsān āsyāvikāram aśnīyāt | tāṃś candrakalābhivṛddhau krameṇa vardhayet | hānau ca hrasayet | amāvāsyāyāṃ ca nāśnīyāt | eṣa cāndrāyaṇo yavamadhyaḥ | pipīlikāmadhyo vā | yasyāmāvāsyā madhyā bhavati sa pipīlikāmadhyaḥ | yasya paurṇamāsī sa yavamadhyaḥ | aṣṭau grāsān pratidinam aśnīyāt sa yaticāndrāyaṇaḥ | sāyaṃ prātaś caturaś caturaḥ sa śiśucāndrāyaṇaḥ | yathā kathaṃcit ṣaṣṭhyonāṃ triśatīṃ māsenāśnīyāt sa sāmānyacāndrāyaṇaḥ” iti (ViDh 47.1–9) || 2 ||
cāndrāyaṇe ‘bhihitasya grāsasya parimāṇam āha |
kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇaṃ tu grāsaṃ vai parikalpayet |
anyathābhāvadoṣeṇa na dharmo na ca śudhyati || 3 ||
[305] iti | uktaparimāṇād adhikaparimāṇatvam anyathābhāvaḥ | tena jāto yo doṣo vratavaikalyaṃ tena doṣeṇa, dharmaś candralokaprāptihetus taporūpo vrataviśeṣaḥ sa na saṃpadyate | nāpy asya pāpāc chuddhir bhavati | cāndrāyaṇasādhyaṃ phalaṃ dvividham | puṇyalokaprāptiḥ pāpanivṛttiś ceti | tad ubhayam adhikaparimāṇagrāsān aśnato na sidhyati | cāndrāyaṇasya phaladvaividhyaṃ yama āha |
yat kiṃcit kurute pāpaṃ karmaṇā manasā girā |
dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ kṛtvā tasmāt pāpāt pramucyate ||
etāni vidhivat kṛtvā ṣaḍbhir māsair haviṣyabhuk |
vyapetakalmaṣo vipraś candrasyaiti salokatām || iti | (YaSm 6.19–20)
|| 3 ||
vratacaraṇāntaraṃ kartavyam āha |
prāyaścitte tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
godvayaṃ vastrayugmaṃ ca dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām || 4 || iti |
saṃkhyāviśeṣānupādānāt śaktyanusāreṇa brāhmaṇabhojanam iti veditavyam || 4 ||
agamyāgamanamātre prāyaścittam abhihitam | tadviśeṣeṣu prāyaścittāni vaktavyāni | tatrātyantanīcajātigamane prāyaścittam āha |
caṇḍālīṃ vā śvapākīṃ vā hy abhigacchati yo dvijaḥ |
trirātram upavāsitvā viprāṇām anuśāsanāt || 5 ||
[306]         saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyadvayaṃ caret |
godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt śuddhiṃ pārāśaro ‘bravīt || 6 || iti |
brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrāj jātā caṇḍālī | ārūṣhapatitāj jātā ca sagotrāj jātā vā | tad etat trividhaṃ caṇḍālatvaṃ yama āha |
ārūḍhapatitāj jāto brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrajaś ca yaḥ |
caṇḍālau tāv ubhau proktau sagotrād yaś ca jāyate || iti | (YaSm 6.3)
etat trividhacaṇḍālasantatau jātā strī caṇḍālī | kṣattograyor jātā strī śvapākī | tad āha manuḥ |
kṣattur jātas tathogrāyāṃ śvapāka iti kīrtitaḥ | iti | (MDh 10.19)
dvijaśabdo ‘tra brāhmaṇaparaḥ | kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ pṛthagvakṣyamāṇatvāt | upavāsitvā upavāsaṃ caritvety arthaḥ | ācārārthakvibantāt śabdaniṣpatteḥ || 5–6 ||
[307]
kṣatriyavaiśyayor dakṣiṇādhikyam āha |
kṣatriyo vātha vaiśyo vā caṇḍālīṃ gacchati yadi |
prājāpatyadvayaṃ kuryāt dadyād gomithunadvayam || 7 || iti |
spaṣṭam || 7 ||
śūdrasya tv alpaṃ vratam adhikā dakṣiṇety āha |
śvapākīṃ vātha caṇḍālīṃ śūdro vā yadi gacchati |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ caturgomithunaṃ dadet || 7 || iti |
nanu – smṛtyantareṣu caṇḍālīgamane prāyaścittāny anyathā smaryante | tatra kānicit ācāryoktāt prāyaścittāt nyūnāni, kānicid adhikāni | yathāha sumantuḥ: “mātṛṣvasṛpitṛṣvasṛsnuṣābhaginī-bhāgineyīgocaṇḍālīnām abhigamane taptakṛcchram” iti |
tad etad akāmataḥ pravṛttasya retaḥsekāt prāṅnivṛttau draṣṭavyam | yat tv aṅgirasoktam,
patitāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
māsopvāsaṃ kūrvīta cāndrāyaṇam athāpi vā ||
[308] iti | tatra cāndrāyaṇaṃ kāmataḥ pravṛttasya retaḥsekāt prāṅganivṛttasyāśaktasyāvagantavyam | śaktasya tu māsopavāsaḥ | godvayadakṣiṇāyuktasya prājāpatyadvayasya mūlavacanoktasya pratyāmnāyakalpanādvāreṇa māsopavāsasamānatvād ayam eva viṣayaḥ | yad api śaṅkhenoktam |
akāmatas tu yo vipraś caṇḍālīṃ yadi gacchati |
taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyeta prājāpatyadvayena vā ||
kāmatas tu yadā vipraś caṇḍālīṃ yadi sevate |
cāndrāyaṇena śudhyeta prājāpatyadvayena ca || iti | (ŚaṅSm 17.19–20)
etanmūlavacanena samānaviṣayam | yamas tu viṣayavyavasthāpūrvakaṃ pakṣadvayam āha |
caṇḍālapulkasānāṃ tu bhuktvā gatvā ca yoṣitam |
kṛcchrābdam ācaret jñānād ajñānād aindavadvayam || iti | (YaSm 6.7)
etac cobhayaṃ retaḥsekaparyantasakṛdgamanaviṣaye | yat tu gautamenoktam: “antyāvasāyinīgamane kṛcchrābdaḥ | amatyā dvādaśarātram” (GDh 24.38–39) [309] iti, tatrābdakṛcchro yamoktasamānaviṣayaḥ | dvādaśarātraṃ tu sumantuproktataptakṛcchrasamāna-viṣayam | yad apy aṅgirasoktam,
antyajānāṃ tu gamane bhojane ca pramāpaṇe |
parākeṇa viśuddhiḥ syād bhagavān aṅgirābravīt || (ĀṅSm 5.19)
iti, tad api taptakṛcchrasamānaviṣayam | yad api vasiṣṭhenoktam: “dvādaśarātram abbhakṣo yo dvādaśarātram upavaset | aśvamedhāvabhṛthaṃ vā gacchet | etenaiva cāṇḍālīvyavāyo vyākhyātaḥ” (VaDh 23.38–41) iti, etad api bṛhadyamoktacāndrāyaṇadvayasamānaviṣayam | yac ca saṃvartenoktam,
yaś caṇḍālīṃ dvijo gacchet kathaṃcit kāmamohitaḥ |
tribhiḥ kṛcchrair viśudhyeta prājāpatyānupūrvakaiḥ ||
iti — prājāpatyataptakṛcchātikṛcchrāṇi pājāpatyānupūrvakāṇi — etac cāndrāyaṇadvayena samānaviṣayam | yad api manunoktam,
[310]        yat karoty ekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanād dvijaḥ |
tad bhaikṣabhug japan nityaṃ tribhir varṣair vyapohati || (MDh 11.178)
iti — vṛṣalī caṇḍālī —, tathā ca smṛtyantare,
caṇḍālī bandhakī veśyā rajaḥsthā yā ca kanyakā |
ūḍhā ca samagotreṇa vṛṣalyaḥ pañca kīrtitāḥ ||
iti, tad ekadinābhyāsaviṣayam | yad api manunoktam,
retaḥsekaḥ svayonyāsu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ viduḥ || (MDh 11.58)
iti, yājñavalkyenāpi,
sakhibhāryākumārīṣu svayoniṣv antyajāsu ca |
sagotrāsu sutastrīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ smṛtam || (YDh 3.231)
[311] iti, etac ca pakṣābhyāsaviṣayam | yac ca manunoktam,
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv antyajāsu ca || (MDh 11.170)
iti, etac ca māsābhyāsaviṣayam | yac ca yamenoktam,
retaḥ siktvā kumārīṣu caṇḍālīṣv antyajāsu ca |
sapiṇḍāpatyadāreṣu prāṇatyāgo vidhīyate || (YaSm 5.41)
iti, etac ca saṃvatsarābhyāsviṣayam || 8 ||
atha mātṛgamanaprāyaścittam ucyate
yady apy etan nopapātakam, tasyātipātakeṣu pāṭhāt, tathāpy agamyāgamanāvāntarabhedavad atrābhidhānam | atrājñānakṛte mātrādigamane prāyaścittam āha |
mātaraṃ yadi gacchet tu bhaginīṃ svasutāṃ tathā |
etās tu mohito gatvā trīṇi kṛcchrāṇi saṃcaret || 8 ||
cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ kuryāt śiśnacchedena śudhyati | iti |
mātaraṃ jananīm, itarāsāṃ mātṝṇām “pitṛdārān samāruhya” (YaSm 10.21) [312] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | bhaginy ekodarā, tasyā eva mukhyatvāt | tathā svasuteti savarṇāyāṃ bhāryāyām utpannā | tatra trīṇi prāyaścittāni | prājāpatyatrayam ekam, cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ dvitīyam, śiśnacchedas tṛtīyam | etac ca maithunaprakārabhedaviṣayatayā yojanīyam | maithunaṃ cāṣṭavidham,
smaraṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ keliḥ prekṣaṇaṃ guhyabhāṣaṇm |
saṃkalpo ‘dhyavāyaś ca kriyānivṛttir eva ca ||
etan maithunam aṣṭāṅgaṃ vadanti brahmavādinaḥ |
viparītaṃ brahmacaryam etad evāṣṭalakṣaṇam || (BraVaiPu 2.19.26–27)
iti smaraṇāt | tatrādyaṃ vratam alpatvāt apravartakasmaraṇādipañcavidhāparādhaviṣayam | dvitīyaṃ tu pūrvasmāt gurutvāt pravartakasaṃkalpādhyavasāyaviṣayam | tṛtīyaṃ tv atimahatvāt kriyānirvṛttiviṣāyam |
nanu – mohita ity abhidhānād akāmakṛtaviṣayam idam | tathā ca saṃkalpādhyavasāyau tatra na saṃbhavataḥ iti cet |
maivam – madāndhakārādau māteyam ity ajñātvā gaviṣyāmy etām iti saṃkalpādhyavasāyasaṃbhavāt | yad vā māteyam iti jñāte ‘pi pratyavāyagauravam ajñātvā pravṛttasya saṃkalpādhyavasāyasaṃbhavāt | na ca – mātari bhaginyāṃ ca samānaṃ prāyaścittam ayuktam iti vācyam, [313] bhaginyām āvṛttiviṣayatvasya kalpanīyatvāt | śiśnacchedottarakālakartavyaṃ manur āha |
svayaṃ vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāv utkṛtyādhāya cāñcalau |
nairṛtīṃ diśam ātiṣṭhed ā nipātād ajihmagaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.104)
uśanā api: “khaṭvāṅgadhārī gurutalpagaḥ savṛṣaṇaṃ śiśnam utkṛtya nairṛtīṃ diśam añjalinādāya vrajed ā nipātāt pūto bhavatīti” (UṣSm 1.19) iti | śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “kṣureṇa śiśnavṛṣaṇāv utkṛtyādāyāvekṣamāṇo vrajet” iti | evaṃ gacchan yatra kuḍyādinā pratibadhyate tatraivāmaraṇāntaṃ tiṣṭhet | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “savṛṣaṇaṃ śiśnam utkṛtyāñjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇābhimukho gacchet | yatraiva prahitas tatraiva tiṣṭhed ā pralayam” iti (VaDh 20.13) | ajñānakṛtābhyāsājñānakṛtaskṛjjanaīgamane ‘pi | pūrvoktād adhikakleśotpādakaṃ vrataviśeṣaṃ vasiṣṭha āha: “niṣkālako ghṛtābhyakto gomayenāgninā pādaprabhṛtyātmānam avadāhayan pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate” (VaDh 20.14) [314] iti | ke śirasy avasthitā alakāḥ keśāḥ kārakāḥ | nirgatāḥ kālakā yasmād asau niṣkālakaḥ muṇḍitaśirā ity arthaḥ |
nanu – asya vadhasya kāmakṛtasakṛdgamanaviṣyatve tato ‘bhyadhikakleśapradasya vadhāntarasyābhāvāt kāmakṛtābhyāse prāyaścittaṃ na syād iti cet |
maivam, gomayasyaiveṣadārdratvātiśuṣkatvādibhedena cirakṣipradāhino vadhabhedasya kalpanīyatvāt | yat tu śiśnacchedādivadhāt nyūnaprayāsasādhyaṃ sūrmyādiliṅganādimaraṇam anyair munibhiḥ pradarśitaṃ tatrobhayecchānyatarecchādibhedena viṣayavyavasthā kalpanīyā | tad āha manuḥ |
gurutalpy abhibhāṣyainas tapte svapyād ayomaye |
sūmīṃ jvalantīṃ vāśliṣya mṛtynā ca viśudhyati ||
svayaṃ vā śiśnavṛṣaṇāv utkṛtyādhāya cāñjalau |
naiṛtīṃ diśam ātiṣṭhed ā nipātād ajihmagaḥ || (MDh 11.103–04)
[315] iti | yamo ‘pi |
gurudārāvagamanaṃ kṛtvā mohena vai dvijaḥ |
jvalantīm āyasīṃ śayyāṃ saṃviśed gurutalpagaḥ ||
sūrmīṃ jvalantīṃ vāśliṣya mṛtynā sa viśudhyati | iti | (YaSm 5.43–44)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
tapte ‘yaḥśayane sārdham āyasyā yoṣitā svapet |
gṛhītvotkṛtya vṛṣaṇau naiṛtyāṃ votsṛjet tanum || iti | (YaSm 3.259)
aṅgirā api |
gurutalpī śilāṃ taptām āyasīṃ vā striyaṃ viśet |
utkṛtya vṛṣaṇau vāp dhārayed añjalau svayam ||
maraṇāya tadā padbhyāṃ pravrajed diśam uttarām |
śarīrasya vimokṣeṇa mucyate karmaṇo ‘śubhāt ||
khyāpayan gurutalpī vā tapte caivāyase svapet |
samāliṅget striyaṃ vāpi taptāṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ naraḥ || iti | (AṅSm 3.37–39)
tapte lohaśayane gurutalpagaḥ śayīta | sūrmīṃ jvalantīṃ vāśliṣyet | liṅgaṃ vā savṛṣaṇam utkṛtyāñjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇāpratīcīṃ vrajed ajihmam ā śartīranipātanāt | mṛtaḥ śudhyati – [316] iti | baudhāyano ‘pi: “gurutalpagas tapte lohaśayane śayīta | jvalantīṃ vā sūrmīm āśliṣyāt | liṅgaṃ vā savṛṣaṇaṃ parivāsyājjalāv ādhāya dakṣiṇāpratīcyor anyatareṇa gacched ā nipātāt” iti (BDh 2.1.13.15) | tatra tayor icchatoḥ saṃyoge taptalohaśayanam | striyā protsāhitasya jvalatsūrmyāliṅganam | ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ tu gamane savṛṣaṇaliṅgotkartanādi || 9 ||
jananīgamane ‘bhihitaṃ yat prayaścittaṃ tat tadbhaginīgamane ‘py atidiśati |
mātṛṣvasṛgame caivam ātmammeḍhranikartanam || 10 || iti |
evam sati sāmānyātideśān mānase krīḍādau vasiṣṭhoktaṃ vratadvayam avagantavyam | meḍhranikartanena maraṇaparyantavratam upalakṣyate | na ca – jananītadbhaginyoḥ samānavratam ayuktam iti śaṅkanīyam, jananyām akāmakṛte tadbhaginyāṃ kāmakṛte ca samānatvasaṃbhavāt | uparitanavacanena tu “ajñānena” iti viśeṣaṇād etasya vacanasya kāmakṛtaviṣayatvam avagamyate || 10 ||
[317]
akāmakṛte mātṛṣvasṛgamane prāyaścittam āha |
ajñānena tu yo gacchet kuryāc cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
daśagomithunaṃ dadyāc chuddhiṃ pārāśaro ‘bravīt || 11 | iti |
gavor dhenuvṛṣabhayor mithunaṃ gomithunam | daśasaṃkhyākaṃ gomithunaṃ daśagomithunam | viṃśatisaṃkhyākā gāva ity arthaḥ |
nanu – yājñavalkyena mātṛṣvasṛgamane gurutalpavratam atidiṣṭam:
pituḥ svasāraṃ mātuś ca mātulānīṃ snuṣām api |
mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm ācāryatanayāṃ tathā||
ācāryapatnīṃ svasutāṃ gacchaṃs tu gurutalpagaḥ || (YDh 3.232–33)
iti |
bāḍham | kāmakṛte gurutalpavratam asmābhir api pūrvavākye darśitam | cāndrāyaṇaṃ tv akāmakṛte ity avirodhaḥ | etad evābhipretya hārīta āha: “pitṛvyastrīgamane svasṛmātṛṣvasṛpitṛṣvasṛgamane kanyāsagotrāsvastrīyāgamane bhāgineyīgamane cāndrāyaṇam” iti (HāSm 23.11) | śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “evaṃ mātulānīmātṛṣvasṛsnuṣāduhitṛgamane tathācāryaduhitari cāndrāyaṇam” [318] iti | bṛhadyamo ‘pi |
caṇḍālīṃ pulkasīṃ mlecchīṃ snuṣāṃ ca bhaginīṃ sakhīm |
mātāpitroḥ svasāraṃ ca nikṣiptāṃ śaraṇāgatām ||
mātulānīṃ pravrajitāṃ sagotrāṃ nṛpayoṣitam |
śiṣyabhāryāṃ guror bhāryāṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
pitṛṣvasā mātulānī śvaśrūr mātṛṣavasā tathā |
etā gatvā striyo mohāc carec cāndrāyaṇavratam || iti |
yady apy akāmakṛtasya mūlavacanena cāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ viṃśatigāvaś cābhihitāḥ tathāpi tasya retaḥsekaparyantagamanaviṣayatvāt | ekacāndrāyaṇavacanānāṃ ca retasekāt prāg eva nivṛttau yojanīyatvān na ko ‘pi virodhaḥ | yad api caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam,
pitṛvyabhrātṛbhāryāṃ ca bhaginīṃ mātur eva ca |
śvaśrūm āruhya dhātrīṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
iti, yad api saṃvartenābhihitam,
bhaginīṃ mātur āptāṃ ca svasāraṃ cānyamātṛjām |
etā gatvā striyo mohāt taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || (SaṃSm 159)
[319] iti, etad ubhayam ārohaṇaparyantaṃ pravṛttasya yoniliṅgasaṃbandhāt prāṅ nivṛttau draṣṭavyam || 11 ||
jananīvyatiritapitṛbhāryāgamane prāyaścittam āha |
pitṛdārān samāruhya mātur āptāṃ tu bhrātṛjām |
gurupatnīṃ snuṣāṃ caiva bhrātṛbhāryāṃ tathaiva ca || 12 ||
mātulānīṃ sagotrāṃ ca prājāpatyatrayaṃ caret |
godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 13 || iti |
pitṛdārāḥ jananīvyatiriktāḥ pitṛbhāryā asavarṇāś ca | mātur āptā mātuḥ priyasakhī | bhrātṛjā jyeṣṭhasya kaniṣṭhasya vā sutā | guruśabdo hi mukhyayā vṛttyā pitaram ācaṣṭte | tathā ca manuḥ |
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi |
saṃbhāvayati cānnena sa vipro guru ucyate || iti | (MDh 2.142)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
sa gurur yaḥ kriyāḥ kṛtvā vedam asmai prayacchati | iti | (YDh 1.34)
pitṛvyatiriktaḥ śrutopakāry api mukhyo guruḥ | tac ca manunaivoktam |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yas śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ |
tam apīha guruṃ vidyāt śrutopakriyayā tayā || (MDh 2.149)
[320] iti | vyāsas tu mukhyāmukhyagurūn sarvān saṃgṛhya darśayati: “guravo mātṛpitṛpatyācāryā vidyādātṛjyeṣṭhabhrātṛṛtvijo bhayatrātānnadātā ca” iti | evaṃ ca saty atra pitṛdārān iti pituḥ pṛthag upādānāt tadvyatiriktācāryādir eva gurupatnīr ity atrānena guruśabdena vivakṣitaḥ | snuṣādayaḥ prasiddhāḥ | akāmataḥ sakṛd gatvā sadakṣiṇaṃ prājāpatyatrayaṃ caret | kāmatas tv agnipraveśaḥ | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
mātaraṃ gurupatnīṃ ca svasāraṃ svasutāṃ tathā|
gatvā tu praviśed agniṃ nānyā śuddhir vidhīyate || iti |
viṣṇusmṛtāv api: “mātṛgamanaṃ duhitṛgamanaṃ snuṣāgamanam ity atipātakāni |
atipātakinas tv ete praviśeyur hutāśanam |
na hy anyā niṣkṛtis teṣāṃ vidyate hi kathaṃcana || iti | (ViDh 34.1–2)
yat tu śaṅkena,
adhaḥśāyī jaṭādhārī parṇamūlaphalāśanaḥ |
ekakālaṃ samaśnāno varṣe tu dvādaśe gate ||
[321]        rukmasteyī surāpaś ca brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ |
vratenaitena śudhyanti mahāpātakinas tv ime || (ŚaṅSm 17.1–3)
iti, tat savarṇottamavarṇapitṛdāragamane akāmato draṣṭavyam | yac ca saṃvartenoktam,
pitṛdārān samāruhya mātṛvarjaṃ narādhamaḥ |
bhaginīṃ mātur āptāṃ ca svasāraṃ vānyamātṛjām ||
etās tisraḥ striyo gatvā taptakṛcchraṃ samācaret || (SaṃSm 159)
iti, tad dhīnavarṇagurudāreṣu retaḥsekād arvāg draṣṭavyam | yac ca yājñavalkyenoktam,
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ samā vā gurutalpagaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasyan vedasaṃhitām || (YDh 3.260)
iti, etat traivārṣikaprājāpatyavrataṃ brāhmaṇīputrasya śūdrajātīyagurubhāryāgamane draṣṭavyam | yadā tu gurupatnīm asavarṇāṃ vyabhicāriṇīm abudhipūraṃ gacchati tadā vedajapasahitatraimāsikacāndrāyaṇam draṣṭavyam | yac ca vyāghrenoktam,
[322]        kṛcchraṃ caivātikṛcchraṃ ca tathā kṛcchrātikṛcchrakam |
caren māsatrayaṃ vipraḥ kṣatriyāgamane guroḥ ||
iti, tatra buddhipūrvaṃ sakṛdgamane ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛtte atikṛcchraḥ | tayā protsāhitasya kṛcchraḥ | svenaiva protsāhitāyāṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | tatrāpy abhyāse maraṇāntikam eva | yathāha devalaḥ |
matyā gatvā punar bhāryāṃ guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ dvijaḥ |
aṇḍābhyāṃ varjitaṃ liṅgam utkṛtya ca mṛtaḥ śuciḥ || iti |
abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane kaṇvoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ taptakṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ tathaiva ca |
sakṛd gatvā guror bhāryām ajñānāt kṣatriyāṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛtte taptakṛcchram | tayā protsāhitasyātikṛcchram | svena protsitāyāṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | abhyāse tu jātūkarṇyaḥ |
guroḥ kṣatrasutāṃ bhāryāṃ punar gatvā tv akāmataḥ |
vṛṣaṇamātram utkṛtya śudhyec jīvan mṛtaś ca saḥ || iti |
vaiśyāyāṃ buddhipūrve sakṛd gamane kaṇva āha |
taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathā sāntapanaṃ guroḥ |
bhāryāṃ vaiśyāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā māsaṃ caret dvijaḥ || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛtte taptakṛcchram | tayā protsāhitasya sāntapanam | ātmanā protsitāyāṃ parākaḥ | abhyāse liṅgasyāgracchedaḥ kāryaḥ | tathāha laugākṣiḥ |
guror vaiśyāṃ punar gatvā gatvā cāpi punaḥ punaḥ |
liṅgāgraṃ chedayitvā tu tataḥ śudhyati kilbiṣāt ||
[323] iti | asmād eva jñāpakād abhyāse yad uktaṃ gurutalpaprāyaścittaṃ tad eva bahuśo ‘bhyāse ‘pi draṣṭavyam | abuddhipūrvake sakṛd gamane prajāpatir āha |
pañcarātraṃ tu nāśnīyāt saptāṣṭau vā tathaiva ca |
vaiśyāṃ bhāryāṃ guror gatvā sakṛd ajñānato dvijaḥ || iti | (=MDh 3.40)
tatrāpy ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttau saptarātram | tayā protsāhitasya pañcarātram | ātmanā protsāhitāyām aṣṭarātram iti | abhyāse tv āmaraṇāntaṃ brahmacaryācaraṇam | tad āha hārītaḥ |
abhyasya vipro vaiśyāyāṃ guror ajñānamohitaḥ |
sa ṣaḍaṅgaṃ brahmacaryaṃ saṃcared yāvadāyuṣam || iti | (HārSm 21.29)
śūdrāgamane buddhipūrve jābālir āha |
atikṛcchraṃ taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā buddhyā vipraḥ samācaret || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttau taptakṛcchram | tayaiva protsitasyātikṛcchram | ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ prāka iti draṣṭavyam | abhyāse tu dvādaśavarṣaṃ brahmacaryaṃ kartavyam | tathāha upamanyuḥ |
punaḥ śūdrāṃ guror gatvā buddhyā vipraḥ samāhitaḥ |
brahmacaryaṃ sa duṣṭātmā saṃcared dvādaśīh samāḥ ||
[324] iti | ajñāne dīrghatamā āha |
prājāpatyaṃ sāntapanaṃ saptarātropavāsanam |
guroḥ śūdrāṃ sakṛd gatvā cared vipraḥ samāhitaḥ || iti |
tatrāpy ubhayor icchātaḥ pravṛttau sāntapanam | tayā protsāhitasya prājāpatyam | ātmanā protsāhitāyāṃ saptarātropavāsa iti | abhyāse tu manunoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīn māsān abhyasen niyatendriyaḥ |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvā vā gurutalpāpanuttaye || iti | (MDh 11.106)
sādhāraṇastriyāṃ gurutalpadoṣo nāstīty āha vyāghraḥ |
jātyuktaṃ pāradāryaṃ vā gurutalpatvam eva ca |
sādhāraṇastriyāṃ nāsti kanyādūṣaṇam eva ca || iti |
yat tu nāradenoktam,
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā |
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā ||
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā |
āsām anyatamāṃ gatvā gurutalpaga ucyate || (NSm 12.73–75)
iti, tad etad akāmato ‘bhyāse kāmataḥ sakṛd gamane ca draṣṭavyam | yac ca vasiṣṭhenoktam: [325] “sakhisayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsu snuṣāyāṃ gavi gurutalpasamānam” (VaDh 1.23) iti, yac ca manunoktam,
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryād retaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣv atyantajāsu ca ||
iti, yad api vyāghreṇa,
āśritasyāpi viduṣa āhitāgneś ca yoginaḥ |
ācāryasya ca rājñaś ca bhāryāṃ pravrajitāṃ tathā ||
naptrīṃ putrīṃ ca pautrīṃ ca sakhīṃ mātus tathaiva ca |
pituḥ sakhīṃ tathā gatvā gurutalpavrataṃ caret ||
iti, saṃvartenāpi,
pitṛvyadāragamane bhrātṛbhāryāgame tathā |
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryān niṣkṛtir nānyathā bhavet || (SaṃSm 158)
iti, yājñavalkyenāpi,
pitṛṣvasāraṃ mātuś ca mātulānīṃ snuṣām api |
[326]        mātuḥ sapatnīṃ bhaginīm ācāryatanayāṃ tathā ||
ācāryapatnīṃ svasutāṃ gacchaṃs tu gurutalpagaḥ || (YDh 3.232–33)
iti, tāny etāni vacanāni nāradavacanavad vyavasthāpanīyāni | yāni tūktaviṣaya eva nyūnaprāyaścittāni cāndrāyaṇādīni tāni saṃbandhakanīyastvam āśritya yojanīyāni | tac ca saṃbandhakanīyastvaṃ sumantunā pradarśitam: “pitṛpatnyaḥ sarvā mātaraḥ | tadbhrātaro mātulāḥ | tadbhaginyaś ca mātṛṣvasāraḥ | bhaginīsapatnyaś ca bhaginyaḥ | tadapatyaṃ bhāgineyam | ato ‘nyathā saṅkarakāraṇāni kṛtvāyājyāḥ patitāś ca bhavanti” iti | atrātideśikavyapadeśadarśanāt prāyaścittālpatvaṃ sidhyati | etad evābhipretya saṃvarta āha |
guror duhitaraṃ gatvā svasāraṃ pitur eva ca |
tasyā duhitaraṃ caiva caret cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam ||
sanābhinīṃ mātulānīṃ snuṣāṃ mātuḥ sanābhinīm |
gacchann etāḥ striyo mohāt parākeṇa viśudhyati ||
sakhibhāryāṃ samāruhya śvaśrūṃ caiva hi mānavaḥ |
[327]        ahorātroṣito bhūtvā taptakṛcchradvayaṃ caret ||
kumārīgamane caiva vratam etat samādiśet || iti | (SaṃSm 156–62)
sumantur api: “mātṛpitṛṣvasṛsnuṣābhaginībhāgineyīgocaṇḍālīgamaneṣu taptakṛcchratrayaṃ sāntapanaṃ ca” iti | caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
bhrātuś caiva kaniṣṭhasya bhāryāṃ gatvā tu kāmataḥ |
sāntapanaṃ prakurvīta kṛcchradvayam athāpi vā ||
mātuś ca svasriyāṃ gatvā pitṛvyatanayāṃ tathā |
taptakṛcchraṃ prakurvīta ṣaḍrātraṃ tatsutāsu ca ||
guror duhitaraṃ gatvā parākaṃ tu samācaret |
bhāgineyīṃ dvijo gatvā carec cāndrāyaṇam vratam ||
mātulasya sutāṃ gatvā pituś ca svasriyāṃ tathā |
prājāpatyaṃ prakurvīta hārītavacanaṃ tathā ||
[328]        mātuś ca svasriyasyaiva bhāryāṃ gatvā tu kāmataḥ |
pitṛvyatanayasyaiva sapādaṃ kṛcchram ācaret ||
dauhitrīṃ putratanayāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
tatsutāṃ ca snuṣāṃ gatvā parākaṃ tu samācaret ||
carec cāndrāyaṇaṃ vipro gatvopādhyāyayoṣitam |
ācāryasya parākaṃ tu baudhāyanavaco yathā ||
saṃbandhinaḥ striyaṃ gatvā sapādaṃ kṛcchram ācaret |
vidhavāgamane kṛcchram ahorātrasamanvitam ||
vratasyāgamane kṛcchraṃ sapādaṃ tu samācaret |
sakhibhāryāṃ samāruhya jñātisvajanayoṣitam ||
sa kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ kṛcchraṃ pādakṛcchraṃ tataḥ punaḥ |
kumārīgamane vipraś carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
patitāṃ tu dvijo gatvā tad eva vratam ācaret | iti |
atra prokteṣu sarveṣu vrateṣu gauravalāghave parīkṣya yathāyathaṃ buddhipūrvābuddhipūrvābhyāsānabhyāsādiviṣayatvaṃ yojanīyam | mūlavacanapaṭhitapitṛdārādi-vyatiriktaparadāragamane vyāghreṇoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇīṃ gacched akāmāṃ yadi kāmataḥ |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryād ardham eva pramādataḥ ||
ardham eva sakāmāyāṃ taptakṛcchraṃ sakṛdgatau |
ardham ardhaṃ nṛpādīnāṃ dāreṣu brāhmaṇaś caret ||
[329]        etad vrataṃ caret sārdhaṃ śrotriyasya parigrahe |
aśrotriyaś ced dviguṇam aguptām ardham eva ca || iti |
kaṇvo ‘pi |
śūdradārān gato vipro hy atikṛcchraṃ samācaret |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ viśo rājñaḥ samaṃ ca brāhmaṇavratam || iti |
yadi ca brāhmaṇenaiva cāturvarṇyaprasūtāsu krameṇa niviṣṭaṃ tadānīṃ brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇīgamane yad uktaṃ tad eva pādahīnaṃ kṣatriyādigamane draṣṭavyam, vyāghravacanāt,
vipreṇa vai niviṣṭāś cec cāturvarṇyaprasūtayaḥ |
krameṇa pādaśo hīnaṃ vrataṃ tāsu gataś caret || iti |
ayam eva nyāyaḥ kṣatriyādiparigṛhītāsv api draṣṭavyaḥ | brāhmaṇabhāryāṃ śūdrāṃ brāhmaṇo gatvā prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt, vasiṣṭhavacanāt: “brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvakaṃ brāhmaṇadārān abhigacchet nivṛttadharmakarmaṇaḥ kṛcchraḥ anivṛttadharmakarmaṇo ‘tikṛcchraḥ” (VaDh 21.16) [330] iti | idam abuddhipūrve sakṛdgamane | buddhipūrve tu dviguṇam, “ardham eva pramādataḥ” iti liṅgāt | brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyādibhāryāgamane yad uktaṃ tad eva kṣatriyādīnāṃ svajātibhāryāgamane draṣṭavyam | kutaḥ,
vipro nṛpasya bhāryāyāṃ yat karoti samāgame |
tad eva kṣatriyasyāpi kuryād ataiva saṃgame || (PraSm 3.69)
iti prajāpatidharmaliṅgāt | garbhaparyante paradāragamane yama āha |
varṣe dve paradāreṣu trīṇi śrotriyadārake | iti | (YaSm 5)
prātilomyena paradāragamane saṃvarta āha |
kathaṃcit brāhmaṇīṃ gacchet kṣatriyo vaiśya eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsārdhena viśudhyati ||
śūdras tu brāhmaṇīṃ gatvā kathaṃcit kāmamohitaḥ |
gomūtrayāvakāhāro māsenaikena śudhyati || (SaṃSm 166–67)
[331] iti | etad atyantavyabhicāribrāhmaṇīviṣayam, itaraviṣaye vadhasmaraṇāt | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śūdraś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacched vīraṇair veṣṭayitvā śūdram agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ kṛṣṇakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | vaiśyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchet lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvā vaiśyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ gaurakharam āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | rājanyaś ced brāhmaṇīm abhigacchet śarapatrair veṣṭayitvā rājanyam agnau prāsyet | brāhmaṇyāḥ [332] śirasi vapanaṃ kārayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ raktakhram āropya mahāpatham anusaṃvrājayet | pūtā bhavatīti vijñāyate | evaṃ vaiśyo rājanyāyām | śūdraś ca rājanyavaiśyayoḥ | iti (VaSm 21.1–11) ||
svairiṇīgamane śaṅkhalikhitāv āhatuḥ: “svairiṇyāṃ vṛṣalyāṃ cāvakīrṇaḥ sacailaṃ snāta udakumbhaṃ dadyād brāhmaṇāya | vaiśyāyāṃ caturthakālāhāro brāhmaṇān bhojayet | kṣatriyāyāṃ trirātropoṣito yavāḍhakaṃ dadyāt | brāhmaṇyāṃ tryaham upoṣya ghṛtapātraṃ dadyāt” iti | bandhakīgamane ṣaṭtriṃanmate prāyaścittam uktam |
brāhmaṇo bandhakīṃ gatvā kiṃcid dadyāt dvijātaye |
rājanyāṃ hi dhanur dadyād vaiśyāṃ gatvā tu cailakam ||
[333]        śūdrāṃ gatvā tu vai vipra udakumbhaṃ dvijātaye |
divasopoṣito vā syād dadyād viprāya bhojanam || iti |
bandhkīlakṣaṇaṃ smṛtyantare ‘bhihitam |
caturthe svairiṇī proktā pañcame bandhakī bhavet | iti | (YaSm 5.37)
idaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ garbhānutpattiviṣayam | tadutpattau yadviśeṣeṇa prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad eva tatra dviguṇaṃ kuryāt | tad āha uśanāḥ |
gamane tu vrataṃ yat syād garbhe taddviguṇaṃ caret | iti | (UṣSm 1.37)
śūdryāṃ garbham ādadhataś caturviṃśatimate viśeṣa uktaḥ: “vṛṣalyām abhijātas tu trīṇivarṣāṇi caturthakālasamaye saktūn bhuñjīta” iti | garbhād arvāk tu tatraivābhihitam |
śūdrāṇāṃ hīnajātīnāṃ striyaṃ gatvā tu kāmataḥ |
prājāpatyaṃ prakurvīta iṣṭiṃ vā vāruṇīṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
pulkasyāsigamane saṃvarta āha |
pulkasīgamanaṃ kṛtvā kāmato ‘kāmato ‘pi vā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt tato mucyeta kilbiṣāt ||
[334]        naṭīṃ śaulūṣakīṃ caiva rajakīṃ veṇujīvinīm |
gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt tathā carmopajīvinīm || iti | (SaṃSm 150–51)
yat tu bṛhatsaṃvartaḥ,
rajakavyādhaśaulūṣaveṇucarmopajīvinīḥ |
etās tu brāhmaṇo gatvā carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam || (BṛSaṃSm 1.27)
iti, āpastambo ‘pi,
mlecchī naṭī carmakārī rajakī buruḍī tathā |
etāsu gamanaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam || (ĀpSm 2.26)
iti, tad abhyāsaviṣayam | yac ca śātātapena,
kaivartīṃ rajakīṃ caiva veṇucarmopajīvinīm |
prājāpatyavidhānena kṛcchreṇaikena śudhyati || (ŚāSm 3.11)
iti — carmopajīvinīṃ gacchann iti śeṣaḥ —, tad retaḥsekāt [335] prāṅnivṛttiviṣayam | kāpālikastrīgamane yama āha |
kāpālikānnabhoktṝṇāṃ tannārīgāmināṃ tathā |
jñānāt kṛcchrābdam uddiṣṭam ajñānād aindavadvayam || iti | (YaSm 5.12)
jātibhedena garbhādhāne caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam |
brāhmaṇīgamane kṛcchraṃ garbhe sāntapanaṃ caret |
rājñī garbhe parākaḥ syād vaiśyāgarbhe tryahādhikam ||
śūdrāgarbhe dvijaḥ kuryāt tadvac cāndrāyaṇavratam |
caṇḍālyāṃ garbham āropya gurutalpavrataṃ caret || iti |
vidhavāgamane caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam |
vidhavāgamane kṛcchram ahorātrasamanvitam |
vratasthāgamane kṛcchraṃ sapādaṃ tu samācaret || iti |
mukhamaithune tu uśansoktam: “yas tu punar brāhmaṇe dharmapatnīmukhe maithunaṃ seveta sa duṣyati | prājāpatyena śudhyati” iti (UṣSm 2.31–32) | rajasvalāgamane saṃvarta āha |
rajasvalāṃ tu yo gacched garbhiṇīṃ patitāṃ tathā |
tasya pāpaviśuddhyartham atikṛcchro viśodhakaḥ || (SaṃSm 163)
[335] iti | āpastambo ‘pi |
udakyāṃ yadi gaccheta brāhmaṇo madamohitaḥ |
prājāpatyena śudhyeta brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanāt || iti | (ĀpSm 2.13)
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
rajasvalāṃ dvijo gatvā parākaṃ tu samācaret |
sāntapanaṃ dvitīye ‘hni prājāpatyaṃ pare ‘hani || iti |
śātātapo ‘pi: “anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe śvakākasparśane sacailasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca | rajasvalābhigamane caitad eva” iti (ŚāSm 1.19–20) | vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “rajasvalādivyavāye śuklam ṛṣabhaṃ dadyāt kṛṣṇaliṅgam” iti (VaDh?) | manur api |
amānuṣīṣu govarjam udakyāyām ayoniṣu |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret || (MDh 11.173)
[337] iti | gautamo ‘pi: “udakyāgamane trirātram” iti (GDh 24.32) | śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “rajasvalāvadhūtādigamane trirātropavāso ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kuryāt” iti | atra yāni hrāsavṛddhiyutāni tāny ubhayecchānuatarecchādiviṣayatvena vyavasthāpanīyāni | pitṛṣvasṛsutādivivāhe prāyaścittam āha sumantuḥ: “pitṛṣvasṛsutāṃ mātulasutāṃ mātṛsagotrāṃ samānārṣeyīṃ ca vivāhya cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | parityajya caināṃ bibhṛyāt” iti | śātātapo ‘pi |
mātulasya sutām ūḍhvā mātṛgotrāṃ tathaiva ca |
samānapravarāṃ caiva dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (ŚāSm 3.23)
baudhāyano ‘pi: “sagotrāṃ ced amatyopagacchet mātṛvad enāṃ bibhṛyāt | prajātāṃ cet kṛcchrapādaṃ [338] caritvā yan ma ātmano nindābhūt punar agniś cakṣur adād ity etābhyāṃ juhuyāt” iti |
nanu – pitṛṣvasṛsutāmātulasutayor vivāhasya tatprakaraṇe aṅgīkṛtatvād atra prāyaścittavidhānaṃ viruddham iti cet |
maivam, āsurādivivāhoḍāsutāyāḥ sāpiṇḍyanivṛttyā vivāhānaṅgīkāreṇa prāyaścittasya tadviṣayatvāt |
nanu – gurutalpagasya pūrvodāhṛtebhyo vratebhyo ‘nyāny api kānicid vratāni smaryante | tatra aṅgirāḥ |
mahāvrataṃ cared vāpi dadyāt sarvasvam eva vā |
gurvarthe vā mṛto yuddhe mucyate gurutalpagaḥ || iti | (AṅSm 6.13)
sumantur api: “gurudāragāmī saṃvatsaraṃ kaṇṭakinaḥ śākhāṃ [339] pariṣvajyādhaḥśāyī triṣavaṇo baikṣāhāraḥ pūto bhavatīti | aśvamedhāvabhṛthasnānena vā” iti | etāny api pūrvoktanītyā gurutāratamyaṃ tatpatnītāratamyaṃ tatsaṃyogatāratamyaṃ copajīvya vyavasthāpanīyāni || 12–13 ||
paśvādigamanasya pitṛdārādigamanād arvācīnatvena tatrālpaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
paśuveśyādigamane mahiṣy uṣṭrī kapīs tathā |
kharīṃ ca sūkarīṃ gatvā prājāpatyavrataṃ caret || 14 || iti |
paśur aśvādiḥ | veśo bhṛtiḥ | tāṃ jīvanahetutayārhatīti veśyā | yady api kapī sūkarī cālpadehatvān na manuṣyair gantuṃ yogyā tathāpi keṣucid deśaviśeṣeṣu prauḍhadehayor api tayoḥ sadbhāvāt tadupagamanaviṣayam idaṃ vratābhidhānam | veśyāyāṃ garbhāt prāg idam avagantavyam | garbhe tu kaṇva āha |
prasūto yas tu veśyāyāṃ bhaikṣyayuṅ niyatendriyaḥ |
śatasāhasram abhyasya sāvitrīm eti śuddhatām || iti |
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi paśugamane prājāpatyam uktam |
sarveṣāṃ paśujātīnāṃ gamane kṛcchram ācaret |
śunīṃ caiva dvijo gatvā atikṛcchraṃ samācaret ||
[340] iti | vedavidas tu tiryaggamane kūṣmāṇḍakair mantrair homaḥ | tad āha gautamaḥ: “amānuṣīṣu govarjastrīkṛte kūṣmāṇḍair ghṛtahomaḥ” iti (GDh 23.40) || 14 ||
paśvantarebhyo goḥ praśastatvāt tadabhigamane ‘dhikaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
gogāmī ca trirātreṇa gām ekāṃ brāhmaṇo dadan | iti |
śudhyatīti śeṣaḥ | etac ca sakṛdgamane retaḥsekāt prāṅnivṛttau draṣṭavyam | retaḥsekānte tu sakṛdgamane saṃvarta āha |
naro gogamane kuryāt kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ tathā | iti | (SaṃSm 155)
āvṛttau jābālir āha |
taptakṛcchraṃ tu gāṃ gatvā paradārāṃs tathaiva ca |
itareṣāṃ paśūnāṃ tu kṛcchrapādo vidhīyate || iti |
bahukṣīrādiguṇavatyāṃ gavi āvṛttau caturviṃśatimate darśitam |
naro gogamanaṃ kṛtvā carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
[341] iti | tatraiva viṣaye ‘tyantābhyāse viṣṇur āha: “kuryāt paradāragamane govratam, gogamane ca | tiryagyonāv ākāśe ‘psu divā goyāne ca savāsāḥ snānam ācaret” iti (ViDh 53.1–4) | vedavidviprasaṃbandhinyāṃ gavi guṇavatyām atyantābhyāse śaṅkhalikhitāv āhatuḥ: “tiryagyoniṣu govarjaṃ sacailaṃ snāto yavasāhāraṃ gobhyo dadyāt | goṣv avakīrṇaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ prājāpatyaṃ caret” iti | yathoktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭāyāṃ savanasthaviprasaṃbandhinyāṃ gavi gautama āha: “sakhisayonisagotrāśiṣyabhāryāsu snuṣāyāṃ gavi ca talpasamaḥ | avakara ity eke” iti (GDh 24.10–11) | gosaṃyuktaśakaṭādivāhane ‘vasthāya striyaṃ bhūñjānasya yama āha |
yadi gobhiḥ samāyuktaṃ yānam āruhya vai dvijaḥ |
maithunaṃ sevane tatra manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt ||
[342]        trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ kṛtvā sacailaṃ snānam ācaret |
gobhyo yavāḍhakaṃ datvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | (YaSm 5.3–4)
manur api |
maithunaṃ tu samāsevya puṃsi yoṣiti vā dvijaḥ |
goyāne ‘psu divā caiva savāsāḥ snānam ācaret || iti | (MDh 11.174)
pūrvaṃ mahiṣyā gamane yat prājāpatyam uktaṃ tad abhyāsaviṣayam | sakṛdgamane tv idānīm āha |
mahiṣyuṣṭrīkharīgāmī tv ahorātreṇa śudhyati || 15 || iti |
mahiṣī ca uṣṭrī ca kharī ca, tā gacchatīti mahiṣyuṣṭrīkharīgāmī | sa ekopavāsena śudhyati iti |
|| 15 ||
athottamajātiprasūtāyā nāryāś caṇḍālasṃparke prāyaścittaṃ vaktavyam | tasya cāpadviṣayatvam abhipretyāpadviśeṣānuvādena tatra tāvat puruṣakartavyam āha |
ḍāmare samare vāpi dubhikṣe vā janakṣaye |
bandhigrāhe bhayārttau vā sadā svastrīṃ nirīkṣayet || 16 ||
[343] iti | ḍāmaraḥ parasainyakṛtopadravādiḥ | samaraḥ senayor yuddham | durbhikṣaṃ vṛṣṭyabhāvādinā aśanābhāvaḥ | janakṣayaḥ mārikādevatānimittaiḥ pracuraiḥ rogaviśeṣaiḥ bahūnāṃ prajānāṃ maraṇam | bandigrāho balātkāreṇa strīṇām apahāraḥ | bhayārttiś corarājādikṛta upadravaḥ | evaṃvidhāsu āpatsu puruṣaḥ svaprāṇarakṣārtha palāyamāno na striyam upekṣeta | kiṃ tu tasyā api yathā rakṣā bhavati tathā nirīkṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 16 ||
yadā puruṣo rakṣitum aśaktaḥ tadānīm āpannāyāḥ striyāḥ kathaṃcic caṇḍālasaṃparke kiṃ kartavyam ity ata āha |
caṇḍālaiḥ saha saṃparkaṃ yā nārī kurute tataḥ |
viprān daśa varān kṛtvā svakaṃ doṣaṃ prakāśayet || 17 ||
ākaṇṭhasaṃmite kūpe gomayodakakardame |
tatra sthitvā nirāhārā tv ahorātreṇa niṣkramet || 18 ||
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā bhuñjīyād yāvakaudanam |
trirātram upavāsitv tv ekarātraṃ jale vaset || 19 ||
śaṃkhapuṣpīlatāmūlaṃ patraṃ vā kusumaṃ phalam |
suvarṇaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca kvāthayitvā pibej jalam || 20 ||
[344]        ekabhaktaṃ caret paścād yāvat puṣpavatī bhavet |
vrataṃ carati tad yāvat tāvat saṃvasate bahiḥ || 21 || iti |
varān “vedavedāṅgavit” ityādiproktaguṇaviśiṣṭān | tādṛśān daśasaṃkhyākān viprān pariṣadaṃ kṛtvā teṣām agre svakīyaṃ pāpam avañcanena nivedayet | yad vā “cāturvedya” ityādivacane proktānāṃ daśānāṃ viprāṇāṃ agre nivedayet | tatas tair anujñātā vratam evaṃ samācaret | kaṇṭhapramāṇaṃ kūpaṃ khātvā sodakena gomayena tam āpūrya tatraikaṃ dinaṃ nirantaram avasthāpyopoṣya paredyur nirgacchet | nirgatya ca yāvakānnaṃ bhuñjīta | tato dinatrayam upoṣya caturthaṃ dinam ākaṇṭhajale sthitvā pañcame śaṅkhapuṣpīmūlādibhiḥ pañcabhiḥ suvarṇena pañcagavyena ca kvathitaṃ jalaṃ pibet | tataḥ ṣaṣṭhadinam ārabhya yāvad ṛtudarśanam ekabhaktaṃ dine caret | vratadineṣu na gṛhe ‘vatiṣṭheta | kiṃ tu bahir eva nivaset || 17–21 ||
[345]
yathoktavratācaraṇānantarakartavyam āha |
prāyaścitte tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
godvayaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ dadyāc chuddhiṃ pārāśaro ‘bravīt || 22 || iti |
ayaṃ ca dakṣiṇāśirasko vrataviśeṣaḥ smṛtyantare ‘pi darśitaḥ |
caṇḍālena tu saṃparkaṃ yadi gacchet kathaṃcana |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā bhuñjīyād yāvakaudanam ||
trirātram upavāsaḥ syād ekarātraṃ jale vaset |
ātmanā saṃmite kūpe gomayodakakardame ||
tatra sthitvā nirāhārā trirātraṃ tu tataḥ kṣipet |
śaṅkhapuṣpīlatāmūlaṃ puṣpaṃ vā kusumaṃ phalam ||
kṣīraṃ suvarṇasaṃmiśraṃ kvāthayitvā tataḥ pibet |
ekabhaktaṃ caret paścāt yāvat puṣpavatī bhavet ||
bahis tāvac ca nivased yāvac carati sā vratam |
prāyaścitte tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam ||
godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt śuddhiṃ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt ||
iti, tad etad akāmakṛtaviṣayam | kāmakṛte tu sakṛdgamane ṛṣyaśṛṅga āha |
saṃpṛktā syād athāntyair yā sā kṛcchrābdaṃ samācaret |
[346] iti | yady āhitagarbhāyā eva paścāc caṇḍālādivyavāyaḥ tadā tenaiva viśeṣa uktaḥ |
antarvatnī tu yuvatiḥ saṃpṛktā yānty ayoninā |
prāyaścittaṃ na sā kuryād yāvad garbho na niḥsṛtaḥ ||
na pracāraṃ gṛhe kuryān na cāṅgeṣu prasādhanam |
na śayīta samaṃ bhartrā na ca bhuñjīta bāndhavaiḥ ||
prāyaścittaṃ gate garbhe vidhiṃ kṛcchrādikaṃ caret |
hiraṇyam atha vā dhenuṃ dadyād viprāya dakṣiṇām || iti |
yadā tu kāmato ‘ntyajasaṃparkaṃ karoti tadā uśanasoktaṃ draṣṭavyam |
antyajena tu saṃparke bhojane maithune kṛte |
praviśet saṃpradīptāgnau mṛtyunā sā viśudhyati || iti | (UṣSm 2.6)
|| 22 ||
retaḥsekāntasya caṇḍālagamanasya prāyaścittam abhidhāya retaḥsekāt prāṅnivṛttau prāyaścittam āha |
cāturvarṇyasya nārīṇāṃ kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam |
yathā bhūmis tathā nārī tasmāt tāṃ na tu dūṣayet || 23 ||
[347] iti | caṇḍālagamanasyātyantajugupsitatvād yoṣitaḥ parityāga eva | na tu vratena śuddhir ity āśaṅkya tām āśāṅkāṃ nivartayituṃ bhūmidṛṣṭāntam upanyasyati | bhūmir hi caṇḍālādivāsenopahatāpi khananalepanādibhiḥ saṃśodhya punaḥ svīkriyate | evaṃ yoṣid api caritavratā punaḥ svīkaraṇīyā | na tu tāṃ dūṣayet | na parityajed iti yāvat | yady apy atra cāturvarṇyasyeti sāmānyenābhihitaṃ tathāpy etat brāhmaṇīvyatiriktāviṣaye saṅkocanīyam, brāhmaṇyāḥ saṃvartena viśeṣābhidhānāt |
caṇḍālaṃ pulkasaṃ mlecchaṃ śvapākaṃ patitaṃ tathā |
brāhmaṇy akāmato gatvā cāndrāyaṇacatuṣṭayam ||
rajakavyādhaśailūṣaveṇucarmopajīvinaḥ |
brāhmaṇy etān yadā gacched akāmād aindavatrayam || iti | (SaṃSm 167–68)
|| 23 ||
āpatkāle caṇḍālavaśaṃgatāyā nāryā retaḥsekāsekayoḥ prāyaścittam abhihitam | idānīṃ āpannāyā eva balāt śūdrādisaṃparke sati retaḥsekāsekayoḥ prāyaścittadvayam āha |
bandigrāheṇa yā bhuktā hatvā baddhvā balād bhayāt |
kṛtvā sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ śudhyet pārāśaro ‘bravīt || 24 ||
sakṛdbhuktā tu yā nārī necchantī pāpakarmabhiḥ |
prājāpatyena śudhyeta ṛtuprasravaṇena ca || 25 ||
[348] iti | rajjvādibhir badhvā kaśādibhir tāḍayitvā bhayam utpādya yā balād bhuktā bhavati sā sāntapanaṃ kṛtvā śudhyed iti parāśarasya matam | prāsādam āruhya prekṣate ity asminn arthe “prāsādāt prekṣate” iti yathā pañcamī tathā bhayam utpādyety asminn arthe bhayād iti pañcamī draṣṭavyā | dvitīyavacane ‘pi hatvā badhvety anuṣañjanīyam | brāhmaṇyāḥ prātilomyena dvijātivyavāye saṃvarta āha |
brāhmaṇyakāmād gacchet tu kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyam eva vā |
gomūtrayāvakair māsāt tadardhāc ca visudhyati || iti | (BṛSaṃSm 5.30)
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘pi: “brāhmaṇī kṣatriyavaiśyasevāyām atikṛcchraṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchrau caret | kṣatriyayoṣito brāhmaṇarājanyavaiśyasevāyāṃ kṛcchrārdhaṃ prājāpatyam atikṛcchraṃ ca | vaiśyayoṣito brāhmaṇarājanyavaiśyasevāyāṃ kṛcchrapādaḥ kṛcchrārdhaṃ prājāpatyaṃ ca | śūdrāyāḥ śūdrasevane prājāpatyam | brāhmaṇarājanyavaiśyasevāyām ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ kṛcchrārdham” [349] iti | yadā tv āhitagarbhaiva paścāt śūdrādibhir vyabhicarati tadā garbhapātaśaṅkayā prasavottarakāle eva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | tathā ca smṛtyantare |
antarvatnī tu yā nārī sametākramya kāminā |
prāyaścittaṃ na kuryāt sā yāvad dharmo na niḥsṛtaḥ ||
jāte garbhe vrataṃ paścāt kuryān māsaṃ tu yāvakam |
na garbhadoṣas tatrāsti saṃskāryaḥ sa yathāvidhi || iti | (ŚāSm 3.7–8)
|| 24–25 ||
yoṣitkṛtāparādhena na kevalaṃ tasyāḥ prāyaścittam | kiṃ tu tadbhartur apīty āha |
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya yasya bhāryā surāṃ pibet |
patitārdhaśarīrasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || 25 || iti |
yasya viprasya brāhmaṇyādiṣu catasṛṣu bhāryāsu anyatamā yā kācit surāṃ pibet tasya viprasya strīpuṃsadvayātmakaṃ yat śarīraṃ tasya śarīrasyārdhaṃ strīrūpaṃ patati | striyā ardhaśarīratvaṃ śrutiprasiddham: [350] “ardho vā eṣa ātmano yat patnī” iti | tatra patitasyārdhaśarīrasya strīrūpasya surāpānaproktavratavyatirktena svalpavratena niṣkṛtiḥ pariśuddhir na vidyate | kiṃ tu surāpānavratam eva tayā kāryam ity arthaḥ | yad vā patitaṃ strīrūpam ardhaśarīraṃ yasya puruṣasyāsau patitārdhaśarīraḥ | tasya svayaṃ surām apibato ‘pi bhāryākṛtāparādhena niṣkṛtiḥ pariśuddhiḥ karmādhikāralakṣaṇā na vidyate | atas tadadhikārasiddhyarthaṃ tena prāyaścittam ācaritavyam | etad evābhipretya manu-vasiṣṭhābhyām etad eva vacanaṃ paṭhitam | yoṣitkṛtāparādhena puruṣasya yathā pratyavāyaḥ tathā puruṣānuṣṭhitadharmeṇa yoṣito niṣkṛtir bhaviṣyatīti na śaṅkanīyam | yato yājñavalkya āha |
patilokaṃ na sā yāti brāhmaṇī yā surāṃ pibet |
ihaiva sā śunī gṛdhro sūkarī copajāyate || (YDh 3.256)
[351] iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
yā brāhmaṇī surāṃ pītavatī na tāṃ devāḥ patilokaṃ nayanti |
ihaiva sā bhramati kṣīṇapuṇyā āsyolkā bhavati śuktikā vā || iti | (VaDh 21.11)
|| 26 ||
patitārdhaśarīreṇa puruṣeṇa yat kartavyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tad āha |
gāyatrīṃ japamānas tu kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ varet | iti |
japamāna iti vartamānaprayogāt yāvad vratasamāptis tāvaj japaḥ kartavya ity avagamyate |
sāntapanasyānekadhā bhinnatvād atra vivakṣitaṃ sāntapanaviśeṣaṃ darśayati |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam || 27 ||
ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ smṛtam | iti |
atra dvirātraṃ saptarātraṃ ceti dvividhaṃ sāntapanaṃ nirdiśyate | tac cobhayaṃ prāyaścittakāṇḍaprathamādhyāye yājñavalkyavacanadvayodāharaṇena viśadīkṛtam || 27 ||
[352]
yathā kathaṃcit parapuruṣeṇa saṃyujya tata uparatāyā anutāpaṃ gatāyā yoṣito yathocitaṃ prāyaścittaṃ pūrvatrābhihitam | athānutāparahitāyā duḥsaṅgād anuparatāyās tyāgam āha |
jāreṇa janayed garbhaṃ mṛte ‘vyakte gate patau || 28 ||
tāṃ tyajed apare rāṣṭre patitāṃ pāpakāriṇīm | iti |
patyau mṛte vā deśāntarasaṃcāreṇājñātatayā gate vā yā strī niraṅkuśā satī jāraṃ svīkṛtyāpatyam utpādayati | pāpaṃ kartuṃ śīlam asyā iti pāpakāriṇī na kadācit pāpād uparatā | ata eva patitāṃ tādṛśīṃ svarāṣṭrād utsārya pararāṣṭre preṣayet |
nanu – strīparityāgaś caturviṃśatimate niṣiddhaḥ |
strīṇāṃ nāsti parityāgo brahmahatyādibhir vinā |
tatrāpi gṛhamadhye tu prāyaścittāni kārayet ||
parityaktā caret pāpaṃ bahv alpaṃ vāpi kiṃcana |
tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā bāndhavān anugacchati ||
yāvanti nārīromāṇi tatprasūtikuleṣu ca |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi parityāgī sa pacyate ||
kumbhīpāke mahāghore jñātayaḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ |
vasanti strīparityāgād yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
[353]        pitṛmātṛparityāgī bhāryātyāgī suhṛttyajaḥ |
asipatravanaṃ sevya caṇḍālānāṃ śataṃ vrajet || iti |
maivam, parityāganiṣedhasyānutāpitaprāyaścittādhikāristrīviṣayatvāt, “prāyaścittāni kārayet” ity abhidhānāt, śvapākopahatānāṃ parityāgasya tatraivāṅgīkṛtatvāt:
catasra eva saṃtyājyāḥ patane saty api striyaḥ |
śvapākopahatā yā tu bhartṛghnī pitṛputragā || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
catasras tu parityājyāḥ śiṣyagā gurugā ca yā |
patighnī tu viśeṣeṇa juṅgitopagatā ca yā || iti | (VaDh 21.10)
juṅgito jugupsitaḥ śvapākādiḥ | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vyabhicārād ṛtau śuddhir garbhe tyāgo vidhīyate |
garbhabhartṛvadhādau ca tathā mahati pātake || iti | (YDh 1.72)
yat tu manunoktam,
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ bhartā nirundhyād ekaveśmani | (MDh 11.176)
[354] iti, na tat mūlavacanena samānaviṣayam, bhartṛrahitastrīviṣayatvān mūlavacanasya, manuvākye tu “nirundhyāt” iti bhartṛkartavyatābhidhānāt, taduparitanākyena prāyaścittābhidhānāc ca |
yat puṃsaḥ paradāreṣu tac caināṃ cārayed vratam ||
sā cet punaḥ praduṣyeta sadṛśenopamantritā |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tad asyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam || iti | (MDh 11.176–77)
yad api yājñavalkyenoktam,
hṛtādhikārāṃ malināṃ piṇḍamātropajīvinīm |
paribhūtām adhaḥśayyāṃ vāsayed vyabhicāriṇīm || (YDh 1.70)
iti, tad api nirundhyād ity anena manuvacanena samānaviṣayam | tasmād yathoktasya strīparityāgasya na kiṃcid bādhakam asti || 28 ||
“jāreṇa janayet” ity anena pramitavyabhicāravatīṃ pratyabhihitam | atha śaṅkitavyabhicārāṃ pratyāha |
brāhmaṇī tu yadā gacchet parapuṃsā samanvitā || 29 ||
sā tu naṣṭā vinirdiṣṭā na tasyāgamanaṃ punaḥ |
[355] iti | striyās tāvad rakṣakā yājñavalkyena parigaṇitāḥ |
rakṣet kanyāṃ pitā vinnāṃ patiḥ putrās tu vārdhake |
abhāve jñātayas teṣāṃ na svātantryaṃ kvacit striyāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.75)
manur api |
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane |
putras tu sthāvire bhāve na strī svāntantryam arhati || (MDh 9.3)
bālye pitur vaśe tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
putrasya sthāvire bhāve na strī svātantryam arhati || iti | (MD 5.148)
atra proktebhyo rakṣakebhyaḥ pitrādibhyo vyatiriktaḥ pumān para ity ucyate | tena puṃsā samanvitā prītyatiśayadyotakahāsyādivyavahārapuraḥsaraṃ samyaganvitā brāhmaṇī strī yadā kenacid vyājena grāmāntaraṃ deśāntaraṃ vā gatvā nivaset sā naṣṭeti vinirdeśyā bandhumadhye prakyāpanīyā | na tu tasyāḥ punaḥ svagṛhāgamanam asti | gṛhaṃ pratyāgatāpi nirvāsanīyety arthaḥ || 29 ||
parapuruṣeṇa saha yathoktasamanvayābhāve ‘pi svātantryeṇa ciraṃ nirgatā strī parityājyety āha |
kāmān mohāt tu yā gacchet tyaktvā bandhūn sutān patim || 30 ||
sātu naṣṭā pare loke mānuṣeṣu viśeṣataḥ || iti |
bandhvādīnām anyatamasya samīpe sthātavyam iti strīdharmaḥ | tathā ca taddharmaprakaraṇe yājñavalkya āha |
pitṛmātṛsutabhrātṛśvaśrūśvaśuramātulaiḥ |
hīnā na syād vinā bhartrā garhaṇīyānyathā bhavet || iti | (YDh 1.76)
evaṃ ca sati yā strī kāmād vā yathoktastrīdharmāparijñānād vā bandhvādīn parityajya grāmāntarādau ciraṃ vastuṃ gacchet sā tu śāstroktadharmollaṅghanāt paraloke naṣṭā narakaṃ prāpnoti | atha kathaṃcit kālāntare nirviṇṇā prāyaścittaṃ caritvā paralokaṃ jayet tathāpi mānuṣeṣu bandhvādiṣu sarvathā praveśaṃ na labhate – ity abhipretya viśeṣata ity uktam || 30 ||
[357]
uktārthasya nimittaviśeṣeṇāpavādam āha |
madamohagatā nārī krodhāt daṇḍāditāḍitā || 31 ||
advitīyā gatā caiva punar āgamanaṃ bhavet | iti |
madaḥ patiśvaśurāditiraskārajanako mānaso doṣaḥ | patyādiśuśrūṣā strīṇāṃ paramo dharma iti etādṛśasya vivekasyābhāvo mohaḥ | uktadoṣadvayopetāṃ nārīṃ śikṣayituṃ vṛddhāḥ patyādayo daṇḍādibhis tāḍayeyuḥ tadā vyathitā sā yathoktabandhvādisahāyaṃ vinā svayam ekākiny eva svecchayā yady api gacchet tathāpi svagṛhe punarāgamanaṃ prāpnuyād ity arthaḥ | “bhūḥ prāptau” ity asmād dhātos tacchabdaniṣpatteḥ | etac ca manunāpy abhihitam |
adhivinnā tu yā nārī nirgacched ruṣitā gṛhāt |
sā sadyaḥ saṃniroddhavyā tyājyā vā kulasaṃnidhau || (MDh 9.83)
[358] iti |
nanu – adhivedanaṃ nirgamananimittam upanyastam | mūlavacane tu tāḍanam iti vaiṣamyam iti cet,
na, tasyāprayojakatvāt, nirgamanabhraṃśābhāvayor ubhayatra tulyatvāt | atas tādṛśī nārī sāntvanādinā kenāpy upāyena gṛha eva niroddhavyā | yadi kathaṃcin niroddhum aśakyā tadāpi kulasaṃnidhau tyājyā taddoṣaśāntiparyantaṃ bandhumadhye sthāpanīyeti || 31 ||
yāvat punar āgamanaṃ bhaved ity atrāgamane pratīkṣaṇīyaṃ kālāvadhim āha |
daśame tu dine prāpte prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 32 ||
daśāhaṃ na tyajen nārīṃ tyajen naṣṭaśrutāṃ tathā | iti |
yā tāḍitā satī nirgatā, tasyāḥ punar āgamanapratīkṣāṃ daśa dināni kuryāt | daśame dine tayā gṛhe prāpte sati neyaṃ prāyaścittabhāg bhavati | ūrdhvaṃ tu kulastrī vyabhicārocitaprāyaścittabhāg bhavati | ato daśāhamadhye tadīyavyabhicārāśravaṇe tāṃ na parityajet | yadi naṣṭatvena sā śrūyate tadā daśāhamadhye tv akṛtaprāyaścittāṃ tāṃ parityajet || 32 ||
atha naṣṭāṃ śrutvāpi bhartrādayas tāṃ na parityajeyuḥ tadā teṣāṃ prāyaścittam āha |
bhartā caiva caret kṛcchraṃ kṛcchrārdhaṃ caiva bāndhavāḥ || 33 || iti |
spaṣṭārthaḥ || 33 ||
[359]
akṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ bhartrādīnāṃ gṛhe bhojanādikam ācarann upavāsena śuddhyatīy āha |
teṣāṃ bhuktvā ca pītvā ca ahorātreṇa śudhyati || iti |
spaṣṭam |
nanu – yā brāhmaṇī parapuṃśa samanvitā bhavati uktas tasyās tyāgaḥ | yā tu tāḍanena vā nimittāntareṇa vā nirgacchanty api na puruṣāntareṇa samanveti tasyāḥ ko nāma doṣaḥ yena daśāhād ūrdhvaṃ tasyā api tyāgo vidhīyate ity ata āha |
brāhmaṇī tu yadā gacchet parapuṃsā vivarjitā || 34 ||
gatvā puṃsāṃ śataṃ yāti tyajeyus tāṃ tu gotriṇaḥ | iti |
yady api krodhādinā nirgacchantī na tadānīṃ puruṣāntareṇa samanveti tathāpi gatvā kālāntareṇa śatasaṃkhyākeṣu puruṣeṣu saṃcaratīti matvā bāndhavās tāṃ parityajeyuḥ | brāhmaṇyā api bahupuruṣasaṃcāriṇyā gaṇikātvaṃ bhavati | tad āha prajāpatiḥ |
abhigacchati yā nārī bahubhiḥ puruṣair mithaḥ |
vyabhicāriṇīti sā jñeyā pratyakṣaṃ gaṇiketi ca || iti |
|| 34 ||
[360]
athoktāyā brāhmaṇyāś caṇḍālasamatvam abhipretya gṛhapraveśaṃ niṣedhati |
puṃso yadi gṛhe gacchet tad aśuddhaṃ gṛhaṃ bhavet || 35 ||
patimātṛgṛhaṃ yac ca jārasyaiva tu tad gṛham | iti |
seyaṃ durbrāhmaṇī svanivāsārthaṃ patyur vā mātur vā jārasyānyasya vā dākṣiṇyaviṣayasya kasyacid bandhor gṛhaṃ praviśati tad gṛhaṃ caṇḍālādhyuṣitagṛhavad atyantam apavitraṃ bhavati |
avijñātas tu caṇḍālo yatra veśmani tiṣṭhati | (PārSm 6.34)
ityādinā caṇḍālavāse tatpraveśe ca yathā gṛhaśuddhir abhihitā tathā puṃścalyā brāhmaṇyāḥ praveśe ‘pi gṛhaśuddhiḥ kartavyā || 35 ||
tacchuddhiprakāram ā adhyāyaparisamāpter darśayati |
ullikhya tadgṛhaṃ paścāt pañcagavyena secayet || 36 ||
tyajec ca mṛṇmayaṃ pātraṃ vastraṃ kāṣṭhaṃ ca śodhayet |
saṃbhārān śodhayet sarvān govālaiś ca phalodbhavān || 37 ||
tāmrāṇi pañcagavyena kāṃsyāni daśa bhasmabhiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ cared vipro brāhmaṇair upapāditam || 38 ||
godvayaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt prājāpatyadvayaṃ caret |
itareṣām ahorātraṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca śodhanam || 39 ||
[361]        upavāsair vrataiḥ puṇyaiḥ snānasaṃdhyārcanādibhiḥ |
japahomadayādānaiḥ śudhyante brāhmaṇādayaḥ || 40 ||
ākāśaṃ vāyur agniś ca medhyaṃ bhūmigataṃ jalam |
na praduṣyanti darbhāś ca yajñeṣu camasā yathā || 41 || iti |
ullekhanaṃ bhūmeḥ | tena kuḍyādilepanādikam upalakṣyate | pañcagavyasecanaṃ bhūmau kuḍyādiṣu ca samānam | mṛṇmayasya bhāṇḍasya tyāga eva | na tu pañcagavyasecanādibhiḥ śuddhiḥ | vastrakāṣṭhayor dhānyādisaṃbhārāṇāṃ ca dravyaśuddhyā yathoktaṃ śodhanaṃ kuryāt | nārikelakapitthālābubilvādiphalasaṃbhūtānāṃ pātrāṇāṃ govālair mārjanam | tāmrasyāmlādinā śuddhiḥ pūrvam uktā | atra tu pañcagavyeneti viśeṣaḥ | kāṃsyapātrāṇāṃ daśakṛtvo bhasmanā gharṣaṇam | gṛhasvāmī tu pariṣadvinirdiṣṭaṃ sadakṣiṇaṃ prājāpatyadvayaṃ caret | anyeṣāṃ tu tadgṛhavasinām upavāsaḥ | pañcagavyaprāśanaṃ vā | tadgṛhavāsibhiḥ saha vyavahartṝṇāṃ gṛhāntaravāsināṃ [362] brāhmaṇādīnāṃ nirdiṣṭenopavādīnām anyatamena śuddhiḥ | tadgṛhasaṃbandhinām ākāśādīnāṃ nirlepān [nirlepatvān?] na prayatnena saṃpādanīyā śuddhir asti | tatra dṛṣṭānto yajñeṣv iti | ṛtviṅmukhasamsparśe ‘pi camasānāṃ yathā nocchiṣṭadoṣaḥ | śāstradṛṣṭyā teṣāṃ nirlepasvabhāvāt | tadvad ākāśādīnām avagantavyam | atra caṇḍālīnyāyasyābhipretatvāt cirakālaviṣayam idaṃ pariśodhanaṃ draṣṭavyam | sakṛtpraveśe tu saṃmārjanādibhiḥ śuddhir nirdeṣṭavyā || 36–41 ||
gamyetarābhigamane sati niṣkṛtir yā
sā yatra sādhu hitakāraṇam abhyadhāyi |
adhyāyam ūrjitamatir daśamaṃ svayuktyā
vyākhyāt parāśarakṛtāv iha mādhavāryaḥ ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravartakaparameśvara-
śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhavāmātysya
kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ || 10 ||
ekādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ
nīlanīradanibhaṃ nirantaraṃ nirjitākhilaniśācaraṃ vayam |
manmahe ‘malaviśālalocanam mārutātmajavibhuṃ raghūdvaham || 1 ||
daśame ‘dhyāye hy upapātakaviśeṣasyāgamyāgamanasya prāyaścittam abhidhāya tatprasaṅgād gurutalpādīnām api prāyaścittam abhihitam | ekādaśādhyāye tv abhojyabhojanasya prāyaścittaṃ prādhānyenopavarṇyate | tac cānupātakam ity eke, surāpānasamatvena manunopavarṇanāt |
brahmaojjhatā vedanindā kūṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ |
garhitānādyayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || (MDh 11.56)
[364] iti | upapātakam ity apare, manunaiva,
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho ninditānnādanaṃ tathā | (MDh 11.64)
ityādinā kānicid anukramyānte,
nāstikyaṃ copapātakam | (MDh 11.66}
iti nigamanāt | atrāyaṃ nirṇayaḥ – yasminn abhakṣyaviśeṣe garhādhikyāt kāmakṛtād abhyāsād vā prāyaścittagauravaṃ smaryate tasyābhakṣyasya bhakṣaṇam anupātakam | yatra prāyaścittālpatvaṃ tad upapātakam | yathā pūrvatrāgamyāgamantvopādhinaikākāre ‘pi prāyaścittālpatvāt pāradāryam upapātakam | mātṛbhaginyādigamanaṃ prāyaścittabāhulyād atipātakam iti viñṇunābhihitam | evaṃ sarvatra nāmasāmye ‘pi gauravalāghavābhyāṃ tattadavāntaraviśeṣo niścetavyaḥ |
tatrādau tāvad amedhyādibhojane prāyaścittam āha |
amedhyareto gomāṃsaṃ caṇḍālānnam athāpi vā |
yadi bhuktaṃ tu vipreṇa kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 1 || iti |
amedhyaṃ viṇmūtrādi | tadupaspṛṣṭasyānnasya bhojanam amedhyabhojanam | na cātra kevalasyāmedhyasya vipreṇa bhojanaṃ kvacit saṃbhavati | tadupaspṛṣṭānnabhojanaṃ tu saṃbhāvyate, bālāpatyasahabhojanasya prācuryeṇa loke darśanāt | retobhojanaṃ tu galayonyādivyādhigrasteṣu saṃbhāvitam | tathā gomāṃsacaṇḍālānnabhojanaṃ bandigṛhītādiṣu | tatra sarvatra cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ | atha vā, caturvidhaṃ medhyam | tadviparītam amedhyam | tad āha devalaḥ |
vakṣyate ‘taḥ paraṃ sarvaṃ medhyāmedhyasamuccayam |
śuci pūtaṃ svayaṃ śuddhaṃ pavitraṃ ceti kevalam ||
medhyaṃ caturvidhaṃ loke prajānāṃ manur abravīt |
dūṣitaṃ kaśmalaṃ duṣṭaṃ varjitaṃ ceti liṅgataḥ ||
caturvidham amedhyaṃ ca sarvaṃ vyākhyāsyate punaḥ |
navaṃ vā nirmalaṃ vāpi śucīti dravyam ucyate ||
śuddhaṃ pavitrabhūtaṃ ca pūtam ity abhidhīyate |
svayam eva hi yad dravyaṃ kevalaṃ dhanyatāṃ gatam ||
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ vāpi svayaṃ śuddham iti smṛtiḥ |
anyad dravyair aduṣyaṃ yat tat pavitram iti smṛtiḥ ||
[366]        atha sarvāṇi dhānyāni dravyāṇy ābharaṇāni ca |
avarjyaṃ bhakṣajātaṃ vā śucīny etāni kevalam ||
ityādinā caturvidhaṃ medhyaṃ nirūpya dūṣitādicaturvidham amedhyam itthaṃ nirūpitam |
śucy apy aśucisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ dravyaṃ dūṣitam ucyate |
mānuṣāsthivasāviṣṭhāretomūtrārttavāni ca ||
kupūyaṃ pūyam ity etat kaśmalaṃ cāpy udāhṛtam |
svedāśrubindavaḥ phenaṃ nirastaṃ nakharoma ca ||
ārdracarmāsṛg ity etad duṣṭam āhur dvijātayaḥ |
vyaṅgaiḥ patitacaṇḍālair grāmyasūkarakukkuṭaiḥ ||
śvā ca nityaṃ vivarjyāḥ syuḥ ṣaḍ ete dharmataḥ samāḥ |
durvarṇakṛmikā bhūmir mattonmattarajasvalāḥ ||
mṛtabandhur aśuddhaś ca varjyate ca svakālataḥ | iti |
atra varṇitena caturvidhenāmedhyenopahatasyānnasya śuddhim akṛtvā bhojane cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | retomūtrādibhakṣaṇe cāndrāyaṇaṃ bṛhaspatir apy āha |
[367]        alehyānām apeyānām abhakṣyāṇāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ śuddhiś cāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
gomāṃsādibhakṣaṇe cāndrāyaṇaṃ saṃvarta āha |
gomāṃsaṃ mānuṣaṃ caiva sūtihastāt samāhṛtam |
abhakṣyaṃ tad dvijātīnāṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
śvabiḍālakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ kaper gomāyukākayoḥ |
prāśya mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā carec cāndrāyaṇavratam || iti | (SaṃSm 195, 192)
manur api |
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (MDh 11.154)
śātātapo ‘pi: “gomāṃsabhakṣaṇe prāyaścittaṃ pūrvam ṛṣibhiḥ kṛtam | samayaṃ nātikrāmet | yady atikrāmet tataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret” iti | vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi |
agamyāgamane caiva madyamogāṃsabhakṣaṇe |
śuddhyai cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt nadīṃ gatvā samudragām ||
cāndrāyaṇe tataś cīrṇe kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam |
anaḍutsahitāṃ gāṃ ca dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
[368] iti | śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “śṛgālakukkuṭadaṃṣṭrikravyādavānarakharoṣṭragajavāji-viḍvarāhagomānuṣamāṃsabhakṣaṇe cāndrāyaṇam” iti | cāṇḍālānnabhakṣaṇe cāndrāyaṇam aṅgirā āha |
antyāvasāyinām annam aśnīyād yady akāmataḥ |
sa tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā || iti | (AṅSm 3.16)
hārīto ‘pi |
cāṇḍālānnaṃ pramādena yadi bhuñjīta yo dvijaḥ |
tataś cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ caret || iti | (HārSm 21.7)
yat tu caturviṃśatimate ‘bhihitam,
viṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe vipraś carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
śvādīnāṃ caiva viṇmūtre carec cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
śvakākocchiṣṭagocchiṣṭakṛmikīṭādibhakṣaṇe |
amedhyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ carec cāndrāyaṇatrayam ||
iti, tad etad abhyāsaviṣayam | mūlavacane cāndrāyaṇarūpaṃ kṛcchraṃ cared ity abhipretya vyākhyātam | yadā tu – kṛcchram ācarec cāndrāyaṇam ācaret – iti vibhajya vyākhyāyate tadā kṛcchraśabdena ṛṣikṛcchrataptakṛcchraprājāptyāni tantreṇābhidhīyante | tatra ṛṣikṛcchram āha aṅgirāḥ |
[369]        alehyānām apeyānām abhakṣyāṇāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇām ṛṣikṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || iti | (AṅSm 3.41)
taptakṛcchram āha devalaḥ |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāśane ‘matipūrvake |
nāśnīyāt tu tryahaṃ matyā taptakṛcchraṃ cared dvijaḥ || iti |
prājāpatyam āha saṃvartaḥ |
viṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe vipraḥ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret | iti | (SaṃSm 189)
taptakṛcchrasya viṣayaviśeṣas tadvacane eva spaṣṭaḥ, “amatyā tryahopavāsaḥ | taptakṛcchraṃ matyā” iti tatrābhidhānāt | yat tu prājāpatyakṛcchraṃ tat bhakṣitodgāritaviṣayam, alpavratatvāt | ṛṣikṛcchraṃ tu pūrvoktacāndrāyaṇadvayena samānaviṣayam | atra sarvatra vratānuṣṭhānānantaraṃ punaḥsaṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ | tad āha yamaḥ |
asurāmadyapāne ca kṛte gobhakṣaṇe ‘pi vā |
taptakṛcchraparikliṣṭo mauñjīhomena śudhyati || iti |
manur api |
ajñānāt prāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭam eva ca |
punaḥsaṃskāram arhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ || (MDh 11.150)
[370] iti | viṣṇur api: “viḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭavānaragomāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca | sarveṣv eteṣu dvijānāṃ prāyaścittānte punaḥsaṃskāraṃ kuryāt” iti (ViDh 51.3–4) | tatra viśeṣam āha kāśyapaḥ: “cīrṇānte prācyām udīcyāṃ vā diśi gatvā yatra grāmyapaśūnāṃ śabdo na śrūyate tasmin deśe ‘gniṃ prajvālya brahmāsanam āstīrya prākpraṇītena vidhinā punaḥsaṃskāram arhati” iti | yac ca bṛhadyamenoktam,
varāhaikaśaphānāṃ ca kākakukkuṭayos tathā |
kravyādānāṃ ca sarveṣām abhakṣyā ye ca kīrtitāḥ ||
māṃsamūtrapurīṣāṇi prāśya gomāṃsam eva ca |
śvagomāyukapīnāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam ||
upoṣya dvādaśāhāni kūṣmāṇḍair juhuyād ghṛtam | iti |
tatra kāmataḥ taptakṛcchraḥ | abhyāse tu kūṣmāṇḍasahitaḥ parāka iti vyavasthā | yat tu uśanasoktam,
[371]        naramāṃsaṃ śvamāṃsaṃ ca gomāṃsaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
bhuktvā pañcanakhānāṃ ca mahāsāntapanaṃ caret || (UśSm 9.25)
iti, tad akāmaviṣayam | yat punaḥ śaṅkhenoktam,
bhuktvā cobhayatodantān tathaivaikaśaphān api |
auṣṭraṃ gavyaṃ tathā bhuktvā ṣaṇmāsān vratam ācaret || (ŚaṅDm 17.28)
iti, tat kāmato ‘tyantābhyāsaviṣayam | yac ca smṛtyantaroktam,
jagdhvā māṃsaṃ narāṇāṃ ca viḍvarāhaṃ kharaṃ tathā |
gavāśvakuñjaroṣṭrāṇāṃ sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā ||
kravyādaṃ kukkuṭaṃ grāmyaṃ kuryāt saṃvatsaravratam |
iti, tad atyantānavicchinnābhāsaviṣayam | yad apy āṅgirasenoktam,
abhakṣyāṇām apeyānām alehyānāṃ ca bhojane |
retomūtrapurīṣāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet ||
padmodumbarabilvānāṃ kuśāśvatthapalāśayoḥ |
eteṣām udakaṃ pītvā saptarātreṇa śudhyati || (AṅSm 3.27–28)
[372] iti, tad īṣadabhyāsaviṣayam | yad api manunoktam,
bhuktvātonyatamasyānnam amatyā tu tryahaṃ kṣapet |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva ca || (MDh 4.222)
iti, abuddhipūrve cchardanāsaṃbhave tryahopavāso draṣṭavyaḥ | gomāṃśabhakṣaṇasyāvṛttyanāvṛttyor vyavasthitaṃ vratam āha prajāpatiḥ |
cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ kuryād abhakṣyapaśubhakṣaṇe |
ekam eva sakṛd bhuktvā cared āha prajāpatiḥ || iti |
mūlavacane caṇḍālānnam ity anena mahāpātakyādīnām apy upalakṣyate | ata eva hārītaḥ |
yad annaṃ pratilomasya śūdrajasyottamastriyām |
mahāpātakinaś caiva yad annaṃ strīkṛtaghnayoḥ ||
ārūḍhapatitasyaiva sagotrābhartur eva ca |
pāṣaṇḍānāśritānāṃ ca yateś caiva tathaiva ca ||
atikṛcchraṃ cared bhuktvā pramādād brāhmaṇaḥ sakṛt |
matyā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryād āmaṃ ced argham eva ca ||
taddhastabhojane vāpi triguṇaṃ sahabhojane |
caturguṇaṃ tad ucchiṣṭe pānīye cārdham eva ca ||
[373]        kṛcchrābdapādam uddiṣṭam abhyāsājñānabhojane |
matyābhyāse tathā kuryāt triṃśat kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ || iti | (HārSm 19.31–35)
uttamastrī brāhmaṇī tasyāṃ prātilomyena śudrād utpannaś caṇḍālaḥ | mahāpātakī brahmahādiḥ | yady api strīyatyor mahāpātakyādivad duṣṭatavaṃ nāsti tathāpy annadānādhikārābhāvāt tadīyam apy annaṃ pātakyannavad garhitam eva | yas tu pareṇopakṛtaḥ san svayaṃ na pratyupakaroti nāpy anumodate pratyutāpadgatasyāpakāritvam āpādayati sa kṛtaghnaḥ | uttamāśramam āruhya taṃ parityajyādhamāśrame unmārge vā vartamāna ārūḍhapatitaḥ | yaḥ samānagotrām udvahati sa sagotrābhartā | vaidikakula samutpanno vedamārgam utsṛjya jainabauddhādimārge vartamānaḥ pāṣaṇḍaḥ | anāśritāḥ saty api sāmarthye nāstikyena kaṃcid apy āśramaṃ na prāptāḥ | eteṣāṃ sarveṣām anne samānaṃ prāyaścittam || 1 ||
viprasyāmedhyādibhojane prāyaścittam abhidhāyetareṣāṃ varṇānāṃ tad āha |
[374]        tathaiva kṣatriyo vaiśyo ‘py ardhaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
śūdro ‘py evaṃ yadā bhuṅkte prājāpatyaṃ samācaret || 2 || iti |
tathaivety atidiṣṭasyopadiṣād alpatvena kṣatriyasya pādonaṃ cāndrāyaṇam avagantavyam | śūdro ‘py evam ity anenāmedhyādibhojanasāmyaṃ nirdiśyate | na tu bhojanasāmyam, vratāntarasya tatra vidhānāt || 2 ||
vratānte caturbhir varṇaiḥ kartavyam āha |
pañcagavyaṃ pibet śūdro brahmakūrcaṃ pibed dvijaḥ |
ekadvitricaturgā vā dadyād viprādyanukramāt || 3 || iti |
pañcagavyabrahmakūrcayor vidhānam uttaratra vakṣyate | tatra śūdrasyāmantrasyāmantrakaṃ pañcagavyam | itareṣāṃ samantrakaṃ brahmakūrcam | vipro gām ekāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | kṣatriyo dve | vaiśyas tisraḥ | śūdraś catasraḥ | gavām asaṃbhave suvarṇaṃ dadyād ity abhipretya vāśabdaḥ paṭhitaḥ | tatra suvarṇasya gopratyāmnāyasya parimāṇam āha prajāpatiḥ |
[375]        gavām alābhe niṣkaṃ vā niṣkārdhaṃ pādam eva vā | iti || 3 ||
viprasya śūdrādyannabhojane prāyaścittam āha |
śūdrānnaṃ sūtakānnaṃ ca abhojyasyānnam eva ca |
śaṅkitaṃ pratiṣiddhānnaṃ pūrvocchiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca || 4 ||
yadi bhuktaṃ tu vipreṇa ajñānād āpadāpi vā |
jñātvā samācaret kṛcchraṃ brahmakūrcaṃ tu pāvanam || 5 || iti |
atra śūdrānnasya garhitatā aṅgirasā pracañcitā |
āhitāgnis tu yo vipraḥ śūdrānnaṃ pratigṛhya tu |
bhogāt tāmasatāṃ yāti tiryagyoniṃ ca gacchati ||
yas tu vedam adhīyānaḥ śūdrānnam upabhuñjati |
śūdre vedaphalaṃ yāti śūdratvaṃ cāpi gacchati ||
ghrātvā caivoṣmaṇā caiva spṛṣṭvā ca pratigṛhya ca |
praśasya svasti cety uktvā bhoktaiva tu na saṃśayaḥ ||
[376]        ete doṣā bhavantīha śūdrānnasya pratigrahe |
anugrahaṃ tu vakṣyāmi manunā coditaṃ purā ||
āmaṃ vā yadi vā pakvaṃ śūdrānnam upasādhayet |
kilbiṣaṃ buñjate bhoktā yaś ca vipraḥ purohitaḥ ||
gurvarthaṃ hy atithīnāṃ tu bhṛtyānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
pratigṛhya pradātavyaṃ na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ ||
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭasya adhīyānasya nityaśaḥ |
japato juhvato vāpi gatir ūrdhvā na vidyate ||
ṣaṇmāsān atha yo bhuṅkte śūdrasyānnaṃ nirantaram |
jīvanneva bhavet śūdro mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate ||
akṛtvaiva nivṛttiṃ yaḥ śūdrānnān mriyate dvijaḥ |
āhitāgnir viśeṣeṇa sa śūdragatibhāg bhavet || iti | (AṅSm 3.7–17)
vyāso ‘pi |
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭasya ditsor api ca nityaśaḥ |
japato juhvato vāpi gatir ūrdhvā na vidyate ||
[377]        mṛtasūtakapuṣṭāṅgo dvijaḥ śūdrānnabhojanaḥ |
aham eva na jānāmi kāṃ kāṃ yoniṃ sa gacchati ||
gṛdhro dvādaśajanmāni daśajanmāni sūkaraḥ |
śvā caiva saptajanmāni ity evaṃ manur abravīt ||
śūdrānnenodarasthena yaḥ kaścin mriyate dvijaḥ |
sa bhavet sūkaro grāmyas tasya vā jāyate kule || iti | (VySm 4.63–66)
āpastambo ‘pi |
yas tu bhuñjīta śūdrānnaṃ māsam ekaṃ nirantaram |
iha janmani śūdratvaṃ mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate ||
[378]        śūdrānnaṃ śūdrasaṃparkaḥ śūdreṇa ca sahāsanam |
śūdrāj jñānāgamaś caiva jvalantam api pātayet ||
āhitāgnis tu yo vipraḥ śūdrānnena pravartayet |
pañca tasya praṇaśyanti ātmā brahma trayo ‘gnayaḥ || iti | (ĀpSm 8.7–9)
sumantur api |
rājānnaṃ taja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaś carmāvakṛntinaḥ ||
kārukānnaṃ prajā hanti balaṃ nirṇejakasya ca |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati ||
pūyaṃ cikitsikasyānnaṃ puṃścalyāś cānnam indriyam |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ śastravikrayiṇo malam ||
etebhyo ‘nye tv abhojyānnāḥ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || (=MDh 4.218–21)
[379] iti | tad idaṃ garhitaṃ śudrānnam ajñānād vāpatkāle vā yo vipro bhuṅkte sa brahmakūrcam ācaret | tasya pāvanatvaṃ bahumantrasaṃskṛtatvāt | yas tu jñātvā śūdrānnaṃ bhuṅkte sa prājāpatyam ācaret | tathā ca manuḥ |
bhuñjāno ‘nyatamasyānnam amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
matyā bhuktvā caret kṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtram eva ca || iti | (MDh 4.222)
na ca – brahmakūrcasyaikopavāsarūpatvāt tryahakṣapaṇena virodhaḥ – iti śaṅkanīyam, tayorḥ śaktāśaktaviṣayatvāt | atra dinatrayopavāsaṃ śaṅkhalikhito ‘py āha |
bhuktvā vārdhuṣikasyānnam avṛttasyāsutasya ca |
śūdrasya tu tathā bhuktvā trirātraṃ syād abhojanam || iti | (ŚaṅSm 11.18)
prajāpatiś cātra vyavasthitāni vratāny āha |
brāhmaṇasyaiva śūdrānnam abhojyaṃ parikīrtitam |
sakṛd bhuktvā tadajñānāḍ brahmakūrcaṃ sakṛt pibet ||
abhyāse ca tad abhyased āmam annam agarhitam |
matyā trirātraṃ kurvīta kṛcchram abhyāsataś caret || iti |
[382] narabaliṃ saṃkalpya manuṣyān mārayitum annamadhye gūḍhaṃ viṣaṃ prakṣipantīty asti mahatī prasiddhiḥ | tādṛśeṣu gṛheṣu viṣaśaṅkāsaṃbhavāt śaṅkitam annam ity ucyate | tat tu na bhoktuṃ yogyam,
viṣaṃ copaviṣaṃ bhuktvā carec cāndrāyaṇavratam |
iti caturviṃśatimate viṣabhojananindāsmaraṇāt | tathā kecit śraddhālavaḥ śūdrādayaḥ svagṛhe śrotriyo na bhuṅkte ity abhipretya śrotriyāntaragṛhe dravyaṃ datvā bhojayanti | tādṛśasya śrotriyāntarasya gṛhe siddham annaṃ nindyatvena śaṅkitam | “pratiṣiddhānnam” goghrātādi | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
[383]        anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsaṃ keśakīṭasamanvitam |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitocchiṣṭaṃ śvaspṛṣṭaṃ patitekṣitam ||
goghrātaṃ śakunocchiṣṭaṃ padāspṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ |
udakyāspṛṣṭasaṃghuṣṭaṃ paryāyānnaṃ ca varjayet || iti | (YDh 1.167–68)
pātre patitasyānnasyārdhaṃ bhuktvā śiṣṭam annaṃ kālāntare bhoktuṃ yadi saṃgṛhyate tadā tad annaṃ pūrvocchiṣṭam ity abhidhīyate | teṣv eteṣv sūtakānnādiṣu pūrvocchiṣṭānteṣu pañcasu bhukteṣu śūdrānnavad brahmakūrcakṛcchrau veditavyau | sūtakānne ye vaktavyaviśeṣās te sarve ‘py upariṣṭād vakṣyante | yat tv abhojyānne sumantunoktam: “abhiśastapatitapaunarbhava-puṃścalyaśuciśastrakāratailikacākrikadhvajisuvarṇakāralekhakalaiṅgika-[384]-ṣaṇḍaka-bandhakagaṇakagaṇikānnāni cābhojyāni | saunikakravyādaniṣādaburuḍacarmakārā abhojyānnā apratigrāhyāś ca aśanapratigrahayoś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret” iti, tat kāmakṛtābhyāsaviṣayam | yac ca śaṅkhenoktam,
śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo bhuktvā tathā raṅgāvatāriṇaḥ |
cikitsakasya krūrasya tathā strīśūdrajīvinaḥ ||
ṣaṇḍhasya kulaṭāyāś ca tathā bandhanarakṣiṇaḥ |
abhiśastasya corasya avīrāyāḥ striyās tathā ||
[385]        carmakārasya vaiṇasya klībasya patitasya ca |
karmārasya tathā takṣṇo rajakasya ca vārdhuṣeḥ ||
kadaryasya nṛśaṃsasya veśyāyāḥ kitavasya ca |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca mṛgajīviśvavṛttinām ||
saunikānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ bhuktvā māsaṃ vratī bhavet || (ŚaṅSm 17.36–38)
iti, etac ca pūrveṇa samānaviṣayam, māsavratasya cāndrāyaṇarūpatvāt | yad api bṛhaspatinoktam |
śauṇḍikānnaṃ naṭasyānnaṃ klībadaṇḍikayor api |
duṣṭānnam apasṛṣṭānnaṃ somavikrayiṇas tathā ||
saunikānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ vārdhuṣeḥ patitasya ca |
eteṣāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuktvā dvādaśāhaṃ yavān pibet || (BṛSm 1.31–32)
iti, yad api śaṅkhalikhitābhyām uktam: “abhiśastapatitarajakacākrikatailikagrāmayājakaśūdra-sāṃvatsarikakulaṭāsuvarṇakāracarmakāracitravṛttipoṣakatantuvāyaraṅgāvatārimānakūṭa-śauṇḍikavadhajīvi-[386]-nṛśaṃsyātmavikrayivārdhuṣikaśyāvakanaṭavṛttivrātyataskara-gaṇānnabhojaneṣv atikṛcchraṃ caret” iti, etad ubhayaṃ pūrvoktaviṣaye cāndrāyaṇaṃ kartum aśaktaṃ prati veditavyam | yad api bṛhaspatinā darśitam,
gāndharvo lohakāraś ca saunikas tantavāyakaḥ |
cakropajīvī rajakaḥ kitavas taskaras tathā ||
yajamānopajīvī ca śūdrādhyāpakayājakau |
kulaṭaś citrakarmā ca vārdhuṣiś carmavikrayī ||
samarghaṃ paṇyam āhṛtya samhārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma yaś ca vṛddhyā prayojayet ||
vṛthārāmāśramāṇāṃ ca bhedakaḥ puṇyavikrayī |
[387]        vikrayī brahmaṇo yaś ca yonisāṃkarikaś ca yaḥ ||
raṅgopajīvī kūṭāśaḥ kuhako guruguptikaḥ |
bhiṣak ca garadaś caiva rūpājīvī ca sūcakaḥ ||
sauniko varṇikaś caiva niṣādena samāḥ smṛtāḥ |
karmaṇā teṣu yo mohād brāhmaṇo vartate sadā ||
prāyaścitte tu carite vyavahāryo bhavet sa hi |
ete brāhmaṇacaṇḍālāḥ sarve brahmahaṇaḥ kila ||
tasmād daive ca pitrye ca varjitās tattvadarśibhiḥ |
eteṣām eva sarveṣāṃ pratyāpattis tu mṛgyatām ||
bhaikṣyānnam upabhuñjānā dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
eteṣāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuktvā datvā kṛtvā pratigraham ||
prājāpatyena śudhyet tu tasmāt pāpān na saṃśayaḥ | iti | (BṛSm 1.30–31?)
tatra cāndrāyaṇaṃ pūrvoktacāndrāyaṇena samānaviṣayam | prājāpatyaṃ tu tasminn eva viṣaye vedavidaṃ pratyavagantavyam, brāhmaṇa iti viśeṣābhidhānāt | yac coktam aṅgirasā,
[388]        abhojyānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ bhuktvā cānnam upaskṛtam |
antyāvasāyināṃ bhuktvā pādakṛcchreṇa śudhyati || iti | (AṅSm 3.19)
yad api viṣṇunoktam,
gaṇagaṇikāstenagāyanānnāni bhuktvā saptarātraṃ payasā varteta | takṣānnaṃ carmakartuś ca | vārdhuṣikakadaryadīkṣitabaddhanigaḍābhiśastaṣaṇḍānāṃ ca | puṃścalīdāmbhikacikitsakalubdhakakrūrocchiṣṭabhojināṃ ca | avīrāstrīsvarṇakārasapatnapatitānāṃ ca | piśunānṛtavādikṣatadharmātmarasa-vikrayiṇāṃ ca | śaulūṣatantuvāyakṛtaghnarajakānāṃ ca | carmakāraniṣāda-raṅgāvatārivaiṇaśastravikrayiṇāṃ ca | śvajīviśauṇḍikatailikacailanirṇejakānāṃ ca | [389] rajasvalāsahopapativeśmanāṃ ca | bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitam udakyāsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ śunā saṃspṛṣṭaṃ gavāghrātaṃ ca | kāmataḥ pādaspṛṣṭam avakṣataṃ ca | mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca | anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsaṃ ca | pāṭhīnarohitarājīvasiṃhatuṇḍaśakulavarjaṃ sarvamatsyamāṃsāśane trirātram upavaset | sarvajalajamāṃsāśane ca | iti | (ViDh 51.7–22)
tatra trirātropavāso ‘kāmakṛtasakṛdbhojanaviṣayaḥ | tasminn eva viṣaye mūlavacanoktabrahmakūrcas tv aśaktaṃ prati veditavyaḥ | saptarātrapayovratam akāmakṛtābhyāsaviṣayam | śaṅkitapratiṣiddhānnayor hārīta āha |
mṛtasūtakaśūdrānnaṃ sadoṣeṇāpi saṃskṛtam |
śaṅkitaṃ pratiṣiddhānnaṃ vidviṣo ‘nnam athāpi vā ||
yadi bhuñjīta vipro yaḥ prāyaścittī dhruvaṃ bhavet |
ekarātropavāsaś ca gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet ||
[390]        prāśayet pañcabhir mantraiḥ pañcagavyaṃ pṛthak pṛthak |
etena śudhyate vipro hy anyaiś cābhojyabhojanaiḥ || iti | (HārSm 13.1–3)
etac ca brahmakūrcavad vyākhyeyam | pūrvocchiṣṭe tu brahmakūrcasamānaṃ vrataṃ smṛtyantare darśitam |
svam ucchiṣṭaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte yo bhukkte muktabhājane |
evaṃ vaivasvataḥ prāha bhuktvā sāntapanaṃ caret || iti || 5 ||
pūrvocchiṣṭasya punar bhojane vratācaraṇam abhihitam | tenaiva dṛṣṭāntena bālādyucchiṣṭasyābhojyatvam āśaṅkya svapaśuddhyā bhojyatvam āha |
bālair nakulamārjārair annam ucchiṣṭaṃ yadā |
tiladarbhodakaiḥ prokṣya śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 6 || iti |
ucchiṣṭam ucchiṣṭatām āpāditam | prokṣitasyānnasya bhojane śraddhājaḍasyocchiṣṭatva-śaṅkānapāyāt punar vratam ācaraṇīyam [391] iti saṃśyaḥ syāt | ato nātra saṃśayaḥ kartavyaḥ ity uktam | prokṣaṇam akṛtvā bhukte sati pañcagavyaṃ pātavyam | tad āha saṃvartaḥ |
śvakākocchiṣṭagocchiṣṭabhakṣane tu tryahaṃ kṣapet |
biḍālamūṣakocchiṣṭe pañcagavyaṃ pibet dvijaḥ || iti | (SaṃSm 189–90)
śāstrīyapañcagavyapāne yo ‘śaktaḥ taṃ praty uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
bhuktvocchiṣṭaṃ tu kākānāṃ viśuddhyeta tryahoṣitaḥ |
bhuktvocchiṣṭaṃ biḍālādeḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet dvijaḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibed brāhmīṃ suvarcalām || (MDh 11.159)
[392] iti | viṣṇur api |
biḍālakākanakulākhūcchiṣṭabhojane brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ pibet | śvocchiṣṭāśane dinam ekam upoṣitaḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet | pañcanakhaviṇmūtrāśane saptarātram | āmaśrāddhāśane trirātraṃ payasā varteta | brāhmaṇaḥ śudrocchiṣṭabhojane trirātram upavaset | [393] dinam ekaṃ codake vaset | madhumāṃsāśane prājāpatyaṃ saptarātram | vaiśyocchiṣṭabhojane pañcarātram | rājanyocchiṣṭabhojane trirātram | rājanyaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśi pañcarātram | vaiśyocchiṣṭāśī trirātram | vaiśyaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśī ca | caṇḍālānnaṃ bhuktvā trirātram upavaset | siddhānnaṃ bhuktvā parākam” iti (ViDh 51.46–59) |
uśanā api |
brāhmaṇocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kuryāt | kṣatriyocchiṣṭabhojane prāṇāyāmasahasram | vaiśyocchiṣṭabhojane daśasahasraṃ prāṇāyāmam | matipūrvaṃ prājāpatyam atikṛcchraṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ ca | śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane saptarātraṃ yavāgūpānam | matipūrvaṃ cat parākaḥ | evaṃ dvijātyucchiṣṭabhojane śūdrāṇāṃ ca | brāhmaṇocchiṣṭānām apāṃ pāne ṣoḍaś prāṇāyāmān dhārayet | [394] kṣatriyāṇāṃ triṃśat vaiśyānāṃ catvāriṃśat | śūdrāṇāṃ kuśavāripānaṃ tryaham | buddhipūrvaṃ cat tryahaṃ caturahaṃ pañcarātraṃ saptarātraṃ ca pañcagavyapānaṃ krameṇa | antyajānām ucchiṣṭabhojane buddhipūrvaṃ mahāsāntapanam | abuddhipūrve cāndrāyaṇa || iti | (UśSm 3.20–32)
manur api |
śudrocchiṣṭāś pītvāpaḥ kuśavāri pibet tryaham |
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ striīśūdocchiṣṭam eva ca ||
jagdhvā māṃsam abhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || iti | (MDh 11.148–52)
jātūkarṇyo ‘pi |
jambūkakākamārjāraśvagodhānāṃ gavām api |
matyocchiṣṭaṃ dvijo bhuktvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret ||
[395] iti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
viprārdhaṃ kṣatriyasya syāt vaiśyānāṃ ca tadardhakam |
tadardham eva śūdrāṇāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidur budhāḥ || iti | (YDh 3.19)
bṛhadviṣṇuḥ: “brāhmaṇaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśane saptarātraṃ pañcagavyaṃ pibet | vaiśyocchiṣṭāśane pañcarātram | rājanyocchiṣṭāśane trirātram | brāhmaṇocchiṣṭāśane ekāham” iti (ViDh 51.50–53) | śaṅkhalikhitau: “brāhmaṇocchiṣṭāśane mahāvyāhṛtibhir abhimantryāpaḥ pibet | kṣatriyocchiṣāśane brāhmīrasapakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa vartayet | vaiśyocchiṣṭabhājanaṃ trirātropoṣito [396] brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ pibet | śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane ṣaḍrātram abhojanam” iti | yamaḥ |
bhuktvā saha brāhmaṇena prājāpatyena śudhyati |
būbhujā saha bhuktvānnaṃ taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati ||
vaiśyena saha bhuktvānnam atikṛcchreṇa śudhyati |
śūdreṇa saha bhuktvānnaṃ cāndrāyaṇam athācaret || iti | (YaSm 6.1–2)
āpastambaḥ: “śūdrocchiṣṭabhojane tu saptarātram abhojanam | strīṇām” iti (ĀpDh 1.9.26.4–5) | śaṅkhaḥ |
śūdrocchiṣṭāśane māsaṃ pakṣam ekaṃ tathā viśaḥ |
kṣatriyasya tu saptāhaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tathā dinam || (ŚaṅSm 17.42)
[397] iti | vṛddhayamaḥ |
mātā vā bhaginī vāpi bhāryā vānyāś ca yoṣitaḥ |
na tābhiḥ saha bhoktavyaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (YaSm 6.9)
āpastambaḥ |
caṇḍālapatitādīnām ucchiṣṭānnasya bhakṣaṇe |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ cared vipraḥ kṣatraḥ sāntapanaṃ caret ||
ṣaḍrātraṃ ca trirātraṃ ca varṇayor anupūrvaśaḥ |\ iti | (ĀpSm 3.63–64)
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
pītaśeṣaṃ tu yat kiṃcid bhājane sukhaniḥsṛtam |
