abhojyaṃ tad vijānīyād bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
pītocchiṣṭaṃ tu pānīyaṃ pītvā tu brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryād vāmahastena vā punaḥ ||
[398] iti | śātātapaḥ |
śūdrasyocchiṣṭabhojī tu trirātropoṣitaḥ śuciḥ |
surābhāṇḍodakaṃ pitvā chardito ghṛtasevakaḥ ||
ahorātropavāsena śuddhim āpnoti vai dvijaḥ || iti | (ŚāSm 5.78)
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
dīpocchiṣṭaṃ tu yat tailaṃ rātrau rathyāhṛtaṃ tu yat |
abhyaṅgāc caiva yac chiṣṭaṃ bhuktvā naktena śudhyati || iti |
atra bālocchiṣṭaprasaṅgād udāhṛteṣu vacaneṣv ekasmin viṣaye śrūyamāṇānāṃ bahūnāṃ vratānām āvṛttitāratamyaviṣayatvena vyavasthā varṇanīyā || 6 ||
bhuktocchiṣṭabhojane prāyaścittam abhidhāya paṅktyucchiṣṭabhojane prāyaścittam āh |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ viprāṇāṃ saha bhojane |
yady eko ‘pi tyajet pātraṃ śeṣam annaṃ na bhojayet || 7 ||
mohād bhuñjīta yas tatra paṅktāv ucchiṣṭabhājane |
prāyaścittaṃ cared vipraḥ kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ tathā || 8 ||
[399] iti | eko vipraḥ sahasā svabhojane samāpte vā bhojanamadhye yena kenacid vighnena vā bhojanapātraṃ parityajya itareṣu vipreṣu bhuñjāneṣv eva satsu svayam utthāya yadi gacchet tadā viprāntaraiḥ svasvapatreṣu bhuktāvaśiṣṭam annaṃ na bhoktavyam | tad imaṃ śāstrīyaniṣedham ajñātvā yo bhuṅkte sa sāntapanaṃ caret | nimittasyālpatvād atra dvirātrasāntapanaṃ veditavyam | asminn eva viṣaye samānaṃ vrataṃ smṛtyantare darśitam |
yas tu bhuṅkte dvijaḥ paṅktāv ucchiṣṭāyām kadācana |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || iti | (AṅSm 5.1)
vahnyādivyavadhāne paṅktyucchiṣṭadoṣo nāsti | etac cācārakāṇḍe darśitam || 7–8 ||
abhinavakṣīrādibhojane prāyaścittam āha |
pīyūṣaṃ śvetalaśunaṃ vṛntākaphalagṛñjane |
palāṇḍuvṛkṣaniryāsadevasvakavakāni ca || 9 ||
uṣṭrīkṣīram avikṣīram ajñānād bhuñjate dvijaḥ |
trirātram upavāsena pañcagavyena śudhyati || 10 || iti |
pīyūṣam abhnavaṃ payaḥ | payaso ‘bhinavatvaṃ prasavakālīnatvam ity eke | daśāhāntaḥpātitvam ity apare | daśāhād ūrdhvam api [4000] prasavaprayuktaraktādyārdratāsadbhāvakālīnatvam iti kecit | śvetaśabdo laśunavṛntākābyāṃ saṃbadhyate | tena raktalaśunakṛṣṇavṛntākādau nedaṃ prāyaścittam ity uktaṃ bhavati | gṛñjanaṃ patraviśeṣaḥ | yadīyaṃ cūrṇaṃ gāyakāḥ kaṇṭhaśuddhyarthaṃ bhakṣayanti | veśyāś ca madādyartham | mūlaviśeṣo vā gājarāparaparyāyaḥ | palāṇḍuḥ sthūlakando laśunaviśeṣaḥ | vṛkṣaniryāso hiṅgukarpūrādivyatiriktaḥ, hiṅguvādīnāṃ bhojyatvābhyanujñānāt | devatārtham upakalpite kṣetrādāv utpannam annaṃ devasvam | kavakaṃ bhūmau chatrādyākāreṇotpannaṃ śilīndraśabdavācyam | pīyūṣādibhojī trirātram upoṣya caturthe divase pañcagavyaṃ pibet | matipūrve tu bhojane manur āha |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ || (MDh 5.19)
[401] iti | amatipūrve sa evāha |
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.20)
tatra saptarātrātmakaṃ sāntapanaṃ veditavyam, “abuddhipūrve sāntapanaṃ saptarātraṃ vā” (GDh 23.25) iti gautamena saptarātropavāsasamavikalpasmaraṇāt | evaṃ ca sati mūlavacanoktatryahopavāso bhakṣitodgāritaviṣayo draṣṭavyaḥ | cāndrāyaṇaṃ tv āvṛttiviṣayam | “śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ” ity etat kusumbhādiṣu draṣṭavyam | tathā ca caturviṃśatimate |
palāṇḍuṃ śvetavṛntākaṃ kusumbhaṃ vanakukkuṭam |
nālikāṃ bālapuṣpaṃ ca bhuktvā dinam abhojanam || iti |
na caitasya palāṇḍor mūlavacanavirodhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ, avāntarajātibhedena vyavasthāpanāt, “palāṇḍor daśa jātayaḥ” [402] ity abhidhānāt | yat tu sumantunoktam: “laśunaplāṇḍugṛñjana-bhakṣaṇe sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa mūrdhni saṃpātān nayet” iti, tad balātkāreṇānicchato bhakṣaṇaviṣayam | tadaikasādhyavyādhyupaśamanārthaṃ bhakṣaṇaviṣayaṃ vā | ata evānantaraṃ tenaivoktam: “etāny evāturasya bhiṣakkriyāyām apratiṣiddhāni bhavanti | yāni cānyāny evaṃprakārāṇi teṣv apy adoṣaḥ” iti | yāni tu vratāntarāṇy anukrāntapīyūṣādiviṣaye munibhiḥ smaryante teṣu sarveṣu vratagauravānusāreṇa vṛttigauravaviṣayatvaṃ kalpanīyam | vratāntarāṇi tu likhyante | tatra śātātapaḥ |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ jagdhvā pāṇḍuṃ ca tathā śunīm |
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca grāmyaṃ kukkuṭam eva ca ||
uṣṭrīṃ ca mahiṣīṃ bhāsīṃ rāsabhīkṣīrabhojanāt |
upanayanaṃ punaḥ kuryāt taptakṛcchraṃ caren muhuḥ || iti | (ŚāSm 5.3)
yājñavalkyaḥ |
palāṇḍuṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca chatrākaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
[403]        laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva jagdhvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (YDh 1.176)
bṛhadyamaḥ |
nālikāṃ nālikerīṃ ca śleṣmātakaphalāni ca |
bhūtṛṇaṃ śugrukaṃ caiva khaṭvāṅgaṃ kavakaṃ tathā ||
eteṣāṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyaṃ cared dvijaḥ | iti | (YaSm 3.49)
viṣṇuḥ: “śunāṃ māṃsāśane ca | chatrākakavakāśane sāntapanam | yavagodhūmajaṃ payovikāraṃ snehāktaṃ ca śuṣkakhāṇḍavaṃ ca varjayitvā paryuṣitaṃ prāśyopavaset | vraścanāmedhyaprabhavān lohitāṃś ca vṛkṣaniryāsān | śeluṃ ca vṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpa-śaṣkulīś ca devānnāni havīṃṣi ca | go’jāvimahiṣīvarjaṃ sarvapayāṃsi ca | anirdaśāhaitāny api ca | syandinīsandhinīvivatsākṣīraṃ ca | [404] amedhyabhujaś ca | dadhivarjaṃ kevalaṃ sarvaśuktāni | brahmacaryāśramī śrāddāśane trirātram upavaset | dinam ekaṃ codake vaset” iti (ViSm 51.33–44) | yamo ‘pi |
laśunaṃ ca palāṇḍuṃ ca gṛñjanaṃ kavakāni ca |
caturṇāṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati || iti | (YaSm 3.19)
kūrmapurāṇe |
alābuṃ kiṃśukaṃ caiva bhuktvāpy etad vrataṃ caret |
udumbaraṃ ca kāmena taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati || iti | (KūPu 1.2.33.21)
śaṅkhalikhitau: “laśunapalāṇdu gṛñjanakriyātukusumbhabhakṣaṇe dvādaśarātraṃ payaḥ pibet | alābuśugruśleṣmātakakovidāraghanachatrākavṛtākabhakṣaṇe pañcagavyaṃ pibet” iti | caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva tṛṇarājaphalaṃ tathā |
vallīṃ caiva dvijo bhuktvā carec cāndrāyaṇavratam ||
kandamūlaphalādīni ajñānād bhakṣayet tu yaḥ |
upavāso bhavet tasya parāśaravaco yathā || iti |
tathā |
strīkṣīraṃ tu dvijaḥ pītvā kathaṃcit kāmamohitaḥ |
punaḥ saṃskṛtya cātmānaṃ prājāpatyaṃ samācaret || iti |
saṃvartaḥ |
ajoṣṭrīsandhinīkṣīraṃ mṛgāṇāṃ vanacāriṇām |
anirdaśāhagoś caiva pītvā dinam abhojanam || iti | (SaṃSm 106)
|| 9–10 ||
pūrvatra gomāṃsasyaiva bhakṣaṇe prāyaścittam uktam | na tv anyasya | idānīṃ māṃsāntarasya bhakṣaṇe prāyaścittam āha |
maṇḍūkaṃ bhakṣayitvā tu mūṣikāmāṃsam eva ca |
jñātvā vipras tv ahorātraṃ yāvakānnena śudhyati || 11 ||
ajñānakṛta sakṛdbhakṣaṇe vratam idam avagantavyam | asakṛdbhakṣaṇe tu uśanasoktaṃ draṣṭavyam: “balākaplavahaṃsakaṃṭakacakravākakhañjrīṭagṛhakapotabhāracaṭaka-raktapādolūkaśukasārasa-[406]-ṭiṭṭibhamadgucāṣabhāsajālapādanakrakrucikavikṛtamatsya-kravyādāmedhyānāṃ bhakṣaṇe pañcagavyaṃ pibet trirātram” iti (UṣSm 2.17) | śaṅkhalikhitau matipūrve pratyāhatuḥ |
bakabalākahaṃsaplavacakravākakhañjarīṭakākakāraṇḍavakaṭabhārakacaṭaka-gṛhakapotaśukasārasaṭiṭṭibholūkaraktapādajālapādacāṣabhāsamadguśiśumāra-nakramakaratimitimiṅgila nakulabiḍālasarpamaṇḍūkamūṣakavalmīkādimāṃsa-bhakṣaṇe dvādaśāham anāhāraḥ | pibed vā gomūtrayāvakam | iti |
etac ca kāmakṛtābhyāsaviṣayam | kāmakṛtasakṛdbhakṣaṇe tu yājñavalkya āha |
[407]        devatārthaṃ haviḥ śigruṃ lohitān vraścanāṃs tathā |
anupākṛtamāṃsāni viḍjāni kavakāni ca ||
kravyādapakṣidātyūhaśukapratudaṭiṭṭibhān |
sārasaikaśaphān haṃsān sarvāṃś ca grāmavāsinaḥ ||
koyaṣṭiplavacakrāhvabalākābakaviṣkirān |
vṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīḥ ||
kalaviṅkaṃ sakākolaṃ kuraraṃ rajjudālakam |
jālapādān khaṅjarīṭān ajñātāṃś ca mṛgadvijān ||
cāṣāṃś ca raktapādāṃś ca saunaṃ vallūram eva ca |
matsyāṃś ca kāmato jagdhvā sopavāsas tryahaṃ vaset || iti | (YDh 1.171–75)
viṣṇus tu jīvaviśeṣeṇa vyavasthitaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
kharoṣṭrakākaśvahaṃsāśane cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt | prāśyājñātaṃ sūnāsthaṃ śuṣkamāṃsaṃ ca | kravyādamṛgapakṣimāṃsāśane taptakṛcchram | kaviṅkaplavahaṃsacakrāhvasārasarajjudālakadātyūhaśukasārikābakabalāka-kokila-[408]-khañjarīṭāśane trirātram upavaset | ekaśaphobhayadantāśane ca | tittirikpiñjalalāvakavartikāmayūravarjaṃ sarvapakṣimāṃsāśane cāhorātram | kīṭāśane dinam ekaṃ brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ pibet | śuno māṃsāśane ca | iti |
(ViDh 51.26–33)
śaṅkhas tu bhakṣyābhkṣyavibhāgapuraḥsaram abhakṣyeṣu prāyaścittam āha |
bhuktvā palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ madyaṃ ca kavakāni ca |
nāraṃ śaunaṃ tathā māṃsaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ kharaṃ tathā ||
gām aśvaṃ kuñjaroṣṭrau ca sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā |
kravyādaṃ kukkuṭaṃ grāmyaṃ kuryāt saṃvatsaravratam ||
bhakṣyāḥ pañcanakhās tv ete godhākacchapaśalyakāḥ |
khaḍgaṃ ca śaśakaṃ caiva tān bhuktvā nācared vratam ||
[409]        haṃsaṃ madguṃ ca kākolaṃ kākaṃ ca khañjarīṭakān |
matyādāṃś ca tathā matsyān balākāḥ śukasārikāḥ ||
cakravākaṃ plavaṃ kokaṃ maṇḍūkaṃ bhujagaṃ tathā |
māsam ekaṃ vrataṃ kuryāt bhūyaś ca na can bhakṣayet ||
rājīvān siṃhatuṇḍāṃś ca saśalkāṃś ca tathaiva ca |
pāṭhīnarohitau cāpi bhakṣyā matsyeṣu śrāddhikāḥ ||
sthalecarāṃś ca jalajān pratudān nakhaviṣkirān |
raktapādāṃs tathā jagdhvā saptāhaṃ vratam ācaret ||
tittiriṃ ca mayūraṃ ca lāvakaṃ ca kapiñjalam |
vārdhrīṇasaṃ vartukaṃ ca bhojyān āha yamaḥ sadā ||
bhuktvā cobhyatodantāṃs tathaivaikaśaphān api |
daṃṣṭriṇaś ca tathā bhuktvā māṃsaṃ māsaṃ samācaret ||
māhiṣaṃ cājam aurabhraṃ mārgaṃ rauravam eva ca |
[410]        bhakṣyaṃ māṃśaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ yac ca vai pārṣataṃ bhavet ||
vārāhaṃ ca tato bhuktvā mahāraṇyanivāsinam |
māṃsam ajñānato bhuktvā māsaṃ proktaṃ samācaret ||
śuno māṃsaṃ śuṣkamāṃsam ātmārthe ca tathā kṛtam |
bhuktvā māsavrataṃ kuryāt prayataḥ susamāhitaḥ || iti | (ŚaṅSm 17.19)
atra śrutvā vrataviśeṣā āvṛttitāratamyaviṣayatvena vyavasthāpanīyāḥ || 11 ||
“śūdrānnaṃ sūtakānnaṃ ca” (ParSm 11.4) ity atra brāhmaṇasya śūdrānnabhojananiṣedham abhipretya tatra prāyaścittam abhihitam | tenaiva nyāyena hīnavarṇatvasāmyāt,”"rājānnaṃ harate tejaḥ” (YaSm 1.56) ityādiśāstrāc ca kṣatriyādyannbhojane ‘pi viprasya prāyaścittaṃ prasajyeta atas tadapanuttaye kṣatriyādyannabhojanam anujānāti |
kṣatriyaś cāpi vaiśyaś ca kriyāvantau śucivratau |
tadgṛhe tu dvijair bhojyaṃ havyakavyeṣu nityaśaḥ || 12 || iti |
kriyāvantau vihitādhyayana yajanādiyuktau | śucivratau vrataṃ smārto dharmaḥ | tac ca yathāvidhyanuṣṭhitaṃ śuci bhavati | [411] tadyuktau śucivratau | śrautasmārtadharmaniṣṭhayoḥ kṣatriyavaiśayor gṛheṣu havye kavye vā śiṣṭair viprair bhoktavyam | kriyāvantau śucivratāv iti viśeṣaṇāt atadvidhakṣatriyādiviṣayāṇi tadannaniṣedhaśāstrāṇīty avagantavyam | tathā ca caturviṃśatimate tadannaniṣedham abhipretya prāyaścittam abhihitam |
sahasraṃ tu japed devyāḥ kṣatriyasyānnabhojane |
tathopavāsaṃ vaiśyasya sahasraṃ sārdham eva ca || iti ||
|| 12 ||
kṣatriyānnavat śūdrānnasyāpi ghṛtādidravyaviśeṣarūpasyābhyanujñām āha |
ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ tathā kṣīraṃ bhakṣyaṃ snehena pācitam |
gatvā nadītaṭe vipro bhuñjīyāt śūdrabhojanam || 13 || iti |
snehapācitaṃ pūrikāśaṣkulyādi | bhujyate iti bhojanaṃ bhojyadravyam | śūdrasya saṃbandhi bhojanaṃ śūdrabhojanam | tādṛśaghṛtādikaṃ yadā bhoktavyaṃ bhavati tadā kṣatriyādigṛheṣv iva na śūdragṛhe bhoktavyam | kiṃ tarhi tad gṛhītvā śūdragṛhān nirgatya nadītīrādau bhuñjīta | etac ca kṣatriyādivarṇatrayabhojanasyābhyanujñānaṃ mārgaśrāntyādau pūrvavarṇāsaṃbhave veditavyam | āpadi [412] yāvatā vinā prāṇarakṣaṇaṃ na bhavati tāvad annam anujñāyate | na tu tato ‘dhikam | ata eva chandogā udgīthaparvaṇy uṣastikāṇḍe parmām āpadaṃ prāptasyoṣaster gajocchiṣṭānāṃ punar gajapālocchiṣṭānāṃ kulmāṣāṇāṃ jīvanāya bhakṣaṇaṃ tato ‘dhikasya tadīyocchiṣṭodakapānasya praiṣedhaṃ cāmananti: “hantānupānam iti ucchiṣṭaṃ vai me pītaṃ syād ity hovāca | nāsvid ete ‘py uccchiṣṭā iti na vā ajīviṣyam imā na khādann iti hovāca | kāmo ma udakapānam iti” (ChUp 1.10.3–4) iti etac ca | [413] “sarvān nānumatiś ca prāṇātyaye taddarśaṇāt” (BrSū 3.4.28) ity asmin vaiyyāsike sūtre samyaṅ mīmāṃsitam | smṛtiś ca bhavati |
jīvitātyayam āpanno yo ‘nnam atti yatas tataḥ |
lipyate na sa pāpena padmapatram ivāmbhasā || (MDh 10.104)
[414] iti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
āpadgataḥ saṃpragṛhṇan bhuñjāno ‘pi yatas tataḥ |
lipyate nainasā vipro jvalanārkasamo hi saḥ || iti | (YDh 3.41)
etad evānekopākhyānapuraḥsaraṃ manur āha |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyād brāhmaṇas tv anayaṃ gataḥ |
pavitraṃ duṣyatīty etad dharmato nopapadyate ||
nādhyāpanād yājanād vā garhitād vā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanārkasamā hi te ||
jīvitātyayam āpanno yo ‘nnam atti yatas tataḥ |
ākāśam iva paṅkena na sa pāpena lipyate ||
ajīgartaḥ sutaṃ hatum upāsapad bubhukṣitaḥ |
na cālipyata doṣeṇa kṣutpratīkāram ācaran ||
[415]        śvamāṃsam icchann ārto ‘ttuṃ dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ |
prāṇānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya vāmadevo na liptavān ||
bharadvājaḥ kṣudhārttas tu saputro nijane vane |
bahvīr gāḥ pratijagrāha vṛdhos takṣṇo mahātapāḥ ||
kṣudhārttaś cāttum abhyāgād viśvāmitraḥ śvajāghanīm |
[416]        caṇḍālahastād ādāya dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ || iti | (MDh 10.102–108
snehapācitety anenāranālādaya upalakṣyante | tathā ca caturviṃśatimate |
āranālaṃ tathā kṣīraṃ kāndukaṃ dadhi saktavaḥ |
snehapakvaṃ ca takraṃ ca śūdrasyāpi na duṣyati || iti |
aṅgirāś ca śūdrapratigrahaṃ prakramyāha |
māṃsaṃ dahi ghṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ kṣīram ājyam athauṣadham |
guḍo tasas tathodaśvid bhojyāny etāni nityaśaḥ ||
aśṛtaṃ cāranālaṃ ca kāndukāḥ saktavastilāḥ |
phalāni piṇyākam athogrāham auṣadham eva ca || iti | (AṅSm 3.40)
|| 13 ||
ghṛtādyannabhojane heyopādeyau śūdrau vivinakti |
madyamāṃsarataṃ nityaṃ nīcakarmapravartakam |
taṃ śūdraṃ varjayed vipraḥ śvapākam iva dūrataḥ || 14 ||
dvijaśuśrūṣaṇaratān madyamāṃsavivarjitān |
svakarmaṇi ratān nityaṃ na tān śūdrān tyajet dvijaḥ || 15 ||
[417] iti |jātiśūdrāḥ nīcāḥ pratilomajāḥ sūtamāgadhādayaḥ | teṣāṃ karma aśvasārathyādi | tasya pravartakāḥ tena jīvitāḥ | tādṛśāt śūdrāt śvapākādivat ghṛtādikaṃ na svīkāryam | ye tu mārgavartinaḥ śūdrā vihite pākayajñādikarmaṇi niratāḥ tān śūdrān ghṛtādibhojane na parityajet || 14–15 ||
“śūdrānnaṃ sūtakānnaṃ ca” (PārSm 9.31) ity atra sūtakānnasya sāmānyena prāyaścittam abhihitam | idānīṃ sūtakijātiviśeṣān anūdya tatra vrataviśeṣāṇ praśnapūrvakam āha |
ajñānād bhuñjate viprāḥ sūtake mṛtake ‘pi vā |
prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ teṣāṃ varṇe varṇe vinirdiśet || 16 ||
gāyatryaṣṭasahasreṇa śuddhiḥ syāt śūdrasūtake |
vaiśye pañcasahasreṇa trisahasreṇa kṣatriye || 17
brāhmaṇasya yadā bhuṅkte dve sahasre tu dāpayet |
atha vā vāmadaivyena sāmnaivaikena śudhyati || 18 || iti |
sūtakinaḥ śūdrasyānnaṃ vipro bhuktvāṣṭau sahasrāṇi gāyatrīṃ japet | sūtakino viśo ‘nnaṃ bhuktvā pañasahasragāyatrījapena [418] śuddhiḥ | kṣatriye sūtakini sati tadannaṃ bhuktvā trisahasragāyatrījapaṃ kuryāt | sūtakino brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ yo bhuṅkte taṃ prati dvisahasragāyatrījapaṃ vidadhyāt | “kayānaś citra ābhuvat” (RV 3.6.24.1) ity asyām ṛcy utpannaḥ sāmagānāṃ prasiddho gānaviśeṣo vāmadevyaṃ sāma | tasyaikasya sāmnaḥ sakṛjjapo dvisahasragāyatrījapena vikalpyate | ete ca japarūpā vrataviśeṣā aśaktaviṣaye veditavyāḥ | śaktaṃ prati tu chāgaleya āha |
ajñānād bhuñjate viprāḥ sūtake mṛtake tathā |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā śudhyante śūdrasūtake ||
vaiśye ṣaṣṭir bhaved rājñi viṃśatir brāhmaṇe daśa |
ekāhaṃ ca tryahaṃ pañca saptarātram abhojanam ||
tataḥ śuddhir bhavet teṣāṃ pañcagavyaṃ pibet tataḥ | iti |
brāhmaṇādikrameṇaikāhatryahādayo yojyāḥ | yat tu mārkaṇḍeyenoktam,
bhuktvānnaṃ brāhmaṇāśauce caret sāntapanaṃ dvijaḥ ||
vaiśyāśauce tathā bhuktvā mahāsāntapanaṃ caret |
śūdrāśauce tathā bhuktvā dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || (MārPu 1.14–15)
[419] iti, etat tu kāmakāraviṣayam | yac ca śaṅkhenoktam,
śūdrasya sūtake bhuktvā ṣaṇmāsān vratam ācaret |
vaiśyasya ca tathā bhuktvā trīn māsān vai vrataṃ caret ||
kṣatriyasya tathā bhuktvā dvau māsau vratam ācaret |
brāhmaṇasya tathāśauce bhuktvā māsaṃ vrataṃ caret || (ŚaṅSm 1.39–41)
iti, tad abhyāsaviṣayam | yad api gargeṇoktam,
śāve ca sūtake caiva matyā bhuktvaindavaṃ caret |
matyābhyāse tathā kuryāt kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇottaram ||
tryahaṃ vā kāmataḥ kuryād abhyāse kṛcchram eva ca |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi ca |
kṣatraviṭśudrajātīnām āśaucaṃ parikīrtitam ||
savarṇānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ vipravan niṣkṛtiḥ smṛtā |
kramād vṛddhaṃ kramād dhīnaṃ hīnajātyuttameṣv api || (GarSm 3.36)
[420] iti, tad etad guṇahīnasūtakiviṣayatayā yojanīyam | guṇavatsūtakiviṣaye tu mūlavacanoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | sarvatrāśaucottarakālam eva prāyaścittaṃ draṣṭavyam, “brāhmaṇādīnām āśauce yaḥ sakṛd evānnam aśnāti tasya tāvad evāśaucaṃ yāvat teṣām | āśaucāpagame ca prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt” (ViDh 22.8–9) iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | yac ca viṣṇunaiva vratam uktam,
savarṇasyāśauce dvijo bhuktvā sravantīm āsādya tasyāṃ nimagnas trir aghamarṣaṇaṃ sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet | kṣatriyāśauce brāhmaṇa etad evopoṣitaḥ kṛtvā śudhyati | vaiśyāśauce brāhmaṇas trirātropoṣitaś ca | brāhmaṇāśauce brāhmaṇas trirātropoṣitaś ca | brāhmaṇāśauce rājanyaḥ kṣtriyāśauce vaiśyaś ca sravantīm āsādya gāyatrīśatapañcakaṃ japet | vaiśyaś ca brāhmaṇāśauce gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet | śūdrāśauce dvijo bhuktvā prājāpatyavrataṃ caret | śūdraś ca dvijāśauce snānamātraṃ samācaret | [421] śūdraḥ śūdrāśauce snātaḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet” (ViDh 22.10–16)
iti, etan mūlavacanena samānaviṣayatayā yojanīyam | yad api vasiṣṭhena |
āśauce yas tu śūdrasya sūtake vāpi bhuktavān |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ tiryagyonyāṃ ca jāyate ||
anirdaśāhe pāraśāve śūdrānnaṃ bhuktavān dvijaḥ |
kṛmir bhuktvā sa dehānte tadviṣṭhām upajīvati ||
dvādaśa māsān dvādaśārdhamāsān vā anaśnan saṃhitādhyayanam adhīyānaḥ pūto bhavatīti vijñāyate | (VaDh 4.31–33)
iti, tad etad atyantābhyāsaviṣayam |
nanu – uktarītyā jātiviśeṣeṇa [422] vrataviśeṣāṇāṃ vyavasthitau satyāṃ brāhmaṇādijātiviśeṣarahitasya sāmānyasūtakinaḥ kasyacid abhāvāt, “śūdrānnaṃ sūtakānnaṃ ca” (ParSm 11.4) ityuktasya viṣayaḥ ko ‘pi na labhyeta | tatra hi prājāpatyaṃ brahmakūrcaṃ ceti vratadvayam abhihitam |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, sāmānyataḥ proktasya prājāpatyasya viṣṇuprokte viśeṣe paryavasānāt | viṣṇunā hi, “śūdrāśauce dvijo bhuktvā prājāpatyaṃ caret” (ViDh 22.16) iti proktam | brahmakūrcaṃ tu gargaproktatryahavratasamānaviṣayatayā yojanīyam | daśāhaviṣaye aṅgirā āha |
antardaśāhe bhuktvānnaṃ sūtake mṛtake ‘pi vā |
traipakṣike tadardhaṃ tu pañcagavyaṃ dvimāsike || iti | (AṅSm 1.19)
mūlavacanoktasūtakānnabhojanaprasaṅgena śrāddhabhojane prāyaścittāni likhyante | tatra  viṣṇuḥ |
[423]        prājāpatyaṃ navaśrāddhe pādonaṃ tv ādyamāsike |
traipakṣike tadardhaṃ tu pañcagavyaṃ dvimāsike || (ViDh 51.50)
iti, idaṃ cāpadviṣayam | anāpadi tu,
cāndrāyaṇaṃ navaśrāddhe prājāpatyaṃ tu miśrake |
ekāhaṃ tu purāṇeṣu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || (HārSm 5.39)
iti hārītoktaṃ draṣṭavyam | “prājāpatyaṃ tu miśrake” iti etad ādyamāsikaviṣayam | ūnamāsikādiṣu pādonaprājāpatyādīni kartavyāni | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
prājāpatyaṃ navaśrāddhe pādonaṃ māsike tathā |
traipakṣike tadardhaṃ syāt pādau dvau māsike tataḥ ||
pādonaṃ kṛccham uddiṣṭaṃ ṣāṇmāse cābdike tathā |
trirātraṃ cānyamāseṣu pratyabdaṃ ved ahaḥ smṛtam || iti |
kṣatriyādiśrāddhabhojane tv anāpadi tatraiva viśeṣa uktaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ navaśrāddhe pādonaṃ māsike smṛtam |
traipakṣike sāntapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ māsadvaye smṛtam ||
[424]        kṣatriyasya navaśrāddhe vratam etad udāhṛtam |
vaiśyasya tv adhikaṃ proktaṃ kṣatriyāt tu manīṣibhiḥ ||
śūdrasya tu navaśrāddhe carec cāndrāyaṇadvayam |
sārdhaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ māse tripakṣe tv aindavaṃ smṛtam ||
māsadvaye parākaḥ syād ūrdhvaṃ sāntapanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
yat tu śaṅkhavacanam,
cāndrāyaṇaṃ navaśrāddhe parāko māsike smṛtaḥ |
pakṣatraye ‘tikṛcchraṃ syāt ṣāṇmāse kṛcchra eva tu ||
ābdike kṛcchrapādaḥ syād ekāhaḥ punar ābdike |
ata ūrdhvaṃ na doṣaḥ syāc chaṅkasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
iti, tat sarpādihativiṣayam, “ye stenapatitaklībāḥ” (MDh 3.150) ity apāṅkteyaviṣayaṃ vā |
caṇḍālād udakāt sarpād brāhmaṇāḍ vaidyutād api |
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca nakhibhyaś ca maraṇaṃ pāpakarmaṇām ||
patanānāśakaiś caiva viṣodbandhanakais tathā |
bhuktvaiṣāṃ ṣoḍaśaśrāddhe kuryād induvrataṃ dvijaḥ ||
iti |
[425]        apāṅkteyān yad uddiśya śrāddham ekādaśe ‘hani |
brāhmaṇas tatra bhuktvānnaṃ śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
āmaśrāddhe tathā bhuktvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati |
saṃkalpite tathā bhuktvā trirātrakṣapaṇaṃ bhavet ||
iti bharadvājena prāyaścittaviśeṣābhidhānāt | brahmacāriṇas tu bṛhadyamo viśeṣam āha |
māsikādiṣu yo ‘śnīyād asamāptavrato dvijaḥ |
trirātram upavāso ‘sya prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate ||
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati | iti | (YaSm 2.19)
idam ajñānaviṣayam | jñānapūrvake tu sa evāha |
madhu māṃsaṃ ca yo ‘śnīyāt śrāddhe sūtake eva ca |
prājāpatyaṃ caret kṛcchraṃ vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet || iti | (YaSm 2.16)
āmaśrāddhe tu sarvatrārdham,
āmaśrāddhe bhaved ardhaṃ prājāpatyaṃ tu sarvadā |
iti ṣaṭtriṃśaṇmate ‘bhidhānāt | yat tu ūśanasoktam,
daśakṛtvaḥ pibed āpo gāyatryā śrāddhabhug dvijaḥ |
tataḥ saṃdhyām upāsīta śudhyec ca tadanantaram || (UśSm 3.7)
iti, tad anuktaprāyaścittaśrāddhaviṣayam | saṃskārāṅgaśrāddhbhojane [426] vyāsena viśeṣa  uktaḥ |
cūḍāhome nivṛtte tu prāṅnāmakaraṇāt tathā |
caret sāntapanaṃ bhuktvā jātakarmaṇi caiva hi ||
ato ‘nyeṣu ca bhuktvānnaṃ saṃskāreṣu dvijottamaḥ |
niyogād upavāsena śudhyate nindyabhojanāt || iti | (VyāSm 2.23–24)
sīmantonnayanādiṣu tu dhaumyo viśeṣam āha |
brahmodane ca some ca sīmantonnayane tathā|
jātaśrāddhe navaśrāddhe bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
tatra brahmaudanākhyaṃ karmādhānāṅgabhūtam, somasāhacaryāt | yat tu bharadvājenoktam,
bhuktaṃ cet pārvaṇaśrāddhe prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret |
upavāsas trimāsādivatsarāntaṃ prakīrtitaḥ ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ vṛddhāv ahorātraṃ sapiṇḍane |
asarūpe smṛtaṃ naktaṃ vrataṃ pārvaṇake tathā ||
dviguṇaṃ kṣatriyasyaiva triguṇaṃ vaiśyabhojane |
sākṣāc caturguṇaṃ caiva smṛtaṃ śūdrasya bhojanam ||
iti, etad apy āpadviṣayam, anāpady adhikaprāyaścittasyoktatvāt || 16–18 ||
[427]
“ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ tathā kṣīram” (ParSm 11.13) ity atra śūdrasaṃbandhināṃ ghṛtādīnāṃ bhojanam abhyanujñātam | idānīṃ śūdrataṇḍulādīn abhyanujānāt |
śuṣkānnaṃ gorasaṃ snehaṃ śūdraveśmana āgatam |
pakvaṃ vipragṛhe bhuktaṃ bhojyaṃ tan manur abravīt || 19 || iti |
śuṣkānnaṃ vrīhitaṇḍulādi | gorasaḥ kṣīrādiḥ | snehas tailādiḥ | tat sarvaṃ śūdragṛhād ānīya brāhmaṇagṛhe yadi pakvaṃ bhavati tadā tad annaṃ bhojyam | yas tv āmapakvayoḥ sādhāraṇo niṣedhaḥ pūrvam udāhṛtaḥ so ‘sacchūdraviṣayaḥ | ata eva “madyamāṃsaratam” (ParSm 11.14) ity uktam || 19 ||
pūrvam “ajñānād āpado ‘pi vā” ity atrāpannasya viprasya śūdrānnabhojane brahmakūrcavratam abhihitam | tad avidvadviṣayam | idānīṃ vidvāmsaṃ praty āha |
āpatkāleṣu vipreṇa bhuktaṃ śūdragṛhe yadi |
manastāpena śudhyeta drupadāṃ vā japec chatam || 20 || iti |
dvividhā hi vidyā | vedapāṭhamātrāvasāyinī [428] tadarthajñānānuṣṭhānaparyantā ca | tayor madhye ‘nuṣṭhātā āhitāgnir manastāpena śudhyati | kevalavedapāṭhakas tu “drupadādiven mumucānaḥ” (TaitBr 2.6.6.4) ity etām ṛcaṃ gāyatrīchandasaṃ śtakṛtvo japet | manastāpasya śuddhihetutvaṃ yājñavalkyo ‘py āha |
paścāttāpo nirāhāraḥ sarve ‘mī śuddhihetavaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.31)
manur api |
khyāpanenānutāpena tapasādhyayanena ca |
pāpakṛn mucyate pāpāt tathā dānena cāpadi || iti | (MDh 11.227)
baudhāyano ‘pi |
parityāgas tapo dānam anutāpo ‘nukīrtitam |
vidyābhyāso hy upasparśaḥ saptāṅgaṃ pāpanāśanam || iti |
drupadāyāḥ pāpavināśakatvaṃ yājñavalkyenoktam |
drupadā nāma gāyatrī yajurvede pratiṣṭhitā |
sarvapāpaharā japtur mahato ‘py enasaḥ smṛtam || iti | (BṛYoYāj 7.81)
|| 20||
[429]
“dvijaśuśrūṣaṇaratān” (ParSm 11.15) ity atra bhojyānnāḥ śūdrāḥ sāmānyena nirdiṣṭāḥ | tān etān idānīṃ viśeṣato nirdiśati |
dāsanāpitagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || 21 || iti |
dāsādaya ātmanivedakāntāḥ ṣaṭ śūdrā bhojyānnāḥ || 21 ||
dāsādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam āha |
śūdrakanyāsamutpanno brāhmaṇena tu saṃskṛtaḥ |
saṃskārāt tu bhaved dāsaḥ asaṃskārāt tu nāpitaḥ || 22 ||
kṣatriyāt śūdrakanyāyāṃ samutpannas tu yaḥ sutaḥ |
sa gopāla iti jñeyo bhojyo viprair na saṃśayaḥ || 23 ||
vaiśyakanyāsamutpanno brāhmaṇena tu saṃskṛtaḥ |
sa hy ārdhikaa iti jñeyo bhojyo viprair na saṃśayaḥ || 24 || iti |
brāhmaṇaḥ śūdrakanyām ūḍhvā tasyāṃ yaṃ putram utpadayati [430] sa yadi amantrakair niṣekādibhiḥ saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛto bhavati tadā dāsa ity ucyate saṃskārābhāve tu yaṃ putram utpādayati sa nāmato gopālety atra vivakṣitaḥ, na tv arthataḥ, gorakṣaṇarūpasyārthasya kṛṣyādivad vaiśyakarmatvāt |
yady api kramaprāptasya kulamitrasyātra lakṣaṇaṃ noktaṃ tathāpi śabdasāmarthyāt prakramabalād vā tallakṣaṇam unneyam | śabdasāmarthyatas tāvat kulasya mitraṃ kulamitram iti vyutpattyā pitṛpitāmahādikramād āyāt āptaḥ śūdraḥ kulamitra ity abhidhīyate | prakramānusāreṇa tv evam upasaṃkhyātavyam | vaiśyaḥ śūdrakanyām ūḍhvā tasyāṃ yaṃ putram utpadayati sa kulamitra iti |
brāhmaṇaḥ vaiśyakanyām ūḍhvā tasyāṃ yaṃ putram utpādayati, saṃskṛtaḥ sa nāmnā ārdhika iti vā ardhasīrīti vābhidhīyate | yas tu vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhis tavāham ity ātmānaṃ nivedayati sa ātmanivedakaḥ | yad vā ārdhikasāhcaryāt kṣatriyāyāṃ brāhmaṇenotpāditaḥ kramataḥ saṃkṛta ātmanivedako ‘stu | asmin pakṣe yady apy aśūdrāvārdhikātmanivadakau tathāpi nīcajātitvasāmānyenābhojyānnatvaśaṅkāyāṃ tadapanodanāyedam abhidhīyate – ity adoṣaḥ | [431] dāsādīnāṃ bhojyānnatvaṃ yājñavalkyo ‘py āha |
śūdreṣu dāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
bhojyānnā nāpitaś caiva yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || iti | (YDh 1.166)
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
ārdhikaḥ kulamitraś ca gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || iti |
dāsādivat kumbhakāro ‘pi bhojyānna eva | tad āha devalaḥ |
svadāso nāpito gopaḥ kumbhakāraḥ kṛṣīvalaḥ |
brāhmaṇair api bhoktavyāḥ pañcaite śūdrayonayaḥ || iti |
smṛtyantare ‘pi: “gopālanāpitakumbhakārakulamitrārdhasīriniveditātmāno bhojyānnāḥ” iti |
|| 22–24 ||
[432]
pūrvatrābhojyānnānāṃ naṭādīnām anne bhukte prāyaścittam abhihitam | idānīṃ teṣām eva jalādau pīte prāyaścittaṃ praśnapūrvakam āha |
bhāṇḍasthitam abhojyeṣu jalaṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ payaḥ |
akāmatas tu yo bhuṅkte prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ bhavet || 25 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā upasarpati |
brahmakūrcopavāsena yājyavarñasya niṣkṛtiḥ || 26 ||
śūdrāṇāṃ nopavāsaḥ syāt śūdro dānena śudhyati |
brahmakūrcam ahorātraṃ śvapākam api śodhayet || 27 || iti |
abhojyeṣv abhojyānnānāṃ naṭādīnāṃ gṛheṣu yad bhāṇḍaṃ tatra sthitaṃ jalādikaṃ pātrāntaravyavadhānam akṛtvā bhuktaṃ cet tadā brahmakūrcaṃ pātavyam | yathā sūtakānne brāhmaṇādivarṇabhedena vratabhedā abhihitāḥ na tathā jalādipāne | kiṃ tu caturṇām api varṇānāṃ brahmakūrcaṃ samānam ity abhipretya brāhmaṇādyupanyāsaḥ | ahorātram upoṣya dināntare brahmakūrcaṃ pātavyam | brahmakūrcahetur upavāso brahmakūrcopavāsaḥ | tena yajanayogyasya traivarṇikasya niṣkṛtir bhavati | śūdrasya tūpavāsapratyāmnāyo dānam | ataḥ śūdro dānaṃ [433] kṛtvā paścād brahmakūrcaṃ pibet | tad idam ahorātropavāsapūrvakabrahmakūrcaṃ śvapākasadṛśam atyantapāpakāriṇam api śodhayati | tatra kim u vaktavyam alpapāpena niṣiddhajalādipānenopetaṃ śodhayatīti || 25–27 ||
brahmakūrcasya dravyāṇy āha |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam |
nirdiṣṭaṃ pañcagavyaṃ tu pavitraṃ pāpaśodhanam || 28 || iti |
gomūtrādikaṃ pañcagavyaṃ yac ca kuśodake tad ubhayaṃ svataḥ pavitram ataḥ pāpaśodhanam iti dharmaśāstreṣu nirdiṣṭam || 28 ||
brahmakūrcāṅgabhūtānāṃ gomūtrādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ kāraṇabhūtāyāḥ pañca gāvaḥ tāsāṃ varṇaviśeṣān āha |
gomūtraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāyāḥ śvetāyāś caiva gomayam |
payaś ca tāmravarṇāyā raktāyā gṛhyate dadhi || 29 ||
kapilāyā ghṛtaṃ grāhyaṃ sarvaṃ kāpilam eva vā | iti |
vicitravarṇānāṃ pañcānāṃ gavām asaṃbhave gomūtrādipañcakaṃ sarvam api kāpilam eva grāhyam || 29 ||
gomūtrādīnāṃ kuśodakāntānāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ pratyekaṃ parimāṇaviśeṣam āha |
[434]        mūtram ekapalaṃ dayād aṅguṣṭhārdhaṃ tu gomayam || 30 ||
kṣīraṃ saptapalaṃ dadyād dadhi tripalam ucyate |
ghṛtam ekapalaṃ dadyāt palam ekaṃ kuśodakam || 31 || iti |
parvadvayopetam aṅguṣṭham | tatroparitanena parvaṇā samānaparimāṇaṃ gomayaṃ grāhyam | dadyāt ekasmin palāśādipātre yathoktaparimāṇāni ṣaḍ dravyāṇi nikṣipet | tatra pātraṃ prajāpatir āha |
pālāśaṃ padmapātraṃ vā tāmraṃ vātha hiraṇmayam |
gṛhītvāsādayitvā ca tataḥ karma samācaret || iti |
vṛddhaparāśaras tu gomūtrādīnāṃ parimāṇāntaram āha |
gomūtraṃ māṣakāny aṣṭau gomayasya tu ṣoḍaśa |
kṣīrasya dvādaśa proktā dadhnas tu daśa kīrtitāḥ ||
gomūtravad ghṛte jñeyaṃ tadardhaṃ tu kuśodakam |
[435] iti | etad aśaktabālādiviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam | prajāpatis tu prakārāntaram āha |
gomayāt dviguṇaṃ mūtraṃ sarpir dadyāc caturguṇam |
kṣīram aṣṭaguṇaṃ deyaṃ dadhi pañcaguṇaṃ tathā || iti |
atra gomayasya parimāṇaviśeṣānabhidhānāt vacanāntarānusāreṇāṅguṣṭārdhaparimitaṃ gomayaṃ svīkṛtya yathoktāttarābhivṛddhyā gomūtrādīni yojayet | etat ca mūlavacanoktaparimāṇena saha vikalpyate, vaiṣamyasyālpatvāt || 30–31 ||
gomūtrādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ pratyekaṃ mantrān āha |
gāyatryādāya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam |
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇas tathā dadhi || 32 ||
tejo ‘si śukram ity ājyaṃ devasya tvā kuśodakam |
pañcagavyam ṛcā pūtaṃ sthāpayed agnisaṃnidhau || 33 || iti |
ṛcetipadaṃ gāyatryādibhiḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | “tejo ‘si” “devasya tvā” ity anayor mantratvasāmyāt ṛktvam upacaritam | tair etair mantrair gṛhītaṃ pañcagavyaṃ homārtham agnisaṃnidhau sthāpayet || 32–33 ||
[436]
sthāpayitvā cāloḍanābhimantraṇe kartavye ity āha |
āpohiṣṭheti cāloḍya mānastoketi mantrayet |
āpohiṣṭhā ityādikaṃ tryṛcaṃ vijñeyam | prāyaśas tathā viniyogāt | mānastoke ity ekaiva ṛk |
atrāvadānahomasādhanānāṃ sruvādīnām abhāvāt kenāvadāya hotavyam, ity ākāṅkṣāyām āha |
saptavarās tu ye darbhā acchinnāgrāḥ śukatviṣaḥ || 34 ||
etair uddhṛtya hotavyaṃ pañcagavyaṃ yathāvidhi | iti |
saptasaṃkhyā avarā adhamā yeṣāṃ darbhāṇāṃ te saptāvarāḥ | saptāṣṭādyadhikasaṃkhyā gṛhītavyā | na tu ṣaṭpañcādinyūnasaṃkhyety arthaḥ | acchinnam agraṃ yeṣāṃ te acchinnāgrāḥ | śukavat tviṭ dīptir yeṣāṃ te śukatviṣaḥ | haridvarṇā iti yāvat | ārdrāṇāṃ chāyāśuṣkāṇāṃ vā bhavati haritatvam | tair darbhair avadāya palāśapatreṇa hotavyam | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
[437]        sthāpayitvā tu darbheṣu pālāśaiḥ patrakair atha |
tat samuddhṛtya hotavyaṃ devatābhyo yathākramam || iti |
darbheṣv ity abhidhānāt pātrasyādhastād api darbhān āstṛṇuyāt || 34 ||
atha homamantrān āha |
irāvatī idaṃ viṣṇur mānastoketi śaṃvatī || 35 ||
etābhiś caiva hotavyaṃ hutaśeṣaṃ pibet dvijaḥ || iti |
iretyādīni trīṇi ṛkpratīkāni | śam ity eṣa śabdo yasyām ṛci asti sā saṃvatī | “śam agnir agnibhiḥ karacchannaḥ” (RV 6.1.26.4) iti vā, “śaṃ no devīr abbhiṣṭaye” (RV 7.6.5.4) iti vā sā draṣṭavyā | etac cāgnaye svāhetyādīnām api mantrāṇām upalakṣaṇam | ata eva prajāpatiḥ |
agnaye caiva somāya sāvitryai ca tathaiva ca |
[438]        praṇavena tathā hutvā sviṣṭakṛc ca tathaiva ca |
evaṃ hutvā ca taccheṣaṃ pibec caiva samāhitaḥ || iti || 35 ||
atha pānasyetikartavyatām āha |
āloḍya praṇavenaiva nirmanthya praṇavena tu || 36 ||
uddhṛtya praṇavenaiva pibec ca praṇaveṇa tu | iti |
āloḍanaṃ hastena | nirmanthanaṃ kāṣṭheneti tayor bhedaḥ || 36 ||
nanu – govadhādiṣu yaḥ pratyavāyaḥ sa kevalādṛṣṭarūpatvāt tasya prāyaścittajanyena sukṛtāpūrveṇa nivṛttir yujyate | abhakṣyabhakṣaṇajanyas tu pratyavāyo na kevalam adṛṣṭarūpaḥ | kiṃ tu dṛṣṭarūpo ‘pi, tasyāhārasya tv agasthyādirūpeṇa pariṇatatvāt | ato na tasya vratena nivṛttir yujyata – ity ata āha |
yat tv agasthigataṃ pāpaṃ dehe tiṣṭhati dehinām || 37 ||
brahmakūrco dahet sarvaṃ pradīptāgnir ivendhanam || iti |
abhakṣyāṇi yathā tv agasthyādirūpeṇa pariṇatāni gomūtrādīny api tena rūpeṇa pariṇamanti | pariṇamya cāgniḥ kāṣṭhānīva svavirodhyabhakṣyapariṇāmān adṛṣṭamukhenāpi [439] vināśayanti | na tu kevalaṃ dṛṣṭenaiva mukhena | tasmād brahmakūrcenābhakṣyabhakṣaṇanivṛttir upapadyate | brahmakūrcasya kālaviśeṣaṃ prajāpatir āha |
caturdaśyām upoṣyātha paurṇamāsyāṃ samācaret | iti |
jābālir api |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā paurṇamāsyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
pañcagavyaṃ pibet prātar brahmakūrcam iti smṛtam || iti |
deśaviśeṣaṃ śātātapa āha |
nadītīreṣu goṣṭheṣu puṇeṣv āyataneṣu ca |
tatra gatvā śucau deśe brahmakūrcaṃ samācaret || iti || 37 ||
brahmakūrcasya sarvapāpadāhakatvam upapādayati |
pavitraṃ triṣu lokeṣu devatābhir adhiṣṭhitam || 38 ||
varuṇaś caiva gomūtre gomaye havyavāhanaḥ |
dadhni vāyuḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ somaḥ kṣīre ghṛte raviḥ || 39 || iti |
varuṇādibhir adhiṣṭhitatvāt pāpadāhakatvam upapannam || 38–39 ||
[440]
abhojyānnānāṃ bhāṇḍe ‘vasthitaṃ jalādikaṃ pītvā vrataṃ cared ity uktaṃ pūrvam (ParSm 11.25) | atha jalasya svamukhaniḥsṛtasya punaḥ pāne prāyaścittam āha |
pibataḥ patitaṃ toyaṃ bhājane mukhaniḥsṛtam |
apeyaṃ tad vijānīyāt pītvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 40 || iti |
jalaṃ pibataḥ puruṣasya mukhaniḥsṛtaṃ bhojanādipātre patitaṃ yat toyaṃ tat traivarṇikaiḥ punar na peyam | kathaṃcit pīte sati cāndrāyaṇena śuddhiḥ | yat tu mukhaṃ praviśya na niḥsṛtaṃ, kiṃ tv ekasmin pātre ardhaṃ pītvāvaśeṣitaṃ tasya pāne trirātropavāsam āha śaṅkhaḥ |
pītāvaśeṣitaṃ pītvā pānīyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ kvacit |
trirātraṃ tu vrataṃ kuryād vāmahastena vā punaḥ || iti | (ŚaṅSm 13.41)
|| 40 ||
śvasṛgālādimaraṇopahatakūpādijalapāne varṇaviśeṣeṇa prāyaścittaviśeṣam āha |
kūpe ca patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā śvasṛgālau ca markaṭam |
asthicarmādi patitaṃ pītvāmedhyā apo dvijaḥ || 41 ||
nāraṃ tu kuṇapaṃ kākaṃ viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrakam |
gāvayaṃ saupratīkaṃ ca māyūraṃ khāḍgakaṃ tathā || 42 ||
[441]        vaiyyāghram ārkṣaṃ saiṃhaṃ vā kūpe yadi nimajjati |
taṭākasyātha duṣṭasya pītaṃ syād udakaṃ yati || 43 ||
prāyaścittaṃ bhavet puṃsaḥ krameṇaitena sarvaśaḥ |
vipraḥ śudhyet trirātreṇa kṣatriyas tu dinadvayāt || 44 ||
ekāhena tu vaiśyas tu śūdro naktena śudhyati | iti |
kūpe patitvā mṛtaṃ śvādikaṃ vā asthyādikaṃ vā dṛṣṭvā tādṛśīr medhyā apaḥ pītvā prāyaścittī bhaved iti śeṣaḥ | narasya manuṣyasya saṃbandhi kuṇapaṃ nāram | kākasya saṃbandhi kākam | viḍvarāhādiṣu saṃbandhavācī taddhito lupto draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca sati vaiḍvarāhaṃ  khāraṃ auṣṭram ity uktaṃ bhavati | gavasya saṃbandhi gāvam | śobhanāḥ puṣṭāḥ supratīkā avayavā yasya gajasya sa supratīkaḥ | tasya saṃbandhi saupratīkam | mayūrasya saṃbandhi māyūram | khaḍgo mṛgaviśeṣaḥ tasya saṃbandhi khāḍgakam | vyāghrasya saṃbandhi vaiyyāghram | ṛkṣasya saṃbandhi ārkṣam | siṃhasya saṃbandhi saiṃham | eteṣām anyatamaṃ patitaṃ kuṇapaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadyuktayoḥ kūpataṭākayoḥ snānapāne yadi kuryāt tadā tādṛśasya puṃsaḥ sarvavarṇasaṃbandhinaḥ | etena – samantaraṃ nirdiśyamānena [442] krameṇa prāyaścittaṃ bhavet | sa ca kramaḥ trirātropavāsādiḥ | tryahādivratacaraṇānantaraṃ pañcagavyaṃ pibet | tad āha viṣṇuḥ: “mṛtapañcanakhān kūpād atyantopahatam udakaṃ pītvā brāhmaṇas tryaham upavaset | dvyahaṃ rājanyaḥ | ekāhaṃ vaiśyaḥ | śūdro naktam | sarve cānte pañcagavyaṃ pibeyuḥ” iti (ViDh 54.2–6) | yadā tu tatraiva śavam ucchūnatayodbhinnaṃ bhavati tadā hārīto viśeṣam āha |
klinne bhinne śave toyaṃ tatrasthaṃ yadi tat pibet |
śuddhyai cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt taptakṛcchram athāpi vā ||
yadi kaścit tataḥ snāyāt pramādena dvijottamaḥ |
japaṃs triṣavaṇasnāyī ahorātreṇa śudhyati || iti | (HārSm 21.3–4)
idaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ kāmato mānuṣaśavopahatakūpajalapānaviṣayam | akāmatas tu ṣaḍrātram,
klinne bhinne śavaṃ caiva kūpasthaṃ yadi dṛśyate |
payaḥ pibet trirātreṇa mānuṣe dviguṇaṃ smṛtam ||
iti devalasmaraṇāt | madyabhāṇḍasthitodakapāne yama āha |
surābhāṇḍasthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibet dvijaḥ |
kuśamūlavipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa śudhyati ||
[443]        dvādaśāhaṃ tu payasā pibed brāhmīṃ suvarcalām |
gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ vā japaṃ kurvīta mānasam || iti | (YaSm 3.41–42)
etan matipūrvakābhyāsaviṣayam | akāmato ‘bhyāse tu manur āha |
[444]        āpaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitās tathā |
pañcarātraṃ pibet pītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ || iti | (MDh 11.147)
yat tu viṣṇūktam: “apaḥ surābhājanasthāḥ pītvā saptarātraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ pibet” (ViDh 51.23) iti, tat kāmataḥ skṛtpāne draṣṭavyam | yat tu śātātapenoktam: “surābhāṇḍodakapāne chardanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanam ahorātropavāsaś ca” iti, tat śuṣkasurābhāṇḍasthodakapānaviṣayam | tatraiva paryuṣitodakapāne tenaivoktam |
surāpānasya yo bhāṇḍe āpaḥ paryuṣitāḥ pibet |
śaṅkhapuṣpyavapakvaṃ tu kṣīraṃ sa tu pibet tryaham || iti | (ŚāSm 5.31)
surāpānasya mukhagandhāghrāṇe tu manur āha |
brāhmaṇas tu surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapaḥ |
[445]        prāṇān apsu trir āyamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | (MDh 11.149)
stryādyucchiṣṭapāne hārīta āha |
striyocchiṣṭasthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ |
śaṅkhapuṣpīvipakvena tryhaṃ kṣīreṇa yāpayet ||
śūdrocchiṣṭasthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ |
kuśamūlavipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa yāpayet ||
śūdrocchiṣṭasthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ |
kuśamūlavipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa pāvayet || iti | (HārSm 11.41–42)
yamaḥ |
śunocchiṣṭasthitā āpo yadi kaścit pibed dvijaḥ |
śaṅkhapuṣpīvipakvena tryahaṃ kṣīreṇa śudhyati || iti | (YaSm 3.60)
|| 41 – 44 ||
[446]
brāhmaṇeṣv eva keṣucd abhojyānnatvam abhipretya tadannabhojane prāyaścittam āha |
parapākanivṛttasya parapākaratasya ca || 45 ||
apacasya ca bhuktvānnaṃ dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret | iti |
parapākanivṛttādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ svarūpaṃ svayam eva vakṣyati | tadīyānnabhojanaṃ yady apy alpanimittaṃ tathāpy abhyāsābhiprāyeṇa mahad vratam aviruddham | anabhyāse tv īdṛśānnabhojane bharadvāja āha |
parapākanivṛttasya parapākaratasya ca |
nirācārasya viprasya niṣiddhācaraṇasya ca ||
annaṃ bhuktvā dvijaḥ kuryād dinam ekam abhojanam | iti || 45 ||
apacasyānnapradatve tadannabhoktur eva pratyavāyo ‘bhihitaḥ | yadā tv apaco bhuṅkte itaraḥ prayacchati tadānīm ubhayoḥ pratyavāya ity āha |
apacasya ca yad dānaṃ dātuś cāsya kutaḥ phalam || 46 ||
dātā pratigrahītā ca tau dvau nirayagāminau | iti |
apacasya apacāyety arthaḥ | tasmai yad dānam anyena kriyate tasmin dāne tasya dātur dānaphalaṃ nāsti | na kevalaṃ phalābhāvaḥ kiṃ tv asau dātā saha pratigrahītrā narakaṃ yāti |
|| 46 ||
[447]
parapākanivṛttādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ krameṇa lakṣaṇam āha |
gṛhītvāgniṃ samāropya pañcayajñān na nirvapet || 47 ||
parapākanivṛtto ‘sau munibhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
pañcayajñān svayaṃ kṛtvā parānnenopajīvati || 48 ||
satataṃ prātar utthāya parapākaratas tu saḥ |
gṛhasthadharmā yo vipro dadāti parivarjitaḥ || 49 ||
ṛṣibhir dharmatattvajñair apacaḥ parikīrtitaḥ | iti |
parasya puruṣāntarasya gṛhe kriyamāṇaḥ pākaḥ parapākaḥ | tasmān nivṛttaḥ parapākanivṛttaḥ parānnabhojanaparityāgīti yāvat | yad vā vaiśvadevādyarthaṃ kriyamānaḥ pāka utkṛṣṭatvāt parapāka ity ucyate | tasmāt parapākan nivṛttaḥ pañcamahāyajñarahita ity arthaḥ | tac cobhayaṃ vivakṣitatvāt tantreṇoccaritam | tathā ca sati parānnavarjanasya guṇatve 'pi pañcamahāyañādirāhityasya [448] doṣatvād asya puruṣasya nindyatvam | akṣarārthas tu – agniṃ gṛhītvā vivāhaṃ kṛtvā smārtāgnau kevalam aupāsanaṃ kṛtvā tam agnim ātmani samāropayati, na tu tasminn agnau vaiśvadevādikaṃ karoti | so 'yaṃ vaiśvadevādyanuṣṭhānarahitaḥ puruṣo 'tra parapākanivṛttaśabdena vivakṣitaḥ | etasmād viparītaḥ parapākarataḥ, ubhayavidhapākarahitatvāt | tatra pañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānasya guṇatve ‘pi parānnabhojanasya doṣatvāt tādṛśasya puruṣasya nindyatvam | pratidinaṃ prātar utthāya yathāvidhi pañcamahāyajñāna kṛtvā parānnenopajīvatīti yojanīyam |
yas tu vipro vidhānena gārhasthyaṃ svīkṛtya annadānādivarjitaḥ kevalaṃ svayam eva bhuṅkte so ‘paca ity ucyate | tasya nindā pratyakṣaśrutāv āmnāyate:
nāryamaṇaṃ puṣyati yo sakhāyaṃ kevalāgho bhavati kevalādī | iti | (RV 8.6.23.1)
“dadāti parivarjitaḥ” ity atra dadātītyarthanirdeśatvād dānakriyām ācaṣṭe, [449] “tyajyateḥ pūrvavattvam” (PMS 7.4.1) iti asmin jaiminisūtre cārthanirdeśe ‘pi śtippratyayaprayogadarśanāt | “ikśtipau dhātunirdeśe” (VāPā 2.15) ity etat tu vārarucaṃ vārtikam pracuraprayogābhiprāyam | na tv arthanirdeśavyavacchedakam |
parapākanivṛttādivad vṛthāpākāder apy annaṃ na bhoktavyam | tadbhojane tu prāyaścittaṃ kartavyam | tad āhatuḥ śātātapa-bṛhaspatī |
yo ‘gṛhītvā vivāhāgniṃ gṛhastha iti manyate |
annaṃ tasya na bhoktavyaṃ vṛthāpāko hi sa smṛtaḥ ||
vṛthāpākasya bhuñjānaḥ prāyaścittaṃ cared dvijaḥ |
prāṇāyāmān trir abhyasya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || iti | (ŚāSm 3.51–52)
“prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā” iti pāṭhe tv āvṛttiviṣayatvaṃ kalpanīyam | vṛthāpāke yat prāyaścittaṃ tad eva brāhmaṇanindakādāv api draṣṭavyam, nindāvacane sahapāṭhāt | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
paṅktibhedī vṛthāpākī nityaṃ brāhmaṇanindakaḥ |
ādeśī vedavikretā pañcaite brahmaghātakāḥ || (VyāSm 4.70)
[450] iti | || 47–49 ||
nanu – uktarītyā kaliyuge sarve ‘py abhojyānnā abhakṣyabhakṣaṇāś ca | tathā saty etadviṣayaṃ prāyaścittaśāstraṃ nirarthakam | kṛte ‘pi prāyaścitte punar api tatpravṛtter uparantuma aśakyatvāt prāyaścittavidhāyikāyāḥ pariṣado ‘saṃbhavāc ca | na hi pāpapravṛttānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ yuktam, “svadharmarataviprāṇām” (ParSm 8.2) iti tallakṣaṇāt | na ca – abhakṣyabhakṣaṇādibhyaḥ pāpebhyo nivṛttānāṃ śiṣṭānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ syāt – iti śaṅkanīyam, tādṛśasya kasyāpy adṛṣṭacaratvāt | ataḥ kaliyuge sarveṣāṃ nindyatvād [451] etad eva yugam uddiśya pravṛttasya pārāśaradharmaśāstrasya nirviṣayatvaṃ syāt – ity āśaṅkyāha |
yuge yuge tu ye dharmās teṣu teṣu ca ye dvijāḥ || 50 ||
teṣāṃ nindā na kartavyā yugarūpā hi te dvijāḥ | iti |
asyākṣarārthaḥ prathamādhyāye varṇitaḥ | ayam āśyaḥ – dvividhā hy adharmapravṛttiḥ | yugaprayuktā pramādālasyādiprayuktā ca | | tatra yugaprayuktāyāḥ pravṛtter aparihāryatvān na tannivṛttaye parāśarasyodyamaḥ | yā tu pramādālasyādiprayuktā pravṛttiḥ tatra sāvakāśaṃ dharmaśāstram | tad yathā | adhyayanavidhis tāvad arthajñānaparyantaṃ sāṅgavedapāṭham ācaṣṭe | na ca – kalau yuge tādṛśaṃ vipraṃ kaṃcid apy upalabhāmahe | tathā brahmacāriprakaraṇe tadāśramadharmā adhyayanadharmāś ca sahasraśaḥ smaryante | na ca tān sarvān yathāvad [452] anutiṣṭhan māṇavakaḥ ko ‘py upalabhyate | yadā adhyayanasyaiva īdṛśī gatiḥ tadā kaiva kathā sāṅgakṛtsnavedārthānuṣṭhānasya | tathā sati śāstrīyamukhyabrāhmaṇyopetasya kasyāpy abhāvāt kṣatriyavaiśyajātyoś ca svarūpreṇaivocchinnatvāt śuśrūṣayitavyānāṃ dvijānām asaṃbhave tacchuśrūṣakasya mukhyasya śūdrasyātyantānāśaṅkanīyatvāt kiṃ cāurvarṇyam uddiśya pravṛttaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ svarūpeṇaiva lupyatām | kiṃ vā mukhyāsaṃbhave ‘pi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ cāturvarṇyam āśritya dharmaśāstraṃ pravartatām – iti mīmāṃsāyām, svarūpalopāt varaṃ yathāsaṃbhavānuṣṭhānam ity abhipretya yugapravṛttāṃ sarvair apy avarjanīyām adharmapravṛttim adoṣatvenābhyupagamya “teṣāṃ nindā na kartavyā” ity uktam | tataḥ saṃbhāvitādhyayanādyupetānāṃ śakyāyāṃ dharmapravṛttau pramādālasyādirahitānāṃ ca pariṣattvaṃ kuto na syāt | kṛtaprāyaścittasya punaḥ pramādālasyādivarjanasya sukaratvāt prāyaścittaśāstraṃ ca sārthakam iti || 50 ||
ittham upapātakaviśeṣasyābhakṣyabhakṣaṇasya prāyaścittam abhidhāya jātibhraṃśakarasya brāhmaṇatiraskārasya prāyaścittam āha |
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvaṅkāraṃ ca garīyasaḥ || 51 ||
snātvā tiṣṭhann ahaḥśeṣam abhivādya prasādayet |
[453] iti | brāhmaṇo vedapārago brahmavid vā | taṃ prati laukike śāstrīye vā vyavahāre tadbhartsanāya huṅkāraṃ yaḥ prayuṅkte yaś ca vayasā vidyayā vā jyeṣṭhaṃ puruṣṃ prati tvam ity ekavacanaṃ prayuṅkte tāv ubhau snātvā yāvad astamayaṃ nirāhārau sthitvā rātrāv abhivādanena taṃ kṣamāpayetām | tatra nirāhāratvaṃ manur āha |
hiṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvaṅkāraṃ tu garīyasaḥ |
snātvānaśnann ahaḥśeṣam abhivādya prasādayet || iti | (MDh 11.204)
yamo ‘pi |
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
ahorātroṣitaḥ snātvā praṇipatya prasādayet || iti | (YaSm 2.6)
śaṅkho ‘pi |
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvaṅkāraṃ ca garīyasaḥ |
dinam ekaṃ vrataṃ kuryāt prayataḥ susamāhitaḥ || iti | (ŚaṅSm 17.50–51)
etad abhyāsaviṣayam |
nanu – [454] “brāhmaṇasya rujaḥkṛtyā” (MDh 11.68) iti jātibhraṃśakareṣu parigaṇanāḍ vakṣyamāṇaṃ tāḍanādikam eva jātibhraṃśakaram | na tūktam, iti cet |
na, huṅkārādināpi brāhmaṇasya manasi ruja utpadanāt || 51 ||
tāḍanādau prāyaścittam āha |
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi kaṇṭhe badhvāpi vāsasā || 52 ||
vivādenāpi nirjitya praṇipatya prasādayet | iti |
yady api tṛṇatāḍanavastrabandhanavivādajayair na śarīropaghātaḥ tathāpi prāyaścittaṃ caritavyam ity api śabdasyārthaḥ | praṇipātenopavāso ‘py upalakṣyate | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
guruṃ tvaṃkṛtya garveṇa vipraṃ nirjitya vādataḥ |
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi prasādyopavased dinam || iti | (BṛSm 2.27)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
guruṃ tvaṃkṛtya hiṃkṛtya vipraṃ nirjitya vādataḥ |
baddhvā vā vāsasā kṣipraṃ prasādyopavased dinam || iti | (YDh 3.292)
yat tu baudhāyanenoktam,
vādena brāhmaṇaṃ jitvā prāyaścittavidhitsayā |
trirātropoṣitaḥ snātvā praṇipatya prasādayet || (BDh 4.1.5)
[455] iti, tad abhyāsaviṣayam | pādasparśādau sa evāha |
pādena brāhmaṇaṃ spṛṣṭvā prāyaścittavidhitsayā |
divasopoṣitaḥ snātvā praṇipatya prasādayet ||
avācyaṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate |
kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ jitvā tu praṇipatya prasādayet ||
ākrośam anṛtaṃ kṛtvā kṛcchraṃ kurvīta mānavaḥ | iti | (BDh 4.1.8–9)
|| 52 ||
brāhmaṇāvagūraṇādau prāyaścittam āha |
avagūrya tv ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ kṣitipātane || 53 ||
atikṛcchraṃ ca rudhire krcchro ‘bhyantaraśoṇite | iti |
avagūrya vadhārthaṃ daṇḍam udyamya dinam ekam upavaset | bhūmau nipātya trirātram upavaset | prahāreṇa rudhire nirgate atikṛcchraṃ caret | anirgataṃ rudhiram antar ekatra ghanībhūtaṃ cet tadā kṛcchraṃ caret | avagūraṇādeḥ śatasaṃvatsarādinarakahetutvopanyāsa-purahsaraṃ pratiṣedhaḥ pratyakṣaśrutāv upalabhyate | yathā ca taittirīyabrāhmaṇam: [456] “yo ‘vaguret śatena yātayāt yo nihanat sahasreṇa yātayāt yo lohitaṃ karavat yāvataḥ praskandya pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāt tāvataḥ saṃvatsarān pitṛlokaṃ na prajānād iti tasmād brāhmaṇāya nāvaguret na nihanyāt na lohitaṃ kuryād etāvatā hainasā yukto bhavati” iti (TS 2.6.10) | gautamo ‘pi: “abhikruddhyāvagūraṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya varṣaśatam asvargyam | nipāte sahasram | lohitadarśane yāvataḥ praskandya pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyāt” (GDh 22.20–22) [457] iti | manur api |
avagūrya tv abdaśataṃ sahasram abhihatya tu |
jighāṃsayā brāhmaṇasya narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇīyād dvijanmanaḥ |
tāvanty abdasahasrāṇi tatkartā narake vaset ||
avagūrya caret kṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam || iti | (MDh 11.206–08)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vipradaṇḍodyame kṛcchas tv atikṛcchro nipātane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchro ‘sṛkpāte kṛcchro ‘bhyantaraśoṇite || iti | (YDh 3.293)
yamaḥ |
[458]        tāḍane chedane caiva śoṇitasya pravartane |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta cāndrāyaṇam athācaret ||
daśasaṃkhyāś ca gā dadyur aṅgacchedo yadā bhavet | iti | (YaSm 5.4–5)
bṛhaspatiḥ |
kāṣṭhādinā tāḍayitvā tvagbhede kṛcchram ācaret |
asthibhede ‘tikṛcchras tu parākas tv aṅṅakartane || iti |
paiṭhīnasir api: “tvaṃkārahuṃkārāvagūraṇanipātanalohitapravartanottarajayeṣu praṇipātaikarātropavāsakṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ caritvā prasādayet yathāsaṃkhyam” iti | etat sarvaṃ sajātīyaviṣayam | vijātīye tu prajāpatir āha |
[459]        dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva caturguṇam athāpi vā |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ brāhmaṇasya vadhe vratam || iti |
ākrośādau śaṅkhalikhitāv āhatuḥ: “ākrośe ‘nṛtavāde vā ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ copavāsaḥ” iti | sumantuḥ: “devarṣigobrāhmaṇācāryamātṛpitṛnarendrāṇāṃ pratiṣedhane ākrośane jihvāṃ dahed dhiraṇyaṃ datvā” iti || 53 ||
“atikṛcchraṃ ca rudhire” (ParSm 11.54) ity uktam | ko ‘sāv atikṛcchraḥ – ity ākāṅkṣāyāṃ tatsvarūpam āha |
navāham atikṛcchrī syāt pāṇipūrānnabhojanaḥ || 54 ||
trirātram upavāsī syād atikṛcchraḥ sa ucyate | iti |
atikṛcchram ācaran vipras trirāvṛttaikabhaktanaktāyācitadineṣu navasu yāvatā pāṇiḥ pūryate tāvad eva bhūñjīta | upavāsadineṣu prājāpatyavad upavāsam eva caret | so ‘yam atikṛcchra ity ucyate | etad evābhipretya yājñavalkyaḥ prājāpatyam upadiśya tasyaiva guṇavikṛtitvenātikṛcchraṃ nirdiśati |
[460]        ayam evātikṛcchraḥ syāt pāṇipūrānnabhojanaḥ | iti | (YDh 3.320)
gautamo ‘pi prājāpatyaṃ prapañcyānantaram āha: “etenaivātikṛcchro vyākhyātaḥ” iti (GDh 26.18) | yad vā nairantaryeṇa navasu dineṣu pāṇipūrānnaṃ bhuktvā triṣu dineṣu upavaset | tad āha yamaḥ |
ekaikaṃ piṇḍam aśnīyāt tryahaṃ kalpe tryahaṃ niśi |
ayācitas tryahaṃ caiva vāyubhakṣaḥ paraṃ tryaham ||
atikṛcchraṃ cared etat pavitraṃ pāpanāśanam | iti | (YaSm 3.71–72)
|| 54 ||
“atimānād atikrodhāt” (ParSm 4.1) ity ārabhya “kṛcchro ‘bhyantaraśoṇite” (ParSm 11.54) ity anteṣv aṣṭasv adhyāyeṣu nānāvidhān pāpaviśeṣān anūdya prāyaścittaviśeṣā nirūpitāḥ | yāni tv anyāni pratipadoktaprāyaścittarahitāni śaṅkitāni teṣāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ prāyaścittam āha |
sarveṣām eva pāpānāṃ saṃkare samupasthite || 55 ||
daśasāhasram abhyastā gāyatrī śodhanaṃ param | iti |
ayaṃ ca gāyatrījapaḥ prājāpatyādīnām upalakṣakaḥ | [461] tāni ca pratipadoktaprāyaścittavyatiriktāni vratāni munibhiḥ prapañcitāni | tatra atri-manu-viṣṇu-viśvāmitriāḥ |
anuktaniṣkṛtīnāṃ tu pāpānām apanuttaye |
śaktiṃ cāvekṣya pāpaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || iti | (MD 11.209)
yājñavalkyaḥ |
deśaṃ kālaṃ vayaḥ śaktiṃ pāpaṃ cāvekṣye yatnataḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ prakalpyaṃ syād yatra coktā na niṣkṛtiḥ || iti | (YDh 3.294)
śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “krayavikrayaduṣṭabhojanapratigraheṣv anādiṣṭaprāyaścitteṣu sarveṣu cāndrāyaṇaṃ prājāpatyaṃ ca” iti | smṛtyantare |
[462]        bhakṣyābhakṣyāṇy anekāni brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
tatra śiṣṭā yathā brūyus tat kartavyam iti sthitiḥ || iti |
śātātapaḥ |
anukteṣu vidhiṃ jñātvā prājāpatyaṃ samācaret |
sarvatra sarvapāpeṣu dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti | (ŚāSm 6.3)
uśanāḥ |
yathoktaṃ yatra vā noktam iha pātakanāśanam |
prājāpatyena kṛcchreṇa śudhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti | (UśSm 3.19)
|| 55 ||
abhojye bhojyatvapramitim abhisaṃkalpya manasā
prasaktiṃ svācchandyāḍ iha vidadhataḥ saṃsmṛtim ataḥ |
mataṃ prāyaścittaṃ dadhatam imam ekādaśam asāv
akārṣīd adhyāyaṃ sphuṭavivaraṇaṃ mādhavasudhīḥ ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājavaidikamārgapravartaka-
parameśvaraśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyaduhrandharasya
mādhavāmātyasya kṛtau parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ
mādhavīyāyāṃ ekādaśo ‘dhyāhaḥ || 11 ||
